# Learnings from the proof-architecture experimental thread

> Companion to [PROOF_ARCHITECTURES_PLAN.md](PROOF_ARCHITECTURES_PLAN.md) (test plan and status table) and the per-architecture READMEs. This document synthesizes what the experiments collectively **teach** about the four candidate proof architectures and the RH landscape, organized by finding rather than by architecture. Updated as new experiments complete.

The companion documents answer "what did each experiment do?". This one answers "what do they collectively tell us?". When a new experiment lands, look here for the cross-cutting interpretation.

---

## Cross-cutting findings

### 189. THE TURNAROUND HUNT: CERTIFIED NO-TURNAROUND THROUGH $a = 4$, RECORDED THROUGH $a = 5$: THE (1.2) OBJECT COLLAPSES, AND THE CONJECTURE'S FATE NOW RESTS ON THE KERNEL-GROUNDSTATE PROXIMITY (e2au, 2026-08-20/21 overnight): B2c-deep2 executed twice ($T = 600$ exploratory, $T = 1500$ certified; 1069 zeros at 110 digits; dps-110 solves to $J = 274$); frontier UNMOVED; P12's Section 6 has its content. Round: BUILDER; [`e2au_turnaround_ladder.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2au_turnaround_ladder.py) (incremental npz tracked), dossier [`e2au_turnaround_ladder.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2au_turnaround_ladder.md). **(i) THE VERDICT.** No turnaround anywhere in $a = 2.5..5.0$: $r(z{=}6)$ pinned at zero from $a = 3$, $r(z{=}4)$ dead by $a = 4$, $r(z{=}2)$ down to $0.075$ by $a = 5$: the unconstrained localized Weil-form ground state: settled at source (#187) as the object of Suzuki's (1.2): collapses monotonically toward an origin spike after its $a \approx 1$ xi-transient (#184). CERTIFIED through $a = 4$ ($\lambda \approx 55$): gates 0.006-0.040, mixing $2\times10^{-11}$ to $4\times10^{-5}$; recorded at $a = 4.5$ (basis gate 0.134) and $a = 5$ (mixing ~1; $\lambda \approx 148$). **(ii) THREE CROSS-VALIDATIONS IN ONE RUN.** The tail arithmetic predicted its own cure twice ($a = 2.5$ mixing: 9.0 at $T=350 \to 9\times10^{-5}$ at $600$; the ladder through $a = 4$ cleaned at $1500$ exactly on schedule); the ratios are BIT-STABLE across the $T = 600 \to 1500$ depth change on every rung; margins fall smoothly $10^{-48.7} \to 10^{-60.3}$ with healthy gaps. **(iii) THE DICHOTOMY, worded with maximal care.** CCM's Lemma 7.3 PROVES $\hat k_\lambda \to \Xi$ interior for their constrained kernel; our certified collapse of the unconstrained bottom through $\lambda \approx 55$ can coexist with it ONLY IF the kernel-groundstate proximity CCM observed at $\lambda \le 6$ fails at larger $\lambda$: otherwise the conjectured limit needs restating (no $c_a$ rescues pointwise convergence off ratios sitting at zero across 2.5 decades of window growth). The decisive instrument is B2c-prox (build $k_\lambda$ ourselves, measure the proximity directly: backlog, HIGH); this round claims exactly the certified collapse and nothing about the kernel. **(iv) INFRASTRUCTURE HANDSHAKE.** The parallel #188 round's tools slot straight in: `zero_polish` makes the next depth doubling (certifying $a = 4.5$-$5$ at $T \sim 3000$) cheap, and `certified_eig`'s precision-starvation detector formalizes this arc's #184/#185 failure class for B2c-prox. Cross-refs: #187 (the mission and the object identification), #185/#184 (the transient and the protocol), #186 (the constraint control and the boundary fact), #188 (the tools), P12 (the note this completes measurement for).

### 188. THE SEQUENCE LEVEL IS ARITHMETIC-BLIND BELOW ITS COLLISION HORIZON, AND THE HORIZON HAS A LAW: CERTIFYING NON-LATTICE-NESS AT SCALE $D$ COSTS $\sim\sqrt{2DL}$ ATOMS (e1ad, 2026-08-20): the sequence-level half of the Christoffel-corpus sweep (next-step 3; the Killip-Simon/Szego register the #172 adversary split off as the surviving half) EXECUTED; both pre-registrations resolved by their own armed kills; #172's pointwise obstruction EXTENDS to the sequence level with a measured price on the horizon; frontier UNMOVED. Round: BUILDER; [`e1ad_sum_rules.py`](spectral/e1ad_sum_rules.py) (21/21 full, 296.5 s; 21/21 quick; npz tracked), dossier [`e1ad_sum_rules.md`](spectral/e1ad_sum_rules.md); no adversary round has run on this rung and no second party has read it. **(i) THE INSTRUMENT.** The truncated Szego sum $S_n = -\sum_{j\le n}\log(1-|\alpha_j|^2) = -\log\min\{\|P\|^2_{L^2(\mu)} : P \text{ monic},\ \deg P = n+1\}$ on finite atomic circle measures: the OPUC twin of e1v's reciprocal Christoffel function (leading-coefficient-normalized where e1v was point-normalized), diverging at $n = M-1$ for $M$ atoms; the finite-$n$ divergence RATE along the diagonal $n \to M-2$ with the measure growing is the sequence-level observable, mirroring #171's Christoffel growth (equal spacing calibrates to exactly zero, so the profile is a pure microstructure meter). Classes under mandatory band equalization: TRUE ($\{k\log p \le \log N\}$ wrapped), SNAP (TRUE on $(1/D)\mathbb{Z}$, $D = 10^6$), RAND, BEUR (the shared Beurling control, by import), plus STRAT/GPERM/JIT typing surrogates; $M = 78/192/465$. Hygiene: two independent routes (Szego recursion vs own Toeplitz Cholesky) agree to $6.5\times10^{-100}$; a declared-then-measured conditioning rule with full dps+40 re-runs of the whole pipeline drifting $\le 3.3\times10^{-51}$; termination detected by a relative cliff, not an absolute threshold (an absolute test silently misses once conditioning eats the margin, measured); no L-function zero list anywhere (teeth-verified source scan). **(ii) [P1] REFUTED AT THE LETTER, AND THE ARMED KILL TYPES THE SPREAD.** The pre-registered family-blindness [P1] ($\le 1.35\times$ on both rates at every size) is refuted: the four-class spread is 1.53-1.76x on the diagonal rate and 2.14-4.00x on the mid-profile rate, with every one of the 8 RAND seed-size pairs at least 1.91x above TRUE's $r_{\rm mid}$: the truncated Szego rate is NOT family-blind at matched (count, support, density). Then [P2] (a stable rate gap = arithmetic content) fires and is KILLED by its own pre-registered sanity check: GPERM, the lattice-destroyed twin carrying TRUE's exact local gap multiset (blocks of 16) and no arithmetic, reproduces 95.6-99.9 percent of the spread and stands 0.97-1.00x of TRUE's own distance from the Beurling fake at every size. The functional reads LOCAL SPACING STATISTICS: the wrapped prime log-lattice is more rigid than Poisson at matched density, and BEUR interpolates via its $\pm0.25$ scrambling scale (visibly why its diagonal rate converges to TRUE's at $N = 3000$, 0.3204 vs 0.3269, while its mid rate stays at RAND's level: the two observables read different scale mixtures). All PNT-level, Level 2-3 in the four-level framing; no arithmetic claim survives. The gauge face (golden-mean wrap) preserves the ordering. **(iii) THE Q-AXIS IS NULL BELOW THE HORIZON.** Snapping TRUE onto $(1/D)\mathbb{Z}$ at $D = 10^6$ moves the per-atom diagonal rate by $\le 1.1\times10^{-6}$ and the whole-profile response sits inside the pinned 4x of the matched-amplitude jitter band at every size (ratios 2.5 / 0.8 / 3.1): rationality is, to this functional, a GENERIC perturbation of amplitude $1/(2D)$, indistinguishable in kind from noise. #172's V5 control transported to the sequence level, below the horizon. **(iv) THE HORIZON LAW (the positive content).** D-ladder at $M = 192$: snapping onto $(1/D)\mathbb{Z}$ merges atoms at exact angular collisions ($M_d$ distinct sites), and the Verblunsky recursion terminates at EXACTLY $n = M_d - 1$ on all five collision rungs ($D = 40/100/400/2000/10^4$, $M_d = 40/94/163/188/191$; detection $n^* = 39/90/116/153/159$ fires at or before it), while at $D \ge 10^5$ (zero collisions) the ladder is blind. So the sequence level sees $\mathbb{Q}$-dependence precisely when it collapses the measure's RANK, and since collisions among $M$ atoms on $\sim DL$ sites appear at the birthday scale $M \gtrsim \sqrt{2DL}$, the register is PRICED: certifying non-membership in the scale-$D$ lattice costs $\sim\sqrt{2DL}$ atoms and comparable sequence depth; $\mathbb{Q}$-linear independence proper is the $D \to \infty$ limit and costs unbounded data. This CONFIRMS the #172 adversary's correction (the limit IS arithmetic-sensitive; the mechanism exists and is exactly rank collapse) while extending the obstruction quantitatively below the horizon. **(v) DISCIPLINES AND SCOPE.** Beurling enters by import, passes every construction step identically, and its one separation is typed by the kill as genuine gap-distribution data below its scrambling scale: nothing here consumes the additive lattice and nothing pretends to (the conservation law respected, not dodged). D-H is out of scope by construction (a counting-side object built from prime data; no FE, no zeros consumed); K1 does not arise. Scope, held honestly: the blindness claim is measured for Szego-type rate functionals of the Verblunsky/moment data; the horizon mechanism is gauge-rational (exact collisions need $DL$ commensurate; at irrational wrap the mechanism would be three-gap/near-collision structure, NOT measured); all finite data is rational, so the experiment grades lattice SCALE (the TRUE-vs-SNAP distinction at finite budget is a distinction between horizons $\sqrt{2D}$ and $\sqrt{2\cdot10^{\rm dps}}$, not between completed totalities); the conditioning coefficient is measured only to $M \sim 500$. Handed forward: (1) the HORIZON-OPTIMALITY question, the sharpest residue: is the birthday scale optimal among ALL functionals of the first $n$ moments, or does a super-resolution register (Prony/ESPRIT, plausibly down to $n \sim DL/\pi$ but no further) beat it; if the information-theoretic wall holds, the sequence-level door closes in principle for this gauge and #172 becomes a two-level theorem (pointwise: continuity vs density; sequence: information vs scale). (2) The rigidity meter: $r_{\rm mid}({\rm TRUE}) = 0.0025$-$0.0098$ is a clean number-rigidity observable of the wrapped prime log-lattice; relating it to an explicit-formula prediction would type this register's residual arithmetic content exactly (expected: Level 2-3, zero-density not zero-location). (3) The BEUR scale-mixture mechanism: vary $\varepsilon$ and watch the diagonal-vs-mid crossover, a cheap confirm-or-kill. (4) The Killip-Simon $\ell^2$ partials, computed and stored, unexploited. Four finite/elementary VERIFIER targets (the extremal identity; the rank/termination law = the theorem form of the horizon mechanism; equal-spacing nullity; the fixed-index continuity extension of #172) and six adversary cases are named in the dossier. Cross-refs: #172 (the split this executes and the pointwise obstruction this extends), #171 (the Christoffel-growth wall the diagonal rate mirrors), #170 (the Killip-Simon template first named), #175(v)-(vi) (the equilibrium-discrepancy and finite-$M$ line this register neighbors), #152 (the Beurling discipline this respects).

### 187. B2c-obj SETTLED AT SOURCE: (1.2)'S OBJECT IS THE UNCONSTRAINED BOTTOM (OURS), THE CCM-NUMERICS TENSION DISSOLVES, AND DEEP2'S MISSION SHARPENS TO THE TURNAROUND WINDOW $a^*$ (source reading of CCM [4], 2026-08-20, following #186 within the hour): frontier UNMOVED; #185 now applies DIRECTLY to the conjecture's object; deep2 UNBLOCKED. Round: SURVEYOR (CCM 2511.22755 §3 read at source; passages quoted in this entry are verbatim from the extracted text). **(i) THE IDENTIFICATION.** CCM §3.1: "We denote by QWλ the restriction of the quadratic form QW to $L^2([\lambda^{-1}, \lambda], d^*u)$": the FULL window space, no constraint subspace; and their Proposition 3.2 map $\kappa(f)(u) = f(\log(\lambda u))$ is a WEIGHTLESS isometry $L^2([0,L], dx) \to L^2([\lambda^{-1},\lambda], d^*u)$, $L = 2\log\lambda$: so their additive picture is exactly ours ($a = \log\lambda$, no $e^{x/2}$ weight, standard Weil-distribution normalization). The vanishing-integral condition of #186 lives ONLY in their Section-7 approximant kernel $k_\lambda$ (needed for the E-map estimates in Lemma 7.3), NOT in the variational problem defining their ground state $\xi_\lambda$. Ergo (1.2)'s $v_a$ = the unconstrained bottom = the object #184/#185 measured with certificates. The e2at constrained ladder re-scopes to a control: the constrained bottom is a DIFFERENT object, and its explosion plus the $\Xi(i/2) = 1/2$ boundary fact stand as structural notes about the approximant space. **(ii) THE TENSION DISSOLVES.** CCM's numerical "proximity between $k_\lambda$ and $\xi_\lambda$" compares two FINITE-$\lambda$ objects, both of which may be narrowed relative to $\Xi$ at finite $\lambda$ (their $\hat k_\lambda \to \Xi$ is a $\lambda \to \infty$ interior limit, and their plotted evidence lives at $\mu = \lambda^2 \le 36$, i.e. $a \le 1.79$, where our measurements agree). No contradiction anywhere: our certified narrowing through $a = 2.5$ and their proven kernel limit COMPOSE into a sharp picture: **(iii) THE TURNAROUND WINDOW $a^*$.** For the kernel, $\hat k_\lambda \to \Xi$ interior is PROVEN (their Lemma 7.3), so the kernel's narrowing must reverse; if the kernel-groundstate proximity persists, the ground state's must too: there exists a turnaround $a^* > 2.5$ where the outbound narrowing (measured, #185) turns inbound toward $\Xi$ (proven-for-the-kernel). DEEP2'S MISSION, sharpened: locate $a^*$ on $a \in [3, 5]$ (zeros to $T \sim 600$ at 110 digits now computing, dps-110 solves, e2as gates), or measure WHERE the proximity fails: either is a first-of-its-kind quantitative contact with the conjecture's mechanism. Cross-refs: #186 (amended: the "identification question the literature has not posed" is now answered at source), #185 (now a direct statement about (1.2)'s object), #184 (the protocol), CCM [4] in references/10_weil_positivity.

### 186. THE LIT PULL AND THE CONSTRAINED LADDER: THE NAIVE RECONCILIATION REFUTED, THE BOUNDARY FACT FOUND ($\Xi(i/2) = 1/2$: XI VIOLATES THE POLE CONSTRAINT), AND THE (1.2) COMPARISON SPLIT INTO THREE OBJECTS (B2c-lit + e2at, 2026-08-20, same day as #185): frontier UNMOVED; #185's wording AMENDED (its narrowing verdict is a statement about the UNCONSTRAINED bottom; whether that is (1.2)'s object is now the sharp open question); deep2 BLOCKED behind the object identification. Round: SURVEYOR + BUILDER; CCM "Zeta spectral triples" (arXiv:2511.22755, Suzuki's [4]) pulled to [`references/10_weil_positivity/`](../references/10_weil_positivity/); [`e2at_constrained_xi.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2at_constrained_xi.py) (4/4 after refutation-typing, npz tracked), dossier [`e2at_constrained_xi.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2at_constrained_xi.md). **(i) THE LIT FINDING.** CCM [4] contains real numerics ($D_{\log}$ spectra vs the first twenty zeros; $e_n(\mu)$ prolate-comparison curves; kernel-vs-ground-state proximity) and a PROVEN interior convergence: their Lemma 7.3, $\hat k_\lambda \to \Xi$ uniformly on closed substrips of the OPEN strip $|\Im z| < 1/2$, for an explicit kernel built WITH the vanishing-integral (pole) condition. No direct measurement of the unconstrained bottom's shape: #185's "first certified probe" survives re-scoped. **(ii) THE REFUTATION.** The candidate explanation for #185's narrowing (we omitted the pole constraint) was tested by exact projection ($b_k = \hat\psi_k(i/2)$ closed-form, residuals $10^{-81}$): the constrained bottom does NOT restore the xi shape: its $z{=}0$-normalized ratios EXPLODE (17-319; $\hat v(0)$ suppressed, lobe off-center) and no rung passes the convergence gate at matched resolution. **(iii) THE STRUCTURAL FACT, mp-verified.** $\Xi(i/2) = \xi(0) = 1/2 \neq 0$: THE XI FUNCTION ITSELF VIOLATES THE POLE CONSTRAINT. (1.2)'s limit lives outside the constrained space; CCM's convergence is interior-only with the pinch squeezed to the boundary as $\lambda \to \infty$; at small windows the pinch contaminates the whole real axis, which is exactly the exploded-ratio phenomenology. **(iv) THE THREE-OBJECT SPLIT (the yield).** (a) The UNCONSTRAINED bottom (Suzuki's $A_a$ as literally defined): certified, converged, NARROWS (#185). (b) The POLE-CONSTRAINED bottom (CCM's kernel space): measured, unconverged at matched resolution, needs an INTERIOR fit ($z \in [4,10]$) and more basis. (c) CCM's explicit $k_\lambda$: constrained, provably $\to \Xi$ interior. Whether (1.2)'s $v_a$ is (a) or (b) is not obvious from the two papers' texts and the two bottoms differ GROSSLY at accessible windows: an identification question the literature has not posed and our instruments have now split experimentally. Handed forward: B2c-obj (read CCM's domain definitions at source; build the interior-fit constrained comparison with proper resolution), then B2c-deep2 on the settled object; the P12 arc gains the caught-own-naive-reconciliation chapter. Cross-refs: #185 (amended as above), #184 (the unconstrained protocol), #182 (the sweep this lit pull completes), #153 (rung 4's first contact with 2511.22755, now at full depth).

### 185. THE DEEP LADDER DECIDES IT: THE NARROWING IS REAL, THE XI SHAPE IS A TRANSIENT AT $a \approx 1$, AND THE APPROACH TO SUZUKI (1.2) IS NON-MONOTONE AT ACCESSIBLE WINDOWS (e2as, 2026-08-20, same day as #183/#184): B2c-deep executed at the measured requirements (169 zeros to $T=350$ at 80 digits, dps-80 solves, per-rung convergence gates); frontier UNMOVED; the arc #180-#185 now ends on a sharp externally-relevant statement. Round: BUILDER; [`e2as_deep_xi_ladder.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2as_deep_xi_ladder.py) (5/5, npz tracked), dossier [`e2as_deep_xi_ladder.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2as_deep_xi_ladder.md). **(i) THE VERDICT.** The 50-digit degeneracy at $a=2$ (#184's gate failure) was PRECISION STARVATION: at 80 digits the refined solve is healthy (gap 2080 vs 0.9) and the gates pass at $a = 1.0, 2.0, 2.5$ (shifts 0.026, 0.006, 0.041). The certified, converged $a = 2.0$ rung shows the ground state's FT at a FIFTH of $\Xi$'s relative mass at $z=6$ (ratio $+0.20$; $a=1$: $+1.12$, the xi shape, reproduced cross-precision to 0.03; $a=2.5$: $+0.03$, consistent continuation but its mixing certificate fails at 9.0 and it is scope-bounded out). The finite-$a$ hard-window Weil-form ground state passes THROUGH the xi shape near $a=1$ and narrows strictly below it. **(ii) WHAT IT MEANS, WORDED CAREFULLY.** If (1.2) is true the shape must RETURN at larger $a$: a concrete measurable prediction ($a = 3$-$5$ needs $T \sim 600$, dps 100-120: costed as B2c-deep2); if the trend continues, the conjectured limit needs modification (no normalization $c_a$ rescues pointwise convergence once fixed-$z$ ratios head to 0). Placement verified at source: Suzuki's own numerics support only HIS limit (1.12) (the $\xi/(\xi+\xi')$ object via the Lagrangian construction), not (1.2), which his paper calls conjectural and attributes to CCM's expectation; to our knowledge this is the FIRST certified numerical probe of (1.2)'s object at finite window, with the caveat held open that CCM's successor paper [4] (the determinant representation; not yet in the library) might carry numerics: the pull is queued and the wording is "first to our knowledge." Physical reading consistent with #180-#183: the quotient rewards hole-concentration; small-$a$ bandwidth forbids it (ratios above 1 at $a \le 1$); growing $a$ affords it while annihilation stays cheap; the xi shape appears exactly at the crossover, and a return would require the annihilation burden to eventually dominate, which is precisely what (1.2) implicitly claims. **(iii) PROTOCOL BANKED.** Per-quantity gating (eigenvalues keep falling under refinement while shapes converge); certificate-scoped claims with the scope boundary self-documented in the checks; zero-cache validated against RvM (169 vs 169.10). Handed forward: B2c-deep2 (decides return-vs-continuation: the (1.2) test where it must show itself); pull CCM [4] and re-run the novelty wording; fold into the P12 packaging case. Cross-refs: #184 (the walls this protocol was built from), #183 (the soft-family typing), #182 (the sweep and the live-corpus placement), #180/#181 (the margin laws the physical reading unifies).

### 184. THE HARD-WINDOW XI-TEST: AT THE CONVERGED WINDOW THE WEIL-FORM GROUND STATE IS THE XI SHAPE (e2ar, 2026-08-20, same day as #183): B2c-hard executed; the first positive numerical contact with Suzuki's conjecture (1.2), gated by three certificates each of which caught a real artifact; frontier UNMOVED. Round: BUILDER; [`e2ar_hard_window_xi.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2ar_hard_window_xi.py) (8/8, npz tracked), dossier [`e2ar_hard_window_xi.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2ar_hard_window_xi.md). **(i) THE INSTRUMENT.** Even degree-12 B-splines strictly inside $[-a,a]$: hard support, 13 orders of endpoint flatness, EXACT 50-digit closed forms for the FTs (sinc powers) and the $L^2$ Gram (degree-25 cardinal spline at integers): no quadrature in the form; zero side on the 50-digit zeros to $T=200$; knot spacing fixed so capacity scales with the window (the first run's fixed-$J$ ladder produced a rising residual that was pure capacity artifact, caught and corrected). **(ii) THE HEADLINE, GATED.** Rigidity is RESTORED by the hard support (within-family spectral gaps 849 to $8\times10^6$ against the soft family's degeneracy), and at the basis-converged window $a = 1$: $L^2$ residual $0.051$ against a fitted multiple of $\Xi$ on $[0,10]$, pointwise within 26 percent to $z = 8$, refinement-stable to $0.026$: versus the soft family's 33-154, the hard support is worth a factor $\sim10^3$: e2aq's family-dependence diagnosis confirmed constructively. Node locking persists ($10^{-18}$-$10^{-28}$ at $\gamma_1$). **(iii) THE THREE CERTIFICATES ALL BIT.** A-posteriori tail: the $a=3$ rung EXCLUDED (type-3 exploits the $T=200$ cutoff: tail $10^{-15}$ vs margin at the dps floor); eigenvector-mixing bound $\le 10^{-3}$ everywhere kept; the BASIS-CONVERGENCE GATE: double-density controls show $a=1$ shape-converged (shift 0.026) while $a=2$ shifts by 20.9 and its refined solve is genuinely degenerate (gap 0.9, $\lambda_0$ at the 50-digit floor): all $a \ge 1.5$ shape statements gated OUT; per-quantity lesson: the eigenvector converges before the eigenvalue (a=1's $\lambda_0$ still falls 3.5 decades under refinement while the shape holds). RECORDED WITHOUT CLAIM: the raw ladder's lobe narrows monotonically through the xi shape near $a \approx 1$ (the $z=6$ ratio $1.28 \to 0.01$); whether the continuum state stays on $\Xi$ (as (1.2) wants) or narrows is exactly what the deeper instrument must decide. **(iv) THE QUANTIFIED EXIT (B2c-deep).** Zeros to $T \gtrsim 350$ at 50+ digits, dps 80+, refinement-to-gate per rung: measured requirements, feasible on this box: the (1.2) test beyond $a \approx 1$ is now a precision-and-cutoff engineering problem, not a search. Cross-refs: #183 (the soft-family typing this fixes), #182 (the sweep that aimed both), #181 (the locking these instruments keep sharpening), the Suzuki/CCM corpus (references/10_weil_positivity).

### 183. THE XI-TEST AND THE OMEGA-LADDER: BOTH PRE-REGISTRATIONS REFUTED AND REPLACED BY SHARPER LAWS: EXACT ZERO-ANNIHILATION, THE GRADED FRONTIER, AND THE MARGIN AS THE LEAK AT THE CAPACITY EDGE (e2aq, 2026-08-20): backlog B2c and B2b executed on the 50-digit protocol; frontier UNMOVED; the C2 corridor gains a unified coordinates-level reading of what the window margin IS. Round: BUILDER; [`e2aq_xi_convergence.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2aq_xi_convergence.py) (10/10, npz tracked), dossier [`e2aq_xi_convergence.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2aq_xi_convergence.md). **(i) THE SOFT-WINDOW XI-TEST (Suzuki (1.2) analogue): FAMILY-DEPENDENCE TYPED.** The Gaussian-mode minimizer's central shape is NOT $\Xi$ (residual 33-154 at every scale, fitted scale sign-unstable), and the mechanism is visible: the Rayleigh quotient REWARDS norm-stuffing in the central hole, so the central lobe is nearly-degenerate noise: the hard-window operator's rigidity (CCM's unique ground state) is exactly what the soft family lacks. NOT evidence against (1.2); the faithful hard-window (sine-basis) test is queued (B2c-hard). What survives, SHARPENED: the zero-locking is EXACT AT WORKING PRECISION: with modes exceeding reachable zeros, nodes sit within $10^{-38}$-$10^{-41}$ of $\gamma_1,\gamma_2,\gamma_3$ at every scale (#181's $4\times10^{-3}$ was grid resolution). The (1.2) circle splits: locking is family-robust, the central lobe family-fragile: that split is where the conjecture's content lives. **(ii) THE NEAREST-GAP LAW IS DEAD; THE FRONTIER LAW REPLACES IT.** Same-gap rungs ($\Omega = 20$ vs $24$, gap $\approx 1.01$) differ by 1.5 decades; the margin RISES monotonically with $\Omega$ ($10^{-47.8} \to 10^{-37.3}$ over $\Omega = 15..42$: a collective density-edge effect); the gap$^2$ regressor is $\sigma$-blind. The real law, mechanism-checked: with the mode grid FIXED, $d(\ln m)/d(\sigma^2) = -85.2$ vs $-(\gamma_8 - \Omega)^2 = -87.0$ (2 percent): the margin is the Gaussian leak onto THE FIRST ZERO THE FAMILY CANNOT ANNIHILATE, and the annihilation frontier sits TWO zeros past the nominal ceiling (spare dimensions kill $\gamma_6, \gamma_7$) and is GRADED: node precision degrading $2\times10^{-35} \to 5\times10^{-29} \to 1\times10^{-23} \to 2\times10^{-10}$ across $\gamma_6..\gamma_9$, about six decades per zero. Instrument lesson with teeth: the naive $\sigma$-sweep has the OPPOSITE sign of the physical derivative ($+25$ vs $-85$: growing $J$ deepens annihilation faster than the Gaussian narrows), and #181's band-ceiling retrodiction ($-12 \approx -12.9$) is accordingly DOWNGRADED to coincidence (its two points had different mode counts). **(iii) THE UNIFIED READING.** Both margin laws are one statement at different capacities: the window margin is always a single-zero Gaussian leak at the family's annihilation-capacity edge: the single-mode family pays $\gamma_1$ (#180's $e^{-\gamma_1^2\sigma^2}$), a $J$-mode family pays $\gamma_{\mathrm{frontier}}(J)$, graded. Weil positivity's uniform statement, in these coordinates: however capacity grows, the leak onto the first unpaid zero never goes negative: every zero eventually paid exactly once. Handed forward: B2c-hard (the sine-basis test: where (1.2)'s content lives); the frontier-capacity function $\gamma_{\mathrm{frontier}}(J,\Omega,\sigma)$ (the graded profile suggests a clean per-zero cost); the #181 citation correction. Cross-refs: #182 (the sweep that aimed this round), #181 (sharpened twice and corrected once), #180 (the single-mode instance of the unified law), the Suzuki/CCM corpus (references/10_weil_positivity).

### 182. SP2 TESTED AT ITS HONEST CEILING, THE DESIGN MATRIX COMPLETED, AND THE MARGIN PROGRAM PLACED IN ITS LITERATURE (e2ap + the prior-art sweep, 2026-08-20): completeness certified at scale inside the object's own coordinates, Euler sourcing verified constructively on the S-finite ladder, the Cramer control lands the pre-registered third point on the descent axis, and the deep prior-art sweep finds our margin/ground-state territory is Yoshida's 1992 localization program, ACTIVE as of two days ago; frontier UNMOVED. Turing-method epistemology rebuilt inside the object's coordinates (known in substance; the coordinates are the point); left-tail term empirically anchored, interval-arithmetic upgrade named for VERIFIER. **(ii) EULER SOURCING, CONSTRUCTIVE (SP2(a)).** $P$-smooth lattices to $e^{50}$: transform $=$ product of local factors to $3.9\times10^{-6}$ at every rung $P = 2..13$; the $\gamma_1$ dip condenses $0.586 \to 0.167$ monotonically while bounded away from zero at every finite $P$; no pole (zero density) and duality defect $\ge 0.23$ at every rung: the S-finite column of the conservation law, with its in-print home cited (Gonek 2007: RH iff short Euler products approximate $\zeta$; GHK hybrid product = the two-meter law's cousin). **(iii) THE CRAMER CONTROL (backlog A1) completes the 2x2.** Jittered integers ($x + O(1)$ counting EXACT, multiplicativity destroyed): residue $1.0005$ at exponent $0.500$; duality defect $0.460$ (the FE is not purchasable by perfect density: the fourth clause survives its pre-registered kill); descent drift $6.9\times10^{-4}$, STRICTLY BETWEEN zeta ($2.1\times10^{-12}$) and Beurling ($0.51$), exactly as pre-registered ($e^{s}$-rate degradation without divergence). All four corners of (Euler $\times$ lattice) now measured through one pipeline. **(iv) THE PLACEMENT (the sweep's verdicts).** The margin-per-unit-$L^2$ object (#180) IS Yoshida 1992's Rayleigh-quotient program (small-window positivity is Yoshida's theorem; Bombieri 2000 proved attainment and the negative-eigenvalue count $=$ half the off-line zeros: the theory behind our indefiniteness detectors); the ZERO-LOCKING (#181(i)) is the empirical face of CCM's real-zeros theorem for the localized ground state and of SUZUKI'S CONJECTURE (1.2) ($c_a\hat v_a \to \xi(1/2+iz)$), in a corpus whose unifier (arXiv:2606.09096) went to v2 on 2026-08-18: LIVE; the two quantitative laws (the $4\sqrt\pi\,\sigma e^{-\gamma_1^2\sigma^2}$ closed form and the band-ceiling saturation) did NOT surface in the corpus and stand as plausibly ours, flagged to the Fourier-optimization school before stronger claims; the Cramer control has no direct prior instance found. HANDED FORWARD, the sweep's best gift: **B2c, the xi-convergence test**: our 50-digit minimizer machinery can test Suzuki (1.2) directly (overlay $\hat g^*_\sigma$ against $\Xi$, measure the convergence). ### 181. THE VISUAL INSTRUMENTS: THE MINIMIZER LOCKS ONTO THE ZEROS AT EVERY SCALE, AND THE MULTI-MODE MARGIN IS GOVERNED BY THE BAND CEILING, NOT THE WINDOW WIDTH (visualizations/research, 2026-08-20): a visualization round (Owen-directed: gallery of 20 themed figures + animations, [`visualizations/research/`](../visualizations/research/)) whose two INSTRUMENT entries returned three measured facts that refine #180; frontier UNMOVED. **(i) UNIVERSAL ZERO-LOCKING.** The Weil form's per-unit-$L^2$ minimizer on the modulated-Gaussian window (zeros to $T=200$, basis small enough for full rank) places a node on EVERY zero its frequency band reaches, to $4\times10^{-3}$, at every scale $\sigma \in [0.2, 0.55]$; its surplus nodes (0/6 at $\sigma=0.2$ growing to 13/32 at $0.55$) park inside the central hole where the form cannot charge them. The near-failure direction's anatomy: interpolate-vanish on the spectrum, spend the leftover freedom in the hole. **(ii) THE SECOND LAW.** With combinations allowed, the margin SATURATES in $\sigma$: 50-digit solves give $10^{-37.9}$ at $\sigma=0.45$ and $10^{-38.4}$ at $0.55$, implied exponent $-12$ per $\sigma^2$ against the single-mode law's $-\gamma_1^2 = -199.8$ (#180). Mechanism: the minimizer cancels every reachable zero and pays only the Gaussian leak past the frequency CEILING $\Omega = 34$ onto the first unreachable zero, so the honest scaling variable for the full test space is $\Omega$, not $\sigma$: the window-width law (#180) and the band-ceiling law are DIFFERENT laws, and RH's uniform statement lives in the $\Omega \to \infty$ direction. Handed forward: the $\Omega$-ladder (measure margin($\Omega$) at fixed generous $\sigma$ with per-rung precision matched to the predicted floor; pre-register the exponent against the gap-to-next-zero prediction). **(iii) THE EIGENVECTOR-LEVEL CERTIFICATION COST.** Beyond $\sigma \approx 0.45$ the margin sits so far below double precision that a naive generalized-eigen solve returns an arbitrary member of a numerical null cone (the first run's $\sigma \ge 0.5$ "shapes" were artifacts, caught by their own $\Delta\omega$-grid oscillation); moreover fp-accurate ZEROS cannot support an eigenvalue at $10^{-38}$ (entries perturb at $10^{-16}$), so the honest instrument runs 50-digit arithmetic ON 50-digit zeros (disk-cached): #180's certification-cost law biting at the eigenvector level, with the working protocol recorded in [`make_figs.py`](../visualizations/research/make_figs.py) (`ground_state_mp`). Also banked from the round: the zeros-hear-primes spike instrument (8,880 locally computed zeros; spikes at $\log$ prime powers with heights tracking $\Lambda(n)/\sqrt{n}$: the statistics DO carry arithmetic yet consume only heights: the sharpest visual form of the #174 blindness statement), and an accuracy audit of all 20 entries against ground truth (one real defect found and fixed: the Riemann-Siegel engine misused below $t \sim 2\pi$ in the first polar figure; the audited gallery is the deliverable). Cross-refs: #180 (both laws), #174 (the blindness the spike plot sharpens), e1y (the near-null ground state this instrument now images for zeta).

### 180. THE SCALING LADDER: THE WINDOW MARGIN HAS A CLOSED FORM, AND CERTIFYING IT PRIME-SIDE COSTS $e^{\gamma_1^2\sigma^2}$ (e2ao, 2026-08-19, same day as #179): the #179 hand-off executed; the round's own registered prediction REFUTED by the run and replaced by a sharper law; C1 does not bind at the tested meters; the D-H invisibility is scale-robust; frontier UNMOVED (measurement on the v0 object). Round: BUILDER; [`e2ao_scaling_ladder.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2ao_scaling_ladder.py) (16/16, npz tracked), dossier [`e2ao_scaling_ladder.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2ao_scaling_ladder.md). **(i) THE MARGIN LAW (SP5 axis, the C2 number).** For the modulated-Gaussian window family $g_\omega = e^{-x^2/2\sigma^2}\cos\omega x$, the Weil-form bottom per unit $L^2$ mass obeys $\mathrm{margin}(\sigma) = 4\sqrt{\pi}\,\sigma\,e^{-\gamma_1^2\sigma^2}(1+O(e^{-\sigma^2(\gamma_2^2-\gamma_1^2)}))$: measured slope $-199.79$ vs $-\gamma_1^2 = -199.79$, intercept $1.959$ vs $\ln(4\sqrt\pi) = 1.959$, $R^2 = 1.000000$, over 38 orders of magnitude ($4.8\times10^{-4}$ at $\sigma=0.2$ to $1.5\times10^{-42}$ at $\sigma=0.7$). The registered guess (dodge into the midgap at $\omega^* = \gamma_1/2$, exponent $(\gamma_1/2)^2 \approx 49.9$) was WRONG: measured $\omega^* = 0$ at every rung. Reason, doing real work: THE POLE DOES NOT PENALIZE THE UNMODULATED BUMP because the explicit formula cancels the pole term against primes+archimedean exactly (#179's $H^0$ cancellation), so the deepest spectral hole is the full central gap $(-\gamma_1,\gamma_1)$ and the margin is carried by the first zero alone. Given $\omega^*=0$ the closed form is elementary; the finding is $\omega^*=0$, the hole-around-the-cancelled-pole geometry, and the price: **(ii) THE CERTIFICATION COST (the C2 price).** The prime side assembles this exponentially small positive number out of $O(1)$ pole/arch/prime terms that cancel; at assembly accuracy $\varepsilon$ it certifies only $\sigma^2 < \ln(c/\varepsilon)/\gamma_1^2$. Measured: bottom rung certified ($\sigma = 0.2$: prime side matches the exact margin to $3.8\times10^{-7}$), floor crossed at $\sigma = 0.3$, every rung beyond saturated. Certifying Weil positivity prime-side at window scale $\sigma$ costs $e^{\gamma_1^2\sigma^2}$ in assembly precision: the determinant-class clause (M4's trace-formula name) PRICED at finite scale, in the object's own coordinates. Instrument honesty banked as design constraints: generalized margins below the $G$-conditioning floor are meaningless; over-complete mode sets make the truncated zero-side Gram rank-deficient (multi-mode bottom = 0 below fp at every rung, checked as such); margins must be Rayleigh quotients per unit $L^2$ mass or basis redundancy fakes marginality (retro-reads v0's raw $-1.9\times10^{-16}$). Scope: the law is the single-mode family's floor, an upper bound on the full-space infimum where Weil positivity's RH-equivalence actually lives; coordinates, not a positivity theorem. **(iii) C1 DOES NOT BIND AT THE TESTED METERS (SP4 axis).** $x_0 = 2..6$ (primes to 403) at the fixed spectral meter (emergent spectrum to $T=100$ at $10^{-4}$): the two-sidedness residual is FLAT at $2\times10^{-8}$-$1\times10^{-7}$ on truth AND on the object's own spectrum (max rel $3.9\times10^{-8}$): every tested prime window closes; the C1 gap sits below this floor throughout, so finding where it binds needs the coupled ladder ($x_0$ and the zero-side ceiling grown together: the two-meter law's growth direction). **(iv) THE CARRIER AXIS.** $L = 8..16$: descent identity $\le 9.7\times10^{-10}$ everywhere; $|m|$ at the nearest grid point tracks the grid offset (corr 0.68-0.88); and the D-H off-line landmark stays invisible at EVERY circumference (rel $|m|$ 0.238-0.413, vs $4\times10^{-4}$ dips at genuine zeros): the completeness failure of the control is not a one-scale artifact. Handed forward: the certification-cost statement as a theorem about this assembly (propagate the quadrature/truncation constants to the floor $\sigma$); the coupled SP4 ladder; the invisibility certificate as a provable coefficient-side lower bound (unchanged from #179). Cross-refs: #179 (the object, its $H^0$ cancellation, the hand-off), #148 (the determinant-class clause this prices), e3v/e3t (the marginality this closed-forms at window scale), #172 (the previous "rate depends only on coarse data" pattern, here with the rate an explicit arithmetic constant $\gamma_1^2$).

### 179. THE SP-OBJECT ASSEMBLED AT FINITE SCALE (e2an, 2026-08-19): the five-component interface has its FIRST BUILT INSTANCE, wrong on purpose, and the wrongness localizes at exactly the two open joints C1/C2 with numbers attached; the conservation law becomes a per-component witness table from one pipeline; frontier UNMOVED by design. Round: BUILDER on Owen's directive ("build one, even if it's wrong"); [`e2an_sp_object_v0.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2an_sp_object_v0.py) (27/27 full, npz tracked), dossier [`e2an_sp_object_v0.md`](arithmetic_geometric/e2an_sp_object_v0.md). **(i) THE ENGINE IS ONE IDENTITY.** Muntz's formula makes the CCM lattice map $\mathcal{E}(f)(u) = u^{1/2}\sum a_n f(nu)$ act DIAGONALLY on the critical line with multiplier $m(\tau) = L(1/2+i\tau)$ computed from the integers alone: extracted $m$ matches $\zeta$ to 2.5e-12 and D-H to 7.7e-14 with an oracle-call counter proving no L-value or zero was consumed (K1-clean by construction, not by claim). The carrier is one circle of circumference $L$ at critical sampling (Fourier grid = Mellin grid), the descent identity fold-equals-sample verified to 3.3e-10: B1 rung 4's door realized as running code. The cokernel of the map is an honest $H^1$ and the flow compressed to it has the ZEROS AS EMERGENT SPECTRUM: all 29 zeros to $T=100$ from integer data, max error 1.25e-4, zero spurious detections, scale-stability 7e-15. The pole is cohomology the carrier sees: the unregularized descent diverges at exactly $e^{S/2}$ (measured exponent 0.5000) with lattice-extracted residue = the counting density (zeta 1.0005; D-H 2.7e-17, entire; Beurling 1.5856 vs its independently counted 1.5870). **(ii) THE BRACKET GOES COMPONENTWISE.** One pipeline, three systems: D-H passes every lattice/FE cell (duality real to 2.2e-11 with its OWN Gamma factor; 100 percent of its on-line zeros found) and fails every Euler cell ($b_6 = 1.936$, 1475 support leaks off prime powers; prime-side trace formula unbuildable) plus completeness: THE OFF-LINE PAIR IS INVISIBLE, $|m_{DH}|$ at the 85.7 landmark staying at 0.242 of local scale against 4e-4 dips at genuine zeros, a 600x contrast: the object's blindness to off-line zeros EXHIBITED, which is the RH-sensitivity statement in one number. Beurling exactly dually: exact von Mangoldt identity on its free semigroup (2.7e-15: Euler is real there) but the descent has no line to converge to (truncation drift 0.505 vs zeta's 2.1e-12, eleven orders) and the borrowed Gamma factor leaves a 0.665 duality defect. The trojan-ledger conservation law now has a measured witness PER CELL rather than per method. **(iii) THE THREE WRONGNESS COORDINATES (the deliverable).** (a) SP2 completeness (emergent = all) IS RH and is not provided: the D-H column proves the mechanism can be blind, so completeness is exactly what a proof must add. (b) SP4 is two-sided to a finite-scale residual (1.0e-7 on the object's own spectrum, 2.5e-8 on truth): the infinite-scale version is Connes 1998 Theorem 5 (global trace formula $\Leftrightarrow$ RH for all Hecke L-functions, proved in positive characteristic, asserted over $\mathbb{Q}$; PDF + verified reading note in-repo): v0's SP4 cell is that equivalence's finite shadow, C1's open half. (c) SP5's window margin is ZERO AT MACHINE PRECISION (zero-side bottom eigenvalue -1.9e-16 on $\lambda_{max} = 1.27$; prime-side -3.1e-7 inside assembly resolution 3.0e-5): the marginal-positivity coordinate (e3v; e1y's near-null ground state) reproduced INSIDE the assembled object; the uniform-in-$L$ margin is M4 and is untouched. **(iv) WHAT IT BUYS.** The satisfiability matrix gains its first BUILT row (SP1 yes-finite / SP2 part / SP3 yes-diagonal / SP4 finite-residual / SP5 empirical-marginal; wall: the limit), and any future candidate drops into the pipeline for a cell-by-cell score. Handed forward: the scaling ladder ($L = 8..14$: measure the decay law of the SP4 residual and the SP5 margin with scale, putting a number on the C2 gap from the object side); the D-H invisibility certificate (lower-bound $|m_{DH}|$ on the landmark window from its coefficients: turn the completeness failure into a theorem about the object); B2, the Lean interface structure with this as the four-of-five inhabitant. Cross-refs: the interface doc (SP1-SP5, C1/C2), #150-#153 (the B1 ladder this assembles), #148 (the determinant-class clause = the limit wall), #128 front 3 and the Connes reading note (SP4's infinite-scale statement), e3v/e1y (the margin), #113/#152 (the bracket this makes componentwise).

### 178. THE ARITHMETIC ETA INVARIANT CONSTRUCTED, CLOSED-FORMED, AND TYPED (e1ac, 2026-08-19, same day as #177): the #177 reopen trigger 2 is DISCHARGED BY CONSTRUCTION (the invariant exists and is not the missing object), the APS dictionary verifies end to end in arithmetic (existence / closed form / torsion shadow = the CS layer / prime-local variation with ZERO archimedean term), and the kill lands as a PARITY DECOMPOSITION: the odd sector of the explicit-formula observable algebra is exactly solvable and exactly RH-blind, so RH is purely even-sector; frontier UNMOVED. Round: BUILDER with pre-registered candidates and exits in the probe docstring; [`e1ac_arithmetic_eta.py`](spectral/e1ac_arithmetic_eta.py) (18/18, npz with the eta table), dossier [`arithmetic_eta.md`](../docs/03_research/arithmetic_eta.md); data = the repo's own TOS Dirichlet zeros (DATASETS §5), six conjugate pairs $q \in \{5,7,7,11,11,11\}$, characters rebuilt from the files' exact rational theta-tables (multiplicativity + FE re-verified at 25 dps, completeness against RvM, random zeros re-verified with SAME-HEIGHT normalization after the first version's low-height scale was caught making the check vacuous, $|\Lambda| \sim e^{-\pi t/4}$). It proves nothing about RH, and for once that sentence is a THEOREM-SHAPE rather than a disclaimer. **(i) THE CONSTRUCTION.** The abelian flat connections of Spec(Z) are the primitive Dirichlet characters; the signed spectrum of $\chi$ is $Z(\chi) = \{\gamma : \Lambda(1/2+i\gamma,\chi) = 0\}$; conjugation gives $Z(\bar\chi) = -Z(\chi)$, so for REAL $\chi$ (and $\zeta$, the trivial connection) every spectral asymmetry vanishes and the invariant lives on the COMPLEX characters, exactly as APS rho lives on nontrivial flat connections. Definition (APS heat form): $\eta(\chi) = \lim_{t\to0^+}\sum_\gamma \mathrm{sign}(\gamma)e^{-|\gamma|t}$. The negative-$\gamma$ zeros of $\chi$ are the positive-$\gamma$ zeros of $\bar\chi$, so conjugate FILE PAIRS carry the full signed spectrum: the dataset already in the repo suffices. **(ii) THE THREE MEASURED LAWS.** EXISTENCE: Abel (heat-sum, $t\to0$ extrapolation) and Cesaro (window means of the counting asymmetry $D(T) = N^+ - N^-$) regularizations agree to $\le 7\times10^{-4}$ on every pair. CLOSED FORM (C1, derived by the argument principle with the $\Gamma$/conductor factors cancelling between $\chi$ and $\bar\chi$ and mean-$S$ vanishing, then measured): $\eta(\chi) = -(2/\pi)\arg L(1/2,\chi)$ with the branch continued ALONG THE REAL AXIS from $\sigma = +\infty$; match $2\times10^{-4}$ absolute on every pair, regression slope $1.0002$ over a family spanning $\eta \in [-0.722, -0.093]$; winding integers all 0 at these conductors and reported as the lift's bookkeeping. TORSION SHADOW (C2): $\eta(\chi) \equiv -(1/\pi)\arg\varepsilon(\chi) \pmod 2$ EXACTLY: the mod-2 reduction of the R-valued lift is the root-number phase, i.e. #177's entire boundary-signature layer, recovered as the APS pattern (rho mod Z = CS of the flat connection) realized in arithmetic. VARIATION (C3): for ODD test functions the Weil explicit formula loses its archimedean term IDENTICALLY (the $\Gamma$-density is even in $r$): $\sum_\rho h(\gamma) = -2\sum_n (\Lambda(n)/\sqrt n)\,\mathrm{Im}\,\chi(n)\,G(\log n)$, sign pinned by measurement (consistent to $3\times10^{-15}$), verified to $5\times10^{-16}$ on 12 cells: eta's variation is PRIME-LOCAL WITH NO BULK TERM, the APS locality property; the even-$h$ control REQUIRES the archimedean integral and dropping it misses by exactly the measured integral ($0.1149$): the bulk term exists, is exactly the archimedean place, and decouples from the odd sector. **(iii) THE KILL, pre-registered and landed twice, and the ROUND'S REUSABLE COORDINATE.** (a) The invariant is EXACTLY RH-blind: $\mathrm{sign}(\gamma)$ never references $\beta$; off-line pairs $(\beta,\gamma),(1-\beta,\gamma)$ count as two zeros at $\gamma$ regardless; moving zeros off the line changes $\eta$ by NOTHING, not by something small. (b) The variation is exactly blind in the D-H direction: the D-H off-line quadruple (coefficient reality + FE force $\gamma$-symmetry) cancels ALGEBRAICALLY in every odd test function, measured $|\Sigma| = 0.0$ against per-term scale $8.6$ at the landmark zero $0.8085+85.699i$, while the even-$h$ control sees it at $O(\delta^2)$ ($1.1\times10^{-4}$, the #158-class weak face). (c) Beurling: UNPOSABLE, type refusal: C3's two sides consume the lattice half (completed $\Lambda$, zero side) and the Euler half ($\Lambda(n)$, prime side): so the construction PAYS THE FULL CONSERVATION-LAW TARIFF AND BUYS AN ODD-SECTOR OBSERVABLE, which sharpens the #170 law: paying the tariff is admission, not the theorem; what you purchase depends on the sector. THE COORDINATE: **the odd sector of the EF observable algebra is exactly solvable and exactly RH-blind; RH is purely even-sector.** This unifies three banked facts as one parity statement: even-sector statistics (GUE/pair correlation) are blind because they see only the multiset $\{\gamma\}$ (#174, PRIME_PATTERNS); the odd sector is blind because off-line motion is $\gamma$-symmetric (this round, exact); the residual RH sensitivity in the EF layer is the exponentially suppressed $\mathrm{Im}\,\gamma$ corrections (#158). Solvable exactly where blind = #170's "free exactly where information-free" as a parity decomposition. **(iv) HONEST PLACEMENT.** The counting-asymmetry constant for complex characters is classical territory (RvM/Selberg line): the novelty claim is confined to the eta-typed ASSEMBLY (the APS dictionary verified end to end) and the exact-blindness kill; C1 as a formula should be assumed known-in-substance. The archimedean-path remark, banked for the record: topology's R-valued eta lift comes from a path through the bulk of connection space; arithmetic character space is archimedean-ly discrete, and the only real path available (used here) is the $s$-plane real-axis path $\sigma:\infty\to1/2$, producing exactly central-argument = special-value-regime data (#113 discriminator), while the paths that DO exist in connection space are $p$-adic (weight space, the Iwasawa direction), matching the ACS corpus's one $\mathbb{Z}_p$ limit and its "related to $p$-adic L-functions" aside: the two-unorderable-completions refusal of #177, one level up. What an eta would have to be to matter for M4: not a number per connection but an eta-FORM, a second variation on a growing space with forced indefinite signature, and that is verbatim the Weil-positivity statement, i.e. M4. TRIGGER LIST UPDATE: #177 trigger 2 DISCHARGED (built, measured, typed: not the missing object); triggers 1 (functorial real-valued lift with canonical bounding object) and 3 (the ACS program's "path integrals of L-function type" producing a positivity) stand. Cross-refs: #177 (the trigger and the torsion layer this lifts), #176 (the FE-side blindness the shadow lands in), #170 (the sharpened tariff reading), #158/#161 (the blind class the even-control face lands in), #174 (the even-sector half of the parity statement).

### 177. THE ARITHMETIC CHERN-SIMONS DOOR MEASURED AND CLOSED AT ITS LINKING LAYER (e2am, 2026-08-19): the #176 handed-forward question DISSOLVES rather than resolves ("indefinite with a sign" is a type error for a torsion form), the boundary layer's entire signature content is $\sigma \bmod 8$ = Gauss-sum/root-number data = the RH-blind FE half, the dictionary underneath is EXACT (Redei 1934 verified 1524/1524 fundamental discriminants), and the wall relocates to the carrier joint; frontier UNMOVED. Round: nine-paper corpus close-read (Kim ACS I/II, abelian-ACS-linking 1706.03336, Lee-Park real places 1905.13610, Hirano-Kim-Morishita DW 2106.02308, Hirano mod-2 DW 1911.12964, entanglement 2312.17138, Ben-Bassat-Gropper pro-p duality 2504.19078, Kim manifesto 1712.07602; filed in `references/05_arithmetic_topology/`, index updated) + probe [`e2am_arithmetic_chern_simons.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2am_arithmetic_chern_simons.py) (20/20), dossier [`arithmetic_chern_simons_door.md`](../docs/03_research/arithmetic_chern_simons_door.md). It proves nothing about RH. **(i) THE CORPUS SCREENS, run exhaustively.** The CS functional is $CS_c([\rho]) = \mathrm{inv}(\rho^*(c)) \in \frac1n\mathbb{Z}/\mathbb{Z}$ via the Artin-Verdier fundamental class ("reminiscent of the fundamental class of a compact oriented three manifold... noted by Mazur around 50 years ago"); the decomposition formula sums local boundary-torus terms over a FINITE set of primes, never all places. Value groups: every constructed invariant is torsion except ONE $p$-adic inverse limit ($H^3(X,\mathbb{Z}_p(1)) \simeq \mathbb{Z}_p$, ACS I); "eta" and "definite" have ZERO corpus-wide hits; so do "Riemann hypothesis", "Hilbert-Polya", "spectral interpretation". L-functions appear as motivation only, graded by the corpus's own words: "far-fetched" (ACS I abstract), "in a way that remains mysterious" (1712.07602). The real place is the corpus's own obstruction: "Because $H^3(\pi_T,\mathbb{Z}/n)\neq 0$ when $X_\infty$ is not empty... the arithmetic CS action with boundary does not seem to extend" (Lee-Park), patched via Tate cohomology of $\mathrm{Gal}(\mathbb{C}/\mathbb{R})\cong\mathbb{Z}/2$. THE SHARPEST FORM OF THE VERDICT falls out of these two screens jointly: the value group's two reachable completions ($\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z}$, $\mathbb{Z}_p$) are both UNORDERABLE, a signature is an order-theoretic quantity, the unique orderable completion of $\mathbb{Q}$ is the archimedean one, and the archimedean place is precisely what the theory cannot close over. The sign needs $\mathbb{R}$; the theory is built to avoid $\mathbb{R}$. **(ii) THE DICTIONARY IS REAL ARITHMETIC, MEASURED (the probe's positive half).** T1: over all 990 pairs of odd primes < 200, the linking symmetry defect $\mathrm{lk}(p,q)\oplus\mathrm{lk}(q,p) = [p\equiv3][q\equiv3] \bmod 4$ PAIR FOR PAIR and the defect count is exactly $\binom{n_3}{2}$ = 276: the entire failure of linking symmetry is the real place (and via $K(p^*,r) = (r|p)$, T0-verified including $r=2$, the Redei matrix inherits the same defect entry by entry; no conflict with 1706.03336's symmetry lemma, which lives on the null-homologous locus where the archimedean term vanishes). T2, the load-bearing measurement: the Redei matrix (built from Kronecker symbols of prime discriminants ONLY, the linking data) computes the 4-rank of the class group at EVERY one of the 1524 fundamental discriminants in $(-5000,0)$, against a genus-theory computation sharing no code and no objects (reduced-form enumeration + genus characters $\chi_{d_i}(f) = K(d_i,r)$), with $h(D)$ itself cross-checked against Dirichlet's exact formula at all 364 cases $|D|\le1200$ and #ambiguous $= 2^{t-1}$ everywhere. Histogram $e_4$ = 0/1/2: 972/550/2, and the two $e_4=2$ cases ($D = -2379 = (-3)(13)(61)$, $D = -4895 = (5)(-11)(89)$) have Redei rank 0 = all pairwise linkings vanish = exactly the arithmetic BORROMEAN configuration where the next obstruction is the Redei triple symbol / Milnor invariant (density result: Ishida-Kuramoto-Zheng 2403.17957). The corpus's own computed examples (Hirano's mod-2 DW: $(-1)^{CS_c(\rho)} = \prod_{i<j,\rho(e_{ij})=-1}(p_j|p_i)$) confirm the computable content at this layer IS the linking matrix; 1706.03336 itself contains NO numeric examples (exhaustive search), so e2am supplies the adjacent computable layer. **(iii) GAUSS-MILGRAM: WHAT A BOUNDARY CAN KNOW OF A BULK SIGNATURE, measured.** Milgram's formula verified on $A_1, U, D_4, E_8, A_1{+}E_8$ with discriminant forms computed exactly from the Gram matrices; the caps: $U$ (indefinite, $\sigma=0$) and $E_8$ (definite, $\sigma=8$) have IDENTICAL discriminant forms, likewise $A_1$ vs $A_1{+}E_8$ ($\sigma$ = 1 vs 9). The torsion boundary determines $\sigma$ mod 8 and CANNOT SEE DEFINITE VS INDEFINITE, the exact distinction M4's polarization is made of. And the mod-8 datum arithmetic does carry is already spoken for: quadratic Gauss phases = 1 or $i$ by $p \bmod 4$ (Gauss sign theorem, verified $p<200$) = the normalized Gauss sum of $\chi_{p^*}$ = the LOCAL ROOT NUMBER (verified), CRT cocycle = the reciprocity sign (verified), Landsberg-Schaar to 2.5e-13 (the metaplectic/Weil-index face, cross-ref e1i). Root numbers are FE-side data, the half D-H possesses, the half #176 proved carries zero bits about zero location. **(iv) THE VERDICT AND THE NEW COORDINATE.** The disciplines are UNPOSABLE at this layer (no L-function is consumed anywhere: type refusal, not a pass; mechanism 2 of #176's table eats neither half, now corpus-confirmed). Lifting the linking form to a real-valued form means choosing a bounding object, the lift is wildly non-unique ($U$ vs $E_8$), so THE SIGN IS NOT A BOUNDARY OBSERVABLE: choosing the bounding object with the right positivity IS supplying M4's carrier (SP1) + polarization (SP5). Conservation law: tariff paid at the carrier joint, as with every measured costume (#160, #171, #172). NEW NAMED COORDINATE for the breadth battery: **the sign lives in the bulk**: any boundary/torsion route (linking forms, class-group pairings, finite-group CS functionals, root numbers) caps at $\sigma \bmod 8$; M4 needs the exact integer; proposed as a disqualifier (not silently added to the tested corpus module). HONEST CAVEAT, the one real-valued export: the entanglement entropy of the ACS state (2312.17138), $\mathrm{Ent} = (\dim\mathcal{F}_{X_S} - \dim(\mathrm{Ker\,loc}_{S_1}{+}\mathrm{Ker\,loc}_{S_2}))\log p$, is real but ENTROPIC, nonnegative by construction, structurally incapable of an indefinite signature: the exact twin of the Bost-Connes kill (strictly positive modular operator, `building_the_missing_positivity.md` Mechanism 2), and its authors' own grade is "do not yet make clear that the notion of entanglement is useful for number theory". The adjacent PROVEN signature bridge, Atiyah-Donnelly-Singer 1983 (Hilbert-modular-cusp signature defect = Shimizu L-VALUE), lands in the special-value regime by the #113 discriminator, not the all-zeros-positivity regime. REOPEN TRIGGERS, three named in the dossier: a functorial real-valued lift with canonical bounding object (M4-hard by construction, treat a claim of it as a claim of the missing object); an arithmetic eta invariant for $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathbb{Z})$ (spectral asymmetry, locally varying; nothing in the searched literature constructs one); the ACS program's own named next step ("path integrals of L-function type") producing a POSITIVITY rather than a special value, at which point it re-enters the M4 screens.
