### 176. THE QUANTUM-GRAVITY INTERFACE SWEPT, AND UNITARITY PROVED RH-BLIND IN THIRTY DIGITS (2026-08-19): the live 2020-2026 "zeta from quantum gravity" literature reproduces the #113 pattern a fourth time, the two live candidates fail in COMPLEMENTARY halves of the conservation law, and the free structure they both lean on is measurably worthless; frontier UNMOVED. Round: literature pull (13 arXiv PDFs into `sources/quantum_gravity/`) + one probe, [`e1ab_automorphic_spectrum.py`](spectral/e1ab_automorphic_spectrum.py) (30/30, `.npz` saved), dossier [`local_quantum_gravity_and_primes.md`](../docs/03_research/local_quantum_gravity_and_primes.md), new dataset (2202 level-1 Maass cusp forms, [`DATASETS.md`](primes/DATASETS.md) §17). It proves nothing about RH. **(i) THE HEADLINE, AND THE ONE REUSABLE DISQUALIFIER: UNITARITY OF THE AUTOMORPHIC SCATTERING MATRIX CARRIES ZERO BITS ABOUT ZERO LOCATION.** Every construction in this family inherits a unitary or self-adjoint structure for free (the Hilbert space is $L^2$, the Laplacian is self-adjoint, the S-matrix is unitary) and treats it as leverage. Run the D-H discipline on that step itself. The zeros enter automorphically as POLES of the Eisenstein constant term $\varphi(s) = \xi(2s-1)/\xi(2s)$ in $E(z,s) = y^s + \varphi(s)y^{1-s}$, at $s = \rho/2$, so RH is the statement that those poles sit on $\mathrm{Re}(s) = 1/4$. Build the SAME phase for Davenport-Heilbronn, $\varphi_{DH}(s) = \Lambda_{DH}(2s-1)/\Lambda_{DH}(2s)$ off the odd-character-mod-5 completion already in `_shared/davenport_heilbronn.py`. MEASURED at 30 dps: $|\varphi(1/2+it)| = 1$ for zeta to $3.9$e-31, $|\varphi_{DH}(1/2+it)| = 1$ for D-H to $2.2$e-30, and D-H's known off-line zero $0.8085 + 85.699i$ puts a resonance at $0.40425 + 42.8495i$, offset $0.154$ from $1/4$. Both scattering matrices are unitary on the critical line to thirty digits and one of them belongs to a function that violates its own RH. The reason is structural, not numerical: unimodularity follows from real Dirichlet coefficients plus a self-dual completion and NOTHING ELSE (real coefficients give $\Lambda(\bar s) = \overline{\Lambda(s)}$, so on the line the phase is $\overline{\Lambda}/\Lambda$ wherever the zeros happen to be), i.e. it is a consequence of the FUNCTIONAL EQUATION ALONE, which is exactly the half of the adelic package D-H already has. So any argument of the form "the theory is unitary, therefore the spectrum is real, therefore RH" passes through a step a known counterexample satisfies identically. Same shape as #170: the free structure is free exactly where it is information-free. This belongs in the [`breadth_program.md`](../docs/03_research/breadth_program.md) disqualifier battery and it retires the whole family including the versions not yet written. **(ii) THE ZEROS ARE NOT THE DISCRETE SPECTRUM OF THE MODULAR LAPLACIAN, so the gravity papers' Hilbert-Polya identification is DEFINITIONAL.** Godet ([arXiv:2405.09833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09833), [2505.03068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03068)) puts pure Einstein gravity on $T^d$ slices, finds the Wheeler-DeWitt operator IS the Casimir on $L^2(SL(d,\mathbb{Z})\backslash\mathfrak{h}_d)$, writes the Hartle-Hawking state as a sum over the zeros, then DEFINES "the Hilbert-Polya Hamiltonian" to have the imaginary parts as eigenvalues. If that were constructive the zeros would be eigenvalues of the operator he actually has, whose $d=2$ discrete spectrum is the Maass cusp forms. Tested on 2202 level-1 forms pulled from LMFDB. THE TRAP HANDLED FIRST, and it is the whole methodological content: the list is NOT complete over its range, and randomly deleting levels drives ANY spectrum toward Poisson, which is the direction of the expected answer, so the completeness horizon is measured before anything else against Weyl with the scattering correction $N(R) \sim R^2/12 - (2R/\pi)\log(R/e\sqrt{\pi/2})$. The staircase tracks Weyl to better than 0.5% up to $R = 100$ (617 forms) and breaks to 0.94 immediately above; on the complete range the fitted leading coefficient is $0.083149$ against $\mathrm{Area}/4\pi = 1/12 = 0.083333$, a 0.22% confirmation of the modular surface's area. Each parity class unfolded separately (mixing two independent spectra manufactures Poisson by itself). RESULT through one identical pipeline at matched $N = 255$, KS 5% critical $0.0852$: zeta zeros KS-to-GUE $0.0625$ (ACCEPTED), Maass odd $0.1822$ and even $0.1919$ (REJECTED at 2.1x and 2.3x critical). Zeta also stays GUE when pushed through the same empirical fit rather than Riemann-von Mangoldt, so the instrument is not the story. The probe deliberately claims ONLY the rejection of GUE and explicitly does NOT resolve which class the Maass spectrum is in: below the horizon Poisson and GOE are not separated at $n \sim 300$ (odd reads GOE $0.1325$ vs Poisson $0.1406$, even the reverse), and the strong Poisson signal on the full list ($0.051$, $0.067$) is confounded by the missing levels. The argument needs only the rejection, so the ambiguity is reported as a printed NOTE rather than papered over. **(iii) THE TWO LIVE CANDIDATES FAIL IN COMPLEMENTARY HALVES, which is the round's most useful product.** Applying the #170 conservation law (consume the Euler product AND the additive lattice at the same joint; D-H detects failure to pay the first, Beurling the second): GODET eats the lattice ($T^d$, $SL(d,\mathbb{Z})$, Poisson) and NO Euler product anywhere, so his "Hilbert-Polya Hamiltonian" can only ever be a name, and indeed he uses RH as INPUT (quoting it as the statement that near-singularity fluctuations are $O(G^{1/3})$ in $d=3$), never as output. LECLAIR ([arXiv:2406.01828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01828)) eats the Euler product AND the FE (his $\vartheta$ enters through the counting condition) but has no Hilbert space and no operator, so his unitarity can only ever be an assumption: he is explicit that "we didn't present a formula for the implicit underlying hamiltonian" and that "the validity of the RH comes down to Proposition 1", which is asserted. Between them they hold all the pieces and no joint. EXTERNAL CORROBORATION WORTH BANKING: LeClair runs the D-H test himself, correctly, and concludes "for the RH to be true, one needs both the functional equation and the Euler product", i.e. he reaches [`trojan_horse_m4.md`](../docs/03_research/trojan_horse_m4.md)'s tariff independently from the S-matrix side without looking for it. **(iv) A FIFTH MECHANISM BY WHICH PRIMES ENTER A PHYSICAL THEORY, new to this repo.** The four already on the books are primes as PLACES (Tate/Connes, eats both halves), as KNOTS (arithmetic topology, eats neither), as CLOSED ORBITS of length $\log p$ (Deninger, chaos C4), and as ENERGY LEVELS $E_p = \log p$ (primon gas / Bost-Connes, killed in `building_the_missing_positivity.md` Mechanism 2 because the modular operator is strictly positive definite and so cannot carry an indefinite signature). Godet supplies a fifth: primes via PRIMITIVITY of a lattice vector, the no-boundary condition forbidding contracting cycles on non-primitive vectors, so $\gcd(n_1,\dots,n_d)=1$ Mobius-inverts to $1/\zeta$ and the state becomes a Mobius average $\psi_{HH} = \sum_m \mu(m) m^{d/2-1} Z_{\mathrm{CFT}}[Cm^d]$. Worth naming because it is the only entry whose primes arrive from a GEOMETRIC condition rather than from multiplicativity, the only one whose native output is $1/\zeta$ rather than $\zeta$, and the only one where RH reads as RANDOMNESS of $\mu$ (Mertens square-root cancellation) rather than as a positivity. Different-shaped door; still not a polarization. **(v) GAPS NAMED AND EXCLUSIONS RECORDED.** Arithmetic Chern-Simons (Minhyong Kim, [1510.05818](https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.05818) / [1609.03012](https://arxiv.org/abs/1609.03012)) is a genuine hole in this repo's coverage and is the most literal existing sense of "quantum gravity on $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathbb{Z})$": a Dijkgraaf-Witten TQFT on a space of Galois representations, with primes as knots. It currently has zero contact with the zeros (mechanism 2, eats neither half), so it cannot see RH as it stands; it is listed because its CARRIER has the right shape for Direction 8 and the open question worth asking is whether the arithmetic linking form can be made INDEFINITE WITH A SIGN. Also flagged for anyone repeating this search: the first page of results contains a large amount of unrefereed machine-generated material (an "E8 holographic resolution of the cosmological constant problem", a "spectral proof of RH employing the Forcing Lemma", an adelic framework claiming to resolve the Hubble tension against LIGO O4 and JWST), none of it used. LMFDB's reCAPTCHA trap fired exactly as `DATASETS.md` §15 warned (HTTP 200 with an HTML challenge body); the working route is the search-page `download=1` endpoint, now recorded. **FRONTIER: UNMOVED.** Quantum gravity is now the fourth independent field to reproduce "realizes $\zeta$ as a trace, carries no polarization" (#113 string theory, `spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md` for every candidate cohomology, the breadth sweeps #119-#121), and (i) explains WHY for this field specifically: the structure it hands you for free is a consequence of the functional equation, and the functional equation is the half D-H already has. Stated as a coordinate rather than a wall, the search space lost the entire "unitarity implies reality" family for a reason checkable in thirty digits. Cross-refs: #113 (the pattern), #170/#171 (the conservation law and the free-structure shape), #152 (the Beurling half), #158 (the blind class the D-H resonance lands in).

### 175. THE TWO CHEAP #169 RESIDUALS CLEARED, AND THE VERIFICATION CEILING LIFTED: the e1p-vs-e1s interlacing "disagreement" dissolves as a level-of-object collision (the twisted-inner-product caveat undercuts ONLY the non-normal M-shadow; e1p's own Q3 block already carries the rigorous form-level statement as a gated check), the D-H lattice undercount is CHARACTERIZED as a conditioning failure the harness itself flags rather than anything arithmetic (e1y), Theorem V2 is SHARPENED (the correct exponent is the equilibrium discrepancy Gamma(sigma), V2 being the sigma = equilibrium case, and the measured 0.48 log T is exactly (1/2) log T), and outside the corridor the certified RH-verification pipeline gains three composable levers (extended-precision phases, an exact parallel split at Gram-law seams, and the C_1 correction) that carry it to height 5e7; frontier UNMOVED, 2026-08-16/17. Both one-look items on the board are now closed and neither moved the frontier, which is what one-look items are for. Artifacts: annotations in [`e1p_rank_one_interlacing.md`](spectral/e1p_rank_one_interlacing.md) and [`e1s_rank_one_interlacing.md`](spectral/e1s_rank_one_interlacing.md); [`e1y_dh_undercount.py`](spectral/e1y_dh_undercount.py) / `.npz` / [`.md`](spectral/e1y_dh_undercount.md) (21/21 full, npz saved, K1-guarded); [`e1z_v2_sharp.py`](spectral/e1z_v2_sharp.py) / `.npz` / [`.md`](spectral/e1z_v2_sharp.md) (11/11 full); `rsz.py` + `e5f_rh_verification.py` (primes tests 55/55). It proves nothing about RH. **(i) THE INTERLACING RECONCILIATION: NOT A DISAGREEMENT.** #169's merge note banked "ONE nuance is unreconciled": this line grades Weyl-on-`Q` RIGOROUS at most 2, while #165/e1p grades the profile not-a-theorem-instance via the twisted-inner-product caveat. Read off the tracked code, the two statements are about DIFFERENT MATRICES. e1p's caveat attaches to the OPERATOR level only (its Q1 object `D = D_0 + P_1`, `P_1 = -|D_0 xi><delta_N|`, non-normal, self-adjoint only in the twisted Weil-form inner product). e1p's Q3 block ALREADY grades the FORM level rigorous, as a gated check rather than a measurement: it computes `eigvalsh` of the symmetrized `Q_full` vs `Q_noPole` and asserts `max shift <= 2` under the in-code comment "unlike Q1's operator-level case this one is provably clean", and that check IS e1s's backbone `|N_Qon(t) - N_Qoff(t)| <= rank(P) = 2`. Both lines also place the `sqrt13` shift-3 exception at the OPERATOR level (e1p: Q2's `slot_shifts(d0, d)` at the separate `sqrt13` point; e1s: the unfiltered `M` displacement at `N = 34`), so the two-line replication is STRONGER than the merge note credited, and no claim in either dossier moves. SHARPENING BANKED: the twisted metric is realized only APPROXIMATELY at float precision. `operator_spectrum` already returns the G-self-adjointness residual `||GM - M^H G||/(||G|| ||M||)` with `G = Q - eps I`, measured 1.3e-5 to 4.9e-4 across the `lam` in [3.0, 4.0] cells, so the caveat is quantitative ("the metric that would supply the hypothesis is realized to 1e-5") and not merely formal, and it is the same knob as the ghost mechanism. **(ii) THE D-H UNDERCOUNT: A VALIDITY-DOMAIN FACT, NOT AN ARITHMETIC ONE (e1y).** #169's ADVERSARY catch (D-H undercounts `floor(T/phi)` by 1-2 ghost-free at `lam` in {3.3, 3.6, 4.0, 4.5} while zeta-OFF anchors the lattice at `sqrt13`) REPRODUCES: five of six D-H cells deviate on a pole-off grid built with `N >= n_hi + 7` so mode truncation never binds. The mechanism is conditioning. At `lam >= 3.3` the D-H Weil form has a near-null ground state (`eps` 1.7e-6 to 7.6e-5, against zeta's -4.8 to -7.3), so the selected even ground state `xi` is nearly orthogonal to `delta` and `operator_spectrum`'s normalization `xi_n = xi/(delta . xi)` -- the quantity it literally divides by -- inflates every secular residue `r_k = L^{-1/2} phi k (xi_n)_k`: median `|delta.xi|` 788x smaller on the deficient builds (7.17e-4 vs 5.65e-1), median top-mode `|r_(n_hi)|` 13.7x larger (6.11e-1 vs 4.48e-2). Those same builds carry `even_assumption_ok = False`, the flag `build_float` records precisely because the CCM paper ASSUMES an even ground state (Remark 2.3). THREE independent confirmations. (a) A T-SWEEP separates an EDGE effect (some placement of the window edge inside the top lattice gap restores the count) from a STRUCTURAL deficit (none does): every zeta-side build is recoverable, five of six D-H builds are not, and the criterion is threshold-free. (b) The NEGATION-SYMMETRY identity `#{Re > 0} = N` (forced by e1p's `J M J^-1 = -M` for even `xi`) holds in every zeta-side cell and FAILS in exactly the five structural D-H cells, a count-free symptom measured independently of any window. (c) CONTROLS BOTH WAYS: `|Lambda_DH|` (signs stripped, same support and magnitudes) and D-H's comb under ZETA's archimedean density each restore a sound ground state (`eps` -1.8 to -2.8) and remove the deficit; while permuting zeta's comb values, replacing it with the flat non-arithmetic comb `Lambda = 1`, and flipping the signs of HALF its support all leave `eps`, `delta.xi` and the count untouched. So the tempting "D-H's Lambda changes sign" reading is refuted by its own converse: signs flipped into zeta do not manufacture the deficit, and the near-null ground state needs the COMBINATION of D-H's dense period-5 stream with D-H's own density (`dens_a = 3/4`, conductor 5). The classification is re-run across 25 admissibility cuts spanning two decades and is stable at every one; the honest caveat is that the separation carrying that stability is between the two BUILDS (zeta-side `|delta.xi| >= 0.46` vs D-H `<= 2.1e-3`, at least 220x) and not between deficient and sound counts (the largest structural `|delta.xi|`, 9.8e-4, is only 2.1x below the smallest recoverable one). CONSEQUENCE FOR THE RECORD: #169's catch stands as a statement about the numbers, and its TYPE changes from "a genuine O(1) deviation at larger lambda" to "the D-H cells at `lam >= 3.3` are outside this harness's validity domain"; on admissible pole-off builds the count is the geometric lattice count up to where the window edge falls. The RIGOROUS backbone is untouched either way, because Weyl-on-`Q` uses the Hermitian form and never the ground state's evenness or the secular normalization. Two claims were DROPPED during the round by the round's own checks: a "lattice pinning" reading (false at `lam = 4.0`, where zeta-off's next root sits 0.99 of a gap above the top lattice point and the count is still exact for every placement of `T`) and a first admissibility criterion keyed on the even-state gap (which mis-graded the `lam = 3.3` cell). ALSO RE-CONFIRMED: the `O(1)` dps sensitivity e1l STEP 5 warned about is visible directly (`lam = 3.6` D-H reads `dev = -1` at dps 15 and `-2` at dps 25), so the classification and not the individual integers is what this probe rests on. **(iii) THE PRIMES-THREAD CEILING, TWO COMPOSABLE LEVERS.** The certified verification chain was complete but capped, and the cap was the ARITHMETIC, not the formula: in the certified budget the Gabcke truncation falls like `t^-3/4` while float64 rounding rises like `t^5/4 log t`, so they cross at `t = 3.0e6` (measured by the new `rsz.rounding_crossover`) and by `1e8` the bound is rounding-dominated 1400x. LEVER 1 (precision): `zed(..., prec="ld")` forms `theta(t) - t log n` and reduces it mod `2 pi` in x86 80-bit extended precision (`u = 2^-64`, 2048x smaller) before dropping to float64 for the cosine. The crossover moves to `1.19e8`, a 40x gain in certified height, and the bound at `1e8` falls 1.8e-4 -> 2.2e-7. VERIFIED, not merely derived: against `mpmath.siegelz` the extended path errs 1.6e-8 where float64 errs 2.8e-7 at `t = 1e8`, both paths stay inside their own bounds at every sampled height, and where it actually counts -- escalations to exact arithmetic on 16,000-index bands at the top of the range -- float64 needs 2 at `t ~ 1e7` and 5 at `3e7` while the extended path needs ZERO at both, for 1.8x cost per point. The float64 path is bit-for-bit unchanged (checked by direct comparison against the pre-change routine), and on a platform whose `longdouble` is really float64 the option REFUSES rather than claiming a bound it cannot honour. THE RUNS: the levers compose, and were used end to end, twice. First a CERTIFIED verification to height 2e7 in 3h52m on 6 workers (44,478,605 zeros, 1,060 escalations, Turing closing at `S(g_m) <= 0.1044`), which doubled the thread's previous height (1e7, 21,136,125 zeros, uncertified, 1h54m serial) and showed the budget had become truncation-limited again (4.25e-7 of 4.35e-7), i.e. limited by the FORMULA rather than the arithmetic, which is what made `C_1` the next lever. Then, with `C_1` built, a certified verification to **height 5e7 in 15h28m on 8 workers: 118,488,122 zeros, every one simple and on the critical line, every sign certified**, 99,464,739 Gram blocks (Gram's law failing on 15.57%, longest block 8), 34 boundary merges, ZERO unresolved blocks, and Turing closing at `S(g_m) <= 0.1237 < 1`. THE NUMBER THAT MEASURES THE ROUND: escalations to exact arithmetic fell to **114 in 118.5 million** Gram points, against 1,060 in 44.5 million at 2e7, a 25-fold drop in RATE at 2.5x the height, because the certified bound at the top of the range went from 7.28e-5 (float64 / `C_0`) to 3.55e-8. LEVER 2 (cores): the Gram walk looks sequential only because each window starts where the last ended, and that dependency dissolves at any Gram point where Gram's law HOLDS, since a band bounded by two such points contains whole Gram blocks and its count is exact on its own. `verify_parallel` cuts `3 x workers` bands at seams found by `good_gram_at_or_after`, weighted `n_i = n_end (i/B)^(2/3)` from the `sqrt(g_n)` cost density, and returns a `tiled` flag asserting consecutive bands meet; serial and parallel agree EXACTLY on found count, expected count and verdict, measured 3.7x on 6 workers with the box otherwise busy. LEVER 3, EXECUTED THE SAME NIGHT (the `C_1` term, and a latent bound gap it exposed): `zed(..., k=1)` adds the `C_1` correction, with `C_0` and `C_1` carried as audited degree-26 Chebyshev fits (`_regen_correction_coeffs()` rebuilds both from the definitions at 80 digits and the test suite compares, so the constants are not magic). TWO THINGS WORTH THE RECORD. (a) The textbook constant `-Psi'''/(2^5*3*pi^2)` is stated for the variable `u = p - 1/2` while this code's is `z = 2u`, so the correct coefficient here is `-Psi'''(z)/(12 pi^2)`, and the factor 8 was caught BY MEASUREMENT rather than by reading: the residual left after the `C_0` term, rescaled by `a^(3/2)`, came out exactly 8.0000x the naive version across the whole range of `z` (median 7.99912 over 800 samples). Verified after the fix against `mpmath.siegelz`: the error falls 295x to 4788x over `t = 1e5` to `1e9`, always inside its own bound, and `k=0` vs `k=1` CERTIFIED verifications agree exactly on the zero count. (b) Implementing it exposed a genuine GAP in the certified claim. The `k=0` remainder is the quotient `cos(2 pi(p^2-p-1/16))/cos(2 pi p)`, which is `0/0` at the removable zeros `p = 1/4, 3/4` and loses most of its digits nearby: measured error up to `8.3e-2`, five orders above typical, and the old rounding bound accounted for the main sum only and did not cover it AT ALL. `rs_rounding_bound` now carries a pointwise `6u/|cos(2 pi p)|` term, so those points escalate to exact arithmetic instead of being trusted, with a regression test pinning it. The completed `2e7` run is unaffected: a Gram point would have had to land within `~1e-13` of `p = 1/4` for the old bound to be violated, expected count `~2e-5` over 44.5M points. Tests 55/55. **(iv) THEOREM V2 SHARPENED, and the measured 0.48 identified as exactly one half (e1z, 11/11 full).** #172 proved Theorem V2 (`lambda_M(0) <= cosh(2nG)^-2` for M atoms in `E = +-[g,T]`, `G` the Green's function of the complement at 0) and V8 measured that its tightness residual `rho` does not saturate but grows like `0.48 log T`, leaving "a sharp version would capture that factor" as the one open item in the coordinate. The sharp form, at the level of the exponential rate: the identity `1/lambda_M(0) = sum_j ell_j(0)^2/w_j` makes this a Lagrange-interpolation problem, and when the atoms' empirical distribution converges to `sigma`, `(1/M) log(1/lambda_M(0)) -> 2 Gamma(sigma)` with `Gamma(sigma) = max_{y in E} U^sigma(y) - U^sigma(0)` the EQUILIBRIUM DISCREPANCY, `U^sigma` the logarithmic potential. Three consequences. (a) **Theorem V2 is exactly the `sigma` = equilibrium case**: there `U^sigma` is constant on `E`, so `Gamma = g_E(0) = G` and the Chebyshev bound is the right rate; measured, `rho -> 1` with the excess contracting 2.02x per doubling of M. (b) **Every other `sigma` is strictly worse** and `rho -> Gamma/G`; measured for equal spacing, `rho` climbs to `Gamma/G = 2.6817` from below (gap contracting 1.75x per doubling, 0.016 at M = 6400 against a distance to 1 of 1.666), and `Gamma >= G` holds for every family tested. (c) **The missing `log T` is the equilibrium discrepancy of EQUAL SPACING**: `Gamma_unif/G = (1/2) log T + O(1)`, with the maximizer of `U^sigma` on `E` at the geometric mean `sqrt(gT)` to five digits at every height, and the slope `d(Gamma/G)/d log T` converging to 0.4998, 0.5000 at `T = 1e6, 1e7`. So the constant is exactly `1/2` and #172's 0.48 was the finite-T approach to it. MECHANISM: a Runge effect. V2's Chebyshev polynomial is optimal for nodes at the mapped Chebyshev points of the band; V2 keeps the atoms' SUPPORT and discards their DISTRIBUTION, and `Gamma - G` is the price. INDEPENDENT REPLICATION BANKED: a different assembly of the same quantity (log-Lagrange weights plus a logsumexp, needing no extended precision and so reaching M well past the ~2000 the direct mpmath product costs) reproduces #172's V8b predictor 8/8 to `5e-4` with slopes to 0.001, so the 0.48 finding is replicated rather than inherited. TYPING UNCHANGED, and this is the point: `Gamma` is a functional of `sigma` alone, and jittering every atom inside its own cell (same `sigma`, every arithmetic relation among the positions destroyed) moves `rho` by 0.018 at M = 1600. Closing this residual therefore CONFIRMS V8c's geometry typing instead of reopening the pointwise route, which is the expected answer given #172's continuity obstruction. WHAT REMAINS: a two-sided FINITE-M inequality with effective error terms, and the unequal-weight case (weights contribute `O(log M)` to `log(1/lambda)` so they cannot move the rate, but a finite-M statement must carry them); four VERIFIER targets are named in the dossier, the cleanest being Frostman's `Gamma >= G` with equality iff `sigma` is the equilibrium measure. **(v) THE ADVERSARY ROUND ON BOTH, self-run 2026-08-17/18 ([`_e1y_adversary.md`](spectral/_e1y_adversary.md), [`_e1z_adversary.md`](spectral/_e1z_adversary.md)); BOTH PASS_WITH_FIXES, with three attacks landing and two of them on this round's own text.** e1y: A1 (protocol, is `N = n_hi + 7` driving it) does NOT land, the class holding across margins {3,7,12,20} at every `lam`. A6 (causality) does NOT land and CONFIRMS the mechanism by direct intervention: scaling `xi_n -> c xi_n` on a HEALTHY zeta-off build, changing nothing else, leaves the count exact at `c = 1, 3` and breaks it to structural at `c = 10` (effective `|delta.xi| ~ 5.6e-2`), saturating at -9 beyond, at both tested `lam` -- so residue inflation alone breaks a sound build, and the construction is MORE sensitive than the quoted 788x median ratio implies. A2 (precision) LANDS and demotes a stated claim: the structural/recoverable class is NOT dps-stable, flipping at two of three cells (`lam = sqrt13` recoverable at dps 15 and structural at 25/35; `lam = 4.0` structural at 15/25 and RECOVERABLE at 35, where D-H's count is exact), so e1y's "the classification, not the individual integers, is what this probe rests on" is FALSE and is removed. THE FOLLOW-UP THAT DECIDES IT (18 builds, both twins, dps {15,25,35}): the instability is ONE-SIDED. Zeta-off is class-stable at 3/3 cells with `eps` reproducing to four digits and `|delta.xi|` to three across a twenty-digit precision change; D-H is stable at 1/3, with `eps` wandering by factors of 4 to 50 INCLUDING SIGN CHANGES, `|delta.xi|` by factors of 20 to 130 including sign changes, and `even_assumption_ok` itself flipping. That is the signature of a quantity that is zero being reported as noise, so the replacement statement is STRONGER than the demoted one: on those builds `delta.xi` is numerically indistinguishable from zero at every reachable precision, `xi` is the eigenvector of a numerically degenerate eigenvalue and rotates freely as precision changes, and `xi_n = xi/(delta.xi)` is UNDEFINED rather than merely ill-conditioned; the count's precision-dependence is a consequence, not a rival explanation. A6 also SPLIT a conflation: `n_pos = N` survives every scale factor up to 3000x while the count deficit appears at 10x, so residue inflation causes the deficit and does NOT cause the conservation failure, and the evenness violation does not either (`lam = 3.3` has `even_assumption_ok = True` at every precision and still breaks conservation). **The cause of the conservation failure is now an OPEN RESIDUAL**, recorded rather than papered over. e1z: B3 (does `rho` overshoot `Gamma/G`) does NOT land, pushed two doublings further to M = 25600 with the gap still positive at +0.0049 and contracting 1.77x. B6, the attack that could have made the whole `Gamma` story an artifact of equal spacing, does NOT land and is the round's strongest confirmation: the ZETA density, whose `Gamma/G = 8.3521` is three times the uniform value, converges to ITS OWN `Gamma/G` from above (10.741, 8.780, 8.442, 8.370 at M = 200 to 12800, gap contracting 2.2x), so three distributions spanning a factor of eight in the predicted rate each land on their own `Gamma`; promoted to a tracked check (e1z is now 12 checks). B2 LANDS on e1z's own text: the equilibrium excess was attributed to "the `log M / M` weight term", but measured against `log(2M)/(2 M Gamma)` the ratio drifts 1.38 -> 0.39 rather than settling, because the excess decays like `1/M` with no `log M`; the attribution is REMOVED and the subleading structure left unidentified rather than guessed at. NET: no e1z claim demoted and its central result strengthened; one e1y claim demoted and replaced by a sharper measured one; the RH-facing reading of both is unchanged. **(vi) THE FOUR-ITEM SEQUENCE THAT FOLLOWED (2026-08-18), three built and one checked.** (1) THE CONSERVATION RESIDUAL, opened by the adversary round and CLOSED the same day. Neither candidate hypothesis survives: the ground state is even to machine precision in the FAILING cells (`1 - even_frac <= 2.2e-16`) and rotating a healthy `xi` toward an odd vector all the way to `even_frac = 0.707` leaves `n_pos = N` untouched, so `J M J = -M` is EXACT and the symmetry is not what breaks. What breaks is the INFERENCE from it. Negation symmetry sorts `spec(M)` into real `+-a` pairs (1 each to `#{Re>0}`), complex quadruples `(z, zbar, -z, -zbar)` (2 each), the exact zero, and one type it is easy to forget: a PURELY IMAGINARY pair `(iy, -iy)`, closed under negation AND conjugation, contributing 0. D-H carries one such pair at `lam` in {3.3, 3.6, sqrt13, 4.0} (at `|Im|` = 28.67, 9.53, 9.05, 6.53) and two at `lam = 4.5` (5.98, 49.19); zeta-off carries none anywhere. So it is the e1l/e1s ghost mechanism in its symmetric special case, a pair leaving the real axis by colliding at the ORIGIN, and it is genuinely distinct from the residue inflation that drives the count deficit: TWO SYMPTOMS, TWO MECHANISMS, both now named. It also repairs e1y's own instrument, since those pairs have `|Re| ~ 1e-10` and `n_pos` at a `1e-6` cut disagrees with the same quantity at `1e-9`; the pair count is threshold-free and is what the new check U2-4 tests. ### 174. THE DUALITY PACKAGE PROVABLY DOES NOT FORCE POSITIVITY, MEASURED ON BOTH SIDES: an executable arithmetic census (FE + integrality + Euler product buy exactly the TRIVIAL region and nothing in the critical strip) matched by a geometric twin in print (Babaee-Huh: PD + HL without HR), with AHK read as the one known template for manufacturing a polarization WITHOUT a variety and its two non-transferable ingredients named. Prompted by the question "if all Weil cohomologies are the same, why does the choice of cohomology matter": the sameness theorem pins down Betti numbers, traces, hence rationality and the functional equation, and is SILENT on positivity, which is not among the Weil cohomology axioms. [`toy/axiom_census.py`](toy/axiom_census.py) makes that silence quantitative in the function-field shadow, where every other ingredient is a theorem. Filters on a degree-$2g$ integer polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_q$: (a) curve FE, (b) integrality, (c) every implied closed-point count a non-negative integer (the honest Euler condition, STRICTLY stronger than $N_n \ge 0$: requiring only $N_n \ge 0$ gives the symmetric band $|a| \le q+1$ in genus 1, the closed-point condition trims the top to $a \le q$), (d) RH. RESULT: (a)+(b)+(c) does NOT imply (d), and not marginally. Genus 1: the axioms permit exactly $a \in [-(q+1), q]$ against RH's $|a| \le 2\sqrt q$, a gap $(\sqrt q - 1)^2$ that WIDENS without bound. Genus 2: the RH-violating fraction of axiom-satisfying models climbs 0% (q=2, where the axioms do still force RH) -> 30% (q=3) -> 83% (q=9), and the worst surviving violation tracks $\sqrt q$ almost exactly (2.966 vs 3.000 at q=9), i.e. $|\alpha|$ reaching $q$. In zeta coordinates: **the three axioms buy exactly $\mathrm{Re}(s) \le 1$, the trivial region the Euler product gives away for free, and nothing inside the critical strip.** CLOSES A GAP THE REPO LEFT OPEN: the fake from #123 ($P = T^4-4T^3+15T^2-20T+25$ over $q=5$), used in three files (e2xx, toy/instances.py, toy/circle_interlacing.py) as THE function-field analogue of D-H, had never been checked against (c); it PASSES (exact to $d=200$, proven beyond by a tail bound since $\max|\alpha| < q$), so it is a genuine witness. D-H itself CANNOT be one, which is the subtlety: its comb goes negative at $n=3$ (#90), so D-H fails (c) and never enters this hypothesis space at all. Before this, the repo separated RH from FE (D-H) and from the Euler product (Beurling) but had NOTHING separating it from all three together. THE GEOMETRIC TWIN (the genuinely new cross-link, from the AHK read): Babaee-Huh [BH17], quoted in AHK p. 4, exhibit a tropical variety satisfying Poincare duality AND hard Lefschetz but NOT the Hodge-Riemann relations. That is the same statement as this census in the other category: duality-type axioms do not force the signature. Two independent counterexample families, arithmetic and geometric, saying the polarization is irreducibly extra. THE AHK TEMPLATE (`references/06_intersection_hodge/`, all 63pp read): Adiprasito-Huh-Katz prove PD + HL + HR for the Chow ring of ANY loopless matroid, including matroids non-realizable over every field (Thm 5.12: a Chow equivalence from a smooth projective variety exists IFF the matroid is realizable), so this is positivity genuinely with no variety, not merely without an explicit one. Positivity is MANUFACTURED, and the mechanism decomposes into four parts worth having as a spec: (1) BASE CASE = Lindstrom-Gessel-Viennot, a non-intersecting-lattice-path sign-of-determinant count standing in for "HR of $\mathbb{CP}^{a} \times \mathbb{CP}^{b}$" with NO Kahler input; (2) LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL: the link at each flat factors as restriction $\times$ contraction, i.e. a TENSOR PRODUCT OF SMALLER INSTANCES OF THE SAME STRUCTURE, and local HR $\Rightarrow$ global HL by a positive-sum-of-local-squares argument; (3) HR ITSELF by degeneration: a matroidal flip (stellar subdivision / blow-up at a flat) with an explicit ample family whose $t \to 0$ limit is the DIRECT SUM of two inductively-known forms, plus rigidity of the integer signature along the convex ample cone; (4) AMPLENESS FOR FREE: the ample cone is nonempty because every Bergman fan is a subfan of the normal fan of an always-existing auxiliary polytope (iterated stellar subdivision of a simplex), a finite poset fact. The §7 machinery is field-agnostic by design (a graded Artinian PD algebra + a nonempty open convex degree-1 cone + local tensor factorization $\Rightarrow$ HL and HR), which is exactly the S1-S7 skeleton shape the breadth program hunts. WHAT DOES NOT TRANSFER, named: (i) the induction is FINITE with strict descent (contraction lowers rank, flips grow the filter); RH's positivity must be UNIFORM over an infinite family, the same scale mismatch already isolated as the uniform determinant-class limit (#148/B1) and as Christoffel/prolate uniformity (#172); (ii) no exchange axiom on primes is known, so there is no candidate restriction/contraction pair and hence no local-product mechanism (the divisor lattice of a squarefree integer IS a matroid, but the Boolean one, realizable everywhere and carrying no zeta content); (iii) no arithmetic analogue of the auxiliary polytope, so ampleness must be manufactured from scratch, which IS SP5/M4. ADVERSARY VALUE: [BH17] is now a named disqualifier for any proposed arithmetic analogue (does the candidate's local structure genuinely factor into strictly smaller copies of itself, or does it merely have duality and Lefschetz while begging the HR question?), and the first question to put to any AHK-style transfer is whether its "local ample data" does anything beyond re-deriving the explicit formula's already-known MARGINAL positivity, in which case it inherits the zero-margin wall (#K1) rather than escaping it: AHK's novelty was not local-sums-to-global (old) but a non-geometric substitute for ampleness PLUS a terminating induction. HONEST SCOPE: this moves nothing. It converts "sameness of cohomologies should help" from a hope into a measured negative with an explicit $4\times4$ counterexample, supplies the missing separator within (a)+(b)+(c), and turns AHK into a checklist a BUILDER must satisfy rather than an inspiration. Literature grounded: Honda-Tate defines Weil numbers BY $|\iota(\pi)| = \sqrt q$ so cannot derive RH; RH for the Selberg class is a separate open conjecture (and zeta is in the class, so no shortcut exists); Howe (arXiv:2110.04221) shows the CONVERSE fails too, genuine Weil polynomials whose place counts go negative, so (c) and (d) are independent in both directions; the Hodge-type standard conjecture is open in char $p$ with Weil's curve proof consuming only the proven divisors-on-surfaces case (Milne arXiv:1509.00797). Toy battery 15/15. Cross-refs: #123 (the witness certified here), #90 (why D-H cannot be the witness), #71 (every candidate realizes a trace, none carries the polarization: this is that statement's finite executable form), #104 (the four faces of the coupling, of which AHK is face (iii)), #172/#148 (the uniformity mismatch), 08A/`missing_object_interface.md` (SP5 = M4 = what remains).

### 172. THE CHRISTOFFEL COSTUME IS GEOMETRY, PROVED (e1v, 2026-08-08): #171's handed-forward corpus question was executed as a LOCAL BUILD instead of a literature sweep, and it answers NEGATIVE AT THE ROOT: the germ-length blowup is now a THEOREM with an explicit potential-theoretic rate depending only on (gap, support, count), the tightness residual is family-blind under the mandatory gap equalization, and the whole functional is PROVABLY unable to see Q-linear independence because it is continuous while lattices are dense. The corpus sweep as posed is unsatisfiable in principle and must be re-aimed at sum rules; frontier UNMOVED. Round: BUILDER [`e1v_christoffel_gauge`](spectral/e1v_christoffel_gauge.md) (23/23 full and quick, npz byte-reproducible, quick clobber-free) with a SELF-RUN adversary round ([`_e1v_adversary.md`](spectral/_e1v_adversary.md), executable [`_e1v_adversary.py`](spectral/_e1v_adversary.py)): five posed attacks run, none landed, one closed an open item, one unposed attack independently confirmed the round's own conditioning diagnosis. Cases 3 (scope of the continuity obstruction) and 7 (independent K1/determinism re-verification) NOT RUN and open; no second party has read the rung.

**THE DURABLE PRODUCT IS A THEOREM, and it discharges e1u VERIFIER target 6 with a rate rather than a shape.** e1u conjectured that the footprint chain's total trace-length "grows like the reciprocal Christoffel function at the footpoint". It does not merely grow like it: the identity is exact, $X_{\rm total} = \sum_k p_k(0)^2 + \sum_k q_k(0)^2 = 1/\lambda_M(0) + Q_M(0)$, certified two ways (chain footprints via Lanczos-plus-recurrence, and pure Lagrange interpolation with no recurrence anywhere; the routes agree to 7e-50 on all 22 build-faces, and the first-kind share is 0.9999+ on every Face A). At full degree $M-1$ the Christoffel extremal problem IS an interpolation problem, giving the closed form $1/\lambda_M(0) = \sum_j \ell_j(0)^2/w_j$. Then: **THEOREM. For an $M$-atom probability measure with all atoms in $g \le |y| \le T$, put $n = \lfloor (M-1)/2\rfloor$ and $G = \frac12\,{\rm arccosh}\frac{T^2+g^2}{T^2-g^2}$ (the Green's function of the complement of $\pm[g,T]$ at 0). Then $\lambda_M(0) \le \cosh(2nG)^{-2}$, hence $X_{\rm total} \ge 1/\lambda_M(0) \ge \cosh(2nG)^2 \ge \frac14 e^{4nG}$.** Proof is two lines of Chebyshev on the square-map image. Verified: 0 violations over 22 build-faces AND over 400 random even configurations with non-uniform weights ($M$ to 24, $g/T$ from 0.005 to 0.9); correct at the $M = 2$ edge; degrades to trivial as $g \to 0$.

**THE TYPING VERDICT, MEASURED.** The rate depends only on $(g, T, M)$, so the exponential order of the type divergence is GEOMETRY, and only the tightness ratio $\rho = \log(1/\lambda)/(2\log\cosh 2nG)$ can carry anything else. Raw Face-A $\rho \in [1.15, 3.32]$ and its raw ordering (zeta tightest, fake loosest) is EXACTLY the gap ordering, i.e. not a family reading at all. Under the mandatory symmetric band exclusion (the e1u U2c control, here treated as a standing precondition and not an afterthought), family means are BEUR 1.206 / D-H 1.309 / ZETA 1.534, spread 1.27x, and with the one near-degenerate build excluded zeta equals D-H at 1.31; the reading survives equalization bands 13.6 / 16 / 20 / 25 (spreads 1.27x / 1.24x / 1.21x / 1.30x) and the family ORDER is not even stable across them. Destroying ALL microstructure at fixed $(g,T,M)$ moves the per-atom rate by $\le 0.061$; position jitter by $\le 0.028$ at $\varepsilon = 0.5$ with the modulus DECREASING in $M$; a structurally different surrogate (permuted gap multiset, preserving the spacing DISTRIBUTION and destroying the density profile) agrees. The Christoffel value at the footpoint is a functional of macroscopic density and gap.

**THE OBSTRUCTION, which is the part that re-aims the sweep.** $1/\lambda_M(0) = \sum_j \ell_j(0)^2/w_j$ is RATIONAL, hence CONTINUOUS, in the atom positions; Q-linear independence is totally disconnected with a DENSE complement (lattices). So no continuous functional of finitely many atom positions can detect it. Demonstrated, not just argued: snapping the true zero sets onto $(1/D)\mathbb{Z}$ (a genuine lattice, maximally Q-DEPENDENT) moves the per-atom rate by $\le 2.4$e-8 at $D = 10^6$, uniformly over $M \in [14, 64]$ (corr with $M$ $= -0.24$), and the COARSE snap at $D = 10^2$ is already a SMALLER perturbation than density-preserving microstructure destruction. **Consequence: PHASE_STATE next-step 2 as written ("any mechanism giving lambda-uniform Christoffel control at a point against atoms at Q-linearly independent positions, for an Euler+lattice reason") is unsatisfiable at the pointwise level. **[CORRECTED SAME DAY by adversary case 3, probe G:** the first version of this entry went on to infer "so do not run the sweep", and that inference is INVALID. The request as written is lambda-UNIFORM, i.e. a statement about the SEQUENCE, which the obstruction does not reach: continuity at every finite index is compatible with an arithmetic-sensitive limit, witnessed by normalized quadratic Weyl sums $c_N(lpha)$, each a trigonometric polynomial in $lpha$ and hence rational-blind by exactly this argument, with $c_N 	o 1/\sqrt q$ at $lpha = p/q$ and $	o 0$ at irrational $lpha$ (reproduced to 4-5 digits at $N = 10^5$). The obstruction stands as a theorem about finite-$M$ functionals; only the action drawn from it was too strong.**] The correct action is to SPLIT the sweep: the pointwise GROWTH-BOUND sub-corpus is dead (and there the Level-3 caution is genuinely sharpened, Lubinsky-type universality having already been out as bulk-asymptotic), while the SEQUENCE-LEVEL sub-corpus is where the surviving question lives, i.e. the Killip-Simon sum rules #170 already named as the proven neighbouring template. A second, INDEPENDENT reason to re-scope is untouched by the correction: the degree-regime mismatch (below).**

**THE PRE-REGISTERED Q4 EXIT FIRED, and the honest reading is finer than the expectation.** Sweeping e1u's U4a collision over ALL pair locations, the Christoffel response CHANGES SIGN (3 of 12 positive at ZETA 3.0, range $[-11.71, +5.89]$): the pre-registered "count-driven, same-signed" instrument was simply WRONG and was replaced in place with its reason recorded. Mechanism of the sign flip is the theorem's own content (removing the LOWEST pair widens $g$ and RAISES the bound), but geometry explains only $r^2 \approx 0.35$; a genuine local-position sensitivity is the rest. That residual is NOT arithmetic: the identical gap-equalized sweep on the density-matched fake gives $r^2 = 0.44$, the same mixture. **Position sensitivity is not arithmetic sensitivity** is the finding, and it is stronger than the expectation it replaced.

**A CONDITIONING LAW, declared and then independently confirmed.** The full grid put every anomaly on ONE build. A rule declared before application (flag a build whose minimal adjacent zero separation is below 1/4 of its median) flags exactly ZETA 3.0 (ratio 0.036; the (40.91, 41.03) pair at separation 0.124, which is e1u's own U4a choice) and ZETA sqrt13; corr(separation ratio, microstructure sensitivity) $= -0.768$; surgical repair of only the tight pairs removes 100% of one build's excess and 70% of the other's, and the leftover is REPAIR-DEPENDENT (removal surgery lands inside the band), so it is a property of the surgery. **Then the unposed attack confirmed it from outside**: checking e1v's re-derived atom sets against e1u's tracked npz, all 22 atom COUNTS reproduce exactly and every germ length reproduces to $\le 1.6$e-8 EXCEPT ZETA 3.0 Face A at 3.7e-4, four to five orders worse. The one build the rule flags is the one that fails to reproduce across machines, for the stated reason. A post-hoc rule that predicts an independent failure is a law, not a rationalization.

**THE INSTRUMENT LESSON, third occurrence in this arc.** Three separate would-be findings in this rung died to the SAME cause, an unequalized Face-A family comparison: a raw $\rho$ ordering, a DMV screen that differenced raw rates instead of displacements, and a defect-sweep comparison that read the fake as "far more geometric" ($r^2$ 0.92 vs 0.50, collapsing to 0.44 vs 0.35 once equalized). Together with e1u's U2c (the mA flip) and U3d (the profile artifact), **symmetric band equalization should be treated as a STANDING PRECONDITION on any Face-A cross-family claim, not a control to be remembered.** Two further instrument corrections are recorded in place: V1's first thresholds sat below the float64 floor of the inherited container (e1u stores lengths as float64, so the identity check is float-floored and the mpmath certificate is the separate route comparison), and the K1 source scan was originally a TAUTOLOGY that passed unconditionally, rewritten and then verified to catch a planted `mp.zetazero(1)` call and only it.

**PORTABILITY FINDING (infrastructure, not mathematics), surfaced by running this arc on a second machine for the first time.** A fresh machine reproduces the tracked e1t/e1u record only to ~3e-11 absolute on the build eigenvalue, and two e1u self-tests fail there (U0b at 1e-15, U5a's fneg_q record check at 1e-9) while EVERY structural number reproduces to printed precision. Diagnosed to the root, three steps: (i) `build_float` and `build_comb` are BIT-IDENTICAL here (max|dQ| = 0.0), so builder provenance is not the cause and the e1t T0a/T0b claim holds on this machine too; (ii) the ambient-dps sweep shows dps 20/25/30 agree to 1e-12 and dps 15 and 35/40 select a DIFFERENT even ground state (eps flips by 100%), so the record is on this machine's branch and the "dps-25 branch" documentation is confirmed, with the branch now bracketed on BOTH sides rather than only below; (iii) the build's archimedean density uses a central-difference digamma at $h = 10^{-5}$ in float64, whose own relative accuracy is measured at 5e-10 against exact mpmath digamma, so eps is determined by the build to ~1e-10 ABSOLUTE and the observed 2.2e-11 deviation sits BELOW the quantity's own accuracy floor. **The two failing checks are same-machine bit-reproducibility assertions, not numerical-agreement checks; they compare tracked build outputs below the noise floor of the quantity itself.** **FIXED 2026-08-09, with evidence, after Owen ratified it** ([`_e1u_portability.py`](spectral/_e1u_portability.py), tracked): the tolerances were PINNED from full-grid measurement, not chosen, and the loosened checks were then verified to retain their teeth. Worst deviations: |d eps| 3.7e-11 (tolerance 1e-9, 27x headroom), |d fneg_q| 1.3e-8 (tolerance 1e-6, 80x), and a THIRD such check found only in FULL mode, U2a's `base_err` against the tracked function-face baseline, 2.7e-7 (tolerance 1e-5, 37x, still ~1400x below the smallest tracked gap). TEETH VERIFIED: the documented dps-branch flip (the even ground-state selection changes at dps 15 and 35) still trips the loosened eps check by 3e5x while the on-branch build passes, so these are numerical-agreement checks now and still catch what they were built to catch. e1u is 21/21 full and 18/18 quick on this machine, matching the record's own claims, and the tracked npz was restored after the full run so this machine's values did NOT replace the original record. SUB-FINDING worth keeping: U2a's four CONTENT clauses (zeta-first ordering, improvement-ratio gap, shape-blindness, no monotone decay) reproduced EXACTLY on the fresh machine and only its record comparison failed, which is the cleanest possible demonstration that all three checks were testing reproducibility of BYTES, not of FINDINGS. Separately, and CORRECTED the same day: the dossier disagreed with e1u's OWN npz about BEUR 3.0/32. The conditioning row read `36 / 41.0` against the npz's `38 / 43.4631` (reproduced here to 3e-12), and the discrepancy propagates to two Q2 gap cells (m-face A BEUR `0.0222, 0.0038` recomputing as `0.0222, 0.0032`; Jacobi shape A BEUR `0.2532, 0.0985` recomputing as `0.2532, 0.1146`), each differing only in the lambda 2.6 to 3.0 entry, i.e. exactly the one involving BEUR 3.0: the dossier's BEUR 3.0 numbers were written from a 36-atom run while the npz holds the 38-atom one. Conditioning row corrected in place, Q2 cells corrected in a note (that table carries adversary annotations). No verdict moves.

**THE HANDED-FORWARD DRIFT QUESTION, ANSWERED SAME WEEK (V8, 2026-08-09; four new builds at lambda = 4.0 all families and 4.5 for zeta, about 40 min of compute; e1v now 26/26 full, extended and quick).** The entry above named the lambda-drift of the tightness residual as the cheapest genuinely new measurement in the uniformity coordinate. It is now measured, and it cuts BOTH ways. **(i) The drift is GEOMETRY**: replacing each real zero configuration by the EQUALLY SPACED one with the same (g, T, M) after band equalization predicts the real residual with correlation +0.967 and worst absolute residual 0.094 over the 11 builds the declared V7 rule does not flag (+0.936 / 0.080 on the pre-extension grid). An arithmetic-free surrogate matched only on gap, support and count reproduces the whole drift to a tenth. **(ii) It does NOT saturate.** With the surrogate licensed the predictor is free to evaluate far past the buildable window (g = 13.6 fixed by the FE budget, T = 2 pi lambda^2, M = 2 x the RvM count in [g, T], used as a synthetic counting model and not as a discriminating input): in the right coordinate the slopes d rho / d log T run 0.303, 0.414, 0.430, 0.444, 0.462, 0.477, 0.489 and converge to c = 0.48, so **rho ~ 0.48 log T, growing logarithmically without bound**. Since rho is measured-over-bound, **Theorem V2's Chebyshev bound is order-tight on the buildable window (rho in [1.08, 1.67] unflagged) but is NOT asymptotically sharp**, and this entry's V2b reading ("the geometric rate is the leading order") is hereby SCOPED to the buildable window rather than asserted as a limit statement. **(iii) The pre-registered Q2 exit nevertheless does NOT fire.** That exit said a growing rho would mean the residual is where everything lives and would REOPEN the pointwise route. The first half happened; the second does not follow, because the growth is exhibited IN FULL by the equally spaced surrogate, a configuration with zero arithmetic content, and (i) shows the real configurations track it. The bound leaves a growing gap; the gap is filled by geometry, not left open for arithmetic. NET: the coordinate is now closed on both halves (the pointwise obstruction, and the drift), and the only thing left open in it is the SHARP form of the theorem, an upper bound capturing the log T factor.

**HONEST LIMITS.** Finite objects only: this decides the POINTWISE question and cannot prove no limit-level mechanism exists (the continuity obstruction is an obstruction to finite-$M$ detection, with its modulus measured stable over $M \in [14,64]$). The degree regime is NOT the corpus's: Totik / Nevai / Lubinsky / MNT study $\lambda_n$ for a FIXED measure as $n \to \infty$, whereas this object is the diagonal $n = M(\lambda)-1$ with the measure moving, where the problem degenerates to interpolation, so the corpus's uniformity mechanisms do not directly apply either way (a real scoping gap, and another reason to re-aim rather than read more). In-sample small grids; the DMV screen ran on two clean matched configs, which is thin. The theorem is ONE-SIDED (no matching upper bound proved), though a fixed-gap-fraction probe shows $\rho$ saturating in $[1.30, 1.42]$ as $M$ grows 12-fold, which also identifies the observed lambda-drift of $\rho_{\rm eq}$ as the gap FRACTION shrinking at fixed $g$, not an $M$ effect. NEXT (handed forward): the $\lambda$-drift of $\rho_{\rm eq}$ is the cheapest genuinely new measurement in the uniformity coordinate (does it saturate on a larger grid?); Theorem V2 plus the two identities are four Lean-ready VERIFIER targets that SUPERSEDE e1u target 6; the modular/Hecke rung (#166-#168) is unchanged as the front. ### 157. THE TAMENESS TRADE IS TWO-BODIED AND ONE LEG IS REFUTED: #156's NG2 is NOT an instance of a general "tameness forbids the prime sum" law; the saturation leg survives (PROVEN) but is orthogonal to the RH engine, the tameness leg is REFUTED (a supersimple structure DOES carry the primes; the archimedean ORDER is the wild ingredient), and the four #155 reading-note items are cleared with zero survivors, 2026-07-10. A SURVEYOR + BUILDER + ADVERSARY + survivor-screen loop on the Section-10 heuristic of [`docs/03_research/model_theoretic_frobenius.md`](../docs/03_research/model_theoretic_frobenius.md) (dossier: [`docs/03_research/tameness_trade.md`](../docs/03_research/tameness_trade.md), self-contained; working files in gitignored `scratchpad/tameness_trade/`). THE SPLIT: the target functional $S(f)=\sum_n\Lambda(n)f(\log n)$ (the explicit formula's prime side) is denied to $K$ by two logically independent legs at different tiers. LEG A (saturation, PROVEN): Lemma P3 = in any $\aleph_1$-saturated $M$ no countably-infinite set is definable over countable parameters (enumerate $D=\{a_i\}$, the type $\{x\in D\}\cup\{x\ne a_i\}$ is finitely satisfiable, realized by $b\in D\setminus\{a_i\}$, contradiction); hence the standard-prime diagonal is undefinable and $S(f)$ is EXTERNAL for TAME and WILD saturated models alike (a wild $\aleph_1$-saturated monster of $\mathrm{Th}(\mathbb{N},+,\times)$ also fails: the standard cut is not definable by overspill, $f$ is external). This generalizes NG2(iii) off the ultraproduct (no $\{0,1\}^\omega$ branching, no CH; countable ultraproducts are $\aleph_1$-saturated, so the branching WAS the saturation). CAVEAT (adversary 1c, load-bearing): the structure that CARRIES the formula is the STANDARD $(\mathbb{N},+,\times)$, which is NOT $\aleph_1$-saturated, so Leg A kills only the twist-to-prime-diagonal move and is ORTHOGONAL to "which engine carries RH"; the substantive content is Leg B. LEG B (tameness forbids the prime set, REFUTED AS STATED): the counterexample is KAPLAN-SHELAH, "Decidability and classification of the theory of integers with primes" (arXiv:1601.07099, read at source this pass) Thm 1.2 = under Dickson, $\mathrm{Th}(\mathbb{Z},+,\mathrm{Pr})$ WITHOUT order is decidable and SUPERSIMPLE of U-rank 1, so a maximally-tame (supersimple) structure DOES carry the prime predicate as a definable set. NAMED SO NO FUTURE SESSION RE-MAKES THE CLAIM: "a tame/simple structure cannot carry the primes" is FALSE. The correct invariant is NOT the prime set but the archimedean ORDER coupled to $+$ and the primes: BJW/Woods use full $\mathrm{Th}(\mathbb{N},+,\mathrm{Pr})$ WITH order (where $<$ is free from $+$ over $\mathbb{N}$), and primes-with-order mod Dickson force $\times$ (undecidable, wild); the order-free additive prime set is TAME. Reinforced: Poizat [Poi14 Thm 25] / Point-Schmidt [PS14] give $\mathrm{Th}(\mathbb{Z},+,P_q)$ SUPERSTABLE (a sparse multiplicative set, tame on its own). So ACFA/the ultraproduct fails to carry $S(f)$ for lack of the ORDER (the incommensurable $\{\log p\}$ scaling, #62/#153), REINFORCED BY but not CAUSED BY supersimplicity: this RE-CONFIRMS #153/#62 from the definability side, the honest humbler net. THE UNCONDITIONAL KEYSTONE ("ordered prime structure forces $\times$ without Dickson") is OPEN; the conditional form is Dickson-dependent. PUBLISHABLE-SHAPED CLAIM WITHDRAWN (inverted central citation now fixed, invariant re-identified, SOTA corpus was unengaged); it could become a legitimate EXPOSITORY structural-obstruction note only after engaging Kaplan-Shelah / Poizat / Point-Schmidt / Bes [Bes01] / Korec [Kor01] with the keystone flagged OPEN, recorded as a possible-future-expository item, NOT a result in hand. C3 (survivor screen, MECHANISM/HEURISTIC, stands, reinforced by order-is-wild): all four #156 survivors pass the two-conjunct WATCH clause; C3 is a CONDITIONAL UNIVERSAL = any survivor that reaches $S(f)$ injects it archimedeanly (Connes = scaling flow $\mathbb{R}^\ast_+\subset C_k$ on $L^2(\mathbb{A}/k^\ast)$; CCM = prolate/Sonin + periodization map $E$ on the log-line; Deninger = foliated $\mathbb{R}$-flow with closed-orbit lengths $\log p$), while Borger is the ODD ONE OUT that does NOT carry $S(f)$ at all (integral/multiplicative-descent side, all primes non-invertible, no $\log$, no summation; carries the Euler PRODUCT not the sum; pushing it to a trace formula needs $\Phi_t=\prod_p\psi_p^{t/\log p}$ = the archimedean injection, landing it back in the analytic family with Connes' K1 wall). ### 156. THE MODEL-THEORETIC FROBENIUS IS CONSTITUTIONALLY GENERIC: the one published global characteristic-0 Frobenius (the ultraproduct $K = \prod_p \bar{\mathbb{F}}_p/\mathcal{U}$ with coordinatewise $\sigma$, a model of ACFA) cannot fill R1, and the failure yields two proven no-gos, a corrected Borger trivialization, and a two-conjunct WATCH clause, 2026-07-09. A SURVEYOR + BUILDER + ADVERSARY loop on a corpus with zero prior repo coverage (Ax 1968 pseudofinite fields; Chatzidakis-Hrushovski ACFA; Hrushovski twisted Lang-Weil arXiv:math/0406514; Shuddhodan-Varshavsky arXiv:2106.10682); dossier: [`docs/03_research/model_theoretic_frobenius.md`](../docs/03_research/model_theoretic_frobenius.md) (self-contained; working files in gitignored scratchpad). NG1 (PROVEN, adversary-rescoped): every unital ring endomorphism of every unital ring fixes the prime ring pointwise, and fixes $m/n$ wherever $n$ is invertible, so on any $\mathbb{Q}$-algebra receptacle (K, the adele ring $\mathbb{A}_\mathbb{Q}$, $\prod \mathbb{Z}/p^2/\mathcal{U}$, Witt ultraproducts) the imported arithmetic is endomorphism-rigid; Fermat's little theorem is the coordinatewise witness ($\sigma \circ d = d$ on the diagonal). So the #145 identity-Frobenius degeneracy is LIMIT-INDEPENDENT rigidity (holds for index-set-quotienting AND index-set-preserving limits), not a limit artifact; the S1 kill is the INFINITUDE of the forced fixed set (prime field $= \mathbb{Q}$ vs finite $\mathbb{F}_q$, the K3-honest disanalogy); scope = ring-endomorphism-shaped fillers only, correspondences/motives/flows escape. NG2 (tiers i, iii PROVEN; ii PROVEN at rescoped conclusion; iv MECHANISM): the ultraproduct provably cannot carry the explicit formula's sum over primes: (i) functorial extractions are $\mathcal{U}$-null-invariant and deleting $p=2$ changes the prime sum; (ii) ACFA is supersimple and $(\mathbb{Z},+,\times)$ has SOP, so arithmetic is not interpretable (this closes the interpreted-arithmetic route only; the primary kill is (iii)); (iii) every internal set is finite or of cardinality $\ge 2^{\aleph_0}$ (ZFC-clean branching, no CH), so NO twist $\sigma \circ \phi$ has fixed-point set the countable prime-indexed diagonal $\{d(p)\}$; (iv) full nonstandard universes are conservative (Henson-Keisler caveat carried). Rider (A1 executed): a canonical prime-indexed EXTERNAL subset $d(P)$ exists (unique ring hom $\mathbb{Z} \to K$), but NG1 proves it is $\sigma$-inert and its analytic content ($\log p$, order, summation) is computed in $\mathbb{Z}$, K a passive container; no canonical index-aware $\sigma$-interacting decoration found. This is the #153 additive-lattice wall met from the model-theory side: the maximal first-order-preserving all-primes-at-once limit forgets exactly $\log p$ (whose lattice is #62's elementary fact), the ordering, and summation, so the #153 glue must be metric/archimedean-aware, not elementary. BORGER FLIP ON RECORD (do not re-make the overclaim): the first-draft headline "the ultrafilter DESTROYS the joint $\Lambda$-structure" is FALSE and withdrawn; the corrected theorem is TRIVIALIZATION: the ultrafilter inverts every standard prime, so $qK = K$ and the Frobenius-lift congruence $\psi_q(x) \equiv x^q \bmod qK$ is stateable but VACUOUS, and by Wilkerson a $\Lambda$-structure on a $\mathbb{Q}$-algebra is just a commuting endomorphism family, so $K \cong \mathbb{C}$ carries continuum-many, all with zero $\mathbb{F}_1$-descent data; ultralimit-along-primes and Borger descent are structurally disjoint (one quotients the index set to a generic point and inverts the primes, the other keeps $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathbb{Z})$ alive with all primes non-invertible). K1 VERDICT (HOLDS, verified at source three ways + live arXiv sweep): every known proof of twisted Lang-Weil consumes Deligne 1974/purity (Hrushovski Prop 11.11 verbatim; Fujiwara-Pink; SV + Bourbaki 2308.16132); untwisted Lang-Weil needs Weil 1948; both tiers consume finite-field RH, so the corpus REDISTRIBUTES RH-over-$\mathbb{F}_q$ (to a.a.-$p$ first-order statements, to char 0 along ultrafilters) and never re-sources it; Ax p. 239 opens the corpus with the dependency and p. 241 concedes "no striking number-theoretic applications". WATCH CLAUSE (adversary-corrected, necessary-only conjunction): the R1 filler must be index-set-preserving AND non-endomorphism-shaped; the adele witness kills sufficiency of the first conjunct (index-preserving, yet NG1-rigid), and Tate's thesis shows restricted products DO carry the prime sum, so "no limit along the primes produces the sum" is true only for index-set-QUOTIENTING limits. Matches the survivors' shapes: Borger, Deninger, CCM $\mathcal{E}$, Connes. NEW PUBLISHED POINTERS: Cherlin-Jarden (via Hrushovski p. 6: the ACFA generic automorphism exists on no field of finite transcendence degree, $\bar{\mathbb{Q}}$ included = a published negative at exactly the global base; primary source unpulled); Dor-Hrushovski arXiv:2212.05366 (valued fields, motivic intersection for difference varieties, the one corner moving toward L-functions = WATCH); Hrushovski v2 abstract (transformal zero-cycles encapsulate zeta/L-data, per-prime). D-H: exempt by non-mimicry (no Euler structure to consume); route declared HOLLOW per #131 (walls at NG1/NG2 before any discriminating positivity, nothing delegated), map contribution real (kills are allowed to be L-function-blind). LEVEL note (#133): everything here is Level-independent no-go cartography, no Level-3 mirror risk. HANDED FORWARD: Lean T1-T4 (T1 = map_ratCast + ZMod.pow_card, cheap; T2 = the ultraproduct object; T3 = the vacuity/trivialization statement, NOT unsatisfiability; T4 = the cardinality dichotomy); ADVERSARY residual A3 (internality of twisted fixed sets under arbitrary parameters); SURVEYOR: the tameness-trade sharpening (is "definable quantitative fixed-point theory $\Rightarrow$ cannot state the summed explicit formula" a theorem? ACFA instance proven, general law HEURISTIC) + whether pseudofinite-dimension theory ever sums over primes. ### 153. THE CCM DOOR PASSES THE DENSITY GATE BY REFRAME: one spectral circle of circumference log(T/2pi) carries the whole budget while the primes to T/2pi enter as form coefficients (the TWO-METER LAW), the lattice map E absorbs the surplus exactly where the Beurling fourth clause demands, and the open front has moved to a uniform determinant-class limit, 2026-07-02. The parallel SURVEYOR pass commissioned at the #151/#152 fork (reading note: [`ccm_zeta_cycle_density_gate.md`](../docs/03_research/reading_notes/ccm_zeta_cycle_density_gate.md); sources arXiv 2106.01715, 2310.18423, 2511.22755, 2511.23257, 2112.05500, and the Connes-Moscovici PNAS UV-prolate paper; sections read via full-text extraction, key quotes double-confirmed, digit-level flags recorded). Q1 PRIME ACTIVATION: activation is by SUPPORT: test functions on [lambda^{-1}, lambda] truncate the Weil form to Lambda(n), n <= x = lambda^2 (2106.01715 eq 2.11; 2511.22755 eq 3.19: "only ... the primes p <= x = lambda^2"); matched heights run to T ~ 2 pi lambda^2 (edge check: 2 pi times 16.5 = 103.67 vs gamma_31 = 103.73; 2511.22755: first 50 zeros with primes <= 13 at errors 2.4e-55 to 1e-3), so the implied activation law is P(T) ~ T/2pi, LINEAR in T, exponentially more primes than #151's direct-sum budget P ~ log T (the P(T) law is the surveyor's labeled inference; no paper states one). Q2 THE REFRAME (correction applied in place to #151 and the interface doc): there is NO overcount because the realized glue is NOT a direct sum of prime circles: ONE spectral circle of circumference log x carries the entire eigenvalue budget, and log x = log(T/2pi) at the matched edge (measured 2.8034 vs 2.8036), so #151's measured budget is confirmed EXACTLY while its per-prime-circles interpretation is superseded. THE TWO-METER LAW: spectral meter = circumference ~ log(T/2pi); data meter = Euler coefficients down to n ~ T/2pi; the exponential mismatch between the meters is absorbed by the LATTICE: the zeros are the spectrum on the orthogonal complement of the lattice-periodization image Sigma_mu E (zeta cycles, Thm 1.1(i)), i.e. ~x surplus states are eaten by the image of E. Q3 WHERE THE LATTICE ENTERS (the #152 fourth-clause audit of the door): three sites: the map E(f)(x) = x^{1/2} sum_{n>0} f(nx) DEFINES zeta cycles and carves the zeros out of the circle (verbatim Def 1.1/Thm 1.1(i)); the operator assembly consumes only explicit-formula coefficients (theta-density, pole, Lambda), lattice upstream; and the lattice re-enters at the UNPROVEN Section-7 step, which CCM themselves call "the main remaining obstacle to our approach to RH" (verbatim). So the door PAYS the Beurling fourth clause through E (a Beurling fake's E_B exists but has no Poisson/FE property, our e2ak C5b), and the discipline's question "which clause does the fake fail" has a named answer for this door. PER-PRIME W6 EXISTS EXACTLY AND THE GLOBAL GLUE IS FORCED TO BE SEMILOCAL: the W6-on-the-derived-base question factors into periodicity (supplied, per prime, = the cyclotomic circles) + a scale-coupled determinant-class gluing (open, now SHAPED: height T sees primes up to ~log T, the CCM prolate shape) + duality (open, PROP-global), 2026-07-02. RESULT (A), the positive half: PER-PRIME W6 EXISTS EXACTLY. In log coordinates each prime carries the circle R/(log p)Z (the cyclotomic structure = the orbit closing at p), and the trace of the translation flow on it is two-sided by POISSON SUMMATION: log p sum_k g(k log p) = sum_n g_hat(2 pi n / log p), verified to 1.3e-15 (p = 2, 3, 5, two Gaussians); the geometric side is the p-branch of the explicit formula's prime side and the spectral side is EXACTLY the poles of the local Euler factor (1-p^{-s})^{-1} at s = 2 pi i n / log p (verified to 1e-12); the cohomological costume of this identity is Hesselholt's per-p TP determinant formula. So the eigenvalue side is not missing per prime; W6 fails only globally. RESULT (the mechanism of the F_q magic): COMMENSURABILITY. Over F_q every place's circumference is a multiple of log q, all local circles sit on ONE circle, and the global zero set is a finite union of arithmetic progressions of step 2 pi / log q (verified on the e2ai control curve: |L| < 1e-9 along the progression); over Q the circumferences {log p} are Q-linearly independent and the zero set has no progression structure (first-ten-zeros gap spread 5.13 vs 0 for a progression). One circle vs incommensurable circles IS the function-field/number-field divide at the W6 face. RESULT (B), the measured constraint: the naive gluing (direct sum of the prime circles) is QUANTITATIVELY WRONG, overcounting eigenvalues 45x at T = 100 against Riemann-von Mangoldt; density matching forces theta(P) ~ log(T / 2 pi e), i.e. HEIGHT T SEES PRIMES UP TO ~LOG T (measured P* = 7, 11, 13, 17 at T = 1e3, 1e4, 1e5, 1e6, with theta(P*) tracking log(T/2 pi e) within one prime's slack). So the glue cannot be a direct sum or any fixed finite assembly: it must be SCALE-COUPLED (semilocal: finitely many places per scale, more at higher scale) and determinant-class (#148's clause). That is exactly the CCM semilocal prolate shape (#111/#114/#118), with the archimedean place entering as the two-clock organ (e2ff/#44). (MECHANISM CORRECTED same day by the #153 survey: in the REALIZED CCM door the growing budget is ONE spectral circle of circumference log x = log(T/2pi), while the primes p <= x = T/2pi enter as FORM COEFFICIENTS, not circumferences: the two-meter law. The circumference budget log(T/2pi e) measured here is confirmed exactly; the "primes up to ~log T active" reading was the direct-sum picture's law and is superseded for the realized door, where the lattice map E absorbs the ~x surplus states.) THE COMPRESSION: the #150 next-rung question does not open a new front; it lands on the already-identified live door. C1 (= SP2^SP3, the counting-side joint) now reads: per-prime circles (HAVE, cyclotomic) + semilocal determinant-class glue (OPEN, SHAPED, = the CCM door) + duality forcing the equality (OPEN, the PROP-global rider). ### 149. THE GAUSS-LEMMA FLOOR IS A MACHINE-CHECKED THEOREM: the vF disc model has no Siegel-lemma slot, so the S4/R1 operator is provably its only open slot, 2026-07-01. The #147 handed-forward probe (the "smallest checkable sub-question" of the van Frankenhuijsen deep read, its Section 8 spec executed verbatim) landed both ways. THE THEOREM (the Gauss-lemma floor): any nonzero f in Z[z] vanishing at z = 1/p with multiplicity at least m_p for each prime p in a finite set P has (pz-1)^{m_p} | f in Z[z] (each pz-1 is primitive, so Gauss's lemma descends the Q[z]-divisibility), hence log|lead(f)| >= sum_P m_p log p; with vF's multiplicities m_p = floor(log_p x) the floor is EXACTLY Chebyshev psi(x) (integer-exactly prod p^{m_p} = lcm(1..x) = e^{psi(x)}), and the canonical product f_x = prod_{p<=x}(pz-1)^{m_p} ATTAINS it: the minimal height of a prime-forced vanisher IS psi(x), zero slack. LEAN ([`GaussFloor.lean`](../lean/ZetaRH/GaussFloor.lean), #GF-1..#GF-5, sorry-free, axiom-clean [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound], full library build green, lean/README target table updated by the verifier): the per-prime floor routes through leading-coefficient multiplicativity and recombines on the INTEGER side (pairwise-coprime prime powers; no polynomial-coprimality argument anywhere), and the capstone `gauss_floor_of_vanishing` takes genuine VANISHING data (rootMultiplicity over Q, where 1/p lives) and descends via Gauss's lemma (a `dvd_of_map_dvd` needing only the divisor primitive). PYTHON ([`e2ah_gauss_floor.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2ah_gauss_floor.py), integer-exact, all PASS): A equality case (x in {2..100}: |lead(f_x)| = prod p^{m_p} = lcm(1..x), sympy expansion vs integer arithmetic as two independent code paths); B cofactor stress (300 random cofactors, floor never violated, equality iff |lead(g)| = 1); C the adversarial half, naive interpolation from the rational nullspace of the derivative conditions: at the minimal degree D = sum m_p the kernel is 1-dimensional and its primitive integer generator equals +/- the canonical product in all six configurations tested, a COMPLETE no-cheaper-vanisher certificate at degree D (uniqueness up to sign), plus 449 random lattice vanishers at D+1..D+3 all obeying the floor. THE NO-GO MEANING: the pigeonhole (S3, Siegel's lemma) can never construct below the floor, because the floor equals the quantity the transferred Stepanov engine is supposed to bound (psi(x) <= x + O(x^{1/2+eps}) is the target; PNT itself is the statement that the floor is ~x); so the vF model's only open slot is the S4/R1 cheap-multiplicity operator, and `gauss_floor_of_vanishing` is the CHEAPEST SCREEN for any future BUILDER candidate claiming a Nevanlinna-style transfer (either make vanishing at {1/p} cheaper than the floor or supply the operator; screen against the floor first, then Vojta's relative-tangent constraint, per sourcing_gap_r1.md). K1-clean (no zeros of zeta appear), RH-independent, elementary: a no-go COORDINATE, not a positivity statement. STATUS (RESOLVED 2026-07-02): the SURVEYOR novelty pass + the PUBLICATIONS gate ran; registered as P10, DEVELOPING, carried by the FORMAL axis. Novelty split: the assembled statement is APPARENTLY NOVEL (vF 2008 Section 4 re-read from the PDF: the setting and N_f(0,1) = psi(x) are there, no lower bound, no Gauss lemma, no lcm; no direct-statement hit in the Gelfond-Schnirelman / Nair 1982 / Pritsker 2005 or integer-Chebyshev literatures, which run the same lcm-vs-polynomial trade in REVERSE), while the mechanism is FOLKLORE (the rational root theorem with multiplicity via Gauss descent; the m = 1 case is Mathlib's `den_dvd_of_is_root`), so the math axis is parked-as-new-math (expository home = P4's counting-roads section; no standalone arXiv note) and the venue is a Mathlib PR (generalize #GF-2/#GF-5 to `den^rootMultiplicity ∣ leadingCoeff` in the `RationalRoot.lean` idiom; Mathlib has the identity side, `Chebyshev.lcmUpto`/`psi_eq_log_lcmUpto`, but no multiplicity or multi-point floor). One scope flag (D1): the uniqueness-at-minimal-degree clause is Python-certified + trivial on paper but NOT in the Lean development; add #GF-6 or scope machine-checked claims to floor + equality. VAN FRANKENHUIJSEN 2008 DEEP-READ: the one published Stepanov-to-Spec(Z) transfer names its own wall (R1 twice + no diagonal) in the author's own words, nobody has filled it in 18 years, and the three-witness convergence lands, 2026-07-01. THE MODEL: split C x C into a geometric coordinate (the polynomial variable) and an arithmetic coordinate (the coefficients); over Z take integer-coefficient functions on the unit disc; then the Nevanlinna counting function N_f(0,1) = psi(x) EXACTLY for f = prod_{p<=x}(1-pz)^{[log_p x]} and Poisson-Jensen = the product formula, so the S2/S3/S5 layer is PRESENT, matching #145's transfer table slot for slot. THE WALLS, VERBATIM FROM THE AUTHOR: "there is no diagonal and we have to force the vanishing of f at the primes in an artificial manner" (= PROP-global) and "We do not know what the analogue of the choice b_i = a_i^{p^mu} could be" (= R1, the S4 cheap-multiplicity slot); and he openly identifies the missing Frobenius flow with the DERIVATIVE operator (via the Deninger/Connes shift-on-R), i.e. makes the Frobenius = derivation identification in print in 2008. Vojta's CIME notes Section 29 (read verbatim this session) supply the missing object's second name plus a SHAPE CONSTRAINT: the number-field case has "no known counterpart to the derivative," and the needed arithmetic derivative must live in the RELATIVE tangent bundle, relative to the arithmetic base (Conjecture 29.1, the tautological conjecture: a named TARGET with a chain rule, not a mechanism, proven exactly where a derivative already exists). No positivity/M4 appears anywhere in the proposal: the M4-in-disguise check comes back negative again, this time from the primary source. ZERO DESCENDANTS, verified three ways: 26 citations, none develops the model; the author's own 2014 book (LMS ST 80, publisher-page depth) moves its weight to the Connes shift-operator frame, walking into the already-mapped K1 positivity wall (one provenance softening flagged against #145's Section 5.6 phrasing: the book's descendant of the proposal is the shift-operator/NCG frame, not a developed Nevanlinna model); post-2008 Nevanlinna-Diophantine progress (Ru-Vojta 2020, McQuillan/Yamanoi function-field abc, difference Nevanlinna) is all on derivative-free entries or where the derivative already exists. ONE GENUINE SHARPENING (the Gauss-lemma floor): in the disc model, any nonzero f in Z[z] vanishing at 1/p with multiplicity m_p for all p <= x has log|lead(f)| >= sum m_p log p (divisibility + primitivity + Gauss's lemma), equality at prod(pz-1)^{m_p}, so the minimal height of a prime-forced vanisher EQUALS the quantity to be bounded: the pigeonhole (S3) can never construct below the floor, the only possible engine is the S4 cheap-multiplicity structure = the R1 operator, and PNT itself is the statement that the floor is ~ x. An integer-exact, Lean-able mini-theorem (Mathlib has Polynomial.content + Gauss's lemma); the probe is IN FLIGHT (parallel agent, 2026-07-01), not duplicated here. THE META-FINDING (the arc's one sentence): three independent witnesses now converge on the same two-clause law: the Stepanov engine audit (#145, the proof-engine side), the sieve corpus (#146, the consumer side), and a 2008 primary source who reached the wall from inside the transfer attempt itself (testimony, not proof). Form roads wall at M4; counting roads wall at R1 + the missing diagonal; and the missing object is ONE object in three names (Frobenius = derivation = diagonal), M4 its polarization face, R1 its operator face. Over Z the form-free moves all EXIST: S2 aux-space = Arakelov h^0 / Bost slopes; S3 Riemann-count = the Bombieri-Vaaler Siegel lemma / van der Geer-Schoof arithmetic RR; S5 degree-bound = the product formula / Liouville; S7 one-sided-to-two-sided = Landau oscillation + the proven FE, so a ONE-SIDED psi(x) <= x + O(x^{1/2+eps}) already forces RH and any counting substitute needs only UPPER bounds (the halved target; stated in print in vF 2008 Table 4.1, #147). The two Frobenius-powered moves are ABSENT and both are R1: S1 (the only ring endomorphism of Z is the identity, which by Fermat IS the Frobenius lift at every p, so graph = diagonal and the fixed-point count degenerates; plus Spec(Z) x Spec(Z) = Spec(Z), the PROP-global rider) and S4 (no derivation Z -> Z; transcendence theory outsources the derivative to the archimedean place, which is why its theorems are about infinity and never about primes; Buium's Fermat-quotient p-derivations are the p-adic shadow, trivial on Z itself). THE M4-IN-DISGUISE CHECK IS NEGATIVE: every positivity the engine consumes (Siegel-lemma convexity, effectivity of zero multiplicities, coefficient non-negativity in S7) exists over Z and is categorically distinct from the (1,n-1) signature; the engine never meets M4.
