### 143. THE CIRCLE-ROOTED INTERLACING ENGINE DOES NOT EXIST, AND THE CLASSICAL CIRCLE CERTIFICATE IS ITSELF A POLARIZATION: the #140 follow-up (survey + toy battery + adversary) shows the MSS engine's missing third on the circle is the extremal-selection order, whose only known manufacturers are the operator (unitarity) or the positivity (Lee-Yang) already graded; Schur-Cohn 1922 independently states the M4 shape, 2026-07-01. Prompted by Owen approving the "poke the self-adjointness crack" pick. THE QUESTION (from #140): the MSS engine crosses R1 for graphs fueled by real-rootedness = self-adjointness; can a circle-rooted variant (for self-inversive polynomials with zeros on |z| = 1, the Frobenius/L-polynomial locus) run without presupposing the operator? THE SURVEY (7 threads, citations author/year/venue-verified): NO such engine exists, and the miss is structural. Two thirds of the engine are already present on the circle: (i) the convolution algebra (Grace-Szego composition; Suffridge 1976; Leake-Ryder, Adv. Math. 2020; and MSS's own Theorem 1.5 legally applies to unitary inputs since unitaries are normal), and (ii) the interlacing ORDER (POPUC angle-interlacing: Jones-Njastad-Thron 1989; Simon 2006 via rank-one perturbations of unitaries; Castillo-Petronilho 2017). The missing third is the EXTREMAL-SELECTION step ("some member at least as good as the average"): MSS selection optimizes a one-sided bound on an ordered real spectrum, while circle-rootedness is an EXACT LOCUS with no native one-sided order; manufacturing the order by passing to trace coordinates x = T + q/T (DiPippo-Howe 1998) just re-poses real-rootedness, the thing self-adjointness was paying for. No paper attempting circle-rooted interlacing families was found (a literature gap, not a proven no-go); no attempt to run interlacing TOWARD arithmetic purity exists (Winnie Li, Phil. Trans. R. Soc. A 2020, presents MSS and the arithmetic constructions as disjoint; the "Sarnak commentary" this session remembered could NOT be located and stays unverified). THE GEM (an all-roads convergence from a corpus never before consumed, classical geometry of polynomials): the classical certificate for circle-rootedness of a self-inversive polynomial (Schur 1917/Cohn 1922, via the Schur-Cohn Hermitian/Bezoutian form, whose signature counts roots inside/outside; the self-inversive case is the maximally singular branch, resolved by Cohn's derivative criterion) is EXACTLY: the functional equation (self-inversiveness, an identical vanishing) PLUS a Hermitian positive-semidefiniteness (of the derivative's Schur-Cohn form). Pairing free, positivity the entire content: the 1922 root-location corpus independently states the M4 shape, rhyming with Caratheodory-Toeplitz on the moment side (#123). THE TOY BATTERY ([`toy/circle_interlacing.py`](toy/circle_interlacing.py), 11/11, adversary-corrected): (1) the substitution DICTIONARY verified end-to-end: 8 brute-force elliptic curves + a genuine genus-2 count (y^2 = x^5+x^3+1 over F_5: N_1 = 9, N_2 = 37, with an N_3 = 108 Newton-vs-brute-force firewall) land on-circle and in-window; the #123 genus-2 fake has h(x) = x^2-4x+5 with roots 2 +/- i (loses real-rootedness itself); the graph D-H keeps a fully REAL spectrum and exits the window. (2) POPUC circle interlacing verified (zeros on-circle to 2e-15; two beta-families strictly alternate). (3) The averaging step: exact +/-1 edge-signing average = matching polynomial (survives, real-rooted); INDEPENDENT-phase diagonal average collapses exactly to z^n; HERMITIAN phase-signing survives (= matching polynomial again); Haar CUE first moment trivially z^N while E[|det(I-U)|^2] = N+1 (Keating-Snaith): on the circle, content survives independent averaging only through conjugate pairing. (4) Z-side landing: the same dictionary over Z is "Xi(t) real-rooted" = the Laguerre-Polya/de Bruijn-Newman face, already mapped (#38/#39). ADVERSARY ROUND (load-bearing, [`../scratchpad/circle_interlacing/01_adversary.md`] untracked): TWO overclaims corrected in place. (a) The failure-mode dichotomy is a POSSIBLE-MODES statement, not a universal: arithmetic admits BOTH modes (witness (T^2-5T+5)(T^2+5T+5) = T^4-15T^2+25 over q = 5, FE-exact, all-real off-circle roots, h = x^2-25 real-rooted but OUT of window), while the graph world admits ONLY exit-the-window (self-adjointness makes real-rootedness free). Henri Lebesgue 2025) shows unitary Brownian motion at finite time has a RICH circle-controlled expected characteristic polynomial (unitary Hermite polynomials, Lee-Yang-connected), and the adversary's own probe found designed conjugate-PAIRED phase subtori whose holomorphic average is nontrivial, provably self-inversive, circle-rooted for m = 2 pairs (proven) and numerically exactly circle-rooted for m = 3, 4 (4e-15 under 9000 Haar samples + direct maximization; mechanism OPEN, a candidate small theorem via Schur-Cohn transport). ### 142. THE ARCHIMEDEAN PLACE IS THE PASSAGE FROM A FINITE ATOMIC SPECTRUM (FLAT, RH-PROVABLE) TO THE CONTINUOUS SPECTRUM OF THE UNIVERSAL COVER (NEVER-FLAT, RH-HARD): a computable model localizing all of M4 at the one archimedean place, unifying #141, #79/#80, and c4, 2026-07-01. Prompted by Owen (the third poke-next pick: the archimedean place as the single missing factor). The function-field world has NO archimedean place, a finite atomic Frobenius spectrum, and RH as a theorem; over Q there is exactly one extra place (the archimedean, carried by the Gamma-factor in xi(s) = pi^{-s/2} Gamma(s/2) zeta(s)), and the whole difficulty localizes there. [`toy/archimedean_place.py`](toy/archimedean_place.py) models what it adds via the flat-extension mechanism (#79/#80: a measure is finitely atomic iff its moment Hankel goes FLAT, a machine-zero eigenvalue at a fixed order, after which Curto-Fialkow pins it, which is why the function-field RH is decidable; a continuous measure never flattens). The NEW content is identifying the archimedean continuous spectrum with the UNIVERSAL COVER: by #141 the (q+1)-regular tree, the universal cover of every finite regular graph, has a CONTINUOUS Kesten-McKay spectrum on [-2 sqrt(q), 2 sqrt(q)]. Verified in three parts: (1) finite graphs are atomic and go flat at their atom count (K_6 at order 1, Petersen at order 2); (2) the Kesten-McKay measure is continuous, its Hankel never flat (min eigenvalue stays positive across orders, m_2 = d exactly); (3) growing finite graphs' normalized moments converge to the Kesten-McKay moments (the distance ||m_finite - m_KM|| falls 0.078, 0.018, 0.0036, 0.0018 for n = 30, 100, 300, 1000), so the atomic measures approach the continuous limit. NET: adding the one archimedean place equals passing to the continuous spectrum of the universal cover equals the flat (RH-provable) problem becoming never-flat (RH-hard). This LOCALIZES all of M4 at the single archimedean place and unifies #141 (the tree as the marginal/extremal continuous limit), #79/#80 (flat vs never-flat), and c4 (the archimedean Gamma-factor is the continuous mean the periodic orbits cannot source). Honest caveat: a structural model of the obstruction SHAPE on the self-adjoint spectrum (#139/#140), computably faithful to the flat-vs-nonflat mechanism, not zeta's arithmetic archimedean content (its archimedean place is the Gamma-factor, not literally a Kesten-McKay measure). 12/12 toy tests. Closes the three-probe arc (#140 the R1 sourcing gap, #141 marginal positivity as extremality, #142 the archimedean place), all reading one story: the graph proven world's entire power is the single self-adjoint operator zeta lacks, and its absence is felt as the R1 sourcing gap, the marginal-positivity extremality, and the archimedean continuous-spectrum obstruction, three faces of the same M4.

### 141. MARGINAL POSITIVITY IS A THEOREM IN THE GRAPH WORLD, AND IT IS EXTREMALITY: Alon-Boppana forces every regular family's nontrivial spectral radius to saturate the Ramanujan bound 2 sqrt(q), which is the universal-cover tree's spectral radius, so the RH-analogue holds exactly at the optimal value with no buffer, 2026-07-01. Prompted by Owen (the second poke-next pick: Alon-Boppana as marginal positivity). The project's central empirical finding (marginal positivity: RH is true only at the margin, no buffer for soft proofs) and the session-019 reading (marginal = extremality, #76) are findings over Z, not theorems. The graph world (Ihara, #139) proves both. [`toy/alon_boppana.py`](toy/alon_boppana.py): graph-RH is |lambda_nontrivial| <= 2 sqrt(q), bracketed by Alon-Boppana (any growing (q+1)-regular family has liminf nontrivial radius >= 2 sqrt(q) - o(1): you cannot asymptotically beat Ramanujan) and Friedman (a random regular graph meets it up to o(1)), so every family's radius CONVERGES to 2 sqrt(q): the bound is universal and saturated, beatable by none, and a Ramanujan graph is EXTREMAL. Verified: cycles give the deterministic version (the margin 2 - lambda_2(C_n) falls 0.59, 0.15, 0.038, ..., 0.0006, no fixed buffer); random d-regular radii concentrate at 2 sqrt(d-1) (within about 0.05 by n = 600) while the native Davenport-Heilbronn graphs (two_clique_bridge, cycle_power) sit strictly above; the mechanism is the UNIVERSAL COVER, since 2 sqrt(q) is the spectral radius of the (q+1)-regular infinite tree (the Kesten-McKay edge, empirical 2.819 vs 2.828 for d=3), which covers every finite regular graph, so a finite graph cannot beat its own universal cover = no buffer. NET: the graph world PROVES marginal positivity as extremality (the RH-analogue holds exactly at the tree's optimal spectral radius, and a soft/buffered proof is impossible even in the toy). Honest caveat: the proof runs on the self-adjoint adjacency spectrum (the tree's REAL spectral radius), the same ingredient zeta lacks (#139/#140), so it validates the FRAME (extremality is the correct reading of marginal positivity) without transferring the PROOF; the self-adjointness gap is unchanged. 11/11 toy tests. Connects the marginal-positivity thesis (proven here as a phenomenon), session-019 (#76, the extremality reading validated in a checkable world), #139 (the Ihara proven world), and #140 (the self-adjointness gap that blocks transfer). A coordinate confirming the search is aimed at the right (extremal/marginal) target.

### 140. THE R1 SOURCING GAP IS SELF-ADJOINTNESS-GATED, NOT MERELY VARIETY-GATED: the graph world has a NON-variety source of the sqrt(q) purity (MSS interlacing families), and it transfers to Spec(Z) iff a self-adjoint operator behind Frobenius exists = Hilbert-Polya = M4, 2026-07-01. Prompted by Owen (the "where would you like to poke next" pick, the MSS transfer probe). The Ihara world (#139) exposed a tension: graph-RH is the same sqrt(q) as the Weil bound, and in arithmetic the purity |alpha| = sqrt(q) is variety-gated (R1, #130: Deligne via a modular curve, Maass open), but in the graph world Marcus-Spielman-Srivastava (Interlacing Families I, Annals 2015) source it with NO variety (bipartite Ramanujan graphs of every degree via expected characteristic polynomials). So the graph world CROSSES R1 combinatorially. [`toy/interlacing.py`](toy/interlacing.py) exhibits the engine and pins why it does not transfer. Part 1 (verified on K_{3,3} and the cube Q_3): Godsil-Gutman (the average over edge-signings of det(xI minus A_s) equals the matching polynomial, error 1e-15), Heilmann-Lieb (the matching polynomial is REAL-ROOTED with all roots inside the Ramanujan window [-2 sqrt(d-1), 2 sqrt(d-1)]), and a good signing exists (best top eigenvalue 2.00 for K_{3,3}, 1.73 for Q_3, both under the bound): a Ramanujan 2-lift sourced with no variety. Part 2 (the fault line): the engine's fuel is REAL-ROOTEDNESS (Heilmann-Lieb, and the interlacing family needs real-rooted polynomials), which holds because the signed adjacency A_s is SYMMETRIC (self-adjoint); the arithmetic L-polynomial (the characteristic polynomial of Frobenius on H^1) is NOT real-rooted, its roots being the Frobenius eigenvalues on the circle |alpha| = sqrt(q), genuinely complex (measured max|Im root| about 0.83 to 0.98 across the curve battery). NET: the non-variety sqrt(q) source is PAID FOR with self-adjointness, exactly the ingredient the Ihara grader (#139) showed is free in the graph world and absent over Z. R1 (the sourcing gap) and M4 (the polarization gap) are two faces of the ONE missing self-adjoint operator, which is Hilbert-Polya; #130 sharpens from variety-gated to SELF-ADJOINTNESS-gated. A coordinate, not a route: the combinatorial method that beats R1 for graphs needs precisely the ingredient zeta lacks. MSS is added to the transfer-search corpus ([`lemma_db/transfer_search.py`](lemma_db/transfer_search.py), role killed, an R1 source not an M4 transfer, sibling to the Lorentzian / real-stable kill; 7/7 tests). 10/10 toy tests green. Connects #139 (the Ihara grader's self-adjointness-is-free lesson), #130 ([sourcing_gap_r1](../docs/03_research/sourcing_gap_r1.md), R1 variety-gated), #128 Front 3 (zeta's zeros are resonances not eigenvalues, so no self-adjoint operator), #97 (the Lorentzian / real-stable kill this is a sibling to), and Hilbert-Polya (the missing operator that would make Frobenius self-adjoint and let the interlacing engine run).

### 139. TWO PROVEN-WORLD PROBES FROM THE CHAOS THREAD: the Ihara graph world is a SECOND checkable M4 grader carrying a NATIVE Davenport-Heilbronn, and a thermodynamic-formalism probe deepens 2R by pinning the dynamical route's obstruction to the archimedean MEAN, 2026-06-30. Prompted by Owen (a chaos-theory deep dive, then "a+b"). Part A (a second, spectral, proven world): [`toy/ihara.py`](toy/ihara.py) plus [`toy/ihara_grader.py`](toy/ihara_grader.py) upgrade the abstract random-matrix Selberg toy (selberg.py) into a genuine finite dynamical zeta. For a (q+1)-regular graph the Ihara zeta is a product over primitive cycles (literal periodic orbits, cycle length the log p analogue), and the Ihara-Bass determinant gives the theorem graph-RH iff Ramanujan iff |lambda_nontrivial| <= 2 sqrt(q) iff all nontrivial poles on |u| = 1/sqrt(q) (Terras 2011); the substitution u = q^{-s} maps |u| = 1/sqrt(q) to Re(s) = 1/2, and the bound is the same sqrt(q) as the function-field Weil bound. Verified on six graphs (Petersen, K_6, C_9 Ramanujan and on-line; two_clique_bridge d=5,7 and cycle_power C_30^3 non-Ramanujan and off-line, max offline defect 0.31 to 0.51). The grader mirrors the function-field grader's four axes with the D-H axis NATIVE, not imported: a non-Ramanujan regular graph has the SAME Ihara functional equation with off-line poles, a genuine graph-world Davenport-Heilbronn. The reference [-1,1] moment form (Hamburger Hankel H0 plus the localizing Hankel H1 on 1 minus nu^2, nu = lambda/(2 sqrt(q))) scores ALL GREEN by the Hausdorff moment theorem; the instructive failure is the Hamburger-only candidate, which REPRODUCES the Ramanujan graphs but FAILS to reject the native D-H, because H0 is PSD for every symmetric A. That is the exact lesson for zeta stated spectrally: self-adjointness (the operator, the real ordinates) is FREE, and the spectral GAP (the 2 sqrt(q) Weil bound, the localizing block H1) is the whole content, which is the polarization = M4. So the graph world supplies a second checkable M4 move (spectral) alongside the geometric function-field one and localizes the identical gap; 9/9 toy smoke tests green. Part B (the dynamical route pinned to the archimedean place): [`chaos/c4_prime_orbit_spectrum.py`](chaos/c4_prime_orbit_spectrum.py) points thermodynamic formalism at 2R's question (can {log p} be a genuine chaotic-flow length spectrum?). (1) The prime orbit count pi(e^L) has topological entropy h approaching 1 (climbs 0.70 to 0.83 over x = 1e2 to 1e7, the counting law of an entropy-1 flow: seductive, not a kill). (2) The Ruelle zeta with orbit lengths {log p} is the Euler product, the passive half. (3) THE OBSTRUCTION, made computable: the smooth zero density N(T) = theta(T)/pi + 1 (Riemann-Siegel theta, the archimedean Gamma-factor) matches Riemann-von Mangoldt to O(1/T) (difference 6.6e-5 to 6.6e-8 at T = 1e2 to 1e5), is 100% archimedean (zero prime input), and NO periodic orbit sources it; the flow is the fluctuation S(T), the MEAN is the archimedean place. (4) The thermodynamic lens SEPARATES zeta from D-H where GUE cannot: prime-power orbit repetitions localize the log-derivative on prime powers (ratio 1.0000) while Lambda_DH delocalizes (0.5002), a step past Level 3. NET: both probes are honest coordinates, not escapes. A reconfirms self-adjointness-is-free and gap-is-M4 in a genuine second proven world with a native firewall; B deepens 2R by pinning the dynamical route's obstruction to the archimedean mean, the lossless / polarization half. Neither touches M4. Connects 2R ([e2r](arithmetic_geometric/e2r_dynamical_zeta.md)), #128 Front 3 (zeta's zeros are resonances not eigenvalues, exactly why the Selberg self-adjointness does not transfer), the acoustic passive-lossless split (#90-94), the #34 archimedean stealth window, and the all-roads convergence. New surface: [`docs/03_research/quantum_chaos_and_the_zeros.md`](../docs/03_research/quantum_chaos_and_the_zeros.md) (the Gutzwiller / explicit-formula dictionary), the [`chaos/`](chaos/) toolbox (C1-C4), and the Lorenz visualization scene 14.

### 136. THE BOMBIERI VARIATIONAL SOS DOES NOT ESCAPE THE 4E.3 FEJER WALL: the one escape route outside the LP/SDP family also saturates, razor-sharply at the cone boundary, 2026-06-30. Direction 6 (the variational SOS, `research_directions/06_bombieri_variational_sos.md`) was the one named escape from the 4E.3 line-restriction lemma NOT in the LP/SDP family (e4e6-e4e8 all saturate Fejer), and was "research-grade, beyond project scope, no code." First computation [`zero_free/e4f_variational_sos.py`](zero_free/e4f_variational_sos.py) (+ .md): the Bombieri relaxation allows the bivariate test polynomial P slightly NEGATIVE, penalized by the L2 norm of its negative part ||P_-||^2, so its feasible set is strictly larger than the non-negative cone (hence outside LP/SDP). Decisive test (Direction 6 sec 5.5): for each target line-restriction ratio r, min ||P_-||^2 s.t. c_1(Q)=r, c_0(Q)=1, Q(theta)=P(theta,2theta), over bidegree (2,2) (effective degree 6, Fejer ceiling 2cos(pi/8)=1.84776), multi-start SLSQP. RESULT: ||P_-||^2 = EXACTLY 0 for every r <= Fejer (a genuinely non-negative P realizes each) and turns positive PRECISELY as r crosses 1.8478 (2.7e-6 at 1.86, 3.2e-5 at 1.88, monotone to 6.7e-4 at 1.96). So Fejer IS the non-negative-cone boundary and super-Fejer c_1/c_0 REQUIRES genuine L2-negativity: the variational relaxation gives NO FREE LUNCH, the 4E.3 line-restriction lemma EXTENDS to the Bombieri variational setting, and the wall survives the one relaxation built to escape it (so it is not an artifact of the cone relaxation). Marginal-positivity thesis on the Architecture-4 side: the figure of merit is pinned at the cone boundary with no soft slack. Milestone 5.6 (translate the small negativity to a zero-free-region constant) predicted negative by sec 6, not run; 5.5 is the decisive test. STRENGTHENS publication P5 (the zero-free-ceiling negative closure, PUBLICATIONS.md): the escape-route closure now covers the non-LP/SDP variational route too. New: `zero_free/e4f_variational_sos.{py,md}`; Direction 6 sec 10 marked milestone-5.5 EXECUTED. Connects #8/#9/#12/#15 (the 4E.3 robustness pattern, now extended), the marginal-positivity thesis.

The honest move was to execute the ONE named, previously-unexecuted probe the four-mechanism construction sweep (`building_the_missing_positivity.md`) left open as "the only step not already foreclosed": promote the Rankin loglog-coefficient c_F (=1 for a primitive Euler product, <1 else; the sharp non-circular multiplicativity discriminator from #53/e3w) from a scalar into the NORMALIZATION of the archimedean block of the non-circular Weil/Rosati form M = A_arch + P_fin + B_pole (e2w/M2.6), to test whether making multiplicativity act ON the continuation (where the off-line zeros enter) injects Euler structure into the GLOBAL signature and breaks the M2.6 stealth window. BUILT + RAN [`arithmetic_geometric/e2w2_loglog_arch_coupling.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2w2_loglog_arch_coupling.py) (+ .md). Baseline reproduces M2.6 exactly (zeta +0.035, Eps47-principal +0.126, D-H +0.094, all POS = no separation, D-H spuriously positive = stealth window). The probe M_c = c_F*A_arch + P + B sends ALL THREE NEG (zeta -4.57, Eps -0.70, D-H -0.51), INCLUDING RH-true Euler zeta whose c_F=1.105>1 scales A_arch UP. So it is not even a (mis-)classifier; it annihilates the signal. MECHANISM, deeper than the predicted #20 non-Euler trap, made quantitative by the block-magnitude diagnostic: the unscaled RH-positivity is a RAZOR-THIN exact cancellation of LARGE blocks (zeta ||A_arch||=44.3 vs ||P+B||=44.4, cancelling to min eig +0.035), so rescaling A_arch by c_F perturbs the form by ~0.1*44~4.6, dwarfing the +0.035 margin and flipping the sign for EVERYONE regardless of RH. Multiplicativity CANNOT "normalize the archimedean coupling" to inject Euler structure into the signature because the signature has ZERO SLACK: it IS the exact A-vs-(P+B) cancellation. The c_F idea fails not via the non-Euler trap (c_F detects Euler-ness not RH) but via the marginal-positivity razor (there is no margin to rescale into) -- a strictly sharper closure. NET: closes the one marginally-live thread the construction sweep left open; P5 unreached; the missing math IS the polarization (not a route to it), and the marginal-positivity thesis now has its most concrete face -- the non-circular Rosati positivity of zeta is a +0.035 cancellation of two norm-44 blocks. Also: a genuinely separable arch-to-finite off-diagonal does NOT exist in the single-basis summed form (there is no coupling to renormalize independently), itself part of the finding. New: `arithmetic_geometric/e2w2_loglog_arch_coupling.{py,md}`. Connects #53 (the c_F scalar + the #20 non-Euler trap), #33 (the M2.6 form + the +0.035 marginal positivity), the marginal-positivity thesis, building_the_missing_positivity.md (the sweep that named this probe).

### 134. GRADIENT-DESCENT FRAMING: RH is already several gradient-descent problems (all MIRROR by marginal positivity + the stealth window); the META-LEVEL policy-gradient against the Lean floor is the only framing whose gradient is off the analytic data, BUILT and Lean-validated on the function-field rehearsal, 2026-06-30. Prompted by Owen ("turn this into a gradient descent problem"). A 12-agent design study surveyed the six existing variational threads and found: RH already IS several gradient-descent problems and they are all built in the repo (Nyman-Beurling/Baez-Duarte = least-squares, the normal equations Gc=b; de Bruijn-Newman = a heat/log-gas flow; the Weil-Gram lambda_min = an SDP; the Frobenius moment matrices = a PSD-cone-distance). Descent on every one converges and none cracks RH, for two reasons that together ARE the marginal-positivity thesis in optimization language: (1) the optimum sits on the ZERO-MARGIN feasibility boundary (d_N~1/logN, lambda_min(M^zeta)->0+, G_m positive-SEMI-definite singular at the RH point), so the gradient vanishes at the answer with no certificate of side; (2) the data feeding the gradient (L on the critical line) is D-H-BLIND via the archimedean stealth window (off-line zero suppressed ~exp(-(pi/4)gamma)~6e-30), so descent on zeta = descent on D-H. Five new framings were scored (best->worst by discrimination then buildability): D4 policy-gradient vs the Lean floor (MAYBE), D1 continuous shadow-battery (LIKELY-MIRROR, but the only one that manufactures a real construction-space gradient; its D-H penalty has no theta-intrinsic source and reduces to the euler_gated fiat), D3 adversarial minimax with D-H as the max player (LIKELY-MIRROR; the max player is only as sharp as the certifier it wraps), D5 test-function discriminator (LIKELY-MIRROR; a sharp quantitative no-go: defeating the stealth window needs prime support to e^85.7), D2 PSD-cone-distance on the moment object (LIKELY-MIRROR; over Z it has no variable domain at all). The fork = WHERE THE GRADIENT LIVES (object-level / construction-space / meta-level); Owen chose meta-level. BUILT it ([`gradient_descent/d4_lean_policy_gradient.py`](gradient_descent/d4_lean_policy_gradient.py) + d4b): a REINFORCE policy proposes a Frobenius (trace t, prime degree p), and the hard reward is whether [`functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix`](../lean/ZetaRH/FunctionFieldRH.lean) (sorry-free) typechecks at the companion matrix of X^2-tX+p. KEY equivalence: that instantiation typechecks IFF isprime(p) and t^2<=4p (the only two numeric obligations are Nat.Prime p and the Hasse bound; everything else is fixed lemmas), so `hasse_valid(t,p)` is a FAITHFUL NON-CIRCULAR Python proxy of the Lean typecheck. Two-tier reward (one `lake env lean` is ~111s cold / 21s warm, too slow per-episode): proxy in the loop, batched real-Lean validation at the end. THREE FINDINGS: F1 the loop works (hit-rate 0.66->0.99, greedy 100%/10 primes, learns |t|<=floor(2 sqrt p)); the gradient is grad_theta log pi, off the analytic data, so it climbs where every object-level form was flat = the structural escape from marginal positivity, paid for in reward SPARSITY not gradient flatness. F2 D-H BY EXCLUSION (identical loop, D-H target = flat 0: no Euler product => no Frobenius matrix => the theorem cannot instantiate, euler_gated firewall fails; discrimination is structural = the easy half, never reads the off-line zero = MIRROR-grade). F3 THE KERNEL CLIFF (extend the dense reward to the Spec(Z) shadow battery: coverage climbs 0.2->1.0 on proven shadows while R_hard on the open arithmetic polarization stays identically 0, because that Lean theorem is unbuilt -- ArithmeticPolarization.lean carries the FE pairing but NOT positivity; the FLT-shaped cliff, now measured: R_hard fires only on the closed function-field analogue). ESCALATION (d4b, "push harder"): swapped the tabular policy for a small NEURAL autoregressive policy (1-hidden-layer MLP H=128, REINFORCE+Adam+annealed-entropy+adaptive-curriculum) emitting a FULL integer matrix A=!![a,b;c,d] one token at a time, validated against the GENERAL theorem (no companion shortcut; the proof reduces det(m.1+n.A) via det_smul_one_add_smul + completed square). Proxy gains a condition: typechecks IFF isprime(p) AND det A=p AND tr^2<=4p. Result: 10/10 primes solved exactly as distinct non-companion Lean-valid matrices. SPARSE-TARGET finding: det=p is a sparse target precisely because p is prime (few ad-bc=p), while the neighbouring COMPOSITE determinant has many matrix representations, so a uniform-prime policy is pulled into the denser composite basin (pre-curriculum p=29 locks onto det=28, off by one); an ADAPTIVE CURRICULUM (sample prime ~ 1-success) closes it to 10/10 (p=29 -> [3,-5;4,3], det=29, tr=6). Difficulty tracks ARITHMETIC SPARSITY = genuine matrix search, not a one-parameter family. Lean tier-two: 10 valid witnesses typecheck clean, negatives (off-Hasse t=5,p=5; non-prime p=9) fail at exactly hHasse/hp, empirically confirming the proxy equivalence. HONEST NET: the meta-level is the only framing whose gradient is off the analytic data and grounded in non-circular truth (the Lean floor); it CRACKS the closed function-field case (machine-validated, both companion and full-matrix) and MEASURES where the open Spec(Z) kernel begins (the cliff); it does NOT touch P5, by construction (no checkpoint and no Lean theorem reaches that locus). This is the marginal-positivity thesis re-derived as "the object-level gradient is structurally flat AND blind; the only place to put a gradient is on the SEARCH, where it climbs the closed analogues and stops at the unbuilt polarization." New: `gradient_descent/{d4_lean_policy_gradient,d4b_neural_matrix_policy}.{py,md}` (+ `__init__.py`). Connects #133 (the NB-BD/dBN object-level mirror verdicts this generalizes), the marginal-positivity thesis, optimizing_rh_for_ai.md (lever A shadow battery = the dense reward, lever B Lean floor = the hard reward), lever-B (the Lean target).

### 133. FIRST-PRINCIPLES AUDIT: the M4 convergence is ~80% law / ~20% one-axiom artifact; BOTH battery-verdicts-graded-by-definition (Nyman-Beurling AND de Bruijn-Newman criticality) computed = MIRROR; Deligne-Weil-II earns the "too shallow" bracket, 2026-06-28. A 27-agent audit (web research + repo audit + the kill-battery as honesty gate, plus a frame-audit-of-the-frame-audits) tested 8 candidate "needle-mover" engines deliberately chosen as the COMPLEMENT of the lateral-imports scan and session-019 (so no re-derivation): Deligne-Weil-II monodromy, de Bruijn-Newman dynamics, Nyman-Beurling-Baez-Duarte, condensed/analytic geometry (Clausen-Scholze), Fargues-Fontaine/Fargues-Scholze, automorphic-family functoriality, extremality/variational, Weil-etale (Lichtenbaum/Flach-Morin). RESULT: 7/8 reduce to M4, the 8th (dBN dynamics) already killed (#38/#39); NO genuinely-new coordinate, NO obstruction structurally distinct from M4, NO imported tool the M4 road lacks. The convergence is REAL (this is its ~13th independent confirmation, now from the monodromy direction). FRAME-AUDIT (the meta-yield): the convergence is ~80% genuine law / ~20% artifact, and the artifact is exactly ONE axiom. Five battery axioms (D-H, R3.5/K1, marginal-positivity, variety-gated, bridge-vs-mirror) are falsification-grounded laws that kill BY COMPUTATION and do not presuppose a polarization. The sixth, "Level 4 = positivity = signature," is a CHOICE stated as a law: it generalizes from two reformulations (Weil, Li) and then enforces convergence by re-describing every Level-4 non-polarization as "the signature wearing different clothes." dBN criticality (Lambda=0, RH-equivalent, genuinely Level-4 and NOT compatible with a beta=0.51 world) is the live witness of a non-polarization Level-4 region that the program subsumed by fiat. RETRACTED that re-description in `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/02_graduate/log_correlated_fields_intro.md` sec 6, and `docs/03_research/research_directions/12_debruijn_newman_criticality.md` (softened to "every Level-4 reformulation analyzed SO FAR reduces to a positivity; the two routes not yet D-H-tested, Nyman-Beurling density and dBN criticality, are the open frontier"). THE ONE PROBE (the only thing the audit said to actually run): the D-H discrimination test on Nyman-Beurling-Baez-Duarte, the lone battery verdict graded by definition rather than experiment. RAN it ([`criticality/e_nb_baez_duarte_dh.py`](criticality/e_nb_baez_duarte_dh.py) + `.md`): genuine Baez-Duarte object, validated three ways (Mobius-SIGN recovery 13/13 squarefree k<=20; d_N(zeta) decreasing 0.183->0.100 over N=5..40; d_40^2*log40=0.037 vs the Burnol/BCF constant 2+gamma-log4pi=0.046). VERDICT: MIRROR, and one step STRONGER than the audit predicted. The audit predicted "discriminates, but by reading the off-line zeros (K1-circular)"; the data shows NB-BD does NOT read the D-H off-line zero at all (it sits at Re=0.808, OFF the line, archimedean-suppressed: including its height 85.7 in the line integral moves d_N by +0.0078, LESS than zeta's pure-truncation-noise baseline +0.0106, and zeta has no off-line zero) and does NOT detect the missing Euler product (with the correct ENTIRE normalization D-H decreases -29% just like the Euler RH-true controls zeta -46% and chi3 -34%; the naive "D-H floors" discrimination was a zeta-pole normalization artifact, since chi3 = Euler+RH-true floors too). Failure mode = the archimedean STEALTH WINDOW (exp(-(pi/4)d*gamma), same mechanism as `e_dbn_kernel` #38), not circular zero-reading. So NB-BD joins Lee-Yang (#95) and the de Bruijn kernel (#38) as a THIRD soft/critical-line object that is D-H-blind by the stealth window. Scope caveat (honest, in the .md): Form B is ONE principled line-driven generalization of the (0,1)-kernel; D-H is not in the Selberg class so there is no canonical (0,1)-kernel NB-BD for it, but the line-driven form is the relevant one for the D-H discipline. SECOND PROBE (closes the LAST untested route, sub-target 12.4', [`criticality/e_dbn_flow_dh.py`](criticality/e_dbn_flow_dh.py) + `.md`): the de Bruijn-Newman CRITICALITY FLOW test was also RUN = MIRROR. The faithful flow puts $e^{tu^2}$ on the Fourier variable (re-weighting the n-th prime term by $e^{t\log^2 n}$); applied to the repo's validated Bombieri prime block it reproduces e3f's W(f_0)=0.09509938457843 BIT-FOR-BIT at t=0 (the mandatory validation gate), and the re-weighted PRIME block sums over n<b^2 and never reaches the off-line zero at gamma=85.699 (archimedean tail, exp(-(pi/4)d*gamma)~5.9e-30), so it separates D-H from zeta no better than the t=0 block (both via low-n coefficient SIGNS b_n^DH, not the off-line zero); the dual zero-side amplification $e^{t\gamma^2}$ DIVERGES (totals 0.3 -> 1.4e14 by t=0.005, no t->0+ limit, no convergent functional). So BOTH non-polarization Level-4 routes (NB density + dBN criticality) are now graded BY EXPERIMENT, both D-H-blind by the SAME archimedean stealth window; the convergence is honest all the way down, and Direction 12's RH content is the flow LOCATING Lambda (Level-3-proven for Lambda>=0, Level-4-open for Lambda<=0=RH), not any prime-block positivity object. DELIGNE-WEIL-II earns a new scorecard bracket (`spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md` sec 3): Rankin-Selberg / Weil-II positivity = "TOO SHALLOW" -- proven, non-circular (K1-clean: from the pole of L(s, pi x pi-tilde) at s=1 and non-negative convolution coefficients, NOT from the zeros), Euler-essential (correctly does NOT fire for D-H), but lives at the Re=1 edge and provably saturates the Vinogradov-Korobov 2/3 ceiling -- joining Faltings-Hriljac "too local", AHK "too blind", de Branges "too strong"; the missing object is now pinned from FOUR sides. Walked to its wall the monodromy engine SPLITS cleanly into R1 (variety-gated purity, re-derived from weights/monodromy) + Architecture 4 (the 2/3 analytic ceiling), both already mapped; "big monodromy for the zeta family" is vacuous (zeta is a single object, not a positive-dimensional geometric family). NET: a clean negative that HARDENS the M4 wall (a deliberately-complementary 8-engine sweep found no escape), plus two real deliverables (the NB-BD mirror is now a COMPUTED theorem with mu-recovery validation, not an assertion; the 4th scorecard bracket) and one honesty correction (the Level-4=positivity retraction, which removes a tautology while keeping every theorem intact). Honest scope: the completeness-critic agent (1 of 27) failed its structured output, so the 8 candidates are the lateral-scan complement but not a provably exhaustive sweep. New: `criticality/e_nb_baez_duarte_dh.{py,md}`, `criticality/e_dbn_flow_dh.{py,md}`; edits to CLAUDE.md, log_correlated_fields_intro.md sec 6, 12_debruijn_newman_criticality.md (12.4' RESOLVED), spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md sec 3.

### 131. THE ARAKELOV FACE IS THE MIRROR OF THE AHK FAILURE, NOT AN ESCAPE, 2026-06-27: executing #129's recorded pivot (a 5-dimension, 11-agent adversarially-verified probe), the Arakelov face does NOT escape the universal gap; it RELOCATES it to the opposite end of the same two-facet gap and ships one new computed artifact (e2ae). Dossier: [`arithmetic_geometric/2L_arakelov_face_probe.md`](arithmetic_geometric/2L_arakelov_face_probe.md). THE MIRROR (the load-bearing localization): AHK and the Arakelov face fail at OPPOSITE ends. AHK is variety-free, so it attacks Spec(Z) directly (HAS the base) but cannot source a weight-1 sqrt(q) carrier (matroid Chow rings are purely Tate) -- dies at facet A (R1). The Arakelov face HAS a genuine carrier and a genuine PROVEN polarization: the single-surface arithmetic Hodge index (Faltings-Hriljac/Yuan-Zhang) IS the per-motive analogue of M4, a theorem (2H, ranks 1-3). So it dies neither at A (R1, Deligne purity holds per motive) NOR at B (M4-positivity is a theorem per surface) but at the BASE: the nonexistent product Spec(Z) x Spec(Z) + Frobenius correspondence Gamma_S with place-dependent (1,p) bidegree that would globalize per-motive theorems onto zeta's actual zeros (= the project's PROP-global conjunct; the variety Arakelov has is relative dim 1, NOT the self-product -- the dimensional reconciliation of "Arakelov has a variety" with "FLT-adjacent inherited gap", #130). SHARPENING of #130's clean two-facet (A=R1, B=M4) split: M4 is NOT what the Arakelov face is missing (the single-surface M4-analogue is a theorem there); the missing thing is the base-for-zeta. THE BSD SEVERANCE (why FH positivity stays off zeta's zeros, faithful to the e2ad correction, both withdrawn moves respected): the only proven height-to-L channel is Gross-Zagier/BSD, equating a Neron-Tate height to the CENTRAL derivative L'(E,1) = order-of-vanishing at the single point s=1, severed from where the OTHER zeros sit; a function can have positive central derivative and off-line zeros elsewhere. Disqualifier #113 (central L-value/order-of-vanishing regime, not signature-across-all-heights) retires the whole Gross-Zagier/Beilinson-Bloch family. The severance is in the L-LINK, not the signature object (the Beilinson-Bloch/Yuan-Zhang height pairing IS a genuine global signature, proven generically by Gao; it just lacks the self-product Gamma_S and SHARP, not generic, positivity = M4). 2026 LITERATURE FRONTIER (WebSearch + arXiv, two facts author+date confirmed): NO generalized arithmetic Hodge index theorem touches zeta's (or any deg>=2 L's) zeros without the product surface; "the gap = the product surface" (2K sec 4) is unmoved. Each surveyed object is on the wrong side: Yuan-Zhang adelic line bundles (Math.Ann.367,2017) + Moriwaki higher-dim AHI = fixed-scheme NODE-fh-too-local; Bost theta-invariants/pro-Hermitian Arakelov (Prog.Math.334,2020) = a Diophantine h^0_theta scalar, WRONG signature class (not indefinite (1,n-1)); Cantat-Gao-Habegger-Xie (Duke 2021) USES, does not extend, the single-variety index; Morishita 2025 + the new Connes-Consani Jacobian (arXiv:2602.15941, 2026, Picard monoid + Abel-Jacobi + explicit-formula-as-Lefschetz-trace) reach the trace/realization, never a pairing/polarization. D-H DISCIPLINE: the Arakelov face is K2-clean by STRUCTURE but the survival is HOLLOW in the marginal-positivity sense -- all zeta-vs-D-H discriminating power is delegated to the unbuilt Gamma_S; every built Arakelov positivity (FH, omega-bar^2=12 h_Fal, lambda_inf) is D-H-blind precisely because it is zeta-blind (certifies a fixed curve's regulator, connects to L only via BSD). D-H is the L-function of NO motive, so there is no D-H Faltings-Hriljac analogue to even ask positivity for: survival by NON-MIMICRY, failing at the motive not the positivity (a face whose only discriminator is unbuilt has not yet entered the discipline's domain). RESULTS: 12 h_Fal = -0.931/+1.480/+4.291; archimedean (Petersson) term carries 81/72/64% of the term magnitude and dominates the finite log|Delta_min| term (parallel to 2H "the regulator is archimedean"). ### 130. R1 (sourcing a weight-1 sqrt(q) carrier without a variety) IS VARIETY-GATED, 2026-06-27: the residual the AHK arc reduced to (#129) is the SOURCING facet of the universal gap, and a SURVEYOR verification (Sarnak GRC notes, Fontaine-Mazur, Lafforgue, Deligne, Drinfeld-module RH, Scholze torsion) confirms there is no known non-geometric source for it. Doc: [`../docs/03_research/sourcing_gap_r1.md`](../docs/03_research/sourcing_gap_r1.md). THE QUESTION: can a non-Tate weight-1 object with all Frobenius eigenvalues |alpha|=sqrt(q) (purity) be sourced WITHOUT a variety? Split: (1) the SHAPE (a weight-1 object + some Frobenius) is doable variety-free (Galois reps, Maass forms, Bost-Connes); (2) provable PURITY |alpha|=sqrt(q) is, on all evidence, equivalent to having a motive/variety. Combinatorial routes fail at (1) (Tate, or tropical = Frobenius-free); automorphic/Galois routes have the shape but not provable purity. FOUR VERIFIED CLAIMS (all HOLD): (i) weight-1 purity is PROVEN only via varieties/stacks (even Lafforgue's function-field GRC routes through the moduli stack of Drinfeld shtukas + Deligne); (ii) Fontaine-Mazur (a geometric Galois rep is motivic) supports "pure weight-1 sqrt(q) => variety" but is a CONJECTURE and presupposes a Galois action a combinatorial object lacks; (iii) THE CLEANEST DRAMATIZATION (Sarnak, Clay 2005 pp.660-664): Ramanujan for HOLOMORPHIC forms is Deligne's theorem via the modular CURVE (a variety), but for MAASS forms it is OPEN because SL_n(C)/SU(n) is non-Hermitian, "no apparent algebro-geometric moduli interpretation" (best bound Kim-Sarnak 7/64, never theta=0) -- the instant you remove the variety, purity becomes open; (iv) no genuinely variety-free pure-sqrt(q) object is known (Drinfeld/Anderson t-motives are geometric + function-field only; Nori/Voevodsky motives are defined from varieties; Scholze torsion classes are extracted from Shimura-variety cohomology, purity open; Amini-Piquerez tropical objects are Frobenius-free, wrong signature). STRUCTURAL FRAMING: the universal gap has TWO variety-gated facets -- (A) sourcing/purity = R1 (over F_q = Deligne purity) and (B) polarization/signature = M4 (over F_q = Weil-Rosati positivity); distinct theorems in general, COINCIDENT in the genus-1 shadow (|alpha|=sqrt(q) <=> NegDef <=> t^2<4q, e2g). R1 is the sourcing facet, = lever-B's residual (#108). STATUS: a GAP not an OBSTRUCTION -- no impossibility theorem; R1 closes only by (a) supplying the geometric source (FLT-adjacent, the Arakelov face inherits it) or (b) a variety-free purity construction (a major theorem). This is why every construction route walls identically: realization (shape) is free, purity-without-a-variety (A) and the polarization (B) are not. Cross-refs: #129 (AHK->R1), #108 (lever-B residual), #30/spec_z_cohomology_landscape (the universal gap R1 refines), #97 (import-not-source). Decisive reference: Sarnak's GRC notes.

### 129. THE AHK ROUTE TO P3 IS A DEAD BRANCH (R2 closes into R1), 2026-06-27: a head-on attack on the 09A P3 gap (the t-carrying degree map) plus an adversarial-verification cycle closes the AHK/combinatorial route, with a precisely-CORRECTED reason that fixes an overstated framing in our own docs. **e2za** ([`arithmetic_geometric/e2za_ahk_p3_super_graft.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2za_ahk_p3_super_graft.py)): the idea was that the t-carrying form lives in H^1 (x) H^1 (Kunneth) and Kunneth of odd pieces is a SUPER-tensor, so P3 might only need an odd generator with P1 surviving. An ADVERSARY pass RETRACTED the headline: "P1 survives" tested only the SCALAR Lefschetz multiplicativity L(CxC)=L(C)^2 (true for any t,q, incl. nonsense), NOT the AHK ALGEBRA-level product structure the deletion-contraction induction needs; "deg carries t" is the Grothendieck-Lefschetz formula restated; the odd H^1 is HANDED IN, not sourced (e2yy unchanged). Survives: a correct DIAGNOSIS (the missing ingredient is an odd H^1) + the relabeling of the gap into R1 (can a combinatorial object SOURCE a non-Tate odd H^1 with modulus-sqrt(q) Frobenius?) and R2 (does AHK deletion-contraction RUN on a super-algebra?). **e2zb** ([`arithmetic_geometric/e2zb_super_ahk_recursion.py`](arithmetic_geometric/e2zb_super_ahk_recursion.py)): R2 settled at the ENGINE level -- AHK's only induction is matroid deletion-contraction (chi_M = chi_{M\e} - chi_{M/e}), whose input is t-BLIND (two decorations of the same matroid U(2,3), same q=25, t=2 vs t=100, have identical induction input but different RH-truth: A's moment form PSD, B's indefinite). So R2 reduces to R1. **VERIFIED (survey + 2 adversarial refutation probes):** the naive closure reason was REFUTED while the conclusion held. "combinatorial => Tate" is FALSE as a theorem (a GAP, not an obstruction) -- Amini-Piquerez prove a non-Tate combinatorial Kahler package with NO ambient variety (off-diagonal tropical h^{p,q}); Belkale-Brosnan + Mnev universality make matroid/Kirchhoff schemes arbitrarily non-Tate; Brown-Schnetz give a modular K3 in phi^4. The tropical Jacobian polarization is INTRINSIC to the metric graph (Mikhalkin-Zharkov), not imported. What ACTUALLY closes R2: no known object is at once (i) bare-combinatorial, (ii) carrying a modulus-sqrt(q) Frobenius on a non-Tate piece, (iii) AHK-machinable -- the split is on the FROBENIUS clause: the non-Tate combinatorial objects are FROBENIUS-FREE (over (R,max,+): no q/sqrt(q)/Galois); the sqrt(q)-carriers IMPORT the polarization from an ambient variety (#97). The tropical lead is DOUBLY dead (no t AND positive-definite signature, never flips off-line, e3r/#97). NET: the AHK route cannot source the polarization without a variety (09A Section 7's risk realized), and even granting t its polarity is the wrong (definite) signature for P6/M4; R1 (sourcing a non-Tate H^1) is the FLT-adjacent residual it shares with lever B. Recommendation (recorded, not yet executed): pivot to the ARAKELOV face (09A Section 5/7), which natively carries both a genuine arithmetic weight (Faltings-Hriljac) and the indefinite Hodge-index signature. P6/M4 UNTOUCHED. Cross-refs: #124/e2yy (the matroid is purely Tate), #123/e2xx (the moment object), #97 (import-not-source; the breadth-engine convergence), #48/e3r (the polarity objection), 09A Section 6D/7. Docs: 09A Section 6D.

### 128. THE M4 CONSTRUCTION ATTEMPT (a genuine, full-effort attempt at RH itself, 2026-06-26): three independent BUILDER fronts -- Weil-form analytic domination, moment-matrix Gram factorization, the Hilbert-Polya / circle-Frobenius operator -- ALL WALL, with NO candidate proof, and ALL on the SAME universal gap: the local-to-global continuation across Re(s)=1/2 (the Euler product converges only Re(s)>1, the zeros live in Re(s)<1; crossing the line = the missing Weil cohomology = M4). A 3-front convergence is the strongest evidence yet that the gap is ONE object. The D-H firewall held on all three. M4 UNTOUCHED -- but the attempt produced three genuine, verified RESIDUES and one correction. (Reports reconstructed at docs/03_research/m4_construction_attempt.md; the original scratchpad/m4_attempt/0{1,2,3}_*.md were not committed.) FRONT 1 (Weil-form domination, 01_weil_domination.md): the genuinely Euler-specific lever is the per-prime POISSON/HERGLOTZ completion -- kappa_p(r) = sum_k p^{-k/2}cos(k r log p) = -1/2 + (1/2)(1-1/p)/|1-p^{-1/2+ir}|^2 is a positive Poisson kernel (verified 2.2e-15), giving the unconditional bound "each prime's contribution to W_p >= -(log p)g(0)", using the prime-power factorization D-H lacks (#20). It WALLS three ways: (W1) WRONG SIGN -- it bounds the prime term from BELOW, Weil positivity needs it bounded ABOVE by the archimedean cushion; (W2) DIVERGENT/LOSSY -- summed per-prime independently it is off by ~g(0)e^{2A} (Chebyshev theta) vs the archimedean ~g(0)log, because it discards the cross-prime cancellation a single spectral weight phi-hat^2 enforces, and that lost cancellation IS RH; (W3) the regularized prime kernel is Re[-zeta'/zeta(1/2+ir)], singular support exactly at the zeros -- every unconditional Euler handle lives on Re(s)>1 and cannot be continued to Re(s)=1/2 without already knowing the zeros (#42). CORRECTION it forced (load-bearing, fixes a standing framing): the natural "A_inf >= 0, just bound the prime sum P" decomposition is FALSE -- A_arch + B_pole is NOT PSD (min eig -1.75 for zeta); the archimedean term is calibrated to be CANCELLED by the primes, and zeta's positivity is the residue of a THREE-WAY near-cancellation, not "archimedean dominates prime." This is the analytic root of the marginal-positivity wall (#18/#19): no buffer is created. (Honest partial: on the small-support cone supp phi in (-(log2)/2,(log2)/2) the prime sum vanishes and W>=0 rigorously, but it is the prime-free base case -- positive for D-H too -- zero RH content.) FRONT 2 (moment-Gram, 02_moment_gram.md + VERIFIED here, e3s_li_hankel_dissolves.py): the moment-MATRIX lift of e2xx (#123) to zeta DISSOLVES. The zero-spectrum (mapped to the circle by w=1-1/rho) accumulates at the archimedean point w=1, so the Toeplitz moment C_n = sum w_rho^n DIVERGES (no single circle/q = the (1,p) bidegree #25). The Gram M=V^T V indexed by zeros is PSD iff RH (circular); factored from primes M = A_arch+P_fin+B_pole, P_fin is the von Mangoldt SUBTRACTION (the obstruction). NEW VERIFIED STRUCTURAL NO-GO: the Li-coefficient Hankel [lambda_{i+j}] is NOT PSD even for zeta (min eig -0.014, -0.055, ... at sizes 2,3,...), because zeta's Li sequence is strictly LOG-CONCAVE (lambda_n ~ (n/2)log n, Bombieri-Lagarias; verified 14/14 log-concave, 0/14 log-convex) while a Hamburger moment sequence must be LOG-CONVEX -- so {lambda_n} is categorically NOT a moment sequence, and the moment-MATRIX framing of M4 is GENUS-FAITHFUL (a finite Frobenius spectrum over F_q), dissolving over Z's infinite accumulating spectrum; only the TERMWISE lambda_n>=0 (the Weil form, Level-4) survives. (The #122 genus-1-faithfulness caveat one level up; sharpens #27 from "non-Euler detector" to "log-concave, not a moment sequence." RH unaffected: RH = termwise positivity, not Hankel PSD.) FRONT 3 (Hilbert-Polya, 03_hilbert_polya.md): the only #4-passing build (genuinely injects the Euler product) is the Connes idele-class scaling operator D; it is self-adjoint with ABSOLUTELY CONTINUOUS spectrum (the whole real line), and the zeros are RESONANCES (poles of xi(2s-1)/xi(2s) in the Eisenstein continuous spectrum), NOT eigenvalues -- self-adjointness fixes gamma in R (automatic) and is SILENT on Re(rho), the one coordinate RH is about (sharper than "no operator exists": a good self-adjoint operator exists, the zeros just are not its eigenvalues). Forcing discreteness via a cutoff IS global Weil positivity = RH (K1-circular). And for ZETA the local factor is weight 0 (alpha_p=1), so the sqrt p circle is the CURVE/ELLIPTIC (Hasse) object, NOT zeta's (a sharpening of e2ad's per-prime framing: that a_p/Hasse structure belongs to L(E,s), the weight-1 motive, not zeta the weight-0 trivial motive). COMPRESSION (the residue): RH <=> PT-symmetry unbroken <=> a positive metric eta>0 with eta H eta^{-1} = H^* exists. The archimedean place gives the PT/FE involution for free (D-H SHARES it, which is why the firewall correctly does NOT separate zeta from D-H at the symmetry level -- D-H's off-line zeros are a PT-conjugate pair); the discriminator is eta>0 = the missing Euler-side polarization = M4. So the spectral route re-derives the IDENTICAL gap as the geometric route (all-roads #30), compressed into one operator-theoretic predicate (Bender-Brody-Muller is the canonical K1 instance). THE SYNTHESIS: three independent attacks on RH, three honest walls, ONE gap -- the Euler product (Re(s)>1, a trace) cannot be continued to the critical line (Re(s)=1/2, where the SIGNATURE / the polarization / eta>0 / the signed pairing lives) without the missing Weil cohomology. The attempt was genuine and full-effort; it did not close (as expected for RH); its value is the 3-front convergence (the gap is one object) + the verified residues (the Poisson completion + the three-way-cancellation correction; the Li-Hankel non-PSD no-go; the PT/eta>0 compression). Cross-refs: #123/e2xx (the moment object front 2 lifts), #124/#126 (the H^1/odd-Frobenius front 3 chases), #127/e2ad (the per-prime framing front 3 sharpens), #18/#19 (marginal positivity = front 1's three-way cancellation), #20/#46 (the Euler/von Mangoldt block, front 1's Poisson lever), #42/#25 (local-to-global, the universal wall), #30 (all-roads / trace-vs-signature), #4 (generic operators, front 3's #4-filter), R3.5/#120/#121 (continuous-vs-discrete, front 3's resonance wall). Files: experiments/positivity/e3s_li_hankel_dissolves.py (the verified Li-Hankel no-go); full dossier reconstructed (the original scratchpad/m4_attempt/ reports were not committed) at docs/03_research/m4_construction_attempt.md.

(2) ONE weak-form refinement of #125: in the e2xx NORMALIZED moment matrix [c_{|j-k|}], the DIAGONAL is the uniform NORM c_0=2g (unconditional) and the RH-detecting FLIP is the OFF-DIAGONAL Frobenius coupling c_1=t/sqrt q (conditional); so Faltings-Hriljac being UNCONDITIONALLY positive-definite (#22-24, #125) is "the right shape for a NORM", NOT "the wrong shape" -- the Arakelov-side analogue of e2yy's wrinkle. WHAT WAS WITHDRAWN (the adversary's kills, decisive): (a) "FH = the (0,0) entry Delta_0^2=-2g" is a TYPE MISMATCH on three counts -- one number vs r x r; NEGATIVE vs positive-definite; product-surface diagonal vs single-surface regulator. FH is the analogue of the WHOLE normalized PD moment structure (an r x r regulator), not a single entry. And the un-normalized asymmetry Delta_0^2=-2g vs Gamma_0^2=-2gq is just the un-divided q-scale (the (1,q) bidegree), NOT a height/Frobenius decomposition -- after normalization the diagonal is uniformly 2g. (b) "Gamma_S assembles the FH height" is MISATTRIBUTED (the sharpest technical kill): Lambda(E,s)=N^{s/2}(2pi)^{-s}Gamma(s)L(E,s) joins the FINITE primes (Euler product, the a_p) + the ARCHIMEDEAN place (Gamma_S) = the FROBENIUS side, as a TRACE; it does NOT contain the Neron-Tate HEIGHT -- the height-to-L link is BSD (s=1, a conjecture), NOT the FE Gamma-factor. (c) Part 2's first-pass "FH height Gram" was a hand-built 3x3 PD strawman (the same toy as e2zz's [C], and inconsistent with the rank-2 curve); the PD-ness rests on #22-24, not the toy -- withdrawn, replaced by a citation + the real regulator value (~0.1525). THE "CERTIFICATE" = #25's place-dependent (1,p) bidegree restated in moment-Gram vocabulary ("the signed-pairing assembly across per-prime scales"); vacuous as a deliverable (not circular), NO localization beyond #25/#44. NET: a re-cataloging of #25/#44/#125/#30, no new theorem/object/localization, one weak-form #125 refinement surviving; confirms the project's caution that more all-roads re-cataloging is not where the leverage is (the 2026-06-26 probes degraded e2yy[wrinkle] -> e2zz[inverted] -> e2ac[restatement] -> e2ad[re-catalog]). M4/#25 UNTOUCHED, correctly. K1-clean (a_p intrinsic, no zeros); K2 (the Frobenius/Euler part is what D-H lacks; the Gamma_S/archimedean part is D-H-shared, #38/#44). ### 126. Chasing the H^1 (the #124 follow-up): the Spec(Z) odd operators (prismatic Sen Theta {-n}, Frobenius F {log p}, the matroid grading) are WEIGHT/orbit operators -- real lines, NOT the circle Frobenius (|alpha|=sqrt q) the e2xx moment object needs; so chasing the H^1 the Tate lattice lacks lands on the prismatic candidate's MISSING SIGNATURE = the live M4 target, viewed from the odd-cohomology side. A SYNTHESIS (mostly #44+#119+#42+#30 restated through the moment lens), NOT a new theorem; PASS-WITH-CORRECTIONS. Built e2ac (experiments/arithmetic_geometric/e2ac_weight_vs_frobenius.py); adversary scratchpad/higher_rank_faces/02_adversary_e2ac.md. THE QUESTION (from #124): a matroid Chow ring is Tate/no-H^1, so "P3 = supply the H^1"; the prismatic/Sen/Deninger world is the home of odd cohomology over Spec(Z) -- does it supply the H^1 with a sqrt q-Frobenius whose moment matrix (e2xx) is RH? THE DISCRIMINATOR (e2xx's moment lens = #119): RH = positivity of the trigonometric moment matrix, which requires the spectrum on a CIRCLE |alpha|=sqrt q (pure weight 1, FE-paired alpha<->q/alpha); a WEIGHT/grading operator has real integer spectrum (a LINE). Circle = Frobenius = signature; line = weight/orbit = trace/realization. THE AUDIT (the category mismatch, the adversary's central correction): the three Spec(Z) odd operators -- the matroid grading (Lefschetz Cartan, degrees {0..r}), the prismatic Sen Theta ({-n}), the Frobenius F/flow ({log p}) -- are WEIGHTS and ORBIT-LENGTHS (real exponents), NOT q-symmetric Frobenius eigenvalues: they are not closed under alpha<->q/alpha and the grading spectra even contain 0, so they CANNOT enter the circle-Frobenius moment object at all (the moment formula errors on the 0 -- the code's own error IS the category-mismatch proof; the first-pass "plug a surrogate line in and get indefinite" was illustrative-to-vacuous = #119 restated). THE CORRECTION (adversary, decisive): the matroid grading and the Sen Theta are the SAME KIND of object (a real-line weight operator), but #124 and #44 are UNEQUAL POSITIONS on the all-roads map -- DO NOT flatten them into "one finding": the matroid (#124) has no H^1 and does NOT even realize zeta as a trace (the less advanced position); the prismatic candidate (#44) DOES realize zeta (both F and Theta halves) and lacks ONLY the signature (the LIVE M4 target). So chasing the H^1 lands on the prismatic candidate's MISSING SIGNATURE = the circle-Frobenius moment positivity = M4, viewed from the odd-cohomology side -- NOT a new place, the live M4 signature gap (#25/#30), in the continuation invisible to the local line data (#42). HONEST NET: mostly #44 + #119 + #42 + #30 restated through the e2xx circle-vs-line vocabulary; the ONE small organizing increment is that vocabulary applied to the odd operators + the corrected reading that the H^1 chase confirms the prismatic candidate as the live M4 target rather than finding a new object. Weaker than e2yy/#124 (which had one genuinely new sound wrinkle). M3/#25 UNTOUCHED. K1-clean (textbook spectra, no zeros); K2 (the moment/Frobenius object needs the Euler product D-H lacks; the Theta/weight side is the D-H-shared Gamma-factor, #38/#44). ### 125. The ARCHIMEDEAN/GLOBAL face (Option C, the Arakelov side of 09A Section 5): e2xx's finite F_q moment problem is the FINITE MODEL of the archimedean Weil/Li positivity, and -- after the adversary INVERTED a wrong-axis first pass -- marginal positivity (#18/#19) IS the infinite-support analogue of the F_q boundary kernel. A reframing of #48/#96/#123/#25, not new content; P6/M4 untouched. Built e2zz (experiments/arithmetic_geometric/e2zz_archimedean_moment_bridge.py); FAIL-then-CORRECTED by the adversary (scratchpad/higher_rank_faces/01_adversary.md). THE FIRST PASS (WITHDRAWN): claimed a DISANALOGY -- "the F_q on-circle moment matrix has an EXACT kernel, but the archimedean Weil-form min eig GROWS with the number of zeros (1.5e-3 at 6 zeros -> 2.2e-2 at 40), so it is NOT at a boundary; marginal positivity != the F_q kernel." THE ADVERSARY'S KILL (decisive): this is measured on the WRONG AXIS and the conclusion is BACKWARDS. [A] F_q grows the matrix DIMENSION m (kernel at dim 2g+1, finite support 2g). [B] the first pass held the Weil-Gram DIMENSION FIXED at K=#test-functions and grew the NUMBER OF SUMMED ZEROS n; on-line each zero adds a rank-1 PSD outer product to the fixed KxK Gram, so by Weyl min eig is monotone non-decreasing in n AUTOMATICALLY -- zero information about a kernel. On the FAITHFUL axis (grow the test-function DIMENSION K, the analogue of F_q's m, at a fixed large zero set) the archimedean min eig COLLAPSES toward 0 (3.4e-2 -> 3.7e-3, condition number 3.7 -> 138), and a TRUNCATED zero set gives an EXACT kernel once K>n_zeros (min eig ~ -1.3e-17). So the crux resolves AGAINST the first pass: the true infinite operator's positivity margin GOES TO 0 (accumulation at 0 = #18/#19), and marginal positivity over Z IS the infinite-support analogue of the F_q boundary kernel -- both are the moment/Gram form at the EDGE of positivity as its dimension grows. The mild true distinction: F_q reaches min eig=0 EXACTLY at the fixed small dimension 2g+1 (finite support 2g); the archimedean form approaches 0 ASYMPTOTICALLY (infinite zeta spectrum). THE SURVIVING FRAME (all reframing): (1) CONNECTION -- e2xx's finite F_q moment matrix is the FINITE MODEL of the archimedean Weil/Li positivity; both are CONDITIONAL forms that FLIP off the symmetry locus (Level-4, #48/#96). (2) POLARITY -- Faltings-Hriljac is UNCONDITIONALLY positive-definite and is not parameterized by a zero configuration at all, so it cannot flip => cannot detect RH = the wrong polarity (09A S5's "wrong signature", #22-24); structural, not a toy (the first pass's hand-built 4x4 "flips=False" was a category slip, corrected). (3) Gamma_S makes the form INFINITE-dimensional (#25, the (1,p) bidegree, the Deninger R-flow), which is WHY its boundary is asymptotic not at-a-fixed-small-dim; the global assembly is the archimedean+finite balance (#23/#24, two-clock 3M); M4 is the open certificate that this infinite marginal balance is a conditional (flipping) positivity. NET: no genuinely new theorem; the corrected UNIFICATION (marginal positivity = the boundary-kernel analogue, the project's recurring "no buffer" wall reframed as the correct, expected shape of the RH object whose F_q avatar is PROVEN -- a compass reading, per the mindset) is the right reading, recovered after the adversary inverted the flawed first pass. Plus a methodological coordinate: the wrong-axis trap (measuring min eig of a fixed-dim Gram against the number of summed zeros = a Weyl-monotone artifact). ### 124. The AHK face (Option 1, 09A Section 5), at the OPERATOR level: a matroid Chow ring cannot natively carry the Frobenius pi whose powers generate P(T), because it is purely EVEN/Tate and has no H^1 where the modulus-sqrt q eigenvalues live. A modest operator-level reframing of e2uu/#105 + #40 + #119, plus one genuinely new wrinkle; adversary-corrected (the "higher-rank" label WITHDRAWN -- it is genus-1). Built e2yy (experiments/arithmetic_geometric/e2yy_higher_rank_p3_frobenius.py); PASS-WITH-CORRECTIONS (scratchpad/higher_rank_faces/01_adversary.md). THE QUESTION: e2xx (#123) showed the moment sequence {t_n}=P(T) is generated by one Frobenius endomorphism pi (char poly P(T), |eig|=sqrt q); does a matroid Chow ring CONTAIN such a pi as a genuine graded-ring endomorphism? THE ANSWER (a near-theorem, the adversary's sharpening of the first-pass three-examples taxonomy): a degree-preserving GRADED-RING endomorphism T of A^1 must respect the Chow multiplication, which on A^1=span(e1,p1) of the (1,2,1) ring is the LORENTZIAN form Q=diag(1,-1) (e1^2=e2, p1^2=-e2; the Hodge index (1,n-1), e2g/#48), so T must be a SIMILITUDE of Q. The similitudes of an indefinite binary form are exactly: (1) ROOTS OF UNITY (diagonal diag(lam,1/lam) preserves Q only for lam^4=1; |eig|=1; = automorphisms, the q=1 shadow #40); (2) hyperbolic BOOSTS (REAL, unequal eigenvalues = the off-line/non-RH half, #119 -- a whole class the first pass OMITTED); (3) the SCALAR lam.Id (forced to the supersingular boundary t=+-2g sqrt q). The GENERIC interior Frobenius pi (RH, |t|<2g sqrt q) has eigenvalues sqrt q*u, sqrt q*conj(u), |u|=1 -- a scaled ROTATION, which preserves the DEFINITE form diag(1,1) (verified, mu=q) but NOT the indefinite Chow product diag(1,-1) (verified, no scalar mu). So the on-circle Frobenius is NOT a graded-ring endomorphism of the lattice at all -- this is #119 (real-root combinatorial vs complex-root Frobenius) AS A NEAR-THEOREM. THE DEEPEST PHRASING (the genuine content of #40): a matroid Chow ring is purely EVEN/Tate (the analogue of H^{2*}, all classes weight 2k, all Frobenius eigenvalues powers of q); the modulus-sqrt q (half-integer-weight) eigenvalues live in ODD weight-1 cohomology H^1, which a Chow ring DOES NOT HAVE. "P3 = supply the arithmetic Frobenius" = "supply the H^1 the Tate-type combinatorial ring lacks." THE ONE NEW WRINKLE (adversary-confirmed sound): the scalar reaches |eig|=sqrt q but, being scalar (alpha=q/alpha) under a real moment sequence, lands EXACTLY on the degenerate supersingular boundary t=+-2g sqrt q (c_1=+-2g), never the interior -- closing the "but a scaling reaches sqrt q" loophole. ADVERSARY CORRECTIONS: (1) DROP "higher-rank" -- the whole experiment is genus-1 (a 2x2 operator on (1,2,1)); no genus>=2 object is built, the "powers generate P(T)" content is e2xx's, and at genus 1 it reduces to e2uu's single t_1 (#105); the "higher-rank" label repeated the #122 genus-1 faithfulness flaw. (2) The first-pass "canonical self-maps = {Lefschetz, automorphism, scaling}" taxonomy was three hand-picked maps + an undefined "structure-respecting", NOT an enumeration; the similitude argument above is the proper (stronger) version, and it omits-then-includes the boost class. (3) "moduli set {0,1} = q=1" sharpened to "the Chow ring is purely even/Tate; sqrt q needs the H^1 it lacks" (Lefschetz, degree-raising, is the ample-multiplication operator, not a Frobenius candidate). NET: #40 + #119 + e2xx at the operator level, made into a near-theorem, plus the scaling-only-boundary wrinkle; NOT a higher-rank result and NOT a new obstruction. K1-clean; K2 (no Euler product => no matroid). P6/M4 untouched. Cross-refs: #123/e2xx (the Frobenius pi / moment sequence), #105/e2uu (the genus-1 P3), #40 (characteristic-1/Tate), #119 (the discriminant screen, here a near-theorem), #48/e2g (the Lorentzian Chow product), 09A_ahk_arithmetic_lattice.md Section 5, scratchpad/higher_rank_faces/01_adversary.md.

### 123. The higher-RANK AHK/Rosati object (the #122 caveat's named-but-unbuilt object) is now BUILT and IDENTIFIED: the primitive intersection form on the Frobenius POWERS = the trigonometric MOMENT MATRIX of the symmetrized Frobenius spectrum, with RH-for-the-curve <=> all G_m PSD (Caratheodory-Toeplitz). A genuine (modest) object-building advance on #122's verbal caveat; PASS-WITH-CORRECTIONS by the adversary, two distinctive headlines withdrawn. THE OBJECT (experiments/arithmetic_geometric/e2xx_higher_rank_rosati.py): on C x C take the Frobenius-power correspondences Gamma_{q^k}=graph(Frob^k), k=0..m; their primitive intersection Gram (project out the hyperbolic <e,f> as in 2G/e2g) is G^prim_{jk} = -q^k t_{j-k}, t_n=Tr(pi^n)=sum_i(alpha_i^n+(q/alpha_i)^n). Normalizing alpha_i=sqrt(q) u_i by q^{k/2}, this is CONGRUENT to the real symmetric Toeplitz moment matrix G_m=[c_{|j-k|}], c_n=sum_i(u_i^n+u_i^{-n})=(q^n+1-#C(F_{q^n}))/q^{n/2}. m=1 reproduces e2uu/e2g EXACTLY ([[-2g,-t],[-t,-2gq]]); the new object CONTAINS the old 2x2 trace-bound form as its degenerate rank-2 corner. The adversary verified the object via Weil's intersection formula (not just the genus-1 match): it genuinely IS the primitive intersection form on the Frobenius powers, not the Rosati trace form "dressed up" -- the two coincide, and that coincidence IS Weil's theorem. m>=2 is a genuine moment problem: by Caratheodory-Toeplitz, {all G_m PSD} <=> {c_n are Fourier coefficients of a positive measure on |u|=1} <=> {all |u_i|=1} <=> RH for the curve. So the higher-rank primitive Hodge form IS the trig moment matrix; its definiteness is RH, not as one determinant but as a positive-definite moment sequence -- the ">2-dim primitive part" #122 named, now explicit/computable. THE GENUINE HIGHER-RANK STRUCTURE (integer-exact): the integer "fake zeta" P(T)=T^4-4T^3+15T^2-20T+25 over q=5 (integer coeffs, exact curve functional equation, but trace poly s^2-4s+5 has complex roots 2+-i => eigenvalues off the circle => RH FALSE => a genus-2 Davenport-Heilbronn analogue, not the zeta of any curve). From its first two point-count moments c_1=1.79, c_2=-2.80, the JOINT rank-3 form G_2 has signature (2,1) INDEFINITE while EVERY 2x2 PRINCIPAL sub-minor of G_2 is PD (dets 12.8, 8.16, 12.8): the indefiniteness is caught ONLY by the full 3x3 determinant, by no 2x2 restriction -- the SMALLEST matrix exhibiting genuine higher-rank Hodge-Riemann structure (indefinite, all 2x2 restrictions definite), invisible to the genus-1 binary form, and its POSITIVITY over Z is M4. Real genus-2 curves (point-counted over F_p and F_{p^2} by a built-in brute-force counter): Weil holds (|alpha_i|=sqrt p exactly), G_m PSD = the polarization. ADVERSARY CORRECTIONS (PASS-WITH-CORRECTIONS, scratchpad/higher_rank_rosati/01_adversary.md, two headlines WITHDRAWN): (1) "caught ONLY at higher rank / genus 1 insufficient via a higher-rank catch" is the wrong framing -- SEEING a violation never needs higher rank (the 2x2 trace-bound FAMILY {|c_n|<=2g : all n} is ALSO equivalent to RH at every genus, since an off-circle pair makes c_n~r^n blow past 2g; it catches THIS violation at n=3). The joint form is the DATA-EFFICIENT detector (catches at rank 3 from (N_1,N_2) what the 2x2 family needs N_3+ to see; off-line-flip Part 5: detects at fixed rank 2g+1 vs the family's n~1/log r = 377 at r=1.001), NOT the only one. The genuine higher-rank DIFFICULTY is PROVING positivity (= the Hodge index theorem = M4/P6), UNTOUCHED, and it bites already at (g=2, n=1): even the trace bound |t|<2g sqrt q is no longer Hasse's elementary binary norm form once g>=2. (2) Part 5 "rank-resolved stealth window / detection rank CLIMBS as r->1" is FALSE: the flip RANK is FLAT at the kernel size 2g+1 (the RH spectrum is a measure on 2g points, PSD with a kernel at rank 2g+1; an off-line pair pushes that borderline zero negative); what vanishes as r->1 is the MARGIN (min eigenvalue -> -8.65e-6 at r=1.001) -- the ordinary #18/#19/#3J marginal-positivity wall, now in a (2g+1)x(2g+1), a MAGNITUDE stealth not a RANK stealth. (mpmath at 60 digits confirms float64 on every flip size; numerics reliable.) (3) P3 sharpening corrected from "the WHOLE moment sequence {t_n}" to "the curve's zeta numerator P(T) = t_1..t_g" (the first g point counts; the rest determined by the functional equation; for g=1 it reduces to #105). (4) the Frobenius powers SATURATE at rank 2g (Cayley-Hamilton: pi satisfies its degree-2g char poly), so "higher rank" here means up to 2g; the object does NOT reach the full rank rho-2 of the primitive part of NS(C x C). THE TWO SHARPENINGS THAT SURVIVE: P3 from "carry t (one number)" (#105) to "carry the curve's zeta P(T)=t_1..t_g"; P6 from "is a binary form definite" (Hasse 1933) to "is the 2g-dim Toeplitz moment matrix PSD" (= the genuine Hodge index theorem direction that does NOT follow from on-circle-ness; over F_q a theorem, over Z the open kernel). K1-clean (built from intersection numbers + a free q-symmetric spectrum; eigenvalue locations never input; the integer witness is a fixed polynomial, not read off zeros). K2: D-H has no Euler product => no Frobenius => no q-Weil spectrum => no Frobenius-power correspondences => no NS(C x C) -- D-H instantiates NONE of the moment matrix (09A's K2, structurally correct, fixing e2ww's Control-A flaw). HONEST about the tautology boundary: on-circle => PSD is the EASY half of Caratheodory-Toeplitz (a restatement of RH-for-the-curve, not a proof); no part claims to PROVE the positivity. NET: a genuine but modest advance -- the higher-rank object is BUILT and IDENTIFIED as the trig moment problem (more than #122's verbal caveat), and P3 is sharpened to P(T); but P6/M4 (proving the positivity) is the difficulty and is UNTOUCHED, biting already at genus 2, and the "genus 1 is insufficient via a higher-rank CATCH" + "rank-resolved stealth window" headlines are withdrawn. Builder -> adversary loop applied, all corrections incorporated. ### 122. Attacking the 09A AHK M4 construction with the breadth fingerprint RE-DERIVES e2uu's "the gap is P3" from a new direction (a CONNECTION, not an advance), and the adversary surfaced the real yield: a FAITHFULNESS CAVEAT -- the breadth fingerprint is GENUS-1-FAITHFUL, and the hard part of M4 (higher-rank Rosati positivity, the archimedean Gamma_S place, the global S->infinity assembly) is exactly what the 2x2 genus-1 shadow discards. A BUILDER built e2ww (experiments/arithmetic_geometric/e2ww_ahk_tslot_flip.py), an ADVERSARY corrected it (scratchpad/ahk_tslot/03_adversary.md), the distinctive first-pass claim WITHDRAWN. WHAT SURVIVES (sound but not new): the FF/genus-1 primitive form Q(a,b,s)=[[-a,-s],[-s,-b]] (a=2g, b=2gq, s=t) is negative-definite (the polarization P6) iff t^2 < 4 g^2 q = the Weil bound, and FLIPS to indefinite off-line (|t|>2sqrt q); run through the breadth corpus battery() it is the MASTER transfer-candidate (Weil/Rosati), passing every fingerprint axis (computed, battery()=[]), and the flip is on the SIGN (passes the #121 selection-not-sign screen Bridgeland failed). So the breadth program, pointed at the construction, recovers the 09A target object and the SAME constructible gap e2uu/#105 found -- P3 (the t-carrying degree map). Two independent directions converge on P3; that convergence is the connection, and it is the entire positive content. WITHDRAWN OVERCLAIM (adversary, decisive): the first pass claimed "the entire arithmetic is the single coupling s=t; M4 localizes to ONE matrix entry; four fingerprint axes free, one arithmetic." FALSE on three counts: (i) the diagonal b=2gq carries q (a separate arithmetic input; the flip threshold 2g sqrt(q) MOVES with q), and the complex-root + fixed-locus axes both REQUIRE the FE relation a*b=4g^2q, so the arithmetic is the JOINT Gram (2g,2gq,t)=P3, not one entry; (ii) "hits all five axes" was prose, not computed (now computed via battery()); (iii) Control B ("a random coupling flips too, so only s=t is arithmetic") is a tautology -- it shows detector faithfulness (the form responds to its own off-diagonal), not concentration; the actual discriminator between s=t and a generic s IS the Weil bound = M4, which no cheap control can exhibit. THE FAITHFULNESS CAVEAT (the load-bearing honest yield): genus 1 is the EASIEST Weil case -- the 2x2 negative-definiteness is just det>0 for a binary quadratic form, which HASSE proved in 1933 from norm-form positivity, BEFORE Weil, with NO Hodge index theorem. So in this shadow P6 (the actual M4 positivity) looks automatic the instant P3 is supplied, which is an ARTIFACT of genus 1. The genuine M4 difficulty -- higher-RANK Rosati positivity (Hodge-Riemann on a >2-dim primitive part), the archimedean Gamma_S place, and the global S->infinity assembly -- is exactly what the 2x2 genus-1 form throws away. THEREFORE: the breadth fingerprint (#120/#121) is a GENUS-1-FAITHFUL shape, necessary but silent about the rank/archimedean/global content where M4 is actually hard; "the fingerprint localizes M4" must NOT be read as "M4 is elementary once P3 is supplied." This guards the breadth program's converged-to-M4 result (#121) against an over-reading. K1-clean (the form is a signature of intersection numbers + a free trace parameter; zero locations never input). K2: D-H has no Euler product => no Frobenius => no q => no NS(CxC) -- it builds NONE of the form (09A's K2). NET: not an advance, P6/M4 untouched; the sound content is e2uu/#105 + breadth #120/#121 + elementary linear algebra, and the durable yield is the genus-1-faithfulness caveat (an anti-overclaim coordinate). The Section-7 compatibility question (can an AHK-machinable lattice carry the joint arithmetic Gram while keeping P1+P4?) is restated, NOT tested, and stays the open construction kernel. Two precise coordinates: (a) Q IS the WEIGHT-2 Hodge-Riemann polarization of the Mukai Hodge structure (signature (2,n-2), the "2" = dim_R C), one Hodge weight ABOVE Weil's proven weight-1 (1,n-1) Hodge index on C x C -- the closest non-arithmetic candidate is off by exactly one weight; (b) no t-slot (the three arithmetic touches all close: Gamma-class -> zeta-values = #121 special-value, motivic-DT -> virtual motive/Euler char = #119 supertrace, arithmetic-K3 Mukai lattice but C-valued charge). NEW DISQUALIFIER (refines #120 F4): the SELECTION-NOT-SIGN screen -- when a field offers a fixed indefinite form Q AND a contingency, ask which object the contingency acts on: does the SIGN of Q flip (definite<->indefinite) on a FIXED object set, or does class-MEMBERSHIP (which objects are in {Q>=0}) flip while Q's signature stays fixed? Selection-not-sign is realization (the selection moves, curatively); having an indefinite form is necessary but NOT sufficient, the SIGN must be what flips. Retires the whole stability/moduli/GIT/cluster/scattering-diagram-with-a-fixed-indefinite-form sub-family. The Gamma conjecture (Galkin-Golyshev-Iritani) is the assigned arithmetic seam and it CLOSES: Gamma-hat_X = exp(-gamma c_1 + sum_{k>=2} (-1)^k (k-1)! zeta(k) ch_k) reads zeta VALUES at integer k>=2, in the convergent half-plane where zeta has NO zeros (Apery constants -> zeta(2),zeta(3), Diophantine content). It CLOSES onto three: Connes (math/9811068, the absorption-not-emission sign = the scattering picture), acq1/CCM (the same Eisenstein xi(2s-1)/xi(2s) channel), and -- the sharp new link -- de Branges #43 (the Lax-Phillips scattering functional model = the de Branges space of xi = the already-refuted object, strictly stronger than RH, fails at k=34; the historical Lax-Phillips/Faddeev-Pavlov scattering route to RH is documented as "later shown to be unfounded").
