### 120. The BREADTH PROGRAM's first AIMED acquisition batch (acq1): the #119 disqualifier-complement (contingent / complex-root / spectral-gap positivity) is OCCUPIED, but by four distinct flavors of "contingent but still wrong", each yielding a reusable screen; assembled, the four screens are a near necessary-and-sufficient FINGERPRINT of M4's polarity (= a polarization). The Breadth Program (docs/03_research/breadth_program.md) ran its first AIMED draw via disqualifier-complement mining (Pillar 5.5): the #119 discriminant screen says RH lives on the complex-root / contingent half, so four SURVEYOR agents swept the complement (Riemann-Hilbert / equilibrium-measure transitions, the Lee-Yang FAILURE regime, transfer-operator / Ruelle / Selberg dynamical zeta, the Berry-Tabor / GUE transition). ALL FOUR CLOSE, and EACH produced a new reusable disqualifier; the corpus engine (experiments/lemma_db/breadth_corpus.py, 14/14 tests) was re-armed with all four. THE FOUR SCREENS: (1) CURATIVE-FLIP / adaptive-locus (Riemann-Hilbert): a contingent positivity is right-polarity only if its flip is PROHIBITIVE (failure forbidden on a locus FIXED a priori by the duality); if the flip is CURATIVE (the support/band/S-curve RELOCATES and the zeros track it), it is realization -- D-H-blind and circular (the locus is SOLVED-FOR = R3.5/K1 made concrete). Operationalizes K1 into a cheap predicate: "is the locus GIVEN or SOLVED-FOR?". (2) WRONG-AXIS (Berry-Tabor/GUE): level statistics constrain the spacing of the zero HEIGHTS (the vertical/Im axis), orthogonal to the Re=beta location RH lives on -- moving a zero to 0.51+85.7i leaves the spacing law unchanged, so it is compatible with beta=0.51 (Level-3); Katz-Sarnak symmetry type is genuinely arithmetic but governs the CENTRAL POINT (rank = #113 BSD regime) and is a corollary of RH where proven (S7-circular). (3) SPECTRAL-GAP = ZERO-FREE-REGION (transfer-operator): the Ruelle/Pollicott gap is contingent but controls a horizontal STRIP WIDTH (Architecture 4 / zero-free region), not the critical LINE (Architecture 3 / M4) -- a refinement of the wrong-axis screen by a third axis. (4) INPUT/OUTPUT split (Lee-Yang failure): Lee-Yang-type theorems put a DEFINITE positivity on the INPUT (membership in a Laguerre-Polya / real-stable / Polya-Schur class; Newman: Lee-Yang class = Laguerre-Polya), whereas RH needs an INDEFINITE (1,n-1) signature of a form built from the OUTPUT (the zeros); class-membership is a kernel property that coefficient multiplicativity does not touch, so it is structurally Euler-blind (Dobner 2020 proves Lambda_F >= 0 for the whole extended Selberg class S^#, D-H included). THE ASSEMBLED FINGERPRINT (the batch's headline yield): a contingent positivity is a transfer candidate for M4 iff it is contingent AND complex-root (#119) AND flips on the LINE axis (#120 wrong-axis, not spacing/central-rank/strip-width) AND is an OUTPUT-indefinite signature (#120 input/output, not input-definite) AND has a PROHIBITIVE flip on a FIXED locus (#120 curative-flip) -- and that profile IS a polarization (Weil/Rosati). So the program aimed at a hole in the map and got back a near necessary-and-sufficient profile of the target; the remaining search is less "find a positivity" and more "find a fixed-locus duality whose primitive form is definite" = the M4 construction itself. THE SHARPEST SINGLE COORDINATE (transfer-operator/Selberg): the ONE proven spectral RH (the compact Selberg zeta) reaches the critical line by self-adjointness of a GENUINE geometric Laplacian -- the realized R3.5 geometric-escape -- but contains no zeta (its length spectrum is geometric, not {log p}); the arithmetic object that DOES carry the Riemann zeros, the NON-COMPACT modular surface SL(2,Z)\H, realizes them as SCATTERING RESONANCES (poles of the scattering determinant ~ xi(2s-1)/xi(2s) in the Eisenstein CONTINUOUS spectrum, at s=rho/2), and resonances are NOT pinned to a line by self-adjointness; the explicit-formula SIGN that makes the zeros resonances-not-eigenvalues is the spectral-side avatar of M4's indefinite polarization sign (Connes' absorption-not-emission, math/9811068). HONEST FLAG (a VERIFIER target): the surveyor's claim "the Hilbert-Polya gap EQUALS M4 modulo R3.5's Arch-1<->Arch-2 translation" is a STRUCTURAL argument, stronger than R3.5 proves (R3.5 shows trace-formula positivity is K1<=>RH and leaves the geometric route open; it does not prove the spectral and geometric gaps are literally one object); tighten lean/ZetaRH/R3_5.lean's GeometricPositivity to separate the Selberg "operator exists" case from the zeta "no operator / scattering resonance" case. ### 119. The FOUR-AREA EXPLORATION SWEEP (Lorentzian polynomials, SUSY/Witten index, index theory, condensed/analytic geometry): all four CLOSE in the same shape -- each supplies REALIZATION (a trace, an index, a perfect duality), NEVER the SIGNATURE (the (1,n-1) polarization carrying t), which is in every case the Hodge-Riemann/Rosati INPUT the tool presupposes; the marginal-positivity thesis confirmed from four more independent bases (twelve total), yielding three reusable disqualifiers, one cross-area convergence, and one better substrate to watch. Four SURVEYOR agents assessed four areas NOT previously swept, each looking for a PROVEN indefinite-positivity tool of the RIGHT polarity (not another realization), held to the D-H + e3r-polarity + L-value + R3.5/K1 disciplines (synthesis: [`exploration_sweep_2026-06.md`](../docs/03_research/exploration_sweep_2026-06.md); dossiers scratchpad/explore_2026-06/). THREE REUSABLE DISQUALIFIERS: (1) THE DISCRIMINANT-COMPLEMENTARITY SCREEN (Lorentzian polynomials / Alexandrov-Fenchel / tropical Hodge-Riemann, the Branden-Huh modern HR engine): the genus-1 Weil/RH bound is |t_p| < 2 sqrt p <=> t_p^2 - 4p < 0, the NEGATIVE-discriminant / COMPLEX-root condition, while Lorentzian / real-stable / log-concave polynomials are REAL-ROOTED (t_p^2 - 4p >= 0, the NEGATION of the Weil bound). RH lives on the complex-root half of the discriminant; the entire convex/log-concave/Lee-Yang engine lives on the real-root half. Both read "one positive eigenvalue" numerically but from OPPOSITE root-geometries -- exactly why one signature is UNCONDITIONAL (wrong polarity, e3r/#48, can never flag an off-line zero) and the other CONTINGENT. The single inequality sign(t^2-4q) vs real-rootedness retires the WHOLE real-stability / Lee-Yang / Lorentzian / log-concave family in one line, unifying #95 (Lee-Yang), #27 (log-concavity), #48 (convex Hodge); Boucksom-Jonsson already killed (#97). (2) THE SUPERTRACE/SIGNATURE GRADING SPLIT (SUSY + index theory, CONVERGENT): Tr(-1)^F is a supertrace = the Lefschetz number = the EULER CHARACTERISTIC (an alternating count; the Weil explicit formula IS a Lefschetz-supertrace, the free realization side), whereas the SIGNATURE sigma = b+ - b- is a DIFFERENT index theorem (Hirzebruch's) whose grading is the Hodge-star / Weil C-OPERATOR (C_E^2 = -1, t-carrying), NOT (-1)^F (J, square +1, t-blind). The index theorem realizes the INTEGER sigma but PRESUPPOSES Hodge-Riemann to define the grading operator, so the grading that turns the supertrace into the signature IS M4. Reinforced by BUCHHOLZ-LONGO (1999): the modulus of any graded/super-KMS functional is proportional to an ordinary UNGRADED KMS state (the twist is weakly inner), so a Z2-graded supertrace over the type-III_1 modular carrier produces NO new sign beyond the plain (definite, D-H-blind) trace (#99/#102) -- sign-from-grading is dead as a free lunch; the canonical SUSY-RH model (Das-Kalauni) is xp/SYK in SUSY clothing (Level 3, FE-only, no Euler product). (3) THE ETA-INVARIANT = L-VALUE BRIDGE (index theory): Atiyah-Donnelly-Singer (1983) proves the eta-invariant signature-defect of a Hilbert-modular cusp = the value at s=0,1 of a Shimizu L-function, so BOTH faces of arithmetic index theory fall to the #113 L-value/order-of-vanishing disqualifier (torsion -> heights = Kudla #113; eta -> L-values, new) -- special-value regime, not zero-location-across-all-heights. THE CONVERGENCE: two independent surveys (SUSY, index theory) landed on the SAME object -- the signature operator's grading is the polarization. The Hirzebruch signature operator's index is a genuine non-circular geometric integer that FORMALLY fits R3.5's GeometricPositivity escape shape but is NOT a Hodge index theorem (it presupposes the self-dual/anti-self-dual splitting = the C-operator = M4), pinning the universal gap to one operator: realization = the index (free), the signature = the grading that defines the signature operator = M4. (Recommended VERIFIER follow-up: tighten the Lean `GeometricPositivity` placeholder in R3_5.lean so "realizes the signature INTEGER" cannot be mistaken for "proves the primitive-part DEFINITENESS".) THE ONE THING TO WATCH (infrastructure, not polarization): condensed/analytic geometry (Clausen-Scholze, the six-functor formalism) closes as a polarization route at MAXIMAL generality (it gives perfect Poincare-Verdier duality, NO sign -- #71 restated structurally), but UPGRADES the substrate: Wagner's STACK OF NORMS N/R_{>0} (analytic stacks, Strasbourg May 2025) surjects onto the extended Berkovich spectrum M(Z)^ext with the ARCHIMEDEAN PLACE as a first-class branch alongside the prime branches (glued by the !-topology, not hand-built Green's functions) -- strictly better than Gurney's prismatization (p-adic, no inf) for the M4 core (the archimedean-dominates-Euler-product #20/3M balance). It carries only perfect duality + classical imported Arakelov positivity; the prerequisite for a native polarization is an "archimedean Deligne-Illusie" (Hodge-de-Rham degeneration at inf), conjectural, the workshop's own closing open problem. WATCH trigger: if the archimedean Deligne-Illusie is proven, re-survey. THE DEEPEST STRUCTURAL STATEMENT: the perfectness/sign cut is now STRUCTURAL -- the six-functor formalism is duality-without-sign made universal (realization, axiomatizable, free), and Hodge-Riemann/Rosati positivity is THE SIGN (M4); every framework that can be AXIOMATIZED gives realization, and the signature is exactly the part no axiomatization supplies. ### 118. The METAPLECTIC OPERATOR (e1i), the one route e1f/g/h left un-eliminated, attacked at its FINITE-LOCAL core via the finite Weil representation over F_p: the metaplectic sign-structure is GENUINE (an exact gate: Tr(F_p)=eps_p, the Weil index, plus the double-cover 2-cocycle), but the finite-local quadratic sign-structure does NOT survive the finite Sonin positivity compression as arithmetic discrimination (a concentration-matched Arbiter puts the genuine chirp in the body, 5/5 primes, false-negative-probed); this does NOT eliminate CCM's semilocal W_{lambda,S} (the global SL~(2,A_S) operator), which stays the un-eliminated door = M4, but it sharpens where the metaplectic arithmetic does and does not survive positivity. A BUILDER built e1i (experiments/spectral/e1i_metaplectic_weil_index.py), self-caught a false-positive control (the value-matched ROUGH scramble conflates "smooth" with "arithmetic"; replaced with a smooth-chirp Arbiter, the e1h discipline), and an ADVERSARY verified it and forced five corrections (scratchpad/ccm_metaplectic/03_adversary.md), all applied. THE EXACT GATE (the metaplectic analogue of e1h's Meixner-Pollaczek identity; two parts, both exact). For the finite Weil representation of SL(2,F_p) on C[F_p] (omega=e^{2 pi i/p}; Fourier F[x,y]=p^{-1/2} omega^{xy}; chirp diag(omega^{b x^2}); torus rho(m(a))f(x)=(a|p)f(a^{-1}x)): (G2) the TRACE OF THE NORMALIZED METAPLECTIC FOURIER IS THE WEIL INDEX, Tr(F_p)=p^{-1/2} sum_x omega^{x^2}=(Gauss sum)/sqrt(p)=eps_p (1 if p=1 mod 4, i if p=3 mod 4), to ~1e-15 for p in {5,7,11,13,17,19,23,29} (Gauss's sign theorem, independently reproduced by the adversary for all primes 3..59); (G5) the metaplectic 2-COCYCLE: SL_2 needs rho(w)^2=rho(m(-1)), i.e. F^2=(-1|p)*parity, but F^2=parity, so they differ by (-1|p)=-1 exactly when p=3 mod 4 (operator-norm gap 0 for p=1 mod 4, 2 for p=3 mod 4) -- the double-cover obstruction, the SAME arithmetic eps_p records. ADVERSARY CORRECTION: the torus character-multiplicativity m(a)m(a')=m(aa') alone does NOT force the Legendre symbol (it passes with the trivial sign +1); the cocycle is the test that exercises the metaplectic correction. So the finite Weil rep is genuine and eps_p is a citable validated metaplectic identity, the double cover non-trivial exactly for p=3 mod 4. THE HEURISTIC (motivation, NOT a mechanism; adversary-qualified): the metaplectic arithmetic (eps_p, the Legendre symbol) is a phase/sign, and a modulus-squared measure |L_p|^2 discards it (|z e^{i theta}|^2=|z|^2). This MOTIVATES why the measure routes e1f/g/h are blind, but is a TAUTOLOGY as demonstrated, and eps_p is not literally phase(L_p) (the load-bearing version is cross-prime interference in arg(prod_v L_v), which |prod L_v|^2 factorizes away); kept as a heuristic for WHERE arithmetic hides, not as a proof. THE SIGNATURE TEST (one-sided, concentration-matched, robust): T1 -- in the *-positivity quantity W(g*g) the Weil index of g* is conj(eps), so eps*conj(eps)=|eps|^2=1: the scalar metaplectic phase CANCELS under the *-operation (the bare normalization carries no surviving positivity content, consistent with e1g/h). T2/T3 -- the genuine quadratic chirp omega^{b x^2}, band-limited to the finite Sonin space S (low modes of the finite oscillator X^2+P^2), is compared to an ARBITER cloud of SMOOTH quadratic chirps omega^{b round(theta xc^2)} with theta symmetric around the genuine theta=1, NO linear shear, concentration-matched (the genuine's concentration sits at the ~30-52nd percentile of the cloud). Robust over b in {1,2,3} x 8 seeds with a ONE-SIDED criterion (a high tail, median pct >= 90, would be arithmetic; a low/central pct is MORE generic, not a signature): all 5 primes {13,17,19,23,29} land in the BODY (Arbiter medians ~18-23%). The first-pass linear-shear bug had produced a spurious p=13 "tail" (the inverse of the e1h z=+2.29 lesson); removing the shear dissolves it (the adversary independently confirmed: with shear p=13 at 92%, without shear 22%, and it flips to body with more seeds). FALSE-NEGATIVE PROBE (the new risk the tighter cloud could hide a real signal): a planted concentration-outlier (a theta=4 chirp) reads 100% TAIL for all 5 primes (the test STILL fires on a real outlier), while the genuine x^2 reads 2-16% (deep body) -- genuinely generic, not insensitivity; body verdict robust across k=6,8,10, primes 13-43, b=1..3, 16 seeds (ZERO primes reach >=90). So the genuine quadratic chirp is NOT special among smooth chirps; its Sonin concentration is metaplectic-covariance generic, not a mod-p arithmetic signal. D-H CONTROL: D-H has no Euler product => no local fields Q_p => no local Weil representation => no Gauss sum / Legendre symbol; the metaplectic operator is unbuildable for D-H by type, for a sharper reason than the measure routes (the very objects that carry the arithmetic are exactly what D-H lacks). K1-clean: no zeta zeros input anywhere; only zeta_L.has_euler_product (a boolean) is read. SCOPE (load-bearing, adversary-enforced): the finite F_p Weil rep models the FINITE-LOCAL quadratic (Gauss-sum) sign-structure at ONE place; it is NOT CCM's actual semilocal W_{lambda,S} (the metaplectic rep of SL(2,A_S) from the degree-1 Euler factors with a GLOBAL S-dependent coupling), so e1i does NOT eliminate the metaplectic route and the eight-angle ledger's "un-eliminated metaplectic door = M4" stands. NET: e1i is the fourth finite/local surrogate to come back signature-blind after e1f/g/h, converting the "#117 un-eliminated by elimination" hedge into a finer map -- the metaplectic arithmetic is genuine (exact gate), the scalar index cancels under *-positivity (T1), and the finite-local compressed sign-structure is generic (T2/T3), so the live RH content is pushed to the global S->infinity uniform domination = M4, reconfirmed from the most pointed spectral direction without claiming CCM's actual semilocal operator is tested or the metaplectic route eliminated. BANKABLE: a phase/sign that genuinely carries arithmetic (the Weil index, the quadratic character) is annihilated by the measure's |.|^2 AND by the scalar part of a *-positivity form; surviving a finite positivity compression as arithmetic DISCRIMINATION is a strictly stronger demand the finite-local metaplectic sign-structure does not meet -- consistent with the project thesis that the signature is irreducibly GLOBAL (M4). ### 117. The FAITHFUL degree-domain CCM prolate operator (e1h, the third and last cheap OP-data route, the #114/#115 BUILDER target) is SIGNATURE-BLIND (genuine, hardened), so all THREE cheap orthogonal-polynomial-data surrogates are exhausted and the metaplectic structure is the un-eliminated route BY ELIMINATION; the one genuine yield is an EXACT validated identity (the archimedean Hardy-Titchmarsh scaling operator IS the Meixner-Pollaczek Jacobi matrix). A BUILDER built e1h (experiments/spectral/e1h_ccm_degree_prolate.py), an ADVERSARY verified it (scratchpad/ccm_degree/{01_builder,02_adversary}.md), two framings corrected. ARCHIMEDEAN GATE VALIDATED EXACTLY: the Jacobi matrix of dm_inf = pi^{-1/2}|Gamma(1/4-is/2)|^2 ds is the MEIXNER-POLLACZEK orthogonal polynomials (lambda_MP=1/4, phi=pi/2): alpha_k=0, beta_k=sqrt(k(k-1/2)), independently re-derived (50-digit mpmath + modified Chebyshev) to 2.8e-47 (the builder's grid recurrence gave 1e-7, a finite-grid artifact; the identity is EXACT). So H=J_inf is genuinely the proven scaling operator -- a clean, citable special-function fact (the archimedean canonical form is Meixner-Pollaczek). THE OPERATOR W_{lambda,S}=(H+1/2)^2+lambda^2 N_S (tridiagonal Jacobi H + diagonal degree number-operator N_S) is a genuine self-adjoint operator with NORMALIZATION-INVARIANT spectrum (3e-13, the e1f gate passed by construction since orthonormal polynomials are normalization-invariant) and grid-stable low eigenvalues (9e-13). It is POSITIVE (n_neg=0) -- and this is CORRECT, not a faithfulness failure: the adversary diagonalized the ACTUAL Connes-Moscovici prolate operator PW_lambda = -d/dx[(lambda^2-x^2)d/dx]+(2 pi lambda x)^2 and it is also positive (n_neg=0 at lambda=1,2,3). The "negative eigenspace = Sonin" / "squares of zeros" structures live in DIFFERENT objects -- the CONCENTRATION operator T=P_W P_T P_W (spectrum in [0,1], the object e1g built, reweighting-blind) and the IR Dirac D^2 -- NEVER in a sign of the prolate operator; the builder's first "needs a negative eigenspace, needs the metaplectic SIGN-structure" caveat was mis-framed on both sides and is withdrawn. THE KEY RESULT -- SIGNATURE-BLIND (genuine, HARDENED): W is a deterministic function of the Jacobi matrix (the moment sequence) alone, so it distinguishes ANY two measures equally and reads MOMENTS, not arithmetic. e1h escapes e1g's SPECIFIC failure mode (the degree-DOMAIN operator is NOT reproduced by the omega=1.37 Lorentzian, max diff 7.9 != 0), but the decisive "Arbiter" distance-matrix control shows the discrimination is GENERIC: prime-2's distances to non-arithmetic controls [7.9,20.8] overlap non-arith-vs-non-arith [9.8,21.1], prime-2 never an outlier (lambda in {0.5,1,2}, 10-panel, triple-checked). The adversary attacked the NEGATIVE (steelmanned the positive, the inverse risk) with four arithmetic-keyed observables and KILLED ITS OWN false positive: a denser panel gave prime-2 z=+2.29, but a NON-ARITHMETIC Lorentzian at the identical frequency log2 scored z=+2.29 (>=prime-2's +2.23) -- because a single prime's measure factor |L_p(1/2-is)|^2 has NO arithmetic content beyond its characteristic frequency log p, which any matched non-arithmetic factor reproduces; the joint multi-prime Mahalanobis test puts {2,3,5} in the BODY of a matched non-arithmetic cloud (52nd percentile in e1h's in-code test; 18th in the adversary's independent panel -- the opposite of an outlier either way); eigenvector rotation sub-threshold. The builder did NOT under-read a signal -- signature-blindness is genuine. NET: all three cheap orthogonal-polynomial-DATA routes are now SIGNATURE-BLIND -- e1f (#114, density surrogate, non-idempotent => not a spectral invariant), e1g (#115, concentration operator, reweighting-blind => L-function-blind by diagonal similarity), e1h (#117, degree-domain prolate, reads-moments-not-arithmetic). The metaplectic sign-structure (the concentration/[0,1]-splitting + the Dirac, NOT the prolate operator) is what none of the three captures; e1h does NOT prove it essential, it REINFORCES it as the un-eliminated route BY ELIMINATION. The CCM door (#114, conditionally-escaping, the deferred metaplectic Jacobi matrix) stays ajar; the cheap OP-data attacks are exhausted. BANKABLE: a spectral operator that is a deterministic function of a measure's MOMENTS (its Jacobi matrix) cannot carry arithmetic content beyond the moments -- a single prime's factor is just a frequency log p (any matched non-arithmetic factor reproduces it), and the joint multi-prime / Euler-product structure does not separate under any tested spectral statistic; so OP-data spectral statistics are signature-blind, and any arithmetic must live in the metaplectic SIGN-structure (the concentration-operator splitting / the Dirac), which the cheap OP data does not reach. ### 115. The FAITHFUL CCM semilocal prolate build (the #114 BUILDER target, attempted) VALIDATES the archimedean harness (the e1f bug provably fixed) and RULES OUT the band-in-s concentration route by elimination -- it is REWEIGHTING-BLIND, hence L-function-blind -- but does NOT touch CCM's actual deferred metaplectic operator; the door stays ajar. TIER-2 = THE BAND-IN-s CONCENTRATION ROUTE IS REWEIGHTING-BLIND (genuine structural negative). M_S = D^{-1}F is a DIAGONAL SIMILARITY on the s-band where the indicator 1_{|s|<=S0} commutes with multiplication-by-D, so M_S^{-1} P_W^(S) M_S = F^{-1} 1_s F and T_S is unitarily equivalent to the ARCHIMEDEAN concentration operator for EVERY S (max|ev_S - ev_arch| ~ 1e-16). So the archimedean positivity is non-circular but (a) K2-BLIND (it is the Gamma-factor term D-H shares verbatim) and (b) partly COMPUTER-ASSISTED (the Toeplitz separation). THE RADICAL of the Weil form = range of the periodization map E(f)(u)=u^{1/2} sum_n f(nu) = the POLE/trivial directions (s=0,1), NOT the zeros; "conditioning by the radical" = projecting off it (archimedean: hand-built Gram-Schmidt + finite constraints; semilocal: a HOPED-FOR spectral splitting, the positive/negative eigenspace orthogonality of W_{lambda,S} with negative eigenspace = Sonin). ### 112. NP-1 (does the finite-prime Bost-Connes/KMS modular carrier continue into the critical strip as a t_p-detecting positivity not shared with D-H?) resolves NO, and the resolution REDUCES TO M4/#104 from the continuation side -- it does NOT upgrade the K2 firewall to a convergence theorem. THE ANALYTIC ANSWER (what establishes NO): (i) the off-line obstruction is a t_p-phenomenon (the genus-1 C_E block B_E(p,t_p)=[[2,t_p],[t_p,2p]] flips PD->indefinite exactly at |t_p|=2 sqrt p, det=4p-t_p^2; verified flip at 2.828/3.464/4.472/5.292 for p=2/3/5/7 -- a GENUINELY t_p-dependent computation); (ii) t_p acts on C_E (the polarization phase), which is NOT modular-carrier data (#101/MC.2: (Delta,J) does not determine the sign C_E); (iii) the carrier (Z_S, Gibbs weights, log Delta spectrum, two-point n^{-(beta-i t_flow)}) is a pure function of (S, beta, t_flow) with NO t_p slot in any region; (iv) every finite-prime Euler product is ZERO-FREE in the strip (min|Z_S| in [0.124, 0.397] across 1/2<Re(s)<1, a real computation that varies with |S| and beta), so the off-line obstruction is absent from every finite truncation and appears only in the infinite-product limit S->all primes + completion. That limit IS the M4 coupling. So "does the finite-prime carrier continue into the strip as a t_p-detecting positivity" = "does the M4 coupling exist" = #104, restated from the continuation side. BOTH LOOPHOLES CLOSED on this analytic argument: (A) the relative modular operator / Connes cocycle (D phi:D psi)_{t_flow} moves GENUINELY under state and t_flow (verified, non-vacuous) but those axes are ORTHOGONAL to t_p; no t_p-bearing modular object exists (a chi-twist is a pure phase that drops out; injecting t_p requires attaching the C_E block, which is non-modular polarization data); (B) the beta=1 / type III_1 boundary leaves no finite-carrier strip shadow (finite Z_S stays finite+nonzero through the strip; the only singularity is the infinite limit = M4). THE INCREMENT OVER #104: one sentence of sharper framing (the continuation-side statement of the coupling reduction), NOT a new convergence theorem and NOT a "measured/structural continuation-invariance theorem"; the global continuation question = the coupling question = M4. HONESTY LOOP (the methodological lesson): the BUILDER's first e2vv "AT-MOD-4 witness" was VACUOUS -- carrier_quantities_at_tp ignored its tp_assignment argument (used zero times; feeding t_p=NaN also gave delta 0), Parts 2/3 compared byte-identical inputs / hard-coded Z_tp_independent=True; the ADVERSARY KILLED it ("the conclusion survives, the experiment measures nothing", a faked measurement-of-a-negative), and the experiment was rebuilt with the two GENUINE computations above and all "MEASURED"/"non-vacuous witness" language struck. The numerics now SUPPORT the analytic verdict, they do not measure it. VT-NP1 narrowed to the one formalizable kernel: finite-Euler-product zero-freeness in the strip + a precise M4-reduction statement (the 4-clause "continuation-invariance theorem" was rfl-vacuous: clauses asserting a definition lacks an argument it never had; K1-noncircular but content-free). (1) `critical_line_iff_fe_reflection`: for any ρ, 1−ρ = conj ρ ⟺ Re ρ = 1/2 (the imaginary parts of 1−ρ and conj ρ always agree at −ρ.im; the real-part equation 1−ρ.re = ρ.re is Re=1/2). (2) `fe_partner_mem_nonTrivialZeros`: the FUNCTIONAL EQUATION provides the perfect pairing ρ ↔ 1−ρ on the nontrivial zeros -- if ρ is a nontrivial zero of ζ so is 1−ρ, via Mathlib's `riemannZeta_one_sub` (ζ(1−ρ)=(factor)·ζ(ρ)=0) plus the strip Re(1−ρ)=1−Re ρ ∈ (0,1); a GENUINE, non-vacuous theorem about ζ's zeros, and it is the "perfectness" half, K2-blind (the FE holds for D-H too). (3) `riemannHypothesis_zeta_iff_fe_polarization`: RH ⟺ for every nontrivial zero, 1−ρ = conj ρ, i.e. the FE pairing coincides with conjugation = the cup product is a polarization. STRUCTURAL PARALLEL (the genuine content): function field has the two roots of X²−tX+q being {α, conj α} (Vieta) with positivity forcing α·conj α = q (circle); arithmetic has the FE pairing ρ↔1−ρ with positivity forcing 1−ρ = conj ρ (line) -- the SAME "a conjugate/dual pairing coincides with the symmetry locus" mechanism, circle vs line. So: perfectness = the functional equation (proved, free, even for D-H); positivity = the coincidence 1−ρ=conj ρ (= RH = the arithmetic Hodge standard conjecture = M4, the deep content NOT in this file). This is the "Lean as non-circular value function" lever (lever B / #98) applied to the real target: the M4 reduction's zero-level core is now machine-checked, and the gap is isolated precisely as "a polarization FORCES 1−ρ=conj ρ". Reflection (NOW PROVED, same module): `riemannZeta_conj` establishes ζ(s̄)=conj ζ(s) for s≠1 -- a genuine result Mathlib LACKS (it has the functional equation but not this reflection), proved from scratch via the L-series tsum on Re s>1 (`zeta_eq_tsum_one_div_nat_cpow` + `conj((n)^w)=(n)^(conj w)` from `Complex.conj_cpow` + `Complex.conj_tsum`) propagated by the IDENTITY PRINCIPLE (`AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_eventuallyEq`) across the connected domain ℂ∖{1}, with conj∘ζ∘conj shown analytic via the anti-holomorphic composition `HasDerivAt.conj_conj` and ℂ∖{1} preconnected via `isConnected_compl_singleton_of_one_lt_rank` + `Complex.rank_real_complex`. Then `reflection_mem_nonTrivialZeros` makes conj ρ a genuine nontrivial zero, so 1−ρ=conj ρ is literally "two genuine zeros (the FE-partner and the conjugate) coincide". riemannZeta_conj is itself upstreamable to Mathlib. Both axiom-clean. ### 109. Lever B M-1 route B: O2 ("deg is a quadratic form") is now a THEOREM, not an assumption -- the Weil-pairing/symplectic-determinant mechanism formalized in Lean. The crux of the elementary-Hasse argument is O2: that the degree map is a quadratic form (equivalently, the dual isogeny is additive). Phase A (#107(e)) made it FREE in the abstract by observing det of a 2×2 IS a quadratic form, but left WHY deg=det as geometric input. This finding formalizes that why -- the Weil-pairing mechanism -- as pure linear algebra (new module lean/ZetaRH/TateModule.lean, build green Lean v4.30.0, all three #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]). Route B's structure: for ℓ≠char, T_ℓ E ≅ ℤ_ℓ² is free rank 2, End(E) acts, and the Weil pairing e_ℓ is alternating with e_ℓ(φx,φy)=e_ℓ(x,y)^{deg φ} (Silverman III.8.6), so an isogeny scales the pairing by its degree. Three theorems. (1) `weilForm x y = x₀y₁−x₁y₀` (the symplectic model of e_ℓ on ℤ²) + `weilForm_mulVec`: the SYMPLECTIC DETERMINANT LAW, weilForm(A·x)(A·y) = (det A)·weilForm(x,y) for any A : Matrix (Fin 2)(Fin 2) R over any CommRing R (proved by Matrix.det_fin_two + Fin.sum_univ_two + ring). THIS is the rank-2 reason deg=det: an endomorphism scales an alternating form by exactly its determinant. (2) `scaling_eq_det`: if A scales weilForm by a constant c then c=det A (evaluate at the standard basis where weilForm e₀ e₁=1). So once deg φ is the Weil-pairing exponent (the geometric input), it is FORCED to equal det of the rank-2 representation -- deg=det with no further work. (3) `functionfield_RH_of_weil_pairing`: the route-B endpoint stated INTRINSICALLY, with NO det in its hypotheses -- given the Frobenius matrix A, a degree function degOf m n presented as the factor by which m·1+n·φ scales the Weil pairing (hscale), and non-negative degrees (hnn), every Frobenius eigenvalue α has |α|²=det A over EVERY finite field (proof: scaling_eq_det turns hscale into degOf=det, hnn gives det(m·1+n·A)≥0, then functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general/#108 closes it). NET: route B's O2 is now machine-checked (it IS the symplectic determinant law), so the elementary-Hasse residual narrows from "O1+O2" to O1 ALONE: construct the rank-2 representation A = Frobenius on T_ℓ, the Weil pairing, and deg≥0 -- the scheme-theoretic content Mathlib still lacks (E[n]≅(ℤ/n)², the Weil pairing, T_ℓ; all FLT targets). This also makes Route B the clear recommendation over Route A′: O2 is not just "free" but already FORMALIZED here, and Route B's deps are FLT-aligned. The deg=det/symplectic-determinant pattern is the literal finite-field rehearsal of the M4 target (deg=det=norm is a quadratic form whose positivity is RH). Cross-refs: #108 (the matrix endpoint this feeds, functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general), #107 (Phase A: det-of-2×2-is-quadratic, the abstract O2 this grounds geometrically), #FF-M1B (VERIFIER target), 08A M4 (the Spec(Z) generalization). Plan: docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md (task 3.5 DONE; O1 the sole residual). NON-VACUITY CHECK (same session, IsogenyDegree.lean, axiom-clean): to guard against the matrix endpoints being TRUE-BUT-VACUOUS (the project's #106 failure mode, the false-vacuous hodge_index_curve_elliptic sorry), the explicit witness is exhibited -- `companion t q = !![0,-q;1,t]` (the companion matrix of X²−tX+q, trace t, det q) with `companion_degForm` (det(m·1+n·companion)=m²+tmn+qn², via det_smul_one_add_smul) and `companion_degForm_nonneg` (for any Hasse-valid (q,t), i.e. t²≤4q, the companion's isogeny degrees are ≥0, via 4·degForm=(2m+tn)²+(4q−t²)n²≥0). So the matrix-endpoint hypothesis is SATISFIABLE and exactly equivalent to Hasse (no stronger assumption smuggled in), and the degForm endpoint (#107) and matrix endpoint (#108) coincide through the companion witness. This is a CONSISTENCY check, NOT a step in the RH proof: it assumes Hasse to produce deg≥0, the reverse of the real chain (where deg≥0 is geometric and Hasse is its consequence). COORDINATE-FREE LAYER (same session, TateModule.lean, axiom-clean): the Weil-pairing mechanism lifted off the fixed basis to the ABSTRACT rank-2 module = the real Tate module (T_ℓ E is free rank 2 with NO canonical basis, and the Weil pairing is an abstract alternating perfect form, not the coordinate weilForm). `bilin_alt_two` (an alternating bilinear form on a rank-2 module with basis b is determined by its single value: ω x y = (x₀y₁−x₁y₀)·ω(b₀,b₁) in coordinates b.repr, via the BilinForm add_left/smul_left/add_right/smul_right expansion + IsAlt.self_eq_zero + IsAlt.neg_eq); **`det_transform`** (the coordinate-free SYMPLECTIC DETERMINANT LAW: for any alternating ω on a rank-2 module and any endomorphism f, ω(f x)(f y) = (LinearMap.det f)·ω x y -- proved by transferring to coordinates via b.repr, where b.repr(f x) = (toMatrix b b f)·(b.repr x) by toMatrix_mulVec_repr, reducing to the coordinate weilForm_mulVec with (toMatrix b b f).det = LinearMap.det f); `scaling_eq_det_free` (basis-free deg=det: if f scales perfect-normalized ω by c then c = LinearMap.det f); `functionfield_RH_of_tate` (the coordinate-free route-B endpoint: rank-2 ℤ-lattice M + basis b + alternating perfect ω + Frobenius frob + degree-as-Weil-scaling + deg≥0 ⟹ |α|²=det frob, all finite fields; via scaling_eq_det_free then toMatrix to #108's matrix endpoint). So O2 is now a THEOREM on the ABSTRACT Tate module (matching the actual T_ℓ E), not a basis-fixed approximation; residual is purely O1 (construct M,b,ω,frob,deg≥0 from a real curve). Lean plumbing: (1) in Mathlib v4.30.0 `Basis` moved to `Module.Basis` (need `open Module`); (2) `LinearEquiv.map_smul` rw fails on `Module.End` (smul-instance mismatch) -- prove toMatrix(m•id+n•frob)=m•1+n•A ENTRYWISE via `ext i j`+`LinearMap.toMatrix_apply`; (3) `LinearMap.det_toMatrix` resists plain `rw` but fires under `simp only`.

### 108. Lever B M-1 Phase A endpoint WIRED (task 5): the route-B chain from "Frobenius is the integer matrix A, deg=det" to "RH for the curve" is now machine-checked end-to-end, sorry-free and axiom-clean; the SOLE open input is the existence of A. The Phase A bridge `hasse_of_matrix` (#107(e)) showed a rank-2 integer matrix A with non-negative isogeny degrees (det(m·1+n·A) ≥ 0 on the lattice) has (tr A)² ≤ 4·det A, but it did not connect the matrix to the eigenvalue extraction (#FF-1). This finding closes that loop in lean/ZetaRH/FunctionFieldRH.lean (build green, 3638 jobs, Lean v4.30.0; all three new #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] only). Two theorems. (1) `matrix_charpoly_root`: a complex α in the spectrum of the complexified Frobenius matrix `A.map (Int.castRingHom ℂ)` is a root of X² − (tr A)·X + (det A). This GROUNDS the previously-abstract Frobenius "roots" of the chain (the bare hypothesis `α² − tα + q = 0`) in the genuine eigenvalues of the rank-2 representation, via Mathlib's `Matrix.mem_spectrum_iff_isRoot_charpoly` (over the field ℂ, spectrum = char-poly roots) + `Matrix.charpoly_fin_two` (charpoly of a 2×2 = X² − C(trace)·X + C(det)), with trace/det commuting through the cast (`AddMonoidHom.map_trace`, `RingHom.map_det` via `RingHom.mapMatrix_apply`). (2) `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix` (the route-B headline): over a PRIME field, a rank-2 integer Frobenius matrix A with det A = p and non-negative isogeny degrees forces every Frobenius eigenvalue α (the complex spectrum of A) to have |α|² = p — the function-field Riemann Hypothesis for the curve. Proof = hasse_of_matrix (Hasse bound) → hasse_strict_of_prime (strict over a prime field, 4p not a perfect square) → root_nonreal + eigenvalue_modulus (#FF-1, Vieta on the conjugate). An intermediate `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_root` exposes the abstract-root form for composition. NET: this is task 5 ("Wire") of the lever-B plan and the literal finite-field rehearsal of the M4 target ("deg = det = norm is a quadratic form whose positivity is RH", here proved at genus 1 over a prime field). Everything downstream of the matrix A — the deg=det quadratic-form structure, the Hasse bridge, the strict boundary, the eigenvalue grounding, the eigenvalue extraction — is now sorry-free and axiom-clean. The ONLY remaining open input across the entire chain is the EXISTENCE of A: the scheme-theoretic Frobenius-on-Tate-module construction (O1+O2, route B: E[n]≅(ℤ/n)² → Weil pairing → T_ℓ E ≅ ℤ² → deg=det on T_ℓ; or route A′: dual isogeny + theorem of the cube), the genuine Mathlib-absent / FLT-adjacent contribution. Plumbing note: `RingHom.map_det f M` is stated in the `f.mapMatrix M` form, NOT `M.map f`, so rewrite needs `← RingHom.mapMatrix_apply` first; `AddMonoidHom.map_trace` is already in the `A.map f` form. Cross-refs: #107 (M-1 algebraic core + Phase A bridge this completes), #FF-1 (the eigenvalue extraction this grounds + invokes), #106 (the conditional chain), #105/#104 (the AHK P3 / coupling faces of the same deg-carries-t geometry), 08A M4 (the Spec(Z) Rosati generalization this rehearses). Plan: docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md (task 5 DONE; O1+O2 the sole residual). VERIFIER: #FF-M1 row updated in lean/README.md. BOUNDARY CASE DONE (same session, all finite fields not just prime): `eigenvalue_modulus_le` extracts |α|²=q from only the NON-strict Hasse bound t²≤4q (vs the strict t²<4q the original eigenvalue_modulus/root_nonreal needed), by casing on whether α is real -- non-real ⟹ eigenvalue_modulus (Vieta on the conjugate, needs only non-realness); real ⟹ the root equation α.re²−t·α.re+q=0 plus t²≤4q force (α.re−t/2)²=(t²−4q)/4 ≤ 0, so (sq_eq_zero_iff) α.re=t/2 and |α|²=α.re²=(t/2)²=q. This handles the supersingular boundary t²=4q (real double eigenvalue ±√q) that the strict chain could not. `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general` then drops the prime-field restriction of functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix: for ANY rank-2 integer Frobenius matrix A with non-negative isogeny degrees (det(m·1+n·A)≥0), every eigenvalue has |α|²=det A, over every finite field 𝔽_q (q=det A=deg Frobenius), prime or prime-power. Both sorry-free, axiom-clean. So the M-4 boundary wire (flagged in the plan) is closed; the prime-field endpoints remain as sharper-statement specializations. Plumbing: push_cast has a norm_cast lemma that pushes ↑(M.det) INTO (M.map ↑).det (toward the leaves), which mangles a `((A.det:ℝ):ℂ)` goal -- convert such ofReal∘intCast casts directly with `Complex.ofReal_intCast` instead of push_cast when a det/trace term is present.

### 107. Lever B milestone M-1 (the isogeny DEGREE API, algebraic core) is DONE sorry-free: the Hasse bound is now DERIVED from degree-positivity, not assumed. The lever-B chain previously carried the Hasse bound as a raw numeric field `hodge_index : t^2 < 4q` of `EllipticFrobeniusData` (#106). M-1 pushes that assumption back to its true geometric source. New module lean/ZetaRH/IsogenyDegree.lean (#FF-M1, build green Lean v4.30.0, #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] only) defines the Frobenius degree form `degForm t q m n = m^2 + t*m*n + q*n^2` (= deg(m.1 + n.phi), with deg 1 = 1, deg phi = q, cross term = the trace t = q+1-#E) and proves the **Hasse bridge** `disc_nonpos_of_int_nonneg`: if the degree form is non-negative on the endomorphism lattice Z.1 (+) Z.phi -- the genuine geometric content "every isogeny has non-negative degree" -- then t^2 <= 4q. The proof has two clean steps: (i) integer -> rational by homogeneity (degForm scales as 1/k^2 under (m,n) -> (m/k,n/k), so non-negativity on Z gives it on Q), then (ii) rational -> real by density (`disc_nonpos_of_rat_nonneg`: if g(x)=x^2+tx+q >= 0 on all rationals then t^2<=4q, via completing the square g(x)=(x+t/2)^2 - (t^2/4-q) and `exists_rat_btwn` to pin a rational within sqrt(eps) of -t/2). This ABSORBS the milestone M-3 integer->real "density glue" that M-0 had punted. Strict prime boundary `hasse_strict_of_prime` handles the milestone M-4 prime-field boundary: t^2<=4p upgrades to the strict t^2<4p because `four_mul_prime_not_isSquare` (if a^2=4p then a=2b and b^2=p, so b | p forces b=1 or b=p, both => p=1, absurd) rules out the equality case. Wired in FunctionFieldRH.lean by the constructor `EllipticFrobeniusData.ofDegreeNonneg {p prime, tz : Z} (hdeg : deg >= 0 on the lattice)` and the endpoint `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_degree`: over a prime field, deg >= 0 on the lattice => |alpha|^2 = p (the curve's zeta zeros on Re=1/2), entirely sorry-free. NET: the Hasse bound is now a THEOREM downstream of degree-positivity, and milestones M-3 (density) + M-4 (prime boundary) fall out of the same construction. The whole elementary-Hasse argument is machine-checked EXCEPT the one irreducible scheme-theoretic fact -- that deg actually IS this non-negative quadratic form for a real curve (isogenies, additivity of deg, deg >= 0) -- which Mathlib lacks and which is now isolated as the single hypothesis `hdeg` (the residual M-1, the genuine FLT-project-adjacent contribution). This is the correct division of labour: the positive-definite-quadratic-form -> Hasse bound logic (the same pattern as 08A's genus-g Rosati M1 and the Spec(Z) target M4) is now a reusable, axiom-clean Lean lemma; only the curve-to-form geometry remains. ### 106. Lever B's function-field RH chain is now FULLY SORRY-FREE (a conditional theorem), and a latent false-sorry was fixed. Discharging #FF-geom (lean/ZetaRH/FunctionFieldRH.lean) revealed that the geometric step `hodge_index_curve_elliptic` was a sorry standing in for a FALSE proposition: it claimed `NegDef 1 q t` for ALL q,t behind a vacuous `True` hypothesis (false whenever t^2 >= 4q, e.g. t=3,q=1), since NegDef 1 q t <=> t^2 < 4q by the keystone. The whole file is now SORRY-FREE (build green, 3147 jobs, Lean v4.30.0; grep confirms no sorry in code, only comment mentions), i.e. a sorry-free CONDITIONAL theorem: GIVEN Castelnuovo-Severi for a genus-1 curve, the Frobenius eigenvalues have |alpha|=sqrt q. The single unformalized input (the geometric inequality) is now an explicit, TRUE hypothesis rather than an admitted (and here false-as-stated) sorry. Discharging it -- proving Castelnuovo-Severi from a curve's definition -- still needs Mathlib algebraic-curve intersection theory (absent), so the gap is unchanged in substance but the formalization is now honest and composable. This is the correct end-state for lever B's function-field scaffold: the eigenvalue-extraction end (#FF-1) + the assembly are machine-checked, the geometric input (#FF-geom) is an explicit hypothesis, and the latent unsoundness is removed. ### 105. The AHK arithmetic lattice (09A), instantiated on the smallest case (9A.1-9A.3), NARROWS the BUILDER target from six properties to ONE: the gap is P3 (the t-carrying degree map); P1/P2/P4/P5 are already combinatorially free. [e2uu_ahk_lattice_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2uu_ahk_lattice_attempt.py) ran the first instantiation of the 09A spec. (1) 9A.3 (FF specialization) VERIFIED: on NS(C x C) genus 1, the degree map gives Gamma.Delta = #C(F_q) = q+1-t (P3 holds, carries t) and the primitive form is negative-definite iff |t|<2 sqrt q (P6 = the Weil bound), matched for (q,t) in {(5,1),(5,3),(7,2),(13,4)} -- the spec is right where M4 is a theorem. (2) 9A.1-9A.2 (abstract lattice) done on the Boolean lattice B_2 on {2,3}: P1 holds (the rank-gen polynomial (1+x)^2 factors over the atoms = the product-star structure), P2 holds (Whitney numbers (1,2,1) rank-symmetric), P4 + P5 hold by AHK 2018 + #48/e3r -- BUT P5 is UNCONDITIONAL (t-blind: the same (1,n-1) for every weighting), and the degree map is a combinatorial integer (# maximal chains = 2) with NO t-slot, so P3 FAILS. (3) VERDICT: the bare combinatorial lattice already supplies P1/P2/P4/P5; the SINGLE missing property is P3 (the t-carrying degree map = q+1-t on the FF shadow), and P3 is exactly what makes P6 (the primitive polarization) t-dependent and RH-meaningful. SPEC REFINEMENT fed back to 09A: P5 is DEMOTED (the (1,n-1) is free + t-blind, #48, not the discriminator; the earlier "primitive form is indefinite (1,n-1)" wording is corrected -- the (1,n-1) is the FULL form, the PRIMITIVE part is negative-definite and IS the polarization, the #101/e2qq carries-t-not-definite-vs-indefinite refinement); P3 is ELEVATED as THE gap. So the AHK BUILDER target NARROWS from "build a 6-property object" to "build a graded prime-lattice whose degree map yields q+1-t"; everything else is combinatorial, and P3 (over Z: an arithmetic intersection theory whose Lefschetz numbers are the local Frobenius traces) is the AHK face of the e2tt coupling (#104) = M4. The genuine division of labour: the combinatorics is free; the arithmetic decoration (P3 => P6) is everything. Not a solve (P3 over Z is the t-carrying arithmetic structure = the missing cohomology), but a sharp narrowing of the target + a clean refinement of 09A. Cross-refs: #104 (the coupling, this is the AHK face), #48/e3r (convex-Hodge (1,n-1) unconditional = P5 free), #40 (t-blindness), #101/e2qq (carries-t refinement), #97 (the sharpened AHK target this executes), 2G (the FF NS(C x C) the spec specializes to). ### 104. The MC.4 coupling cannot be SOURCED from the modular carrier (AT-1 closed in the negative), and the coupling has FOUR unified faces, all = M4. [e2tt_coupling_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2tt_coupling_attempt.py) executes the e2ss/#103 adversary test AT-1: is there a coupling between the per-prime twisted blocks sourced ONLY from the modular/KMS data (beta>1) that forces |t_p|<2 sqrt p? Answer NO, three assertions: (1) the carrier data is t-INDEPENDENT (the BC modular weight spectrum {log n - log m} (81 weights), the Gibbs weights {n^{-beta}}, and any carrier-derived coupling e.g. (pq)^{-beta} I are functions of (prime set, beta) ALONE; the carrier never takes a trace as input, t enters only via C_E, MC.2/#101); (2) the constraint |t_p|<2 sqrt p lives in C_E (t-dependent), not the carrier (a t-independent operator cannot encode a t-dependent constraint), so no carrier-sourced coupling forces the bound; (3) FF template: the cup block B_E = -G_prim is positive-definite (the Hodge index, a theorem) exactly on |t|<2 sqrt q and CARRIES t (t=0,2,4 in-window PD, t=5 off-window not) = the target shape, a t-carrying GLOBAL cup, not a t-independent carrier coupling. NET: the coupling is IRREDUCIBLE to the carrier (which supplies only the t-independent scaffolding, the weight ladder + FE duality MC.1); it must be a t-carrying coupling of the C_E polarization phases = the global cup product. THE SYNTHESIS (the genuinely useful consolidation): the coupling is ONE object with FOUR presentations, all open over Spec(Z) and all = M4 -- (i) modular: a t-carrying coupling of the C_E phases (this thread); (ii) function-field: the cup H^1 x H^1 -> H^2, neg-def primitive = Castelnuovo-Severi (2G, a THEOREM); (iii) AHK: the t-carrying submodular Lefschetz + indefinite primitive form on the arithmetic prime-lattice (09A); (iv) Arakelov: the Faltings-Hriljac product + the Gamma_S place (2H is the single-surface theorem). The modular / AHK / product-surface threads are NOT separate attacks; they are four windows onto the same missing coupling. HONEST SCOPE: a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate (rules out the "clever KMS coupling" hope) + a unification; it does NOT solve M4 and nothing cheap can (the coupling must carry t; the only available t-carrying data is the zeros (circular) or a new global object). Next on this front is construction-grade: build one of the four faces (09A the most explicitly scoped) or formalize the FF cup in Lean (#FF-geom). Cross-refs: #103 (MC.4, the residual), #101 (C_E carries t, log Delta does not), #100/#102 (scaffolding + euler-gating), #97 (sourced-not-propagated), #70 (C_E), #42 (continuation/global-assembly gap), #68 (transport), #2G/09A/2H (the other three faces). ### 103. MC.4 (= M4) on the smallest case is K1-CIRCULAR, with a sharp new mechanism: the modular/KMS carrier lives at Re(s)>1, so it is BLIND to the off-line obstruction (which lives in 1/2<Re(s)<1); the residual is the per-block COUPLING (= the product-surface assembly), M4 undiminished. An adversarially-honest BUILDER attempt ([e2ss_mc4_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ss_mc4_attempt.py)) assembled the smallest-case modular carrier: the BC truncation on a finite prime set P, with the genus-1 Euler-Sen primitive block per prime, Q_p = Omega(.,C_E.) = B_E(p,t_p)/sqrt(4p-t_p^2) (PD iff |t_p|<2 sqrt p), assembled as the KMS-weighted block-direct-sum M = (+)_p p^{-beta} Q_p; "positive on the primitive part" = every |t_p|<2 sqrt p = local RH at every prime. The disciplines run (56-row sweeps, e2ss_mc4_attempt.npz): fq-shadow PASSES (twist PD exactly on the Hasse-Weil window, 0 mismatches), K2 PASSES by construction (zeta's comb >=0 so the carrier forms; D-H's comb negative at n=3 so it does not, inherited from MC.3/#102), but K1 FAILS (circular): the modular weight grading log Delta is t-INDEPENDENT (529 weights, identical for every trace assignment), so the carrier does NOT supply t_p; positivity = (every |t_p|<2 sqrt p) = the conclusion. THE SHARP MECHANISM (the genuinely new content): the BC/KMS carrier lives at beta>1 i.e. Re(s)>1 (absolute convergence of the Euler product), while the local GL_2 factor 1 - t_p p^{-s} + p^{1-2s} has its zeros on Re(s)=1/2 at |t_p|=2 sqrt p, splits off the line for 2 sqrt p<|t_p|, and reaches Re(s)>1 (carrier-visible) only at the full Hasse bound |t_p|=p+1; the ENTIRE nontrivial off-line range 2 sqrt p<|t_p|<p+1 is INVISIBLE to the carrier. That is exactly why log Delta is t-blind: the marginal-positivity wall localized per prime, the same shape as the M2.6 stealth window. It is NOT (yet) the #80 continuous-spectrum wall directly (the finite block-sum is full rank); #80 is what the GLOBAL coupling would hit if one forced t intrinsically rather than asserting it (the block-sum has no literal stealth window only because it is decoupled, the same coin as "positive iff you assume each t in-window"). RESIDUAL (the one sentence MC.4 needs): construct the COUPLING between the per-prime twisted blocks (the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed-trace pairing = the product-surface / Poincare-duality assembly) that FORCES |t_p|<2 sqrt p from the modular flow itself rather than asserting it block by block; the decoupled carrier cannot (log Delta is t-blind), so t must come from the coupling, which is M4 undiminished. This matches the #97 doctrine exactly: the signature must be SOURCED (from the coupling), never PROPAGATED (asserted per block). NET: the cheap modular-carrier milestones MC.1-MC.3 are done; MC.4 is confirmed to BE M4 (K1-circular at the smallest case), with the residual named as the coupling and a sharp Re(s)>1-vs-critical-strip mechanism for why the modular carrier cannot reach it. The modular framing did not move the wall (as #98 P1-P5=NONE predicted); it sharpened the statement of where the wall is. Lean handoff (VT-1/2/3, AT-1) recorded in the e2ss .md. Cross-refs: #102/#101/#100 (MC.3/2/1), #99 (trace route closed), #98 (frame-audit), #97 (sourced-not-propagated), #70 (C_E K1 underdetermination), #80 (continuous-spectrum wall), #52/M2.6 (the stealth window). [e2ss .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ss_mc4_attempt.md).

### 102. MC.3: the finite-prime modular (Bost-Connes) structure is EULER-GATED, so the modular polarization carrier passes the K2 firewall by construction. [e2rr_modular_euler_gating.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2rr_modular_euler_gating.py) checks the K2 leg of the modular-carrier program. The finite-prime modular structure is the BC Gibbs/KMS structure: Hamiltonian with eigenvalues {log n}, partition function Z(beta) = Tr e^{-beta H} = sum_n n^{-beta} = zeta(beta) = prod_p (1-p^{-beta})^{-1}, pole at beta=1 (type III_1, #81); its "interaction" is the von Mangoldt comb Lambda_f(n) = a_n log n - sum_{d|n,d<n} a_{n/d} Lambda_f(d), and the Euler product = the comb being non-negative + supported on prime powers (#90/#37). Three assertions hold: (1) zeta's comb is non-negative (min Lambda = 0 over n<=60, 0 negatives, supported exactly on prime powers) so the positive Gibbs weights / KMS state form; (2) D-H's comb goes NEGATIVE (Lambda_DH(3) = -0.3121 = -kappa log 3 in closed form, first negative at n=3 the first prime = 3 mod 5, 21 negatives by n<=60), so NO positive Gibbs state -> the finite-prime modular structure does NOT form (reproduces #90 from the modular side); (3) zeta(beta) is the Euler product (prod_{p<5000}(1-p^-2)^-1 = zeta(2), residual ~2e-5, a truncation tail) with the pole at beta=1 (Z(1.02)=50.6), while D-H has NO Euler product but DOES have the functional equation -- so the archimedean Gamma-factor is SHARED and inert for K2; the discriminator is purely the finite-prime comb (#44: K2 lives on F not Theta_Sen). NET: the finite-prime modular structure is euler-gated; the carrier built on it passes K2 BY CONSTRUCTION (D-H cannot form it), exactly as the oracle's NoEulerProduct and the Lean no_dh_cupTarget do. This completes the cheap modular-carrier milestones: MC.1 (#100, the modular structure supplies the weight ladder + FE duality the trace lacks), MC.2 (#101, it factors the gap: C_E and N are extra data beyond log Delta), MC.3 (#102, the finite-prime modular structure is euler-gated). The open kernel MC.4 is unchanged = M4: prove the C_E-twisted form on the finite-prime modular carrier is positive, carrying t, without RH input. Cross-refs: #100/#101 (MC.1/MC.2), #90 (the passivity comb), #81 (BC type III_1), #44 (the finite/archimedean split), #37 (the prime-side discriminator). [e2rr .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2rr_modular_euler_gating.md); modular_polarization_carrier.md MC.3.

### 101. MC.2, the modular-Weil link, FACTORS the M4 gap rather than closing it: the modular scaffolding is t-independent, and the t-carrying polarization needs C_E (the phase, != J) and the monodromy N (!= log Delta), with positivity the open kernel. [e2qq_modular_weil_link.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2qq_modular_weil_link.py) tests the #100 dictionary against the actual polarization data (the Weil operator C_E #70/e2lo and the Sen flow Theta #41) on the finite genus-1 Euler-Sen model (Omega=[[0,1],[-1,0]], B_E(q,t)=[[2,t],[t,2q]]=-G_prim, A_E=Omega^{-1}B_E, A_E^2=(t^2-4q)I, C_E=A_E(-A_E^2)^{-1/2}). Three assertions hold. (1) Theta = -1/2 I + N is NON-semisimple (N != 0, N^2 = 0), hence NOT self-adjoint, hence NOT log Delta for any state (log Delta is self-adjoint): the modular Hamiltonian matches only the SEMISIMPLE part of Theta (the weight -1/2), and the monodromy N is INVISIBLE to it (= #69, the Petrov non-semisimplicity; the weight filtration is modular, the monodromy is extra). (2) The Weil operator C_E is a complex-linear COMPLEX STRUCTURE (C_E^2 = -I), NOT the antilinear modular conjugation J (J^2 = +1, e2pp): the naive "J = C_E" is a CATEGORY ERROR; C_E is the polarization phase, J the real-structure conjugation, so C_E is EXTRA data beyond J. (3) The C_E-twisted polarization B_E CARRIES t (B_E(t=1) != B_E(t=3), positive-definite iff t^2 < 4q; off-line t=5 gives 4q-t^2 = -5 < 0 so C_E is not real and there is no polarization), whereas log Delta and any GNS trace form are t-INDEPENDENT: t is injected by C_E, not by the modular Hamiltonian. NET: the modular structure (e2pp/#100) supplies the t-independent scaffolding (the weight grading + the FE duality J Delta J = Delta^{-1}); the t-carrying polarization needs the phase C_E (!= J) and the monodromy N (!= log Delta); M4 is proving the assembled C_E-twisted form positive without RH input. The gap is FACTORED (modular scaffolding free + C_E + N + positivity), not closed. REFINEMENT OF #99/e2oo: both the trace form and B_E are positive-DEFINITE, so the genuine discriminator is CARRIES-t (B_E depends on t, the trace does not), NOT definite-vs-indefinite; the indefinite object is the cup form Omega / G_prim that C_E polarizes. This sharpens e2oo's verdict without changing it (the trace route is wrong because it is t-blind and structureless, not merely definite). Cross-refs: #100 (the dictionary this tests), #99 (the trace route closed, the phrasing refined), #70 (C_E = the phase), #69 (the monodromy N the modular Hamiltonian misses), #41 (Theta), #68 (transport: the C_E-twist must source the indefinite B), #40 (t-blindness). [e2qq .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2qq_modular_weil_link.md); modular_polarization_carrier.md MC.2.

### 100. The modular-carrier PoC: the type III modular structure SUPPLIES the weight grading + the FE/Poincare duality the trace lacks (the positive counterpart of #99), unifying C_E / Theta / the weight ladder as one Tomita-Takesaki object. #99 closed the trace route and steered: the M4 polarization must ride on the MODULAR structure of the type III_1 arithmetic algebra (the modular flow = the Frobenius/scaling dynamics), not a trace. [e2pp_modular_weight.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2pp_modular_weight.py) tests the cheap feasibility question that steer implies (does the modular structure supply what the trace cannot?) on a finite Tomita-Takesaki model phi(x)=Tr(rho x) on M_n, three assertions all holding: (1) a TRACE is weightless (rho=I/n => Delta=I, log Delta spectrum all 0), so it cannot carry the Hodge weight filtration; (2) a MODULAR (non-tracial) state supplies a nontrivial weight grading (log Delta spectrum {log(rho_i/rho_j)} = the candidate weight ladder, #93 Poincare halving / Frobenius moduli q^(w/2)); (3) the modular conjugation J is an antilinear involution that DUALIZES Delta: J^2=1 and J Delta J = Delta^{-1} exactly (residual 4.4e-16), so the weight spectrum is symmetric under negation (Gr_w <-> Gr_-w = the s<->1-s functional equation). So the modular (type III) structure supplies, FOR FREE, the weight ladder AND the FE/Poincare duality the trace lacks; this is why "the arithmetic lives in the modular structure, not the trace". THE DICTIONARY (docs/03_research/modular_polarization_carrier.md): modular flow sigma_t = Frobenius/scaling flow (= Theta, #41); log Delta = the weight operator (= the weight filtration #93); J = the Weil/Hodge C-operator (= C_E, #70); J Delta J = Delta^{-1} = FE/Poincare duality (perfectness, free, #61); type III_1 = no trace (#81, #99); and the OPEN part = the J-twisted INDEFINITE form Q(.,J.) being a polarization that carries t (= M4, 08A). So the realization+duality half is the modular structure (free); the signature/positivity half is M4, unchanged. HONEST SCOPE: this is a SHAPE-SHARPENER, not a shortcut: the frame-audit (#98) already scored P1-P5 (Connes/Tomita-Takesaki KMS) = NONE, so the modular route does NOT make the blind spot cheap; the K1 risk is the Lagarias/Connes lossless-on-the-line positivity (R3.5-circular), the K2 discriminator must live in the FINITE/Frobenius half of Delta (D-H shares the archimedean Gamma half, #44). The value is unification (C_E #70 + the weight ladder #93 + the flow Theta #41 = one Tomita-Takesaki structure) + a precise BUILDER target (the polarization is the J-twisted indefinite form, not the tracial one; milestones MC.1-MC.4). Cross-refs: #99 (the steer + the trace route closed), #70 (C_E = J), #81 (BC type III_1), #41 (Theta = log Delta / the flow), #93 (the weight ladder = log Delta spectrum), #44 (F vs Theta_Sen = the finite/archimedean split for K2), #68 (the form is transport; the J-twist must source the indefinite B), 08A (the Rosati ladder, where MC.4 = M4 lives).

### 99. The P3-P4 von Neumann probe CLOSES the one live frame-audit edge: a faithful trace upgrades the edge only to the WRONG positivity (definite + D-H-blind), so P5 stays isolated and the residual is NAMED. The frame-audit (#98) left exactly one live edge, P3-P4 (noncircular <-> euler-gated), CORRELATED in the Rosati/Frobenius-algebra frame, with the sharpest next probe: does REQUIRING the arithmetic Frobenius algebra A to be a finite von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal tracial state upgrade CORRELATED -> GENUINE (make "A exists and is euler-gated" co-extensive with "A carries an intrinsic positive Rosati involution")? [e2oo_vonneumann_rosati_probe.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2oo_vonneumann_rosati_probe.py) answers it on small models, four structural assertions all holding: (1) a faithful trace DOES give an intrinsic positive form for free (the GNS Gram tau(b* a) on M_2 is positive-definite, min eig 0.5), so the upgrade's premise holds; (2) BUT that form is positive-DEFINITE by construction (tau(x x*) >= 0, = 0 iff x = 0), signature (4,0,0), so it can NEVER be M4's polarization, which is NOT positive-definite (the full Hodge index is indefinite (1,n-1); equivalently the 2G primitive form is negative-definite under Hasse-Weil) - the very faithfulness that makes the upgrade work is what fixes the WRONG signature; (3) the natural arithmetic state is a KMS state with a nontrivial modular flow (the scaling/Frobenius dynamics), NOT a trace (omega(E12 E21)=0.731 != 0.269=omega(E21 E12) at beta=1), i.e. Bost-Connes is type III_1 (#81) with NO faithful normal trace, so the hypothesis is not even satisfied by the natural algebra; (4) the trace form is D-H-BLIND (defined for any *-algebra, needs no Euler product; exists for zeta AND D-H), a soft positivity, not the euler-gated M4 form. NET: the upgrade reaches P3-from-P4 only as a DEFINITE, D-H-blind positivity on a type II_1 algebra the arithmetic does not provide (it is type III_1). So the edge is CORRELATED, not GENUINE, for the RH-relevant (indefinite, euler-gated) positivity; P5 stays isolated (confirms #98's prediction). The named residual: M4 = (indefinite (1,n-1), euler-gated Hodge form) minus (definite trace form) = the 2MM transport result (#68) in operator-algebra language (a faithful trace fixes the form PD; M4 needs it indefinite). A sharp NEGATIVE coordinate: it closes the "make P3-P4 an iff via a trace" route and tells the construction work that the polarization on A must be the indefinite MODULAR/Hodge form (the type III modular flow sigma_t = the Frobenius/scaling dynamics is where the arithmetic lives), not the tracial one. Cross-refs: #98 (the frame-audit + the value-signal program), #81 (BC type III_1, the KMS machinery reused), #68 (2MM transport), #43 (de Branges, another wrong-positivity), 08A (the Rosati ladder). ### 98. The regime-two FRAME-AUDIT is the FIFTH all-roads convergence, and it yields a new structural datum: the M4 property graph is two cheap edges around the Euler-product vertex with the blind spot ISOLATED. The one unspent discovery axis (#97) was "does a distant frame collapse two of the M4 conjuncts into ONE statement, as the acoustic frame collapsed carries-trace into passivity?". Run as a structured 11-agent workflow over all ten pairs of the five M4 properties (P1 carries-trace, P2 global, P3 noncircular, P4 euler-gated, P5 = the blind-spot indefinite (1,n-1) polarization), each pair searched for a co-extensive (iff) frame, each genuine claim adversarially verified. RESULT: nine pairs NONE, one (P3-P4) CORRELATED, ZERO new genuine collapses survived = the fifth independent convergence (after #90-94 acoustic, #95 Lee-Yang, #96 the 7-candidate screen, #97 the proof-engine sweep). TWO sharp sub-results. (a) P5 IS AN ISOLATED VERTEX: every pair containing the blind spot (P1/P2/P3/P4-P5) returned NONE in the strongest available frames (Connes/Tomita-Takesaki KMS with de Branges as its analytic face; Bombieri-Weil explicit-formula positivity on the adele class space; Hodge-Riemann/AHK; Bost-Connes KMS), so NO cheap property is co-extensive with P5; the AHK Hodge-Riemann frame, the only R3.5-escaping frame even capable of touching P5, was examined and found NOT to deliver the iff unconditionally = the marginal-positivity wall restated as an audit (the one frame that could jackpot is exactly the one whose verdict is "not unconditional"). (b) THE NEW DATUM: the property graph over {P1..P5} has exactly TWO edges, both at the cheap/cheap layer and both sharing the Euler-product vertex P4: P1-P4 (acoustic, known, #90-94) and P3-P4 (Rosati/Frobenius-algebra, CORRELATED). The "easy four" are NOT five independent asks; they are coalescing around P4 while P5 stays a lone island. Marginal positivity re-derived from graph connectivity. The D-H discipline held throughout (every NONE on a P4-pair is the firewall: no Euler product => no object). HIGHEST-VALUE NEXT PROBE surfaced: attack the live edge, not the dead island. Can P3-P4 be UPGRADED from CORRELATED to GENUINE by requiring the arithmetic Frobenius algebra A to be a finite von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal positive trace state (not a bare *-algebra)? I.e. does "A exists and is euler-gated" become co-extensive with "A carries an intrinsic positive Rosati involution"? If yes it names the residual to P5 as one concrete operator-algebra deficiency; cheap to falsify against the existing BC/KMS machinery + the D-H control (does the BC trace state induce a Rosati dagger whose positivity = euler-gating?). Expected NONE-or-CORRELATED, but it interrogates NEW structure.

This finding closes the "optimize RH for an AI to attack" session ([optimizing_rh_for_ai.md](../docs/03_research/optimizing_rh_for_ai.md)), whose thesis is that the binding constraint is the value-signal blind spot (no gradient at M4), so the optimization is to MANUFACTURE gradients + SHRINK the gradient-free kernel. Two apparatus deliverables shipped alongside the frame-audit. **Lever A (built):** the proven-case shadow BATTERY ([shadow_battery.py](lemma_db/shadow_battery.py) + test 6/6) generalizes the function-field shadow (6d) from one mirror into a graded battery of five proven cases (CP-fq Weil, CP-hodge surface Hodge index, CP-ahk matroid HR, CP-fh Faltings-Hriljac height, CP-af Alexandrov-Fenchel), scored by coverage with the D-H firewall as a hard side condition: "no gradient at M4" becomes "k of 5 proven cases + the firewall", and the gradient already separates the three known failure modes (genuine-m4 FULL 1.00; convex-only PARTIAL 0.60; arithmetic-blind/#40 BROKEN at CP-fq via t-blindness; too-strong/de Branges BROKEN at CP-hodge via wrong signature; off-line-forgery BROKEN). The positive mirror of oracle.py; FULL is necessary-not-sufficient (only Lean validates). **Lever B (scoped):** Lean as the non-circular value function. The keystone negDef_iff_hasseWeil (#2G-1, HodgeIndex.lean) is sorry-free, as is the formal core (Euler-Sen algebra rosatiPos_iff_hasseWeil, Class A/C no-go guards, the Frobenius cup formation guard no_dh_cupTarget = K2 as a type constraint, the digamma kernel); the GAP to an end-to-end function-field RH is the GEOMETRY (curve/divisor/intersection theory on C x C + the Frobenius graph), absent from the repo AND Mathlib, ~2-3 weeks of mostly-upstreamable formalization around (not in) the keystone. B is the prerequisite for any verifier-trained generate-loop (lever 5). ### 97. Breadth-over-PROOF-ENGINES converges exactly like breadth-over-STATEMENTS (6b): a 5-engine sweep of signature-MANUFACTURING mechanisms is a CLEAN KILL (0 surviving bridges), a FOURTH all-roads confirmation, and it closes the from-below axis. An 11-agent workflow (~860k tokens) deployed the AI's distinctive edge (breadth of math held at once) on the one axis the cohomology landscape and transfer-search (6b) had NOT spent: matching PROOF ENGINES (HOW a theorem manufactures a (1,n-1) signature), not theorem STATEMENTS (which 6b/#46 already matched and found converged). Five from-below engines were fingerprinted against the 7-property M4 spec and run through five kill-criteria (off-line-flip / indefinite-gate / euler-archimedean / fq-shadow / k1-noncircular). ALL FIVE KILLED; anti-theater tally 5 cheap kills (coordinates), 0 surviving bridges. (1) AHK FLIP-INDUCTION (Adiprasito-Huh-Katz) is the ONE retained POSITIVE coordinate: it PASSES k1-noncircular (the Hodge-Riemann signature comes from the submodular-Lefschetz flip, never read off zeros) and fq-shadow (recovers the full Frobenius/Hodge-index machine on the realizable face), making it the unique existence-proof that a Hodge-index signature is manufacturable K1-clean with NO ambient variety (the one M4 conjunct, PROP-noncircular, with no other positive witness). It is KILLED as a finished object by off-line-flip + indefinite-gate (the Chow-ring form is t-blind, identical (1,3) for t=2 and t=100 per #40; the primitive form is positive-DEFINITE, the indefinite (1,n-1) lives only in a derived 2-plane, AHK Lemma 9.6) + euler-archimedean (atomic; the degree map yields an integer, not q+1-t). (2) Lorentzian polynomials (Branden-Huh), semismall / relative-HR (de Cataldo-Migliorini, BHMPW), and tropical / non-archimedean Monge-Ampere (Babaee-Huh, Boucksom-Jonsson) are all the AHK "too-blind" bracket in new vocabulary: unconditional, arithmetic-blind (no t-slot), cannot flip when a zero leaves the line, cannot separate zeta from D-H. (c) R3.5 / circularity: "xi is a ferromagnetic Lee-Yang partition function" is a tautology for RH (ferromagnetic => zeros on the locus => RH), and the only R3.5-escaping object (the off-shell Hodge form) is INDEFINITE Lorentzian (1,n-1), which is exactly where Lee-Yang's all-couplings-nonneg hypothesis FAILS. ### 94. The Darlington construction closes the acoustic arc (EAC.5): network synthesis builds a passive system from a prescribed response, and Darlington's theorem realizes any passive impedance as a lossless 2-port + ONE resistor. The function-field weight ladder IS this network on the critical circle |s|=1 (s=alpha/sqrt q): H^0 (|s|=1/sqrt q, inside) = the RESISTOR, H^1 (|s|=1) = the lossless reactive 2-port (the zeros), H^2 (|s|=sqrt q, outside) = the SOURCE, with H^0<->H^2 paired by the FE (verified 7/7). Reactive sections are Blaschke factors; RH <=> every section degenerate (zero=pole on the circle), an off-line zero splits one into a dissipative C-section. Single-resistor insight (suggestive, not a theorem): Darlington concentrates all dissipation in ONE resistor and the arithmetic has exactly ONE archimedean place; identifying them gives the archimedean place the role of sole dissipator, all finite places lossless reactive, RH = the reactive resonances on the lossless circle. WHERE IT STALLS: zeta's Z is not rational, so the distributed Darlington = canonical system / Krein string = Suzuki's Hamiltonian-from-L, constructible unconditionally for omega>1 and lossless-for-all-omega>0 IFF RH. The construction route is real but lands on the SAME RH-equivalent gap; not a shortcut.

### 93. The reverse-engineering converges (EAC.4): the halving is POINCARE DUALITY placing the self-dual middle weight at the geometric mean of the extremes. Frobenius moduli on a curve are |H^0|=1=q^0, |H^1|=q^{1/2}, |H^2|=q=q^1, and the RH modulus q^{1/2} = sqrt(|H^0|.|H^2|) EXACTLY (verified every curve): the critical-line modulus is the Poincare-duality geometric mean of the weight-0 and weight-2 extremes, with H^1 self-dual. So the halving (exponent = weight/2, middle = 1/2) is Poincare duality + self-duality of the middle, NOT a separate positivity. In the primitive form: |Delta_0^2|=2g is q-free (diagonal/weight-0 leg), |Gamma_0^2|=2gq carries the q (Frobenius/prime leg), bound 2g sqrt(q) = their geometric mean, exponent 1/2 = average of leg weights {0,1}. Arithmetic: the functional equation xi(s)=xi(1-s) IS the Poincare duality (verified, residual ~1e-32), pairing the pole at s=1 (H^2 leg) with s=0 (H^0 leg), fixed locus Re=1/2 = the self-dual middle. zeta HAS the duality (FE) + the passive medium (Euler product); the MISSING (iii) is the weight-graded cohomology whose self-dual middle is forced to Re=1/2 = Deninger's missing motivic cohomology, with a one-line job description. D-H has no pole = no H^2 leg = no geometric mean = no halving (the geometric face of "no surface"). The acoustic reverse-engineering thus lands on the known Deninger/motivic-weight target via a NEW path that also explains the 2/3 ceiling (halving is geometric not analytic), the D-H discipline (no weight ladder without an Euler product), and marginal positivity (reciprocity saturates passivity). Convergence onto the target, not a construction of it.

Stronger and more on-point: Neshveyev-Stammeier (arXiv:1912.03141, Thm 4.5) prove the multiplicative Toeplitz system T(N^x) (the dossier's actual ambient algebra) has a ONE-POINT KMS simplex at EVERY beta > 0, and their Prop 3.8 cylinder-forces the underlying measure to the flat ray: an independent literature confirmation of #82 Lemma 1. Corrections to the working picture: on Laca-Raeburn's T(N x N^x) (arXiv:0907.3760) there are NO KMS states for beta < 1 (empty at beta = 1/2, not rich), uniqueness on [1,2] is Laca-Neshveyev (arXiv:1010.0599, type III_1), richness only at beta > 2; the genuine small-beta richness example is Laca-Schulz 2025 on T(N^x x| Z) (arXiv:2503.03033, Bauer simplex on all of (0,1]). **P3:** Neshveyev's criterion (arXiv:1106.5912, J. Operator Theory 70) verified, weights extension verified (Christensen, full generality; Thomsen arXiv:1306.5118 precursor). The groupoid question resolves NEGATIVELY with two named structural reasons: the (EF) constraint is INHOMOGENEOUS (the pole term makes W_zeta an affine slice, while cocycle-quasi-invariance always cuts a homogeneous cone) and FOURIER-NONLOCAL (an atom-to-continuum oscillatory coupling, while groupoid quasi-invariance is orbit-local positive transport). So W_zeta = (groupoid KMS cone) intersect (EF slice), and ALL the rigidity lives in the slice; the "find the LCC groupoid" search is killed as a category error, not a dead end of effort.

The open core (P2a/P2b/P4) is an explicit-formula positivity problem, full stop. Two unconditional theorems extracted by the ADVERSARY.** (i) P1 closed: every log-crystal has smoothed Chebyshev slope exactly 1 (translate bumps `g_T`: the pole atom grows as `4 cosh(T/2) int phi cosh(u/2)` while the spectral side stays bounded for any tempered mu; with `b >= 0` upgrades to sharp `psi_b(x) ~ x`). "The simplex is a point" is the exact statement shape of LCC/EFR's composite-pinching target (#76, the ghost-crystal kill); the technology is NOT rank rigidity (flat extension) but scaling-quasi-invariance + ergodicity (group-measure rigidity).

**Finite-window quantification (first 10,453 primes, all exact integer / finite-Euler-product arithmetic, 6/6 checks).** Ghost marginals on a window `(Z/M)^*` have component count `g(m) = [(Z/M)^* : <q mod M : q in S_m>]` (organ A) and within-coset diameter `u(beta, m) = TV(nu, uniform)` with Fourier side a finite-Euler-product ratio `prod (1-p^{-beta})/(1-chi(p)p^{-beta})` (organ B). Measured: organ A collapses to index 1 by `m* <= 5` on all windows (median 2, max 4 over prime windows p < 300; a Linnik-flavored least-prime-in-subgroup statistic). Organ B exhibits the phase transition at the pole: `u -> 0` stretched-exponentially at `beta = 0.7`, log-slowly at `beta = 1` exactly (`u * log p_m` stabilizes at 0.379/0.707/1.146 for M = 5/8/21), and PLATEAUS at `beta = 1.5` (ratio 0.9984 between m = 1,000 and 10,000; atom budget stalls at `1/zeta(1.5)` to 4 decimals). Amputations: removing the pole (beta > 1) or removing rotations (stream only primes = 1 mod 5: index pinned at 4 forever despite divergent `sum 1/p`) each preserves ghosts: BOTH organs are load-bearing, mirroring the #34 organ anatomy. The marginality fingerprint: at criticality the atom budget obeys Mertens, `-log(A * log p_m) -> 0.5775` vs `gamma = 0.5772`, so the #63 constant `e^gamma` reappears as the RATE of uniqueness at the pole: the simplex collapses, but barely (log-slow), which is the marginal-positivity fingerprint in the uniqueness costume.
