### 80. The zeta-side moment mirror EXHIBITS the flat-extension transfer fail mode: zeta's zero-measure moment matrix never goes flat (ghost room persists ~15 orders of magnitude above the F_q floor), so the F_q composite-pinching mechanism has no purchase on zeta.

[e3gg_zeta_moment_mirror.py](positivity/e3gg_zeta_moment_mirror.py), the zeta-side follow-on to e2ll (#79). e2ll showed composite pinching holds over F_q via flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem (Curto-Fialkow): the Frobenius measure is 2g atoms, so its moment Toeplitz goes FLAT (rank stabilizes at 2g) and the representing measure / comb is UNIQUE. This experiment runs the SAME machinery on zeta's zeros to make the transfer obstruction visible. The mirror: F_q's empirical Frobenius measure `sum delta_{theta_j}` becomes zeta's empirical ZERO measure, ordinates `gamma_n` placed on the circle at `theta_n = alpha gamma_n mod 2pi`, normalized trig moments `c_k = (1/N) sum_n e^{i k theta_n}`, Toeplitz `C = [c_{i-j}]` (correlation matrix, `c_0=1`).

**The fail mode, quantitatively.** (i) F_q (flat/pinched): rank stabilizes at 2g (2 for an elliptic curve, 4 for genus-2 over F_7), ghost room (relative smallest eigenvalue) COLLAPSES to `~1e-16` at `m=2g`, normalized moments `|c_k|` are `O(1)` (atomic; the genus-2 curve gives the clean period-4 pattern `|c_k|={0,0,0,1,...}`). (ii) zeta (never flat / not pinched): rank stays FULL (`m+1` through `m=12`), ghost room PERSISTS at `0.48-0.99` (mean `~0.74`), about 15 orders of magnitude above the F_q floor; `|c_k|` are small `O(1/sqrt(N)) ~ 0.06` (the continuous limit `C -> I`), not decaying in `k`. The truncated moment problem is maximally INDETERMINATE (a continuum of ghost measures), so the moments do NOT pin the comb. Robust across circle-map scale `alpha in {0.5,1,2}`. (iii) D-H ALSO never flat (ghost room `0.46-0.93`): the continuous-spectrum obstruction is shared by every degree-1 L with infinitely many zeros, so this is a MECHANISM illustrator, NOT a zeta-vs-D-H detector.

**Takeaway.** The flat-extension uniqueness that pins the comb over F_q has no purchase on zeta: the pole-sourced CONTINUOUS archimedean spectrum keeps the moment matrix full rank (`|c_k| -> 0` as `N` grows, `C -> I`). The composite-pinching transfer cannot run through flat extension; it needs a moment-uniqueness theorem for a measure with a continuous (archimedean) component PLUS the Euler structure that singles zeta out -- LCC/EFR's named open core (#76, #79), now exhibited as a concrete ~15-order-of-magnitude ghost-room gap.

**Honest scope:** proves nothing new; repurposes the known equidistribution of zero ordinates (`sum_n e^{ik alpha gamma_n} = o(N)`, the explicit formula) to make the #79 transfer obstruction visible and quantitative. Not a detector (D-H behaves identically). A synthesis coordinate; soft-detector freeze respected (no margin, no positivity claim). Cross-refs: #79 (e2ll, the F_q side this mirrors), #76 (LCC/EFR and composite pinching), #52/#34 (the marginal wall / stealth window, the other faces of the same archimedean-continuum difficulty), #43 (the de Branges soft detector this deliberately avoids -- this is the uniqueness/rank angle, not pointwise positivity).

### 79. The function-field wind tunnel CONFIRMS composite pinching (the program's single named target) and identifies its mechanism: flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem; the transfer gap to zeta is the discrete -> pole-sourced-continuous archimedean spectrum.

[e2ll_ff_crystal_cone.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_ff_crystal_cone.py), idea 1 (the top pick) of the local-compute workaround slate ([local_compute_workarounds.md](../docs/03_research/local_compute_workarounds.md)). Tests the composite-pinching lemma shared by the session-019 survivors Lonely Crystal and Euler Facet Rigidity (#76, "a fully-saturated point of the Weil cone has no mass off the prime powers") in the one setting where RH is a THEOREM and every object is finite and exact: a curve `C / F_q`. The number field's composite structure (von Mangoldt supported on prime powers) has the exact function-field analogue `N_k = #C(F_{q^k}) = sum_{d|k} d a_d` with `a_d = #{closed points of degree d}` (the divisor sum is the composite structure; the `a_d` are the "primes"). Unitarizing the Frobenius eigenvalues, the integer power sums `s_k = sum_j alpha_j^k = q^k+1-N_k` (exact via Newton's identities on the integer zeta numerator `P(T)`) give the trigonometric moments `r_k = s_k/q^{k/2}` of the empirical Frobenius measure, and `RH for C <=> the Hermitian Toeplitz R = [r_{i-j}] is PSD <=> all angles real`, with `rank(R) = #distinct angles <= 2g`.

**Composite pinching HOLDS, on every curve.** Across 7 curves (elliptic + genus-2 over `q in {5,7,11,13}`): (a) `R` is PSD with `|alpha_j| = sqrt(q)` to `0.00e+00`; (b) the closed-point counts `a_d` recovered by exact Mobius inversion are nonnegative INTEGERS for every curve and degree (exact `Fraction` arithmetic) -- the composite/divisor-sum structure inverts with no slack; (c) Prony recovery from the moments returns exactly the `rank(R)` distinct Frobenius angles (error `0.00e+00` to `2e-16`), and the rank STABILIZES with truncation (flat extension), so the representing measure is UNIQUE -- a saturated point's mass is forced onto the closed-point/Frobenius atoms. The supersingular-type `y^2=x^5+x+1 / F_5` (`rank=2 < 2g=4`, only 2 distinct angles) is a STRENGTHENING witness: pinching holds even there, so the lemma needs no generic-angle assumption. Anti-curve control (the FF D-H discipline): planting one off-line modulus `|alpha| = sqrt(q)*1.15` makes `|r_k| > 2g` and `R` non-PSD (min-eig `-6.7`/`-5.6`), so the detector fires exactly on the RH violation.

**MECHANISM identified + the transfer gap localized.** The function-field composite-pinching IS the flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem (Curto-Fialkow): a flat PSD Toeplitz has a unique representing measure. That is the technology LCC/EFR need over Q. The obstruction is precisely the SPECTRUM TYPE: here the spectrum is finite and DISCRETE (`2g` atoms), whereas zeta's saturated point pairs against the POLE-SOURCED CONTINUOUS archimedean spectrum, outside Curto-Fialkow / Olevskii-Ulanovskii / Bondarenko-Radchenko-Seip (which cover uniformly-discrete spectra). The wind tunnel confirms pinching where the spectrum is discrete and shows the gap is the discrete -> continuous transfer, exactly LCC's stated open core (#76), NOT the pinching idea itself.

**Honest scope:** proves nothing new about zeta; it confirms the named target is TRUE in the theorem-world, names the exact transfer technology and the exact obstruction, and rules out the cheapest kill (no ghost comb exists over `F_q`). A clean positive coordinate. ### 78. The EDC octave ladder (first test of the session-019 Eratosthenes Descent survivor) SURVIVED: the Haynsworth-telescoped self-reducing certificate has a sound skeleton, healthy per-octave margins, and NO octave-graded wall, with the global marginality emerging as the telescoped accumulation EDC predicted.

[e3dd_edc_octave_ladder.py](positivity/e3dd_edc_octave_ladder.py), idea 4 of the local-compute workaround slate ([local_compute_workarounds.md](../docs/03_research/local_compute_workarounds.md)) and the first experiment ever run against EDC (the second of the five session-019 PURSUE survivors, #76). EDC reads Weil positivity as a SELF-REDUCING certificate graded by support octaves `a_k = a_0 2^k` (here `a_0 = 0.34`, just below `log 2 / 2` so octave 0 is prime-free): the cumulative Weil Gram `G_k` (basis functions with support half-width `L <= a_k`) is a principal block of `G_{k+1}`, so Haynsworth inertia additivity gives the EXACT identity `In(G_{k+1}) = In(G_k) + In(S_k)` with `S_k` the octave Schur complement. Clause (i): every `S_k` is PSD (then telescoping gives global PSD = RH). Clause (ii): the margins `lambda_min(S_k)` decay at worst exponentially in the LEVEL `k`, not in the SUPPORT `a_k` (the latter would be the `e^{-4 pi x}` marginal wall #52 in octave clothing, voiding the certificate). Built on the M2.5/M2.6-validated blocks (`arch_block_bombieri` + `finite_block` + `pole_block`), run two ways: Part A on the genuine non-circular input-side form `M = A_arch + P_fin + B_pole` (no zeros; the actual certificate), Part B on the zero-side Gram (the 3J detector, circular but reaches higher support).

**Clause (i) CONFIRMED.** Every octave Schur complement is PSD (`In(S_k) = (+,0,0)`, `n_- = 0`) for zeta and chi_3 on the non-circular input-side form, and for zeta out to 7 octaves on the zero side. The Haynsworth identity holds EXACTLY at every octave (consistency gate passed) with `cond(G_{k-1}) <= ~90`, so the inertia counts are trustworthy. The telescoping skeleton is sound and computable.

**Clause (ii): NO octave-graded wall.** Zero-side zeta margins stay in `[0.014, 0.039]` across 7 octaves, support `b in [1.5, 2.8e9]` (a 64x span in log-support), with no exponential-in-support collapse: the smallest margin sits `~1e117` ABOVE a nominal `e^{-4 pi a_k}` wall at the top octave. Input-side zeta margins (`0.054/0.055/0.047/0.037/0.034` over 5 octaves) fit LEVEL `k` far better than SUPPORT `a_k` (`R^2` 0.98 vs 0.86). And the telescoping picture is exactly EDC's dossier prediction ("a self-reducing certificate telescopes a healthy per-octave positivity into a globally marginal one"): the global `min-eig(G_K)` is MARGINAL (`+0.007` zero side / `+0.023` input side) while every per-octave Schur margin is healthy (`O(0.01-0.05)`); the global marginality is the telescoped accumulation of healthy octaves, not any single octave being marginal.

**HONEST SCOPE (the load-bearing caveats; soft-detector freeze respected, no certified margin claimed).** (a) The zero-side flatness is nearly TAUTOLOGICAL under RH: with all zeros on the line the zero-side Gram is a sum of real rank-1 PSD outer products, so it is robustly PSD at every octave by construction; it shows scale/conditioning control, not positivity per se. The genuine test is the input side. (b) The input side (the actual non-circular certificate) CAPS at `n_oct = 5`: its prime sum runs to `n <= e^{2 a_max}` (`~5.3e4` at `a_max = 5.44`, but `~2.8e9` one octave further), so clause (ii) on the side that matters is only 4 Schur points, suggestive not decisive; that ceiling IS the feasibility wall, and whether the input-side octave margins stay bounded below as octaves grow is an ANALYTIC question, not a compute task. (c) The off-line BREAK (idea-4's promised integer discriminator, the octave-graded refinement of 3J's `schur_neg = #off-line heights`, #19) is NOT exhibited: D-H and Epstein d=47 stay fully PSD on both sides at `m = 3`/octave. This is a RESOLUTION limit, not a refutation: 3D.3 needed `K = 100-1000` to resolve D-H's off-line eigenvalues (a `-2.6%` effect), and the octave detector at `K = 15-21` is far below it; the input-side D-H PSD-ness is additionally the M2.6 stealth window (#34), and Epstein is untestable input-side (`a_1 = 0`).

**Verdict:** a falsification instrument that EDC passed. Clause (i) holds, the Haynsworth skeleton is exact and well-conditioned, and clause (ii) shows the encouraging direction (no support-wall; healthy per-octave margins telescoping into the global marginality). EDC stays a live PURSUE direction, sharpened: the open core is now precisely (b) the input-side margin lower bound past the feasibility ceiling (analytic) and (c) reproducing the off-line break at high octave resolution. The second of the three session-019 queued tests; the first (EDF, #77) also fired positive. ### 77. The EDF under-decimation discriminator (the cheapest decisive test of the session-019 Euler Decimation Flow survivor) fired POSITIVE on both sub-tests: zeta's Weil positivity is critically tied to the full Euler product via a low-frequency mechanism, and the single-prime center-fixed-point identity is exact.

The first of the three session-019 falsification experiments ([e3aa_edf_under_decimation.py](positivity/e3aa_edf_under_decimation.py), built on the validated M2.5/M2.6 Weil-form blocks `arch_block_bombieri` + `finite_block` + `pole_block`). It tests two EDF (Euler Decimation Flow) clauses. **Part A (Clause C, the critical decimation window):** on test functions of support `[-t/2, t/2]` the "matched" Weil form `M = A_arch + P_fin + B_pole` (full prime sum, `p^k <= e^t`) stays marginally POSITIVE (min-eig `+0.029 / +0.025 / +0.020` at `t = 8/9/10`, `K=16`), but DELETING the top octave of primes (keep only `p <= e^{t/2}`, the "under-decimated" form) collapses min-eig to `-46.0 / -77.7 / -128.7`, deepening monotonically with `t`. The negative eigenvector is the BROADEST (lowest-frequency) basis mode at every `t`, confirming the predicted MECHANISM: a prime `p` resonates at frequency `xi = 2pi/log p`, so the large primes (`log p` near the support edge) resonate at LOW frequency, exactly where the archimedean kernel is most negative (`Omega(0) ~ -5.4`); they are load-bearing for low-frequency positivity, and removing them exposes the trough. **Part B (Clause B + the single-prime companion):** the angular-mean-one identity `mean_phi (1-1/p)/|1-p^{-1/2}e^{i phi}|^2 = 1` (the EDF "zeta is a center fixed point" claim) is exact to `~1e-16` for `p=2,3,5`; the `p=2` block dip depth `log2((1/2)(1-2^{-1/2})^{-2}-1) = 3.3468` matches the closed form exactly; the single-prime symbol at the resonance `xi = 2pi/log2 = 9.0647` is `-2.98`, and a genuine compactly-supported `V_8` Hann-cosine packet realizes `-1.70`, both inside the predicted band `[-3.2,-1.0]`. **HONEST SCOPE (the load-bearing caveat):** a falsification instrument that came out positive, NOT a proof or a certificate, and it respects the soft-detector freeze (no certified margin is claimed; the matched marginal positivity sits near the float64/`e^{-4 pi x}` wall, #52). Crucially, D-H ALSO goes negative under decimation (`-6.0 / -1.8 / -2.2`), so "under-decimation breaks positivity" is NOT by itself a zeta-vs-D-H separator; the clean EULER-STRUCTURED content is the center-fixed-point identity and the single-prime resonance dip, which have NO D-H analogue (D-H has no Euler product, so no per-prime block: its `-L'/L` delocalizes onto all `n`, archimedean `Omega_DH(xi) = +1.98` with no dip). The result keeps EDF a live PURSUE direction and confirms its core bet (positivity is a fixed-point property of the full Euler product, with marginality the fingerprint of a center) at the discriminator level; the open core (flow well-posedness, the Clause-E Mobius-dressing SOS witness, and turning the matched marginal positivity into a proof) is unchanged. First of the three queued tests (TODO session 019); the LCC LP triple and the ECC spectral-leakage test remain. Cross-refs: #76 (the conjecture program), #52/#56 (the marginal wall the matched form sits on), #34 (the D-H stealth window the matched D-H `+0.058` reproduces).

### 76. A nine-lens first-principles conjecture program (session 019) produced five new RH attack SHAPES that survive the D-H discipline, two clean kills, and the unifying observation that every survivor reads marginal positivity as EXTREMALITY rather than as an obstacle.

A multi-agent run (nine BUILDER lenses, one ADVERSARY pass) was asked to be creative and propose genuinely new conjectural routes to RH, then kill the ones that fail the project's own discipline (D-H instantiability, the four-level framing, K1 non-circularity, the dead-branch list). Dossier: [first_principles_conjecture_program.md](../docs/03_research/first_principles_conjecture_program.md). **Five survive (PURSUE):** Lonely Crystal (LCC, the cone of positive log-crystals for zeta's source is a SINGLETON = von Mangoldt; provable D-H firewall via the negative archimedean density at `r=0` plus no pole; open core = composite-pinching / the ghost-crystal kill); Eratosthenes Descent (EDC, octave-nested Weil positivity with the EXACT Haynsworth identity `In(G_{k+1})=In(G_k)+In(S_k)`, a self-reducing Pi^0_1 certificate where the sieve is the formation rule D-H lacks); Euler Crystal (ECC, the primes as a literal Berry-Keating scatterer crystal with the boundary condition DERIVED from the adelic product formula, distinct from the killed postulated-Hamiltonian 1C because it CONSTRUCTS the operator from local factors; open core = E3 completeness + a K1 risk in the Krein-de Branges identification); Euler Facet Rigidity (EFR, the dual of LCC in coefficient space: the saturated face of the Weil cone is `{Lambda}`, extreme rays = primes; theorem-grade D-H exclusion on three layers); Euler Decimation Flow (EDF, zeta as a CENTER fixed point of a coefficient flow, with the angular-mean-one identity explaining WHY RH is barely true, and the sharpest falsifiable predictions: critical-window exponent 1, D-H Lyapunov exponent exactly `2*beta_1-1=0.617`). **Two kills (negative coordinates):** Semicircular Polarization (free probability) is PROVEN dead by a constant: the free-convolution edge grows as `~2*sqrt(2)*log X` (Mertens) while the archimedean curvature grows as `~(1/2pi)*log T` at matched `T=X`, a ratio `~4*pi*sqrt(2)~18 >> 1`, so its central inequality is false and salvage collapses to RH itself. Adelic Ferromagnet (Lee-Yang, p-adic Teichmuller spins) is a #42 restatement: a factored product measure averages out the cross-place phase coherence and cannot reach the continuation `Re(s)<1` where the zeros live (Newman's classical no-go is the shadow). **Two reshapes:** Habiro Ladder (a Galois-Liouville `q->1` limit; load-bearing gap is the covolume `phi(N)^{1/2}` vs stealth decay `N^{-c}`, needs `c>1/2` unestablished) and Arithmetic Seifert Flow (RH as "the prime link is algebraically fibered", an INTEGER signature-flow identity; underspecified at its linking blocks and `det=|xi|^2` claim, with an unresolved amphichirality risk). **The pattern:** the `+0.035` residue and `370x` cancellation (#52/#56) become the predicted fingerprint of an extreme point, a center fixed point, or a rigid crystal, none of which can be marginally true by accident. Two high-value merges: LCC+EFR (one classification, spectral and arithmetic faces, shared composite-pinching core) and EDC+EDF (the exact Haynsworth skeleton for the richer flow). Three cheap falsification tests named (LCC LP triple `e3x_lonely_crystal_lp.py`; EDF under-decimation discriminator extending `e3c2`; ECC spectral-leakage `e1x_euler_crystal.py`). **Honest scope:** no theorem, no gap crossed; every survivor has an RH-equivalent clause (attainment / cone-membership) marked as such, and the contribution is the separable unconditional half plus the new proof topology, not a reduction in difficulty (the #73 caveat). ### 75. Two of the five no-go classes are now MACHINE-VERIFIED in Lean (sorry-free, axiom-clean): Class A (the Sen-module obstruction, UNCONDITIONAL in any positive-definite cup form via the CFC square root) and Class C (trace-blindness: no `t`-blind signature decides Hasse-Weil).

**Class A** ([lean/ZetaRH/SenDefiniteObstruction.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/SenDefiniteObstruction.lean), 5 theorems; `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` only). `skew_nilpotent_eq_zero`: a real skew-symmetric nilpotent matrix is zero, by the ELEMENTARY trace identity `tr(N^T N) = -tr(N^2) = 0` (no spectral theorem; this is the compact-group/definite-form kernel of #72/08D made into a one-screen proof). `sen_no_nilpotent_part` / `sen_nilpotent_part_not_isNilpotent`: the standard-frame Sen no-go. `bskew_nilpotent_eq_zero_of_gram`: the obstruction for any Gram factor `B = S^T S` with `S` invertible. `bskew_nilpotent_eq_zero`: the UNCONDITIONAL version, for ANY positive-definite real cup form `B` a `B`-skew nilpotent `M` vanishes, with the factor CONSTRUCTED as `S = CFC.sqrt B` (continuous functional calculus), not assumed. So `nu != 0` (Petrov) => no positive-definite invariant cup form holds for an arbitrary definite `B`, not merely the orthonormal frame. **Class C** ([lean/ZetaRH/TraceBlindObstruction.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/TraceBlindObstruction.lean), axiom-clean). `negDef_depends_on_trace`: the Hasse-Weil/Rosati `NegDef g q t` predicate is non-constant in the trace `t` (true at `t=0`, false at `t=2` for `g=q=1`). `no_trace_blind_signature`: NO predicate `P g q` of the curve data alone (with no slot for `t`) can decide `NegDef g q t`; the arithmetic-blind AHK combinatorial signature is exactly such a `t`-blind invariant (#48), so it cannot carry RH-positivity. Built against the existing `negDef_iff_hasseWeil` in `HodgeIndex.lean`. **Honest scope (both):** the LINEAR-ALGEBRA / LOGICAL kernel is machine-checked; the modeling onto the actual Bhatt-Lurie prismatic Sen operator (A) and the matroid Chow ring (C) is the paper argument (#72/#48), not formalized. Both are K5-clean CONDITIONAL no-gos: they prune the archimedean-Sen and combinatorial-signature polarization sources and relocate the demand to the Frobenius/F half; neither disproves RH. VERIFIER targets #ClassA-1, #ClassC-1.

### 74. The conditional no-go program (08E): the disprove direction is sound only as CONDITIONAL no-gos (kill criterion K5), and the deepest candidate theorem (the K2 pincer) is a COMPASS, not a wall, because the target object straddles both halves by construction.

The logical landmine: joint-realizability of the four gap properties IMPLIES RH (by the function-field template), but RH => joint-realizability is OPEN (= "does RH have a motivic/cohomological proof"). So an UNCONDITIONAL "the four are not jointly realizable" would prove RH FALSE. **K5** (new kill criterion, [08E_no_go_program.md](../docs/03_research/research_directions/08E_no_go_program.md)): a no-go is valid only if its construction class `C` is provably STRICTLY SMALLER than "all constructions" (it must name a smallness witness outside `C` and a relocation target). The ledger: Class A (source from the archimedean Sen module) = THEOREM; Class C (combinatorial AHK form) = THEOREM for fixed type; Class D (de Branges kernel) = REFUTED (Conrey-Li, positive density ~6-13%, #43); Class B (truncated determinant/trace) = STRONG EVIDENCE only (the stealth-window/soft-detector wall #18-20; the unbounded version is K5-malformed); Class E (the Frobenius/F-half ample class) = the LIVE BOUNDARY = the universal gap itself, never targeted (a full-E no-go would disprove RH). **The decisive ADVERSARY finding:** the K2 pincer (the discriminating positivity must ride the Euler/F half to separate zeta from D-H, yet must reach the off-line zeros which only the archimedean continuation reaches) obstructs the SOFT/LOCAL attack (this is WHY every soft detector failed) but NOT the target object, which straddles by construction: over `F_q` one object, the Frobenius correspondence on `C x C`, fuses `t`-carrying (its self-intersection) and zero-reaching (Rosati positivity over all eigenvalues) in a single correspondence. The straddler PROVABLY exists over `F_q`; its existence over `Z` is exactly RH. So the no-gos corral the answer from strictly inside and cannot close E. Net: "RH needs a structure not yet invented" is slightly too strong (the structure is invented in outline, the `F_q` straddler); the SOUND statement is "RH cannot be proved by any half-specific, local, combinatorial, or kernel shortcut; it requires constructing the global product straddler (`Spec(Z) x Spec(Z)` + `Gamma_S` + the arithmetic Hodge index) over Z." K5-clean throughout; nothing here disproves RH.

### 73. A purpose-built primitive system for RH plus a DuckDB lemma-graph confirm, by a third independent route (after #30 and the 15-candidate scorecard), that the 17 Spec(Z) cohomology candidates COLLAPSE onto one gap node, and the gap is the CONJUNCTION of four proven-droppable properties.

The bespoke axiomatic system ([rh_primitive_system.md](../docs/03_research/rh_primitive_system.md)) makes the SIGNATURE the primitive: the Rosati trace form `B(x,y)=Tr(x y^dagger)` is the core object, the trace `Tr` is demoted to a supporting (realization-side, D-H-buildable) functional, and RH reduces to ONE open axiom `AX-POL` (= the arithmetic Hodge standard conjecture = 08A M4). D-H-unbuildability is a property of the PRIMITIVES (no Euler product => the Frobenius algebra `A` does not exist), mirroring `lean/ZetaRH/FrobeniusAlgebra.lean`. Honest verdict: a reduction in CONFUSION, not difficulty (`AX-POL` over Z is equivalent to RH). The DuckDB lemma-graph ([experiments/lemma_db/](../experiments/lemma_db/)) operationalizes the project's epistemics as queries: a load-bearing (`depends_on`/`specializes`) vs annotation edge split, with `build_db.py` exiting NON-ZERO if any `dh_buildable` content node lies on a load-bearing path to `TGT-rh` (the D-H discipline as a CI gate). The candidate import forced all 17 candidates from [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md) into the graph and decomposed the gap node into four INDEPENDENT properties: PROP-global, PROP-carries-trace (the Frobenius/von-Mangoldt `t`), PROP-rh-equivalent (not de-Branges-stronger), PROP-noncircular (K1). RESULT: 9 realization candidates collapse onto the single gap node `TGT-m4-hodge-standard`; 4 are off-to-side proven-signature brackets each dropping EXACTLY ONE property (Faltings-Hriljac drops global, AHK drops carries-trace, de Branges drops rh-equivalent and is refuted, the Connes trace-formula family drops noncircular); 2 are pre-realization; 2 are the K1 wall. The gap is the conjunction of all four; each is individually proven-droppable; no proven object has all four; an object with all four proves RH. For the LITERAL target, global and carries-trace fuse into one datum (the product + `Gamma_S`, #70/2LO), so the two live near-misses (AHK trace-injection, FH product-construction) are two faces of the SAME object, which is precisely why the diagnosis is collapse and not a soft seam.

### 72. A compact-group theorem closes the "polarize the Sen module" branch and ANSWERS the 2KK open probe: a non-semisimple Sen operator (Petrov nu!=0) admits NO positive-definite invariant cup form, so #66 (hyperbolic) and #68 (free transport) are its ONLY non-trivial outcomes; positivity is never sourced by the archimedean Theta, only imported, = the arithmetic ample class on the Frobenius/F half (K2), = RH.

2SEN ([e2sen_polarization_obstruction.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2sen_polarization_obstruction.py), writeup [08D_sen_nonsemisimplicity_obstruction.md](../docs/03_research/research_directions/08D_sen_nonsemisimplicity_obstruction.md)) resolves the genuine Petrov question the 2KK ADVERSARY flagged as untested ("can an intrinsic positive cup form survive a non-semisimple Sen module?"). **The theorem (one line):** the Tate-equivariant cup duality `Theta^T B + B Theta = -w B` says `Theta + (w/2)I` is in `so(B)`; if `B` were positive definite then `O(B)` is COMPACT, so `so(B)` is semisimple, forcing `Theta` semisimple, i.e. `nu=0`. Contrapositive: `nu!=0` (Petrov) => NO positive-definite invariant cup form. **The trichotomy (computed, size-3 Jordan blocks, `numpy` solve of the homogeneous Sylvester):** self-paired Tate-center (`2mu=-w`) gives solution dim 2, inertia `(1,2)`, NO definite element (sign imported = #68); cross-paired (`mu1+mu2=-w`) gives dim 3, inertia `(3,3)` HYPERBOLIC (wrong polarity = #66); non-resonant gives `B=0` (the 2KK "B=0" = non-resonant placement). The semisimple control `Theta=-1/2 I_3` (`nu=0`) has a 6-dim form space CONTAINING the definite identity `(3,0,0)`; turning on `nu` collapses it to a 2-dim family with no definite element. **So #66 and #68 are the only two non-trivial outcomes, and the reason is the compact-group theorem: a definite outcome is forbidden by linear algebra the instant `nu!=0`.** Routable (monodromy-weight filtration `M(nu)`, CKS form `Q_l(x,y)=Q(x,nu^l y)` on primitives) hence NOT fatal to 08B, but the route IMPORTS the positive `Q` (no upstream VHS over `Spec(Z)` => import = RH). **K2 control in the same run (>=40 digits):** FE residual `~1.8e-43` (zeta) and `~1.1e-40` (D-H) => the Sen/archimedean/FE block is rung-(ii)-free for BOTH, so Petrov's `Theta`-obstruction is K2-blind by type; only the Euler/F half separates them (zeta `-zeta'/zeta` = von Mangoldt on prime powers; D-H `a(n)` non-multiplicative, `a(6)=+1` vs `a(2)a(3)=-0.0807`, no Euler product => polarizable algebra uninhabited for D-H by type). **Verdict:** answers 2KK = NO intrinsic positive cup form; lands at M4 as a negative coordinate that PRUNES "obtain the polarization by fixing/decomposing the Sen grading" and REDIRECTS the positivity input to an arithmetic ample/Lefschetz class on the Euler-pole `H^2` (the `F` half), which Petrov's theorem leaves untouched. K1 PASS (structural, no zeros used). Sharpens 08B step (4)(b): it must be rephrased on `M(nu)`, not on `Theta`-eigenspaces.

### 71. No recent construction supplies the global signed trace pairing carrying `t` (2P+): Tang's prismatic Poincare duality gives PERFECTNESS not the Hodge-Riemann/Rosati sign, Gurney's prismatization over Z gives the global SUBSTRATE not the cup/sign/`t`, and the Connes-Consani 2026 Jacobian gives the TRACE/realization not a signed product pairing. The three candidates are three different two-thirds of the SAME construction, each missing the sign that carries the Frobenius trace `t`.

2P+ ([2P_recent_global_signed_trace_pairing_probe.md](arithmetic_geometric/2P_recent_global_signed_trace_pairing_probe.md)) is the literature-grounded Direction 8 audit after 2LN (#69) and 2LO (#70) reduced the WCart route to one open datum: the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed trace pairing carrying `t = q+1-#E`, equivalently the product-surface correspondence `Gamma_S`. It scores the three strongest recent constructions against exactly the (iii)-polarization column (a signed pairing on the global `H^1` whose value sees `t`, not just the `(1,p)` bidegree), using the D-H discipline as a real-time classifier (a construction is RH-relevant iff it CANNOT be built for D-H: no Euler product => no Frobenius => no `t`). **Tang** (*Syntomic cycle classes and prismatic Poincare duality*, [arXiv:2210.14279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.14279), Compositio): constructs F-gauges, syntomic cycle classes, and proves perfect prismatic Poincare duality `RGamma(X)^vee ~= RGamma(X){d}[2d]` with a diagonal copairing and trace maps. This is ingredient (ii) (a perfect cup into a fundamental class) at the complex level with NO positivity: perfectness, not the sign (the entire gap per 2HH/#61). **Gurney** (*Prismatization over Z*, [arXiv:2301.12392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12392)): the global integral prismatization substrate over `Spec(Z)` with perfect cohomology under proper smooth hypotheses, but no cup form / signed pairing / trace formula carrying `t`: the substrate in which a future theorem lives, not the theorem. **Connes-Consani 2026** (*On the Jacobian of $\overline{\mathrm{Spec}\,\mathbb{Z}}$*, [arXiv:2602.15941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15941)): Picard/Jacobian monoids, spectral realization, Weil's explicit formula reinterpreted as a trace formula; the same trace-side landing every NCG framework hits (R3.5: trace positivity <=> RH, the K1 wall, #65), not a signed product pairing. **Net:** a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate. None supplies the signed `t`-carrying pairing; they stop at perfectness (Tang), the global substrate (Gurney), and the trace/realization (Connes-Consani). The post-2LO gap is unchanged and is the SAME theorem for all three: construct `Gamma_S`, form the primitive cup into the Euler-pole `H^2`, and prove the Hodge-Riemann/Rosati sign without RH input. Confirms the universal-gap reading of [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md): every candidate realizes the trace or a perfect pairing; none carries the polarization; supplying it IS RH. **Honest scope:** a literature classification (Tang web-confirmed; Gurney and Connes-Consani consistent with the repo-verified landscape entries checked against the originals); proves nothing about RH; the deliverable is the per-candidate naming of which third of the construction each lacks and that they are the same theorem. ### 70. The Euler Weil operator polar formula `C_E = A_E(-A_E^2)^{-1/2}` recovers finite-field Rosati positivity AFTER `B_E` is supplied, but cannot construct `B_E` from `(1,p)` bidegrees plus archimedean data (2LO): the bidegree `(1,q)` is shared by all curves over `F_q`, while the Frobenius trace `t = q+1-#E(F_q)` is strictly finer, so the non-circular inputs underdetermine `B_E`. The sign gap is the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed trace pairing, equivalently the product-surface / prismatic Poincare-duality assembly.

2LO ([e2lo_euler_weil_operator.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2lo_euler_weil_operator.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2lo_euler_weil_operator.md)) is the follow-up to 2LN (#69): 2LN relocated the WCart positivity gap to the SIGN of the primitive monodromy form, fixed by the archimedean Weil operator `C_E`; 2LO tries to BUILD `C_E` from the construction target in [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md), `A_E := Omega^{-1} B_E`, `C_E := A_E (-A_E^2)^{-1/2}`. **Part 1 (function-field specialization):** over `F_q` with `B_E = [[2g,t],[t,2gq]]` and `Omega` symplectic, `A_E^2 = (t^2-4g^2 q) I`, so `-A_E^2` is positive definite iff `t^2 < 4g^2 q`; then `C_E` is an `Omega`-compatible complex structure (`C_E^2 = -I` to `7e-15`) and `Q(x,y)=Omega(x,C_E y)` is positive iff `t^2 < 4g^2 q` (206/206 rows over `g in {1,2}`, `q in {5,7,11,13}`, 0 mismatches; reduces to `HodgeIndex.negDef_iff_hasseWeil`). The polar formula WORKS. **Part 2 (the K1 audit, the crux):** the `(1,q)` bidegree is shared by every curve over `F_q`, while `t` is strictly finer; nine admissible traces over `F_5` give nine distinct `C_E`, so the bidegree + archimedean data DO NOT determine `t`/`B_E`. K1 fails CONSTRUCTIVELY: the only non-circular source of `t` is the global Frobenius point count, the product-surface / prismatic-duality assembly (Direction 8). **Part 3 (kill condition 2):** an FE/archimedean-only `C_E` forms for D-H (which has a functional equation), hence is D-H-blind and dead; the discriminating content must ride the Euler/Frobenius side. **Part 4 (K2 / D-H discipline):** the data-based `has_euler_product` guard blocks D-H and a renamed D-H; the injected off-line zero gives the 2HH/2JJ defect `|1-2 beta| ~ 0.617` (#61/#63). **Audit:** basis invariance under symplectic-compatible conjugation holds (max transform residual `2.6e-13`). **Verdict:** a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate. The polar `C_E` construction is sound and transports a supplied `B` exactly, but does not construct the arithmetic `B` geometrically; the residual M4 sign gap IS the global Frobenius trace `t`, the same object the bidegree cannot see, equivalently the product surface. Confirms the 2MM (#68) K1 burden with the precise missing piece named. **Honest scope:** finite linear algebra plus a degree-of-freedom count, not absolute prismatic cohomology; proves nothing about RH. Cross-refs: #69 (2LN, which relocated the gap to the sign that 2LO tries to supply), #68 (2MM transport / K1 burden), #25/#42/#65 (the `(1,p)` bidegree underdetermines the point count `t`), #61/#63 (the `0.617` polarization defect), 08A M4 / Direction 8 (the product surface = the same gap).

### 69. Petrov non-semisimplicity is ROUTABLE but UNHELPFUL on the primitive quotient (2LN): the defective Sen nilpotent dies on the monodromy primitive quotient by Jacobson-Morozov, so non-semisimplicity is NOT the wall, but the positivity gap relocates undiminished to the SIGN of the primitive monodromy form `Q_k`, which the archimedean Weil operator `C_E` (not `N`) fixes. The direct Hermitian Rosati/Lyapunov metric fails on the full nonsemisimple block yet exists on the semisimple quotient; the missing datum is the geometric Frobenius/Lefschetz operator supplying the primitive decomposition, not `Theta`.

2LN ([e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.md), proposal [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md)) is the only WCart direct-polarization escape hatch left after #67/#68. It builds a faithful defective Sen model from `sl2` irreps (genuine Jordan blocks, fixing the 2KK defect where the "Jordan block" collapsed to a relabeled diagonal): `N` the lowering operator, `Theta = -1/2 I + N` at the Tate center, the cup form `Omega` solving `Theta^T Omega + Omega Theta = -Omega` (residual `0`), the monodromy weight filtration `W(N)`, and the primitive forms `Q_k(x,y) = c_E * Omega(x, N^k y)`. **Part 1 (the 2LM direct metric):** the Hermitian Rosati/Lyapunov metric `Theta^* H + H Theta = -H, H > 0` is feasible iff `A = Theta + 1/2 I = N` is similar to skew-Hermitian (diagonalizable, purely imaginary spectrum); a genuine nilpotent is not diagonalizable (eigvec cond `inf`), so NO positive `H` exists on the full block. **Part 2 (Jacobson-Morozov erasure):** the same-weight endomorphism action of `N` on the primitive graded quotient is `0.0e+00` (N lowers weight by 2, hence the zero endomorphism on each graded piece), and the Lyapunov metric on the semisimple quotient operator IS feasible. So the primitive quotient erases the Petrov nilpotent for free. **Part 3 (the residual gap is the sign):** `Q_k` is `+definite` for `c_E=+1` and `-definite` for `c_E=-1` with identical `|defect|`; the polarization sign is unconstrained by `N`, governed by the archimedean `C_E`, and IS the unmoved M4 / arithmetic Hodge-standard-conjecture gap. **Part 4 (Outcome-3 control):** with a misaligned (non-canonical) nilpotent `L` the Petrov nilpotent SURVIVES inside `ker L` (`||N|ker|| ~ 0.29` vs `0` for the `N`-aligned choice), isolating the missing datum as the geometric Frobenius/Lefschetz operator, not `Theta` alone. Robust on block patterns `(3,)`, `(4,)`, `(2,2)`. **Audit A-E all pass:** sign convention (`-Omega` is the cup), pure cup symmetry type matching block parity, derivation nullspace dim equals the Tate-dual Jordan matching count `sum_{a,b} min(m_a,m_b)`, basis invariance under random conjugation, and the D-H discipline (formation guard blocks D-H; an injected off-line zero at `beta ~ 0.8085` gives polarization defect `|1-2 beta| ~ 0.617`, reproducing #61/#63). **Verdict:** WCart stays ALIVE (the direct route is not closed by non-semisimplicity), but the primitive quotient buys NO positivity; the next task is geometric (construct the Frobenius/Lefschetz operator and the archimedean `C_E` sign), exactly the #68 M4 burden restated on the primitive quotient. **Honest scope:** finite linear algebra on a faithful `sl2`/Jordan model, not absolute prismatic cohomology; the erasure is structural (Jacobson-Morozov) and the sign is supplied as a phase, not derived; proves nothing about RH. A sharpening NEGATIVE-as-coordinate that resolves the Petrov question and relocates the gap. Cross-refs: #66 (2KK wrong polarity, which asked for higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star positivity), #67 (2LL survival), #68 (2MM transport / K1 burden), #61/#63 (the `0.617` polarization defect), 08A M4 / [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md) (the universal polarization gap).

### 68. The Euler-Sen finite formalism is a TRANSPORT theorem, not a positivity theorem (2MM): `Q_N(x,y)=Omega(x,N y)` has exactly the signature of the supplied symmetric form `B`, for arbitrary `B`. This sharpens the K1 caveat on #67.

2MM ([e2mm_euler_sen_k1_audit.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2mm_euler_sen_k1_audit.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2mm_euler_sen_k1_audit.md)) adversarially audits the Euler-Sen proposal after #67. It keeps the same Tate-centered defective block (`Theta=-1/2 I+N`, `N^2=0`) and exact cup equation, but replaces the function-field Rosati input by arbitrary real symmetric matrices with every nondegenerate signature in dimensions `1..6`, plus singular controls. **Result:** across `32` rows, `signature(Q_N)=signature(B)` with `0` transport mismatches; the 2KK diagonal-star baseline has the expected rigid hyperbolic signature `(rank B, rank B, 2 nullity B)` with `0` mismatches. Function-field anchors agree: `q=5,t=4` gives `(2,0,0)` and `q=5,t=5` gives `(1,1,0)`, exactly because those are the signatures of the supplied `B_E`. The D-H discipline is also repaired at toy-Python level: the formation guard reads `L.has_euler_product`, so zeta/chi3/chi4 form and both Davenport-Heilbronn and a renamed D-H wrapper fail before the cup package forms. **Verdict:** #67 survives as a coherent monodromy slot, but it creates no positivity. The full M4 burden is unchanged and now stated without ambiguity: construct the arithmetic `B` geometrically from Euler/Frobenius data plus the archimedean metric, then prove `B` is positive on primitive pieces without zero-side or RH input. Importing `B` from a Weil form or known zeros would be K1-circular.

### 67. The Euler-Sen monodromy primitive form SURVIVES the first cheap kill (2LL): a genuine defective Sen block placed at the Tate center `-1/2` satisfies the exact cup equation and turns the alternating cup form into the Euler/Rosati matrix via `Q_N(x,y)=Omega(x,N y)`, escaping 2KK's rigid hyperbolic diagonal-star trap in the function-field specialization.

2LL ([e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.md), proposal [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md)) executes the first falsification attempt against the new Euler-Sen proposal. Model: for the function-field/Rosati matrix `B_E(g,q,t) = [[2g,t],[t,2gq]]`, build `H = P_top + N(P_top)`, `N(top_i)=lower_i`, `Omega(top_i,lower_j)=B_E[i,j]`, and `Theta=-1/2 I+N`. This is genuinely non-semisimple (`rank N=2`, `N^2=0`, geometric/algebraic multiplicity `2/4`) and still satisfies the cup-target derivation equation exactly (`Theta^T Omega + Omega Theta = -Omega`, residual `0.000e+00`) because the defective block sits at the Tate center. **Result:** the primitive monodromy form `top^T Omega N top` equals `B_E`; across `q={5,7,11}`, genus `1`, and integer traces around the Hasse-Weil window, positivity matches `t^2 < 4g^2q` with `0` mismatches. Representative `q=5`: `t=4` gives primitive signature `(2,0,0)` positive, `t=5` gives `(1,1,0)` indefinite; the 2KK-style diagonal-star baseline is rigidly hyperbolic `(2,2,0)` in both cases. **Verdict:** SURVIVES AS FORMALISM, NOT AS PROOF. It shows the right operation is monodromy primitive polarization, not a diagonal Hodge-star on the cup form, and it recovers the known function-field Rosati/Hasse-Weil sign after the 2G sign flip. **Critical caveat:** `B_E` is supplied as input; defining `B_E` geometrically over `Spec(Z)` from Euler/Frobenius data and the archimedean metric, without zero-side or RH input, remains exactly the M4 gap. K1 risk is therefore live, not discharged. K2 is represented by a data guard (`has_euler_product=False` blocks formation), now aligned with the Lean `FrobeniusAlgebra.CanFormCupTarget` guard, but still toy-level.

### 66. The WCart Hodge-star route to organ (a) is WRONG POLARITY at rank 2 (2KK): the trial Hodge-star polarization of the alternating cup form on the truncated diffracted Hodge complex is unconditionally hyperbolic (signature (1,1) on its rank-2 support) for every Hodge-star phase and every K in 2..32, so it can never flip to flag an off-line zero (the AHK pattern #48 again). A real RH-relevant polarization needs higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star (Frobenius-weighted trace) positivity.

2KK ([e2kk_wcart_finite_model.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2kk_wcart_finite_model.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2kk_wcart_finite_model.md)), built by a BUILDER agent and then re-run and audited by an ADVERSARY agent during the "where do we go from here" deliverables workflow (2026-06-04). It is the FIRST WCart probe to actually form the cup pairing as a matrix and read its signature (2PR.1/2DD only located the trace-halves as determinants). Model: a finite truncation of the Bhatt-Lurie diffracted Hodge complex, graded weights n=0..K, Sen operator Θ acting by -n (BL 3.5.8); the alternating Θ-compatible cup form B solved from the Sylvester equation Θ^T B + B Θ = -B (H^2 = C(-1), Tate weight -1); trial polarization Q = B^H · (Hodge-star phase i^w). **Robust result:** the signature is (1,1,K-1) across all four Θ rows and stable for every phase and every K (2..32) -- unconditionally hyperbolic on the rank-2 support, hence wrong polarity (like the convex-Hodge/AHK signature #48, it cannot detect an off-line zero). **The defensible takeaway:** a rank-2 alternating cup on a SINGLE weight pair is hyperbolic by linear algebra alone; organ (a) therefore cannot be carried by a Hodge-star on the obvious cup form, and a genuine RH-relevant polarization needs either higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star (Frobenius-weighted trace) positivity. **Methodological catch (ADVERSARY, three defects, all flagged in the .py STATUS banner and the .md correction blocks):** (1) Gate 1 "D-H non-formation" is a STRING MATCH on L.name, not a structural test -- the cup form is built from Θ(K) alone, so the L-function never enters the matrix and zeta/D-H would coincide if both reached the solver (the K2 exclusion is hand-imposed, correctly motivated but not discovered by the model); (2) the Petrov non-semisimplicity crux (arXiv:2302.11389, the actual content of the probe) is NOT exercised -- theta_jordan collapses to a relabeled diagonal, no genuine defective Jordan block is built, and a real one DESTROYS the cup form (B=0); (3) the off-line polarity probe is INVALID -- the off-line Θ has no exact null vector, so the solver silently returned a non-cup-form (derivation residual 1.82 vs 0.0 on-line). **Honest scope:** the wrong-polarity conclusion is solid as linear algebra on a rank-2 truncation, NOT a theorem about absolute prismatic cohomology; it closes the cheap Hodge-star finite model, not the real object. Retained as a recorded NEGATIVE coordinate (do not re-run; do not cite the three broken gates). The genuine open question Petrov poses -- can an intrinsic positive cup form survive a non-semisimple Sen module -- remains untested and is the next honest WCart probe if the route is pursued.

### 65. The soft-max de-idempotentization does NOT restore the Frobenius trace t on the Connes-Consani square, so the C-C signed-pairing handle on Direction 8 is closed; the better handle for milestone M4 is the Bhatt-Lurie WCart substrate (Frobenius F + Sen Θ on one stack).

2CC.4 ([e2cc4_adversary_probe.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc4_adversary_probe.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc4_adversary_probe.md)), generated and run by an ADVERSARY agent during the "where could we move the needle" workflow (2026-06-04), then reviewed and re-run by hand. Tests the #42 follow-up speculation that the finite-β soft-max (which removes the characteristic-1 idempotency, scalar defect log2/β ≠ 0) could, carried into the bilinear form, restore the trace t the β=∞ mixed-volume form froze at Δ·Γ = p−1 (vs the function-field target Δ·Γ = q+1−t, t roaming the Hasse-Weil window). **Three honest readings** of "soft-deform the pairing": R1 (soft|det| in each Gram entry), R2 (smooth the polygon corners, recompute exact Euclidean mixed area), R3 (is there ANY free knob at fixed p?). **Result:** both R1 and R2 recover the frozen p−1 at β=∞; at finite β, Δ·Γ(β,p) is a SINGLE deterministic number per (β,p) with no independent parameter (R1 deviations vanish as p grows -- pure scalar softening; R2 deviations are the perimeter/area inflation, monotone in p). No reading ever roams the Hasse-Weil window at fixed p. **Verdict: t is NOT restored.** The combinatorial area pairing is a determinant |det(u,v)| of lattice direction vectors, which has no t to begin with: t is a point count q+1−t, strictly finer than the (1,p) bidegree (#25), and four genus-1 curves over F_5 share identical slope data. Soft-max cannot refine a bidegree into a point count. **Consequence:** closes the soft-max route to the missing signed pairing for the whole C-C-square family, which is why the needle-map routes M4's most attackable handle to WCart (F = Euler/von Mangoldt half, Θ = Γ-divisor and a proven derivation, cohomological dimension 1, Tang's prismatic Poincaré duality to build on), not the C-C signed pairing. **Honest scope:** R1/R2 decisive computations on the divisor shadow (the e2cc machinery), R3 a degree-of-freedom count; falsifies the SPECIFIC soft-max mechanism, not every conceivable de-idempotentization; proves nothing about RH; a pruned Tier-2 handle.

### 64. RH is a Π⁰₁ sentence, so undecidability is a BACK DOOR to truth, not an escape: a proof that RH is independent of ZFC would itself prove RH true. The Lean substrate now carries the Π⁰₁ kernel witness, and the need for ever-sharper effective bounds at the tight (highly composite) n -- with no uniform soft bound covering all n -- is the formalization-layer shadow of marginal positivity.

A logical-status excursion (doc [rh_logical_status.md](../docs/03_research/rh_logical_status.md), Lean [RHEquivalences.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/RHEquivalences.lean)), adversarially verified by two multi-agent workflows. **The lever (PROVEN):** RH is provably equivalent to a Π⁰₁ arithmetic sentence (Robin 1984 / Lagarias 2002 / the DPRM Diophantine form), so (i) a false RH has a finite checkable counterexample, hence is refutable already in Robinson arithmetic Q -- the engine is Σ₁-COMPLETENESS, not Σ₁-soundness (the usual misattribution of the slogan); (ii) therefore irrefutability ⟹ truth, so "prove RH undecidable" is not a third outcome beside true/false: succeeding at it would PROVE RH true, in a metatheory forced to be Con(ZFC)-strong by Gödel II. (iii) Forcing provably cannot show RH independent (it does not change ω, hence not arithmetic truth); large cardinals CAN prove new Π⁰₁ truths so could in principle prove RH, but cannot exhibit it as two-sided independent. The dead corners (independence-as-a-program, Chaitin "true for no reason" which Chaitin himself disclaims, busy-beaver state-count records, program-size bit counts) carry no metamathematical content; the only genuinely open technical core is the reverse-math strength of the zero-counting step N(T) (bracketed WKL₀-up-to-ACA₀). **Lean (8 sorry-free anchors, all `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`):** RHEquivalences.lean defines Robin/Lagarias/Mertens/Li-Keiper/Nyman-Beurling as Props over `zeta` and `RH_arith := lagariasInequality` (the Π⁰₁ surrogate); proves the definitional iff, the Mathlib bridge, `rh_arith_refutable` (¬RH_arith ↔ ∃ one refuting n -- the Σ⁰₁ "no false-but-unrefutable" dual made formal), kernel-computable σ at 6/12, and the Lagarias inequality at n=1 (equality, the reason the criterion is ≤ not strict <), n=3 (order-1 effective bounds: e^{11/6}≥e·(11/6), log(11/6)≥5/11), and n=2 (the hardest small case, needing integer-vs-rational power comparisons e^{3/2}≥4 from (e^{3/2})²=e³=(e¹)³≥(27/10)³, log(3/2)≥2/5 from (e^{2/5})⁵=e²≤(68/25)²≤(3/2)⁵). **The marginal-positivity shadow (corrected):** n=2 needs sharper bounds than n=3 because its margin (0.32) is smaller than n=3's (1.62) -- the margins are NON-monotone in n (0, 0.32, 1.62, 0.98, 4.38 for n=1..5), and the genuinely tight cases are highly composite n (n=12 margin ~0.01, where σ(n) is large). No UNIFORM soft bound covers all n, because that would be proving RH; that, not a spurious "escalating with n" trend, is the real formalization-layer trace of marginal positivity. **Honest scope:** the five deep equivalences (Robin/Lagarias/Mertens/Li/NB ⟺ RH) are documented sorries (#RB-1/#LG-1/#MT-1/#LI-1/#NB-1), none dischargeable without proving RH; this is a SIDE excursion that sharpens the target (no undecidability escape, soft arguments structurally barred, reinforcing the Level-4 commitment) but supplies no polarization. Distinct from ExplicitFormula.lean's Weil-form criterion (#EF-2).

### 63. Brick 3 (capstone): the D-H discipline upgrades from D-H-EXCLUDED to D-H-AWARE. The duality-vs-polarization defect D(gamma)=|1-2beta| is a D-H-AWARE detector: identically 0 for zeta (25 zeros, RH) and a 0.617 SPIKE at D-H's off-line zero gamma~85.7, flat on D-H's 35 on-line zeros. The #34 stealth window is a RESOLUTION COST (resolving height gamma needs primes to e^gamma; e^{85.7}~1e37), NOT intrinsic blindness: the exact form is D-H-aware, only the reachable reconstruction is blind.

2JJ ([e2jj_dh_aware_detector.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2jj_dh_aware_detector.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2jj_dh_aware_detector.md)) executes brick 3 of state_of_candidate_ABF, closing the brainstorm's mistake (iii): the D-H discipline ("if it fires for D-H, discard") is a sufficient SAFETY filter but too blunt to CHARACTERIZE correctness -- the right object should APPLY to D-H and correctly output its OFF-LINE zeros while giving zeta its ON-LINE zeros. Using 2HH's (#61) handle, the defect D(rho)=|（1-rho)-rho-bar|=|1-2beta| as a function of HEIGHT is the D-H-aware detector. **Results:** Part 1: D(gamma) identically 0 for zeta (25 zeros to T=90, all on line); for D-H, 0 on its 35 on-line zeros (to T=60) and a SPIKE to 0.617 at the off-line zero gamma~85.7 (beta~0.8085) -- the detector LOCATES the off-line obstruction, does not fail to see it. Part 2: the stealth window (#34) is a RESOLUTION cost -- resolving height gamma via the non-circular prime side needs test functions of frequency ~gamma, hence primes up to e^gamma; e^{85.7}~1.6e37 primes, unreachable, while reachable b<=6 resolves heights only to log(b^2)~3.58 (below the first zeta zero at 14.1). So the EXACT form (from zeros) sees the off-line zero for free; the reconstruction provably cannot. The stealth window is the e^gamma cost, NOT intrinsic blindness of the Weil form to D-H. Part 3: the reframed discipline -- OLD "is it silent on D-H?" -> NEW "does it give each L its TRUE zero locus?"; the exact Weil form / D(gamma) passes (D-H-aware: spikes at D-H off-line, flat for zeta), the cohomological reason being 2HH/2GG (on the line FE-partner = conjugate = cup product is a polarization; D-H's off-line zero is where that fails). **Verdict:** the stealth window is a TARGET, not a wall; the D-H discipline upgrades from D-H-EXCLUDED to D-H-AWARE; the right object gives D-H its off-line zeros and zeta its on-line zeros from one mechanism (cup-is-a-polarization, 2HH). **Honest scope:** D(gamma) is computed from the actual zeros (a localization demonstration, circular as a proof); the resolution argument is quantitative and non-circular; nothing proves RH. The reframe is the deliverable.

### 62. Brick 2: the candidate-F bridge is WEAKER than hoped (clean negative). The Q-linear independence of {log p} is ELEMENTARY (unique factorization), the Weil margin (+0.035) sits ABOVE the effective-gap scale (~0.001), and forcing commensurability does NOT degrade positivity beyond an equal-RMS incommensurable perturbation (z-scores -0.20, -0.67). So candidate F imports no Baker/Diophantine machinery; the deep discriminator stays the Euler-product H^2 (2GG/#60), not the transcendence of {log p}. Refines (not contradicts) #59.

2II ([e2ii_transcendence_bridge.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ii_transcendence_bridge.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ii_transcendence_bridge.md)) executes brick 2 of state_of_candidate_ABF: does Weil positivity carry a transcendence/independence condition on {log p} (candidate F)? **The deflating fact:** {log p} Q-linear independence is ELEMENTARY -- sum a_i log p_i = 0 <=> prod p_i^{a_i} = 1 <=> all a_i = 0 by unique factorization. So the qualitative incommensurability 2FF/#59 used (organ (b), "no single period") is FREE, not a deep transcendence statement; candidate F's deep version would need an EFFECTIVE (Baker) bound or independence with the archimedean periods. **Results:** Part 1: smallest |sum a_i log p_i| (p<=11) shrinks 0.0465 -> 0.0019 as |a_i| grows 1->3 but stays >0 (one-line proof). Part 2: effective gaps reach ~0.0013 (6 primes); the Weil margin 0.035 (#34) is ~25x ABOVE -> positivity not at the Baker scale. Part 3 (controlled sensitivity): rebuilding the prime block with sampling positions {log n} snapped to a commensurable grid degrades min eig(M) (+0.026 -> -0.39 at grid 0.10) but NO MORE than an equal-RMS incommensurable random perturbation (-0.33 +/- 0.34, n=12): commensurability z-score -0.20 (and -0.67 at grid 0.20), nowhere near -2. The large control variance shows it is displacement MAGNITUDE, not commensurability, that moves positivity. **Verdict:** candidate F is a WEAKER bridge than hoped; the independence it supplies is elementary, the margin sits above the effective-gap scale, and positivity is not specifically resonance-sensitive. The 2FF organ-(b)<->F bridge connects organ (b) to a FREE fact, importing no Baker machinery. Refines #59: the obstruction to a single period IS the independence of {log p} (true) but that independence is elementary, so the bridge is real and SHALLOW. **Net consolidation:** the deep content concentrates on the H^2 / Euler-product side (organ (a), 2GG/2HH), away from the transcendence side -- a clean negative that prunes a hoped-for import and sharpens where the work is. **Honest scope:** Part 1 elementary; Part 2 a scale comparison (gaps shrink with more primes/coeffs, so "above the gap scale" is for the reachable regime); Part 3 a controlled truncation-level sensitivity test, not a theorem. Locates where candidate F lives; not a statement about RH.

### 61. Brick 1 / organ (a) made exact: RH <=> the Poincare-duality cup product is a POLARIZATION <=> FE-partner (1-rho) = conjugate (rho-bar) <=> Re=1/2. PERFECTNESS is free (the FE gives a perfect cup-product duality even for D-H, residual 6e-30); POSITIVITY is the gap (D-H's off-line zero displaces FE-partner from conjugate by |1-2beta|=0.617). The defect is a FIXED structural quantity (stealth-free as a STATEMENT), but its non-circular evaluation is still the analytic gap.

2HH ([e2hh_cup_is_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2hh_cup_is_polarization.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2hh_cup_is_polarization.md)) executes brick 1 of state_of_candidate_ABF (organ (a), the prismatic Poincare duality) and answers its perfectness-vs-positivity question. **The characterization:** a polarization = a duality compatible with a positive Hermitian structure (Hodge-Riemann). On H^1 the cup product (Poincare duality, from the FE) pairs rho with 1-rho; the Hermitian polarization (Hodge star / conjugation) pairs rho with rho-bar; they coincide iff 1-rho=rho-bar iff Re(rho)=1/2. So RH <=> the cup product IS a polarization <=> FE-partner = conjugate for every zero. **Results:** (1) zeta: first 10 zeros all beta=1/2 exactly, defect |1-2beta|=0 (cup IS the polarization, perfect AND positive). (2) D-H: (a) FE residual 6e-30 -- D-H HAS the duality, so the cup product is a PERFECT pairing for D-H too (perfectness is NOT the discriminator); (b) off-line zero rho~0.8085+85.699i (verified, |L|=1.1e-5) has FE-partner 1-rho~0.1915 DIFFERENT from conjugate rho-bar~0.8085, displacement 0.617 -- the cup product is NOT a polarization for D-H (positivity fails, perfectness holds). (3) the displacement 0.617 is a FIXED structural quantity, not a reconstruction-floor quantity: unlike the stealth window (#34) it does not shrink with truncation, visible at any resolution including the off-line zero. CATCH (honest): evaluating it needs the zero location (circular) OR the non-circular reconstruction (back under the stealth floor) -- so the CHARACTERIZATION is exact and stealth-free, its NON-circular evaluation is the analytic gap. **Verdict:** POSITIVITY, not perfectness, is organ (a)'s gap; the open content is "the FE-duality is Hodge-Riemann positive" = RH, on the infinite-dim H^1, importing the standard-conjecture frame on the H^2/duality face. Combined with 2GG (#60): D-H fails twice (no nonzero H^2 unit; AND FE-partner != conjugate), both saying its duality is not a polarization. **Lean:** added typed placeholder targets to PrismaticCohomology.lean (cup_product, fundamental_class, Q3a_fundamental_class_nonzero, Q3b_cup_is_polarization_iff_RH) in the existing Unit/True-sorry idiom; NOT compiled this session (no toolchain), to be verified on the owner's build. **Honest scope:** an exact elementary characterization demonstrated on real zeros (not a non-circular certificate); proves nothing new about RH; a sharpening coordinate.

Decisive contrast: fundamental class 1 vs 0. **The DUALITY (FE) does NOT distinguish zeta from D-H; the FUNDAMENTAL CLASS (Euler pole) DOES.** Organ (a)'s obstruction is the nonzero unit, not the pairing -- K2 in cohomological language: D-H has the pairing but H^2 = 0, so its duality is not a polarization (the cleanest cohomological form of the D-H discipline yet, and it grades probe B's rank-1 ample direction as the s=1 H^2). Verified against 79 zeta zeros up to T=200 (clock 1.03 at T~18 -> 3.45 at T~197; per-gap GUE fluctuations give 15% residual on the smoothed match). **Conclusion:** organ (b) is the scaling flow R_+ (Deninger) with the incommensurable spectrum {log p}; the additive clock is the running log(T/2pi) set by the Gamma factor; the surviving universal constant is 2pi (Mellin-Fourier period s <-> q^{-s}); the clock log q dissolves into the flow. q->1: the FF period 2pi/log q -> infinity, lattice -> continuum, zeta = the q=1 limit. K2: D-H has no Euler product => no {log p} => no flow/clock (C2). **The bridge:** organ (b) and candidate F are the same problem from two sides -- the period cannot be a number BECAUSE {log p} is transcendentally independent, so the geometric gluing and the transcendence statement are dual descriptions of the one missing input. Converts organ (b) from "find a period" to "build the R_+-flow with spectrum {log p} and prove the running archimedean clock balances the incommensurable prime clock at every height." **Honest scope:** the FF lattice period, the incommensurability of {log p}, and the running density log(T/2pi) are known facts; this makes the two-clock structure quantitative and concludes period = flow. ### 56. The M2.6 stealth window is a HUGE-CANCELLATION residue, and the archimedean block is NOT a cushion (it is indefinite). The three input-side blocks of the Weil/Rosati form have norms 55 (A_arch), 69 (P_fin), 123 (B_pole) but their sum has norm 0.33: positivity for zeta is the residue of a ~370x cancellation, NOT "a positive archimedean cushion dominating an indefinite prime perturbation." A_arch is itself indefinite (min eig -55; Omega(0) < 0), correcting a false claim in the e3m docstring. The stealth window follows mechanically: the off-line obstruction (~2.6% of the tiny net, absolute ~0.05) sits below the reconstruction residual of the huge canceling blocks (~10% of the net, absolute ~0.19), ratio 0.25 < 1, so it is invisible to any input-side certificate at reachable K.

Supervised follow-up to M2.6 (#34), prompted by the overnight C3 attempt that flagged A_arch as non-PSD. Calibrated exactly against e3m (reconstruction residual 0.078 for zeta, recon rel-min +0.0805).

**Computed (K=10, b in [1.3,6]).** Blocks (norm, min eig): A_arch (55.3, -55.2), P_fin (69.0, -68.9), B_pole (123.2, +0.0 to +123.2, rank-1 from the s=1 pole), M = A+P+B (0.33, +0.019), M_zero (0.31, +0.013), reconstruction residual 0.078. Cancellation ratio max||block|| / ||M_net|| = 372x. By Weyl, A_arch + P_fin alone has min eig <= -52; only the large positive pole block lifts the sum to the tiny positive M_zero. D-H: net norm 1.86, zero-side rel-min +0.011 (off-line obstruction hidden = spuriously positive), input-side rel-min +0.044, residual 0.104; off-line absolute ~0.048 vs residual absolute ~0.194 (ratio 0.25). The cancellation ratio is large and roughly stable across K in [6,12] (348-429x, not strongly monotone in this reachable range); the true infinite-resolution margin is the e3v doubly-exponential wall e^{-4 pi x} (#52).

**The finding.** This is the mechanism behind the M2.6 stealth window (#34) and a correction: (1) the e3m "A_arch is manifestly positive-semidefinite" claim is FALSE (A_arch min eig -55; the symbol Omega is negative near t=0), corrected in both e3m and e3y. (2) The marginal-positivity thesis gets its sharpest input-side form: the positive Weil form is the ~370x-cancellation residue of three large blocks, so its sign is buried under the reconstruction error of those blocks, which is precisely why no input-side (non-circular) certificate can see D-H's off-line zeros at reachable K. (3) It confirms the correct off-line detector is the ANSWER-side Schur complement (e3j), which works in the zero basis and is not limited by input-side reconstruction error. The session-long seam thesis (off-line content in the prime/Euler block, archimedean shared with D-H) is consistent: here the archimedean AND prime blocks are both large and indefinite, and the off-line obstruction is a small fraction of the net residue, below the reconstruction floor.

**Honest scope.** A clean float64 computation calibrated exactly against e3m (same blocks, same residual). The cancellation ratio and the indefiniteness of A_arch are robust facts; the "deepens with K" reading is weak (the ratio is ~400x and roughly flat in K in [6,12], not strongly monotone), so the entry claims only that the ratio is large and that the true margin is the e3v wall. The "2.6% off-line fraction" is imported from #18/3D.3, not re-derived here. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; it is the mechanism of the stealth window and a docstring correction. ### 55. The Bost-Connes multiplicativity obstruction (overnight C4): D-H provably has no equilibrium product state. The pointwise multiplicativity defect d(L) = mean |a_{mn} - a_m a_n| / mean |a_m a_n| over coprime pairs is 0 for Euler products (zeta, chi3; the KMS equilibrium is a pure product state (x)'_p rho_p) and > 0 for non-Euler functions (D-H 1.16, Epstein-d47-principal 5.14). Because d > 0 for BOTH the RH-false (D-H) and the RH-true (Epstein) non-Euler controls, the obstruction detects NON-EULER-ness, not RH-failure: it is the Bost-Connes form of the K2 firewall, necessary-not-sufficient. The algebraic twin of e3w's analytic loglog-coefficient (#53).

Overnight cycle C4 ([e3z_multiplicativity_obstruction.py](positivity/e3z_multiplicativity_obstruction.py)). An Euler product <=> a_{mn} = a_m a_n for gcd(m,n)=1 <=> the Bost-Connes partition function factors Z(beta) = prod_p Z_p(beta) <=> the beta>1 KMS state is the restricted tensor product (a pure product state). A sum of two distinct primitive L-functions (D-H = c1 L(chi1) + c2 L(chi2)) is a convex MIXTURE of the constituents' product states, a product state iff the constituents coincide.

**Computed (coprime m,n>=2, mn<=400, 486 pairs).** d(zeta) = d(chi3) = 0 to machine precision; d(D-H) = 1.16; d(Epstein-d47-principal) = 5.14. Sample: D-H has a_2 a_3 = -0.08 but a_6 = 1.0 (defect 1.08); Epstein has a_2 a_7 = 0 but a_14 = 4 (defect 4). 

**The finding.** This makes concrete the construction sweep's Mechanism-2 claim (#53) and sharpens the 2A_R1 D-H exclusion from the prose "D-H is a linear combination, not a geometric object" to the operator-algebra statement "D-H has no equilibrium product state at all." It is a clean, non-circular, single-number, D-H-discriminating invariant (the algebraic counterpart of the analytic loglog-coefficient #53), and it lands in the SAME place: necessary-not-sufficient (the Epstein trap, #20/#27). Two independent invariants (analytic density #53, algebraic multiplicativity #55) now confirm the seam from the construction sweep: multiplicativity fixes the object's EXISTENCE (product state / Euler factorization), but the off-line-zero content lives in the shared archimedean continuation, so neither invariant can be promoted to a positivity that implies RH.

**Honest scope.** A direct, exact computation (the defects are algebraic, no approximation). The Bost-Connes / KMS interpretation is standard (Bost-Connes 1995); the new content is the quantified defect across the project's controls and the explicit Epstein trap confirming necessary-not-sufficient. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; a K2-firewall sharpening. Cross-refs: #53 (the construction sweep + the analytic loglog twin), #20/#27 (the von-Mangoldt fingerprint / Epstein trap family), #46 (the Euler block carries the sign), 2A_R1 (the D-H exclusion this sharpens). References: Bost-Connes 1995 (KMS / product states); reuses the shared dirichlet_coefficient interface.

### 54. The function-field polarization, made concrete (the missing object where it is a theorem). Built the intersection form on NS(E x E) for 23 elliptic curves over F_p: signature is exactly (1,3) for EVERY curve (Hodge Index Theorem), the primitive part (orthogonal to the ample h+v) is negative-definite, and the RH-for-E bound a^2 <= 4q holds with a HEALTHY buffer 4q - a^2 = O(q) (median 2.88 q). This exhibits exactly what the integer case is missing: over F_q the polarization is automatic with an O(q) buffer; over Z the analogue Weil form has only the e^{-4 pi x} marginal buffer (#52) AND the surface Spec(Z) x Spec(Z) carrying the Frobenius class is not even constructed.

Overnight cycle C1 ([e3x_function_field_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e3x_function_field_polarization.py)). On S = E x E the classes h (horizontal), v (vertical), Delta (diagonal), Gamma (graph of q-Frobenius) have intersection numbers h.v = h.Delta = v.Delta = h.Gamma = 1, v.Gamma = q, Delta.Gamma = #E(F_q) = q+1-a (all self-intersections 0 by adjunction). Point-counted a_p for y^2 = x^3 + x + 1.

**Computed.** Across p in [5, 97]: the 4x4 Gram has eigenvalue signature (1,3) for all 23 curves (1 positive, 3 negative); the primitive-part restricted form is negative-definite for all; |a| < 2 sqrt p (Hasse) for all; buffer 4p - a^2 ranges 11..387, scaling O(p). 

**The finding.** This is the Level-5 object (the polarization, 08A M4 / spec_z landscape column iii) in the one setting where it is a proven theorem, and it is DEFINITE with room to spare, not marginal. The contrast with the integer case is the sharpest statement of the gap: (i) over F_q the Hodge Index Theorem gives signature (1, n-1) for free, forcing a^2 <= 4q with an O(q) buffer; (ii) over Z, even if the analogous Weil form is positive (RH), e3v shows its buffer would be e^{-4 pi x} (doubly-exponentially marginal), and worse, the product surface and the Frobenius correspondence Gamma do not exist as constructed objects. D-H underscores why: no Euler product => no Frobenius endomorphism => no Gamma class => the construction does not even start, which is exactly the K2 signature of an RH-relevant object. The missing math is this polarization plus its substrate; this experiment is the concrete picture of the target.

**Honest scope.** A direct verification of Weil's classical argument on E x E (Hodge index + Rosati positivity), not new mathematics; its value is the explicit, side-by-side contrast (definite O(q) buffer vs the integer e^{-4 pi x} wall) that names the missing object precisely. The 4-class sublattice may not be all of NS (rank can be larger for special/CM curves), but the signature on this sublattice is already (1,3) and the RH bound is recovered, which is all that is claimed. Cross-refs: #52 (the integer marginal wall this contrasts), #53 (the construction sweep that concluded the missing math is the polarization), #18/#30 (the signature is the content), all_roads / 08A M4 / spec_z landscape (the missing polarization).
