### 48. Wrong-polarity check: the convex-Hodge (Kahler / Hodge-Riemann / AHK) signature is UNCONDITIONALLY $(1, n-1)$ for every weighting including the arithmetic $w_p = \log p$, so it can never flip to flag an off-line zero (WRONG polarity for a detector); the Weil form, by contrast, flips PSD $\to$ indefinite exactly when an off-line zero appears (RIGHT polarity). Combined with #40 (arithmetic-blind), the weighted-tropical-Kahler accident channel needs a genuinely new theorem, not weight injection.

Cheap-probe 6 of the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md)), testing a B-credible accident channel (the weighted tropical Kahler package, Amini-Piquerez) head-on against the ADVERSARY's polarity objection. 3R ([e3r_convex_hodge_polarity.py](positivity/e3r_convex_hodge_polarity.py)) contrasts two signature forms on small explicit models.

**Computed.** (Part 1) The mixed-area (Alexandrov-Fenchel / Lorentzian) form of $n=8$ segments has signature $(1, 7)$ for the uniform, the arithmetic $w_p=\log p$, the steep $w=p$, and adversarial weightings, and for 2000/2000 random (weights, directions) the positive count is exactly 1 (one near-degenerate gave $(1,6,1)$, still pos $=1$). The signature is INVARIANT $(1, n-1)$: unconditional definiteness on the primitive part. (Part 2) The augmented Weil/Schur Gram (e3k) is PSD with only on-line zeros (signature $(10,0)$, min-eig $-1.4\times10^{-16}$) and acquires a NEGATIVE eigenvalue when an off-line pair ($\beta=0.7$) is injected (signature $(11,1)$, min-eig $-0.104$): conditional definiteness.

**The finding.** A proven Kahler / Hodge-Riemann package gives a signature that is definite-on-the-primitive-part for ALL admissible weights, so injecting arithmetic weights $w_p=\log p$ (the move that would defeat #40's arithmetic-blindness) cannot make it FAIL when a zero leaves the line. WRONG POLARITY: a detector needs the form to be definite IFF RH, and the Kahler package is unconditionally definite. The Weil form has the RIGHT polarity (it flips). So the convex-Hodge B-credible accident channel is blocked by TWO independent obstructions: #40 (the signature does not move with the Frobenius trace $t$) and #48 (even if it did, the signature cannot fail). The dossier's #1 watchlist signal -- a HR theorem whose signature BOTH moves with $t$ AND can fail -- therefore requires a genuinely new theorem, not weight injection into an existing Kahler package.

**Honest scope.** A SIGNATURE-LEVEL polarity argument on a small exact model (segments / mixed areas, a clean Lorentzian instance) plus the known Weil-form behavior (e3k). Does NOT prove no Kahler-type RH detector can exist; it shows the naive "inject arithmetic weights into a Kahler package" route has the wrong polarity. Complements #40 (arithmetic-blindness) and the AHK "too blind" bracket in the landscape. Cross-refs: #40 (the C-C square mixed-volume Lorentzian-for-free / arithmetic-blind), #21/#30 (the signature is the content), #18 (the Weil form's conditional definiteness). References: the accident dossier (cheap-probe 6); Alexandrov-Fenchel; Adiprasito-Huh-Katz; Amini-Piquerez.

### 47. Sharp-margin recovery test: the off-line margin is a clean $-c\,\varepsilon^2$ law at the D-H height, the float64 stealth window is a REMOVABLE eigensolver-cancellation artifact (NOT structural), but removability is moot for certification because the certificate is DOWNSTREAM of rigorous verification and has no independent disproof leverage. The margin must be recovered analytically.

Cheap-probe 4 of the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md)), operationalizing the acceptance criterion "RECOVERS THE MARGIN, NOT JUST THE SIGN". 3Q ([e3q_margin_recovery.py](positivity/e3q_margin_recovery.py)) injects a hypothetical off-line zero at the Davenport-Heilbronn zero HEIGHT ($\gamma_0 = 85.699$) and sweeps $\varepsilon = \beta - 1/2$ toward the line, answering the question #3K left open: is the stealth window fundamental or a float64 artifact?

**Computed (K=200, on-line cushion T_max=120, prec=30 / dps=50).** (Part 1) The off-line margin $\min\mathrm{eig}(M_{\mathrm{aug}})$ scales as $-3.1\,\varepsilon^2$ (the #3K law) at the D-H height; the REAL D-H zero ($\varepsilon=0.3085$) gives margin $-0.77$, trivially detected. The float64 full-Gram detection reaches $\varepsilon^* \sim 7\times 10^{-8}$ (where $-3.1\varepsilon^2$ meets the $\sim 10^{-15}$ eigenvalue cancellation floor); the #19 Schur relative-signal detector is looser ($\sim 10^{-5}$); both straddle the rigorous Platt-Trudgian bound $10^{-7}$. (Part 2) The off-line signal SOURCE $\|\mathrm{Im}\,\phi(\rho)\|^2$ is a clean $\varepsilon^2$ (ratio constant $0.103$) and float64 EQUALS mpmath to relative $0$ all the way down to $\varepsilon = 10^{-12}$.

**The finding.** The stealth window is NOT in the data: $\|\mathrm{Im}\,\phi\|^2$ (the source of the off-line negative eigenvalue) is computed cleanly to $\varepsilon = 10^{-12}$ even in float64 (because $\phi$ is evaluated in mpmath internally). The float64 stealth is therefore a pure EIGENSOLVER CANCELLATION artifact: extracting a $-\varepsilon^2$ eigenvalue sitting on an $O(1)$ on-line cushion loses it to roundoff at $\sim 10^{-15}\|M\|$. Computed from the source, or with a higher-precision eigensolver, the margin is recoverable to arbitrarily small $\varepsilon$. The stealth is REMOVABLE, not structural. This corrects a natural misreading of #3K (the window is not a fundamental resolution limit).

**The sharp point (sharpens #3K, the real content).** Removability is moot for CERTIFICATION. Detecting an off-line zero at distance $\varepsilon$ requires KNOWING its location to precision $< \varepsilon$ and injecting it, which is exactly what rigorous verification (Platt-Trudgian) already supplies. So this certificate is DOWNSTREAM of the rigorous check and has no INDEPENDENT disproof leverage; it cannot certify RH on its own at any precision. A proof must recover the margin ANALYTICALLY (engage the exact off-line structure of $\zeta$), not via finite eigenvalues of a hand-injected zero. The marginal-positivity thesis, sharpened: the bottleneck is not precision but the absence of an independent analytic handle on the margin.

**Honest scope.** This is the corrected experiment: the first version had two bugs (a Schur-magnitude sign/degeneracy that produced a spurious $\varepsilon^*$, and a small-K Part 2 where the off-line direction was absorbed by the cushion so $\min\mathrm{eig}$ did not track it); both were caught and fixed (Part 1 now uses the clean full-Gram margin, Part 2 the unambiguous signal source $\|\mathrm{Im}\,\phi\|^2$). PROVEN/COMPUTED: the $-c\varepsilon^2$ law, the float64 vs mpmath agreement of the source, the $\varepsilon^*$ floors (all numerical facts). STRUCTURAL READING: "removable but downstream, no independent leverage" rests on the precision argument plus the input-location requirement, not a proved impossibility. Does NOT prove RH, does NOT change M3. Cross-refs: #3K (the disproof-leverage finding it sharpens), #19 (the Schur stealth window), #45/#46 (the other two executed accident probes), the marginal-positivity thesis. References: the accident dossier (cheap-probe 4); reuses e3c/e3j/e3k; Platt-Trudgian rigorous verification.

### 46. Prime-block ablation confirms the M_euler certificate is K2-GENUINE: its RH-discriminating sign is carried by the Euler / {log p} block, not the archimedean block that Davenport-Heilbronn shares. Zeroing the prime-power block makes zeta FAIL the positivity certificate exactly as D-H does, so the discrimination lives on the half D-H structurally cannot build.

Cheap-probe 2 of the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md)), operationalizing the D-H filter's mandatory test #3 (the discriminating SIGN must be carried by the Frobenius/{log p} half). 3P ([e3p_prime_block_ablation.py](positivity/e3p_prime_block_ablation.py)) ablates the validated M3 discriminator $M_{\mathrm{euler}} = A_{\mathrm{arch}} + P_{\mathrm{pp}} + B_{\mathrm{pole}}$ (#35-37) by scaling the prime-power block by $\alpha \in [0,1]$, reusing the exact e3m/e2v block-builders.

**Computed (K=8, b in [1.3,6], prec=30).** At $\alpha=1$ (full primes) $M_{\mathrm{euler}}$ separates the controls by SIGN: zeta $+0.035$ (passes), D-H $-0.929$ (fails), Epstein-d47-principal $+0.676$ (passes). At $\alpha=0$ (primes zeroed) the archimedean block alone gives zeta $-2.342$ and D-H $-4.635$: BOTH negative, so NEITHER passes the positivity certificate and the sign-discrimination is GONE. The prime-power block is what lifts zeta over zero (Euler-product structure), and it does not lift D-H far enough (its remaining deficit is the deleted composite block $P_{\mathrm{comp}}$, the stealth-window compensator of #34). CONTRAST built in: the FULL $M = A + P_{\mathrm{pp}} + P_{\mathrm{comp}} + B$ does NOT sign-separate even at $\alpha=1$ (zeta $+0.035$, D-H $+0.094$ WRONG SIGN), the M2.6 stealth window (#34); deleting $P_{\mathrm{comp}}$ is what exposes the prime-carried sign this ablation then confirms K2-genuine.

**The finding.** The certificate's RH-verdict ($\min\mathrm{eig} \ge 0$) for zeta is CREATED by the Euler/{log p} block, not the archimedean block shared with D-H. Zeroing the primes collapses zeta to D-H-like failure, so the discrimination cannot be archimedean (K2-blind); it lives on the Frobenius half D-H structurally lacks. This is the POSITIVE counterpart to #45 (where the PSLQ-accessible quantities were the shared archimedean half, hence K2-blind): here the certificate's discriminating content is verified to sit on the unshared Euler half, the right kind. A reusable K2-soundness test for any future certificate.

**Honest scope.** Validates the K2-SOUNDNESS of the existing $M_{\mathrm{euler}}$ discriminator (its sign is prime-carried); does NOT prove RH, and does NOT make $M_{\mathrm{euler}}$ intrinsic (deleting $P_{\mathrm{comp}}$ is still the Euler-product assumption, #37 caveat stands). Necessary-not-sufficient, like the whole M2.5/M2.6/M3 chain. Cross-refs: #34 (the stealth window in the full M), #35-37 (the M_euler discriminator), #45 (the dual archimedean-side result), #20 (the place-type split). References: the accident dossier (cheap-probe 2); reuses e3m/e2v.

### 45. The "numerical-stumble" accident slot is precision-confined to the D-H-shared archimedean half (K2-blind by computability), and the de Branges trap is reproduced and defeated mechanically by a precision-stability test. The integer-relation (PSLQ) surface and the RH-discriminating surface are disjoint: a lucky closed-form identity cannot, by construction, distinguish zeta from its counterexample.

Motivated by the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md), slot 4: the unforeseen exact identity / numerical stumble, Tao's "surfaces first as a coincidence" pattern). 3O ([e3o_pslq_stumble.py](positivity/e3o_pslq_stumble.py)) runs the canonical accident-detector (PSLQ integer-relation search) with the dossier's strictest guard baked in.

**Computed (mpmath dps=80 for PSLQ, dps=30 for Li).** (A) VALIDATION: PSLQ re-discovers all 3 known archimedean relations and a precision-stability test confirms each is REAL (residual falls from ~1e-81 at dps=80 to ~1e-201 at dps=200): the Bombieri kernel C_μ(0) = log π + γ_E + 2 log 2, the Bombieri-Lagarias constant ASYMP = (1 − γ_E − log 2π)/2, and log 2π = log π + log 2. (B) SEARCH: with a disciplined bound (maxcoeff 1e4, tol 1e-72) NO new integer relation appears among the independent high-precision constants {γ_E, log π, log 2, ζ(2), ζ(3), Stieltjes γ_1, γ_2} AND the Euler-side prime zetas {P(2), P(3)}. The de Branges trap is then REPRODUCED on purpose: loosening the discipline (maxcoeff 1e9, default tol) makes PSLQ "find" a relation with 8-digit coefficients (max |coeff| 4.5e7) and residual 2.26e-61 at dps=80, but the SAME integer combination stays at 2.26e-61 at dps=220 instead of vanishing. It does not improve with precision, so it is a chance artifact (tol ~ maxcoeff^{-(n-1)}), the exact 12-digit-agreement-then-failure failure mode of #43, caught automatically. (C) GUARD LAYER: the first 12 Li coefficients are all POSITIVE for every control (zeta, Davenport-Heilbronn, Epstein d15 RH-true, Epstein d47 off-line); no discrimination at low n, consistent with #2 (D-H Li negativity is a large-n effect, scale n ~ 3.2e5). The per-zero tail term at n=12 is already ~1e-2 (truncation ~ n² log T / T), so usable precision is ~1 digit.

**The finding (a NEGATIVE coordinate, new angle on #30/no-free-lunch).** The quantities reachable to PSLQ precision are exactly the closed-form ARCHIMEDEAN / Γ-factor constants (γ_E, log π, Stieltjes, C_μ, ζ-values), and that half is SHARED verbatim by D-H (same functional equation, same Γ-factor), so any PSLQ-discoverable identity is RH-agnostic by construction (K2-blind). The quantity that DISCRIMINATES zeta from D-H is the Li coefficient sequence, but (i) its discriminating signal is a large-n phenomenon (#2) and (ii) its truncation error has already destroyed precision by then, so the Li data is never simultaneously discriminating AND PSLQ-precise. The numerical-stumble accident is therefore precision-confined to the half of the problem that cannot tell zeta from its known counterexample. This is the dossier's no-free-lunch verdict for slot 4, demonstrated by a computability argument rather than asserted.

**Honest scope.** PROVEN/COMPUTED: the three archimedean relations and their precision-stability (textbook, correctly rederived, none new); the spurious-relation reproduction-and-refutation; the low-n all-positive Li table (reconfirms #2, not new). STRUCTURAL READING (not a theorem): "the stumble slot is K2-blind by computability" rests on the PSLQ precision requirement vs the truncation floor, both demonstrated numerically, not on a proved impossibility. Does NOT construct any accident, does NOT prove RH, does NOT change M3. Durable value: a reusable disciplined PSLQ harness with the de Branges-trap precision-stability test and the mandatory D-H/Epstein gate, and a sharp coordinate (the lucky-identity accident lives on the RH-agnostic half). Cross-refs: #2 (Li discrimination scale), #18/#19 (stealth windows), #27 (Jensen/Turan stealth window), #30 (all roads to the signature), #43 (de Branges 12-digit trap), #44. References: the accident dossier; Bombieri-Lagarias (1999); Li (1997).

### 44. Direction 8 / prismatic side (2PR.1): Bhatt-Lurie's WCart carries TWO operators, and prismatic cohomology supplies BOTH trace-halves of completed zeta as regularized determinants — the Frobenius F (spectrum {log p}) the finite Euler factors (#26/#41), the SEN operator Θ (Hodge-Tate weights {−n}) the archimedean DIVISOR (√(2π)/Γ(s), Lerch). REAL-BUT-ONLY-HEURISTIC (spectral coincidence; place-independence of Hodge weights, Deninger 1992 / Serre 1970), supplies the trivial-zero divisor NOT the analytic Γ_R / π-conductor, RH-agnostic, and NEITHER half contains the non-trivial zeros: the M3 signature gap is unchanged. A CONNECTING coordinate like #41.

2PR.1 ([e2pr_sen_archimedean.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2pr_sen_archimedean.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2pr_sen_archimedean.md)) locates the archimedean carrier (#42/#43) on the leading prismatic candidate, Bhatt-Lurie's Cartier-Witt stack WCart, which carries TWO different operators. (a) The Deninger-Hesselholt FLOW generator (q^Θ = Frobenius; spectrum {log p}, the primes; det_∞(s−Θ) = ∏ₚ(1−p⁻ˢ)⁻¹ = ζ): the finite Euler factors / von Mangoldt trace (#26/#41). (b) The Bhatt-Lurie SEN operator (γ_u = exp(log(u)Θ) = cyclotomic action; spectrum {−n}, the Hodge-Tate weights, BL Example 3.5.6): the weight/grading direction.

**Computed (mpmath dps=40, all reproduced; the π-conductor correction is built in).** (1) PROVED Lerch: det_∞(s−Θ_Sen) = ∏ᵣₑg_{n≥0}(s+n) = √(2π)/Γ(s), ratio 1.000 at s=2, 0.5, 3.7, 1/2+14.13i. Even weights {−2n}: ∏ᵣₑg(s+2n) = 2^((1−s)/2)√(2π)/Γ(s/2), ratio 1.000. (2) BLINDNESS: |det_∞(ρ−Θ_Sen)| = 4.391e9 (NONZERO) at the first zero ρ=1/2+14.13i, while |ζ(ρ)|=6.3e−16 — the non-trivial zeros sit in the ζ(s) factor, never in the Γ factor. (3) SPECTRAL COINCIDENCE: three unrelated {−n}-spectrum "operators" give the identical det (2.5066); a zeta-regularized determinant is a class function of the eigenvalue multiset alone. (4) π-CONDUCTOR ABSENT: the even reg-det is ~1/Γ(s/2) (reciprocal), NOT Γ_R(s)=π^(−s/2)Γ(s/2); (even reg-det)·Γ_R(s) is NOT constant (0.564, 2.239, 0.118, 2.04−0.92i; spread 1.74), so only the trivial-zero DIVISOR {0,−2,−4,…}=poles of Γ_R matches. (5) Frobenius contrast: ∏_{p<5000}(1−p⁻ˢ)⁻¹ matches ζ(2) to 3.5e−5.

**The finding (honest, ADVERSARY-checked, score 5/10, a CONNECTING coordinate like #41).** Prismatic cohomology supplies BOTH trace-halves of the completed zeta as regularized determinants (F → finite Euler factors; Θ_Sen → archimedean divisor), pinned to one paper (Bhatt-Lurie). But it is the TRACE, not the signature: NEITHER half contains the non-trivial zeros (blindness numeric), which are the SIGNATURE of how F and Θ combine on the global H^1 — a polarization WCart does not carry (coh dim 1, Chern classes, no Hodge-index/intersection-form). Sharpens "all roads to the signature" (#30) from the WCart side; the prismatic mirror of the de Branges converse (#43, which reached the zeros with the wrong positivity; here the trace reaches the Γ-factor divisor but is blind to the zeros).

**Honest scope.** PROVED: the Lerch and even-weight identities (ratio 1.000), the blindness numeric, the spectral-coincidence control — all textbook regularized-product facts correctly rederived, NONE new. CITED (statement-level): Bhatt-Lurie Thm 3.5.8 / Ex 3.5.6 / Prop 3.7.1 / Thm 3.9.5 (Sen spectrum = {−n}); Deninger 1992 Invent. Math. 107 (archimedean factor = inverse reg-det of an integer-spectrum flow generator, the 30-year precedent); Serre 1970 (Γ-factor from Hodge weights); Connes-Consani 2015 Thm 4.2 (intrinsic Γ-factor). STRUCTURAL READING (real-but-only-heuristic): "Θ_Sen carries the archimedean factor" means only that Θ_Sen and Deninger's archimedean generator share the Hodge-weight spectrum {−n}, hence the same regularized DIVISOR, because the Hodge-weight grading is place-independent — an ANALOGY across places, NOT a prismatic computation of the archimedean place (WCart is over Spf(ℤ_p), unramified, no archimedean fiber, BL Rmk 1.4.3). Does NOT supply Γ_R or the π-conductor (only the divisor); does NOT contain the non-trivial zeros (the M3 gap is unchanged); has ZERO K2 leverage (D-H shares the Γ-factor by construction, #38/#43; K2 lives on F not Θ). A relocation onto the WCart/Sen vocabulary that completes the trace picture from #41 and reconfirms the signature as the irreducible content. References: Bhatt-Lurie arXiv:2201.06120; Deninger Invent. Math. 107 (1992); Connes-Consani arXiv:1502.05580; Lerch.

### 43. The de Branges / Conrey-Li per-zero cross-term Q(ρ) = -Re{ξ'(ρ)ξ(1+ρ)} is a NEGATIVE coordinate and the exact CONVERSE of #42: the GLOBAL signed pairing de Branges already built DOES see the zeros, but its pointwise positivity is strictly-stronger-than-RH and FAILS at POSITIVE DENSITY (~6%) even when RH holds. So the M3 signed pairing must be RH-EQUIVALENT (a sum, like λ_n), not the pointwise Hermite-Biehler cross-term.

**2DB.2 update (K=500, density revision; [e2db2_debranges_k500.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2db2_debranges_k500.py) + .npz).** 2DB.1 ran k=1..50 and saw a SINGLE negative (k=34), which read as "sporadic." Extended to K=500 (precision scaled with γ; deepest |Q| ~ 10⁻⁵⁵³ at γ≈811, guard band ≥65 digits), the negative-Q set is **32 of 500 (~6% density)**: {34, 71, 106, 127, 144, 173, 184, 186, 196, 233, 257, 265, 282, 289, 298, 315, 334, 363, 368, 380, 394, 401, 409, 423, 436, 453, 462, 477, 483, 485, 492, 497}, tracking the zero density (per-100 windows 2,7,6,6,11; no strong clustering, gaps 2..37). **K=1000 follow-up ([e2db2_debranges_k1000.npz](arithmetic_geometric/e2db2_debranges_k1000.npz), verified): 80/1000 = 8.0%, per-100 windows [2,7,6,6,11,8,10,8,13,9] — the density does NOT stabilize at 6%; it drifts UPWARD (~5% at low zeros to ~9-13% higher up). So the de Branges pointwise failure is a generic positive-density phenomenon (and mildly increasing), reinforcing the lesson; the "~6%" of the K=500 sample was a low-zero undercount.** Anchor Q(ρ₃₄) = -5.389101e-69, ratio 1.000000 to Conrey-Li; slope log₁₀|Q| vs γ: full -0.6768, tail -0.6785, converging one-sided to -(π/2)/ln10 = -0.68219 (two-factor decomposition -0.3385 + -0.3381). **The "sporadic single k=34" reading of 2DB.1 was a small-sample artifact; pointwise de Branges (3.1) fails at positive density.** This SHARPENS but does not change the lesson: the RH-equivalent signed pairing must be a global SUM (λ_n), not the pointwise cross-term Q. COMPUTED (independently re-derived at sampled indices, anchor verified); the npz is canonical. K2 (D-H) not re-run at K=500 (inherited from 2DB.1, T≤90); M3 unchanged.

2DB.1 ([e2db_debranges_crossterm.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2db_debranges_crossterm.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2db_debranges_crossterm.md)) is the converse of #42's local-data-blindness. #42 showed the Connes-Consani LOCAL Euler/orbit data is blind to the zeros (it converges only Re(s)>1; the zeros live in the continuation Re(s)=1/2). de Branges built the opposite object: a SIGNED inner product realizing the continuation directly, the Hilbert space H(E) of entire functions with structure function E(z)=ξ(1-iz), whose Hermite-Biehler symmetry IS the functional equation ξ(s)=ξ(1-s) (the would-be Poincaré duality) and whose reproducing kernel pairs the GLOBAL zeros. Conrey-Li (IMRN 2000 No.18; arXiv:math/9812166) proved the de Branges positivity (3.1) ⇒ RH, but it is strictly stronger than RH (⇒ GRH for all Dirichlet L at once) and FAILS for zeta. The pointwise necessary consequence at each zero is Q(ρ) := -Re{ξ'(ρ)ξ(1+ρ)} ≥ 0.

**Computed (independently reproduced, not transcribed).** (1) ANCHOR: with ξ(s)=s(s-1)π^{-s/2}Γ(s/2)ζ(s) (Conrey-Li's no-1/2 normalization), Q(ρ_34) = -5.38910050718e-69 at the 34th zeta zero ρ_34=1/2+111.0295…i (ON the critical line, RH-TRUE), matching Conrey-Li's published -5.389100507182945e-69 to 12 sig figs (ratio 1.000); Riemann's 1/2-normalized ξ gives exactly 1/4 of this (Q is bilinear in ξ). (2) SIGN SEQUENCE: among the first 50 zeta zeros, Q(ρ_k)<0 for EXACTLY ONE index, k=34 — a global pairing that sees the zeros fails sporadically even under RH. (3) SUPPRESSION LAW: log10|Q| vs γ has finite-size slope -0.655 (k=1..50; tail -0.665), converging to -(π/2)/ln10 = -0.6822 — TWICE as steep as #38's single-Gamma -(π/4)/ln10 = -0.3411, because Q=ξ'(ρ)·ξ(1+ρ) carries TWO completed-ξ factors (measured sub-slopes -0.328 and -0.326, each ≈ -(π/4)/ln10, summing to the double-Gamma law). (4) CONTROLS / K2: the buildable direction is RH-AGNOSTIC — χ3 (RH true): 0 negatives of 46 zeros to T=100; χ4 (RH true): 1 negative (k=30) to T=100, so the density is L-function-dependent not generic; Davenport-Heilbronn (RH FALSE): completed self-dual Λ_DH verified (|Λ(s)-Λ(1-s)|~1e-62), its ON-LINE zeros ALSO show sporadic Q<0 (1 of 28, γ≈40.16), identical to RH-true zeta/χ4, so Q<0 is NOT an RH-violation signal; the actual off-line obstruction (pair at γ≈85.7) is double-suppressed to |Q|~1e-56, far below #38's 1e-29 detection floor (the off-line sign split is a shift artifact; K2 rests on magnitude only).

**The finding.** A NEGATIVE coordinate, the exact CONVERSE of #42: #42 says the local data cannot see the zeros; here the GLOBAL de Branges pairing DOES see them, but its positivity is the WRONG (pointwise, strictly-stronger-than-RH) one. It is the THIRD soft detector after #38 (heat kernel) and #39 (Rodgers-Tao log-gas), and the actual RH violation (D-H's off-line pair) is archimedean-suppressed below detectability — now by the DOUBLE-Gamma law -(π/2), sharper than #38's single-Gamma -(π/4). IMPLICATION FOR DIRECTION 8 / M3: the signed pairing realizing the continuation must be RH-EQUIVALENT — a global SUM like the Li coefficients λ_n (positive for zeta in 3A, negative for D-H/Epstein at large n in 3B/3B.4) — NOT the pointwise Hermite-Biehler cross-term. A naive cup-product whose positivity is the de Branges / HB condition is doomed for the reason Conrey-Li found: the archimedean derivative cross-term already has the wrong sign at a single on-line zero.

**Honest scope.** (i) the k=34 reproduction and (ii) the "only k=34 negative of the first 50" are NUMERICAL FACTS / observations (dps=80). (iii) the double-archimedean slope is a finite-size FIT (-0.655) converging to -(π/2)/ln10 only asymptotically, with the two-factor decomposition as the structural reading, not a proved identity. (iv) "de Branges positivity is the wrong, strictly-stronger-than-RH positivity, doomed as a naive Arch-2 cup-product/HB signature" is a STRUCTURAL READING built on Conrey-Li's PROVED theorems, not a new theorem. It does NOT construct the Arch-2 signed pairing / Weil cohomology (the M3/#25 gap is UNCHANGED), does NOT prove RH, does NOT yield an RH test (the condition fails for the RH-true case), and does NOT discriminate zeta from D-H in the buildable direction. The one circular fragment (HB admissibility = monotonicity of |E(x+iy)| in y = RH repackaged, which D-H fails) is flagged and deliberately NOT computed as a test. NOT a reinvention of 3A (3A/3N are global RH-equivalent sums λ_n, positive for zeta; Q is per-zero, strictly-stronger-than-RH, and fails for zeta). Durable content: the per-zero sign sequence (new), the double-archimedean law -(π/2) (new, sharper than #38), and the converse-of-#42 connection that tells Arch 2 the correct M3 signature is RH-equivalent. References: Conrey-Li, IMRN 2000 No.18 (arXiv:math/9812166); de Branges, Hilbert Spaces of Entire Functions (1968).

### 42. Direction 8 / the q-lift attempt (probe a): the q-lift DECOMPOSES into (un-idempotent-ization = formal, done) + (per-prime lift = the known FF Hodge index) + (the GLOBAL local-to-global assembly = the gap). The attempt crosses the first two and localizes the third precisely: the zeros live in the analytic continuation (Re(s)<1), invisible to the local Euler/orbit data (converges only Re(s)>1). The gap is the signed pairing realizing the continuation = the missing Weil cohomology (M3/#25), unchanged.

2CC.3 ([e2cc3_q_lift_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc3_q_lift_attempt.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc3_q_lift_attempt.md)) is an honest ATTEMPT at probe (a): un-idempotent-ize the C-C square (the proposed fix for 2CC's idempotency = no signed pairing). It decomposes the q-lift and crosses the formal/per-prime parts but not the central global part.

**(1) Un-idempotent-ization -- formal, DONE.** Tropical `max` is the `beta->inf` limit of the soft-max `a (+)_beta b = (1/beta) log(e^{beta a}+e^{beta b})`; for finite beta it is NOT idempotent (`a(+)_beta a = a + (log2)/beta != a`, verified), so subtraction / a Grothendieck completion exists. The 2CC idempotency obstruction is FORMALLY REMOVABLE; a signed pairing becomes possible. **(2) Per-prime lift -- KNOWN.** At a single scale q the un-idempotent-ized form IS the finite-q function-field Hodge index (2G): `Delta.Gamma = q+1-t`, signature `(1,3) <=> |t| < 2g sqrt(q)`; it carries the trace. The obstruction is GLOBAL: Spec(Z) has no single q (#25/2Q). **(3) Global half -- THE GAP.** The zeros (the H^1 whose signature is RH) live in the analytic continuation Re(s)<1, where the C-C local data cannot reach: the Euler product / orbit spectrum `prod_p (1-p^-s)^-1 = sum Lambda(n) n^-s` converges only for Re(s)>1 (no zeros there); on the critical line it does NOT converge (generic s=1/2+20i: partial products oscillate 1.30, 1.05, 1.84, 0.40, no limit, since `sum_p p^{-1/2}` diverges). So zeta on Re(s)=1/2 comes from the continuation, not the local product, and the local C-C/orbit data is BLIND to the zeros. Realizing the continuation as a signed pairing on the H^1 is the missing Weil cohomology = M3/#25.

**Verdict.** The q-lift decomposes into un-idempotent-ization (formal, done) + per-prime FF lift (known) + the GLOBAL local-to-global assembly (the gap, unchanged). The attempt's value is LOCALIZING the gap precisely: it is not the idempotency per se (removable) nor the per-prime structure (known), but the local-to-global continuation -- turning the prime/orbit data (Re(s)>1) into the global zeros (Re(s)=1/2) with a signed pairing. The archimedean place (A_arch #34; the Gamma-factor intrinsic to the C-C site, reading note #5) is the carrier of the continuation; the gap is its signed pairing. K2: D-H has no Euler product => no local orbit data to lift (#41). Consistent with the session: the realization/trace/local data (even the un-idempotent-ization) is buildable; the SIGNATURE -- the global positivity on the H^1 encoding |alpha|=sqrt q / Re(rho)=1/2 -- is the irreducible content the local tropical/orbit structure cannot supply. HONEST: probe (a) is NOT crossed; the attempt sharpens/localizes the gap, no new theorem. The "Euler product blind to the zeros" is the classical Re(s)>1 convergence fact, here tied to the q-lift decomposition.

### 41. Direction 8 / the q-lift (refines #40): the Connes-Consani self-composition Id_eps (Psi(lambda) o Psi(lambda^-1), Thm 7.7) DOES carry the von Mangoldt / -zeta'/zeta spectrum -- but as the TRACE (the flow-generator determinant), not the signature. It refines 2CC: the DIAGONAL self-intersection is trace-rich where the off-diagonal point-count froze. The q-lift gap (trace -> signature) is unchanged.

2CC.2 ([e2cc2_ideps_vonmangoldt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc2_ideps_vonmangoldt.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc2_ideps_vonmangoldt.md)) probes the DIAGONAL self-intersection where 2CC (#40) probed the off-diagonal. Connes-Consani Thm 7.7: the self-composition of Frobenius correspondences is `Psi(lambda) o Psi(lambda^-1) = Id_eps`, the tangential deformation of the identity (`l_eps(q^n)=q^{(1+eps)n}`), the analytic shadow of `Gamma_S^2` (reading note point #4), which 2R pinned to `-zeta'/zeta = sum_n Lambda(n) n^{-s}`.

**The identification.** The Id_eps tangent is `d/deps [l_eps(q^n)/r_eps(q^n)]|_0 = n log q` -- the NUMBER / SCALING operator `n`, which is the Deninger-Hesselholt flow generator `Theta` (`q^Theta = Fr`, #29). And `-d/ds log det_inf(s-Theta) = -d/ds log prod_p (1-p^{-s})^{-1} = -zeta'/zeta = sum_n Lambda(n) n^{-s}`. The C-C multiplicative composition `Fr_{1,p}^k = Fr_{1,p^k}` (slope p^k) is the iterate structure of the primitive orbit at `p`: the prime slopes `{p}` are the primitive periodic orbits, log-scales `{log p}`. So Id_eps carries the full von Mangoldt arithmetic.

**Computed.** (1) `prod_p (1-p^{-s})^{-1} = zeta(2)` (rel err 4.6e-6, converging); `-zeta'/zeta = sum Lambda(n) n^{-s}`, von Mangoldt prime-power-supported (0 off-prime-power leaks below 200). Reproduces 2R, tied to the C-C composition law. (2) CONTRAST refining 2CC: off-diagonal `Delta.Gamma_p = p-1` (t-frozen tropical shadow) vs self-intersection coefficient `Lambda(p) = log p` (trace-rich) -- the diagonal sees the arithmetic the off-diagonal lost. (3) K2: `Lambda_DH` delocalizes (mass 37.4 off prime powers vs 36.9 on, n<=60, first leak n=6 -- matches 2R/#20 exactly) => no primitive orbit structure => no Id_eps / flow spectrum.

**Honest.** Id_eps carries the von Mangoldt sum as a determinant/TRACE (the realization of zeta as a flow-determinant, the EASY half, #30), NOT a Hodge-index SIGNATURE; RH is the signature of the H^1/TP_odd/numerator (2S), still absent (2CC: no signed pairing). The Id_eps = Theta identification is a structural reading (the tangent IS the number operator; identifying it with Deninger's generator is the natural/consistent reading per #29), not a theorem proved inside the C-C framework; the computed facts are the classical dynamical-zeta identities + the contrast + the D-H delocalization (reproducing 2R/#20). A connecting/refining coordinate: it locates the trace on the DIAGONAL (Id_eps) vs the frozen off-diagonal, connecting C-C <-> 2R <-> Deninger/Hesselholt, and confirms the trace/signature dichotomy from the self-intersection side. The q-lift gap -- turn the Id_eps trace into a Hodge-index signature (supply the numerator/H^1 carrying |alpha|=sqrt q) -- is unchanged.

### 40. Direction 8 (the product surface): the Connes-Consani square's natural mixed-volume form gives a Lorentzian (Hodge-index) signature FOR FREE by Alexandrov-Fenchel, but it is the characteristic-1 SHADOW -- arithmetic-blind, it loses the Frobenius trace t that carries RH. The signed-pairing gap is sharpened to "the arithmetic q-lift that restores t," with the (1,p) bidegree as its locus. (First numerical probe of the C-C square; ADVERSARY-checked, softened.)

2CC ([e2cc_tropical_shadow.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc_tropical_shadow.py), writeup [e2cc_tropical_shadow.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc_tropical_shadow.md)) is the first numerical probe of the Connes-Consani arithmetic-site square -- the `Spec(Z) x Spec(Z)` product surface Direction 8 chases (reading note Connes-Consani-2015). The square's structure sheaf `Conv_>=(Z x Z)` = convex Newton polygons under (convex-hull-union, Minkowski-sum); its operations are IDEMPOTENT (no subtraction), so the reading note isolated the gap as "no signed intersection number, hence no Hodge-index signature."

**The probe.** The Minkowski-sum structure carries a canonical bilinear form, the mixed area `V(A,B) = (Area(A+B)-Area(A)-Area(B))/2` (2D mixed volume). By the Alexandrov-Fenchel / Teissier-Khovanskii mixed Hodge index theorem it is LORENTZIAN (<=1 positive eigenvalue). So a Hodge-index signature exists for free on this form. **Honest framing (load-bearing, ADVERSARY-flagged):** mixed-volume = intersection-number is a TORIC theorem (BKK); the C-C square is a characteristic-1 TOPOS, not a toric variety, and its polygons are sheaf sections, not divisors -- so this is a PROPOSED shadow of the missing pairing, NOT a constructed intersection theory of the topos.

**Results.** (A) AF holds: mixed-area Gram of 6 lattice polygons has signature (1,1,4), exactly one positive eigenvalue, 0 Minkowski violations; ADVERSARY confirmed `<=1` positive across 2000 random collections. (B) The divisor shadow `{e=(1,0), f=(0,1), Delta=(1,1), Gamma=Fr_{1,p}(Delta)=(1,p)}` via edge-segment mixed areas EQUALS the function-field Gram `ff_gram(p, t=2, g=1)` with e<->f swapped -- it does NOT independently recover the (1,p) bidegree (it produces `{p,1}`, swapped) and the signature agreement is that relabeling. The robust content is t-BLINDNESS: the mixed form has NO free real parameter t (the value t=2 is the edge-segment representative's; full 2D polygons give other constants), so it can never be the continuum `q+1-t`. (C) The genuine FF Gram has `Delta.Gamma = q+1-t` and signature `(1,3) <=> |t| < 2g sqrt(q)` = Hasse-Weil = RH-for-C, which REQUIRES t; the shadow's `(1,3)` is UNCONDITIONAL (it reads (1,3) for t=2 and for t=100 alike), so passing it does NOT imply RH. (D) K2: D-H has no Euler product => no (1,p) bidegree (2Q/#25) => no `Gamma_p` polygon => no shadow at all (clean).

**The finding.** A canonical Lorentzian convex-geometry form exists for free on the C-C square, but it takes NO arithmetic input and is therefore RH-agnostic -- the TRIVIAL EXTREME of the soft-positivity pattern (#38 dBN kernel, #39 Rodgers-Tao functional, which at least take zeta as input; 2CC takes none). This RECONCILES with the reading note's "the signed pairing does not exist": the RH-agnostic convex-geometry shadow exists; the ARITHMETIC signed pairing carrying the trace t does NOT. So the Direction-8 gap is sharpened, not closed -- the RH content is the trace t, lost in the idempotent/tropical structure, and the missing object is the arithmetic q-LIFT that restores t (the "suitable Weil cohomology" Connes-Consani name, end of their section 4). The **(1,p) bidegree is the locus where that lift must inject t**: the shadow gets the degree data but not the point-count refinement `q+1-t`.

**Why a coordinate.** It realizes Direction 8 attack angle 4.A (tropical/AHK Hodge) on the SPECIFIC C-C surface and shows exactly why it is insufficient: 4.A's stated obstacle ("tropical lives in R^n not over Spec(Z)") is RESOLVED by the C-C square (objects live as polygons in R^2), but the resulting tropical Hodge index is arithmetic-blind -- so the real obstruction is the q-lift / trace, not the lack of a tropical model. And it triangulates the marginal-positivity thesis a third way this session (heat kernel / zero-dynamics / product-surface shadow all RH-agnostic; RH lives in the exact arithmetic). **Honest scope:** a PROPOSED shadow, not a constructed pairing; matches the FF Gram only at genus 1, single point t=2, e<->f swapped; the "free" signature is pure convex geometry (not progress toward RH, only a sharpening of where the arithmetic must enter). Next: the arithmetic q-lift -- deform the mixed-volume form (or build a Weil cohomology) whose `Delta.Gamma` is the point count `q+1-t`; candidate carriers are the `q->1` un-idempotent-ization and the `Psi(lambda)oPsi(lambda^-1)=Id_eps` tangential-deformation self-intersection (Connes-Consani Thm 7.7) vs 2R's `-zeta'/zeta`. References: Connes-Consani arXiv:1502.05580; Khovanskii-Teissier / Alexandrov-Fenchel; Adiprasito-Huh-Katz, Ann. Math. 188 (2018).

### 39. Sub-target 12.4 RESOLVED (bridge false, with reason): Rodgers-Tao's `Lambda >= 0` is NOT a proven fragment of the Weil/Hodge positivity. Its monotone functional is a log-gas (Dyson) Hamiltonian on the zeros -- RH-AGNOSTIC in sign, a Level-3 statistical object -- not the Weil quadratic form. The two share only the explicit formula's DOWNSTREAM statistics (pair correlation) as input. The proven half of marginal positivity lives at Level 3; the signature (Level 4) remains the irreducible target.

Following E_DBN1 (#38), sub-target 12.4 asked whether Rodgers-Tao's monotone functional (the one whose monotonicity proves the de Bruijn-Newman constant `Lambda >= 0`) can be IDENTIFIED with a fragment of the Weil/Rosati/Hodge positivity -- which would make `Lambda >= 0` a proven piece of the arithmetic Hodge standard conjecture and `Lambda <= 0` (= RH) the exact remaining fragment. A 3-agent equation-level literature survey (Rodgers-Tao internals; the Weil-form decomposition; existing bridges) settled it.

**The Rodgers-Tao functional (Rodgers-Tao 2020, arXiv:1801.05914, eqs (5),(6),(56)-(58)).** The backward-heat flow `d_t H_t = -d_zz H_t` moves the zeros `x_j(t)` by the repulsive gradient flow `dx_k/dt = 2 sum_{j!=k} 1/(x_k - x_j)`, which is the gradient flow of the **log-gas / Coulomb (Dyson) Hamiltonian** `H(t) = sum_{j!=k} log(1/|x_j - x_k|)`, with the monotonicity `dH/dt = -4 E(t)`, `E(t) = sum_{j!=k} 1/|x_j - x_k|^2 >= 0`. So `H(t)` is non-increasing and convex; the flow relaxes toward the equilibrium configuration (zeros at the Riemann-von Mangoldt mean-spacing arithmetic progression). This functional is a **function of the zero positions only**: NO archimedean/Gamma term, NO prime term, and it is NOT a quadratic form in a test function. It is a Level-3 (statistical-mechanics) object (Rodgers-Tao cite Dyson Brownian motion / Erdos-Schlein-Yau).

**The unconditional input is Level-3 statistics, not Weil positivity.** The proof assumes `Lambda < 0`, deduces that nearly all zero gaps would equal the mean (local equilibrium, eq (93)), and contradicts this with **Montgomery's pair correlation** + the **Conrey-Ghosh-Goldston-Gonek-Heath-Brown** small-gap theorem (a positive proportion of gaps `<= 0.77 x` mean). These ARE derived through the explicit formula (Montgomery's `F(alpha,T)` off-diagonal = the prime-power sum `sum_n Lambda(n) n^{-it}`), but they are consumed as pair-correlation / gap statistics, NOT as the Weil quadratic form. The Euler product / Weil positivity is never used as a positive-definite form.

**Why the bridge is false (the directional-mismatch crux).** The Weil form `W(f) = A_arch + P_fin - B_pole` (#20/#34) is a quadratic form in a test function pairing zeros against archimedean + prime data; its negative-eigenvalue count is exactly half the off-line zeros (Bombieri), and RH = "A_arch dominates the prime obstruction." Its easy/hard split is INTERNAL to one form. By contrast Rodgers-Tao's `dH/dt = -4E <= 0` holds **always** (E >= 0 unconditionally); the sign that carries RH (all zeros real) is INVISIBLE to E. **The RT functional is unconditional precisely because it is RH-AGNOSTIC -- the opposite of being "the easy half of Weil positivity."** This is exactly E_DBN1's finding (#38) that the `t=0` kernel positivity is RH-agnostic. And dBN's split (`Lambda >= 0` vs `Lambda <= 0`) is ACROSS two different arguments for two bounds on one number (de Bruijn real-zero deformation for `<= 1/2`; log-gas + pair correlation for `>= 0`), not an internal split of a single positivity. No unified de Branges / reproducing-kernel object carries both, and de Branges' positivity conditions are known to FAIL for zeta's kernel (Conrey-Li 1998, arXiv:math/9812166). Connes-Consani's proven archimedean Weil-positivity fragment (the Sonin-space trace, arXiv:2006.13771) exists but makes no contact with the dBN flow.

**Four-level placement (the clean coordinate).** `Lambda >= 0` is PROVEN from Level-3 statistics (pair correlation, gap moments, moments of S(t)); `RH = (Lambda <= 0)` is the Level-4 signature (Weil/Hodge positivity). The de Bruijn-Newman squeeze `0 <= Lambda <= 1/2` straddles the Level-3/Level-4 boundary, and Rodgers-Tao proved the Level-3 half. This is fully consistent with the project's structural commitment (the four-level framing): Level-3 statistics cannot close RH, and indeed `Lambda >= 0` is exactly the half that does NOT imply RH. So Direction 12's flow does NOT short-circuit to the signature, and the signature remains the irreducible target -- now even more sharply isolated.

**The only non-pre-empted, salvageable open target (BUILDER).** Via the Gaussian-smoothed explicit formula (Balanzario-Cardenas 2023, arXiv:2312.00108: a Gaussian-over-zeros sum = a Hermite-weighted von Mangoldt prime sum + an archimedean `log(xi/2pi)` term), rewrite the heat-flow zero-sum `H_t` as `arch(f_t) + prime(f_t)`, compute `dH/dt` in that representation, and test (D-H discipline) whether it reproduces a DISCRIMINATING sub-block of Bombieri's `W(f)`. **Prediction (from #38's suppression law):** it FAILS the D-H test -- the log-gas energy is archimedean-suppressed on the off-line obstruction to `~exp(-(pi/4) d gamma)`, confirming the RT functional is the RH-agnostic archimedean piece, NOT the discriminating fragment the bridge wanted. Low prior; high payoff only if it surprises. No `d/dt W(f_t)` monotonicity-of-the-Weil-functional statement exists in the literature -- a genuine gap.

**Net.** 12.4 is a confirmed negative coordinate: the RT-Weil identity is false, for a precise reason (RH-agnostic dynamical functional vs RH-carrying quadratic form), and it reinforces the whole program -- the proven half of marginal positivity is Level-3, the signature is Level-4, and they do not collapse. References: Rodgers-Tao, Forum Math Pi 8 (2020); Bombieri, *Weil's quadratic functional* (2000); Connes-Consani, Selecta 27 (2021); Conrey-Li, IMRN (2000); Balanzario-Cardenas, arXiv:2312.00108 (2023); Montgomery (1973); Conrey-Ghosh-Goldston-Gonek-Heath-Brown, Q.J.Math 36 (1985).

### 38. The de Bruijn-Newman / Pólya kernel positivity `Phi >= 0` is ORTHOGONAL to RH and is the SOFTEST detector in the project (the stealth window in the heat basis, with an exact suppression law `exp(-(pi/4) d gamma)`). Verified correct but substantially PRE-EMPTED by the dBN literature -- a confirmed coordinate that imports three references corroborating the marginal-positivity thesis, not new mathematics.

E_DBN1 ([criticality/e_dbn_kernel.py](criticality/e_dbn_kernel.py), writeup [e_dbn_kernel.md](criticality/e_dbn_kernel.md)) is Direction 12's first computational experiment, closing the heat-side gap that 3e deferred as "computationally heavy." Hook: the project's marginal-positivity thesis IS Newman's 1976 slogan ("RH, if true, is only barely so"), avatar `RH <=> Lambda = 0`, with `Lambda >= 0` a theorem (Rodgers-Tao 2018). de Bruijn's half (`Lambda <= 1/2`) rests on the Pólya kernel positivity `Phi >= 0` -- a theta/modularity fact, NOT the Euler product. The question: is `Phi >= 0` a face of the D-H discipline?

**Test (Bochner, the project's idiom).** `Xi_f(z) = (completed f)(1/2+iz)` is real, even, exp-decaying, so `Phi_f >= 0 <=> Xi_f` positive-definite `<=>` every Toeplitz `[Xi_f((j-k)d)]` PSD. Two rigorous one-sided FAIL witnesses: (a) Toeplitz min eig `< 0`; (b) `max_z |Xi(z)| > |Xi(0)|` (a PD function attains its max modulus at 0). Validated on zeta: reconstructed `Phi_zeta >= 0`, matches Pólya's closed form to shape error 4.4e-16.

**Result (clean degree-1 comparison; D-H & Dirichlet are entire, no `s(s-1)` factor confound).** zeta, chi3, chi4 (Euler) AND Davenport-Heilbronn (non-Euler, RH FALSE) show NO resolvable failure of `Phi >= 0` (`max|Xi|/Xi0 = 1.000`, Toeplitz PSD to the floor). The canonical RH-violator is indistinguishable from zeta on kernel positivity, so `Phi >= 0` does NOT discriminate RH. **Why:** an off-line zero at height `gamma` enters `Phi` only at the archimedean-suppressed level `exp(-(pi/4) d gamma)`, because `|Xi(1/2+iz)| ~ exp(-(pi/4) d |z|)` (the Gamma factor). Measured: D-H decay rate ~0.79 ~ pi/4, `|Xi_DH(85.7)| = 1.5e-29`. So D-H's RH violation is buried ~29 orders down -- the stealth window (#18/#19/#34) in the heat basis, the SOFTEST detector yet (raw Weil Gram saw off-line at 2.6%; the kernel at ~1e-29). **Degree-2 corroboration:** `Phi >= 0` is moreover orthogonal to RH -- with the canonical `1/2 s(s-1)` factor the RH-TRUE Epstein-principal BREAKS PD (`max|Xi|/Xi0 = 1.85`, factor-induced, decomposes 2.125 x 0.109, not a zero effect) while RH-FALSE non-principal passes; bare, both pass.

**Consequence.** de Bruijn's `Lambda <= 1/2` uses `Phi >= 0` together with the kernel's super-exp decay; all RH content is the gap `(0, 1/2]` = the FLOW (sign of Lambda), not the t=0 kernel. **K2 (D-H discipline): FAIL** for any route built on `Phi >= 0`. K1: the flow positivity that would reach `Lambda <= 0` is governed by `Xi` (the primes via the explicit formula), funneling back to the signature ("all roads to the signature"). This VINDICATES Direction 12's focus on the flow and EXPLAINS its difficulty (the cheap t=0 object is K2-failing and RH-orthogonal; the Polymath15-style flow is forced).

**Verification (workflow, 4 agents).** Independent re-implementation (dps=40, no shared code paths) reproduced every number: D-H FE `Lambda(s)=Lambda(1-s)` to 1e-41 (eps=+1), `max|Xi_DH|/Xi0 = 1.000000` verified to z=90 (past the off-line height), Toeplitz PSD (wide grids reaching z=90 give strictly positive min eig), off-line zero independently refined to `0.80852 + 85.6993i`. Adversarial math-check: PASS (sound-with-caveats); fixed a degree-2 decay-rate precision-floor artifact (true rate ~1.5 -> pi/2, so the law holds BETTER than first reported) and softened "D-H satisfies Phi>=0" to "no resolvable failure."

**Literature (honest framing; the big verification finding).** The conclusion is correct but PRE-EMPTED. (i) **Dobner 2020** (arXiv:2005.05142, Acta Arith.) proves `Lambda_F >= 0` for the entire extended Selberg class `S#`, which DROPS the Euler axiom and so INCLUDES Davenport-Heilbronn; the kernel is never assumed `>= 0`, and "mock xi" functions (any Dirichlet series x any Gamma factor) have off-line-zero deformations for all t<0. His Lemma 1 IS the suppression law. (ii) **Newman-Wu 2019** (arXiv:1901.06596) exhibit nonnegative kernels with EMPTY real-zero set -- the abstract "Phi>=0 orthogonal to real-rootedness" -- and the 3-fact decomposition isolating `Phi>=0` as powering only `Lambda<=1/2`. (iii) **Michalowski 2026** (arXiv:2602.20313, Feb) is CONVERGENT independent work: same kernel, same Toeplitz-minor/mpmath-interval method, proves it is not PF_5 and states it "should not be confused with Lambda." Also Stopple 2013, Andrade-Chang-Miller 2013 (generalized Lambda for Dirichlet/function-field/automorphic L). **Project-own durable contributions:** the `Phi>=0`-as-a-face-of-the-D-H-discipline / K2-FAIL packaging; the quantitative detector-resolution law `exp(-(pi/4) d gamma)` tied to the Weil-Gram 2.6% figure; the explicit D-H numerics (D-H is outside the named dBN literature); the synthesis to "all roads to the signature."

**Net.** A confirmed negative coordinate consistent with everything: kernel positivity is a Level-3 soft property that cannot close RH, and the literature independently agrees. It retires the cheap t=0 kernel as an RH test, sharpens the stealth window to an exact law, imports three corroborating references, and isolates a Level-4 follow-up: **can Rodgers-Tao's proven `Lambda >= 0` Lyapunov functional be identified with a fragment of the Weil/Hodge positivity, making half the two-sided squeeze `0 <= Lambda <= 1/2` a proven piece of the signature?**

### 37. The 8B/8C conjecture "absolute prismatic cohomology forces P_comp = 0" is VACUOUS for zeta and INAPPLICABLE to D-H: it is not the real Direction 8 gap, and M3 did not advance the geometric/signature program. (Thread 2 of the M3 stress test.)

This is the structural reading of M3 once the discriminator (#35, #36) is in hand. The 8B/8C docs proposed proving, as the load-bearing geometric step, that the canonical prismatic filtration kills the composite cross-terms (`P_comp = 0`), so that the hand-deletion in M_euler is a geometric fact. Worked through, that step dissolves:

1. **`P_comp = 0` <=> Euler product, by definition.** `P_comp` is built from the composite-supported part of the von-Mangoldt-analogue coefficients (`-L'/L`). Those coefficients are prime-power-supported iff `L` has an Euler product. Confirmed numerically: `||P_comp|| = 0` exactly for zeta, chi3, chi4 (Euler), and `!= 0` for D-H, Epstein (no Euler).

2. **For zeta, `P_comp = 0` is a THEOREM (the Euler product), needing no geometry.** Hence `M(zeta) = M_euler(zeta)` identically (the experiments show the two columns equal for zeta). The conjecture "absolute prismatic cohomology forces `P_comp = 0`" is therefore VACUOUS as applied to zeta: it would re-derive a known triviality. It adds zero leverage for RH, the only L-function we actually need.

3. **For D-H / Epstein there is no surface at all**, by the project's own K2/2Q discipline (#25): no Euler product => no local `(1,p)` bidegrees => no Frobenius correspondence `Gamma_S` => no `Spec(Z) x Spec(Z)`. So `M_euler` for D-H is NOT "the geometric trace with `P_comp` killed by geometry"; it is a number obtained by forcibly applying zeta's prime-power template to D-H's data. It is the trace of no geometric object. `M_euler(DH) < 0` is a discriminator reading, not a failed Arakelov bound on an existing surface.

4. **Therefore M3 reorganized bookkeeping; it did not add geometric content.** For zeta -- the only input with a (conjectural) surface -- `M_euler = M` was already known and positive at M2.5 (#33). Splitting off `P_comp` changes nothing on the zeta side. The genuine Direction 8 gap is exactly the pre-M3 one: construct `Gamma_S` / the surface for zeta and prove its cup-product Hodge-index signature is positive. M_euler is at most a convenient numerical TARGET (the positive number a future construction must reproduce), not a new sub-theorem.

**Consequence for the docs and the ladder.** 8B/8C should NOT list "prove `P_comp = 0` geometrically" as a milestone; it is vacuous-or-inapplicable. The honest milestones are unchanged from before M3. Net assessment of M3 after both stress-test threads: a validated numerical RH discriminator (#36), with NO advance on the geometric signature it was framed as delivering. This is the marginal-positivity compass again -- the soft/bookkeeping move does not engage the exact structure; only constructing the surface for zeta will.

### 36. M3.3: a SECOND off-line control was constructed and verified, and M_euler fires negative on it -- removing the single-EXAMPLE fragility of #35 (though not the single-FAMILY one). (Thread 1 of the M3 stress test.)

3M3 ([e3m3_second_offline.py](positivity/e3m3_second_offline.py)) answers #35's gating question by building a Davenport-Heilbronn-TYPE function at a NEW conductor and verifying every property from scratch before M_euler is allowed to see it. `DHTypeL(q, chi)` forms the real-coefficient combination `c_n = Re chi(n) + kappa Im chi(n)` (period q, `c_1 = 1`) of a primitive complex odd character chi mod q and its conjugate; `kappa` is PINNED numerically so the completed `Lambda_f(s) = (q/pi)^{(s+1)/2} Gamma((s+1)/2) f(s)` satisfies an FE, then the FE is VERIFIED as an identity at independent points.

**Construction validated.** `DHTypeL(5)` reconstructs `kappa = -3.5201 = -1/0.28408`, the conjugate FE branch of the classical Davenport-Heilbronn `kappa = 0.28408` (the two branches are related by `kappa -> -1/kappa`, i.e. the two root-number signs); FE residual `~1e-45`. So the constructor reproduces the literature.

**The q=7 control (order-6 character).** `kappa = -1.50335`, `W = -1`, `a_1 = 1`, FE residual `~1e-16` at the pinning scale and `~1e-51..1e-63` verified at independent generic points (a genuine identity). Off-line zero exhibited and high-precision-verified at `rho = 1.0616 + 28.443 i` (`|f(rho)| ~ 2e-50`; FE partner `1 - rho` also a zero): RH is FALSE for it. (Zeros with `Re > 1` are normal for non-Euler functions -- no Euler product to forbid them.)

**Result.** `min eig(M_euler) = -0.8395 < 0` on the q=7 control, matching its RH-false status. So M_euler now fires correctly on TWO off-line witnesses (q=5 and q=7), plus stays positive on three Euler inputs (zeta, chi3, chi4) and one non-Euler RH-true input (principal Epstein d=47): a clean 7-for-7 across independent inputs.

**Honest scope.** Both off-line witnesses are Davenport-Heilbronn-TYPE (character-combination) constructions, so this removes the literal single-EXAMPLE fragility of #35 but not the single-FAMILY one: the structurally independent off-line mechanism (Epstein) remains untestable (`a_1 = 0`, #35). And by #37 this strengthens M_euler only as a DISCRIMINATOR, not as geometry.

### 35. M3.2 stress-test of the M_euler discriminator: it survives the false-NEGATIVE probe (independent Euler products stay positive, P_comp = 0 exactly) but the false-POSITIVE probe is STRUCTURALLY UNAVAILABLE -- every Epstein off-line control has a_1 = 0, so M_euler's off-line discipline still rests on Davenport-Heilbronn alone.

3M2 ([e3m2_stress_test.py](positivity/e3m2_stress_test.py)) attacks the M3 (`e3m`) claim that `M_euler = A_arch + P_pp + B_pole` is "the true geometric RH test". M_euler is formed by deleting the composite block `P_comp` by hand; for a non-Euler `L` that deletion equals the Euler-product assumption, and M3's three controls (zeta, D-H, principal Epstein) were favorable. The stress test adds the controls M3 omitted and reuses e3m's validated blocks, so any sign behavior is attributable to the L-data, not a kernel change.

**PROBE A (false negative) DEFEATED.** The two independent real Dirichlet L-functions chi3 (cond 3) and chi4 (cond 4) are genuine Euler products with GRH believed. Both give `min eig(M_euler) > 0` (+0.067, +0.065) with `||P_comp|| ~ 1e-31` (machine zero, as an Euler product must). So A_arch dominating the prime-power block is NOT a zeta-specific accident: it holds for genuine Euler products other than zeta. This is real positive evidence for the M_euler construction on the Euler side, and the cleanest new content of the stress test.

**PROBE B (false positive) STRUCTURALLY LIMITED, not passed.** The decisive independent off-line test would be the non-principal Epstein form of d=47 (off-line zero at `rho ~ 0.634 + 32.05 i`, RH FALSE), which shares the EXACT archimedean setup of the principal d=47 form e3m found positive (+0.676). But it cannot be run: off-line Epstein zeros occur ONLY for non-principal reduced forms (`a >= 2`), which do not represent 1, so `a_1 = r_Q(1) = 0` and the von-Mangoldt recursion `a_n log n = sum_{d|n} Lambda(d) a_{n/d}` (divide by `a_1`) is undefined. Both Eps47_off and Eps15_off return UNDEFINED. This was noted in passing at #32 ("Epstein d47 non-principal excluded: a_1=0") but never reconciled with the M3 "stealth window BROKEN" framing: M3's discrimination, like the raw-Gram detectors before it, still validates its negative reading on Davenport-Heilbronn ALONE. The Epstein hardening of the D-H discipline (3L #19, 3B.4 #22), which the project added precisely to stop resting on one example, does NOT transfer to M_euler.

**Net.** M_euler is strengthened on the Euler side (PROBE A) and remains a single-example detector on the off-line side (PROBE B unavailable). It does NOT prove the Direction 8B/8C conjecture (`P_comp = 0` geometrically), but PROBE A removes the crude "Euler-assumption-in-disguise" objection for Euler inputs. **New gap (now the gating question for M3->8):** construct a SECOND integer-supported (`a_1 != 0`) off-line control -- a Davenport-Heilbronn-type linear combination at a different conductor, or any engineered Dirichlet series with a functional equation, `a_1 = 1`, and a known off-line zero -- and confirm `M_euler < 0` on it. Until that exists, "M_euler detects RH" is supported by exactly one off-line witness. This is the marginal-positivity thesis again: the discriminator must be tested against the exact off-line structure, and the Epstein route cannot supply it.

### 34. Milestone M2.6: the general-mu archimedean Bombieri kernel is now CORRECT and validated (`C_mu = log pi - psi((1+mu)/2)`), but the four-way RH-vs-Euler discrimination via raw min-eig FAILS -- D-H reads spuriously POSITIVE because its off-line obstruction sits below the marginal-positivity cancellation floor (the stealth window, now on the non-circular Rosati side). Consequence: M3 must be ANALYTIC, not a finer numerical truncation.

2W ([e2w_rosati_fourway_M2_6.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2w_rosati_fourway_M2_6.py)) supplies the M2.5 control gate and runs the four-way verdict.

**Technical success (the M2.6 fix).** The M2.5 control bug (#33) was the archimedean constant's psi-argument: `C_mu = log pi - psi(1/2+mu)` (wrong; coincides with the truth only at mu=0) should be `C_mu = log pi - psi((1+mu)/2)`. Verified: mu=0 gives `log pi - psi(1/2) = log 4pi + gamma_E` (3F); mu=1 gives `log pi - psi(1) = log pi + gamma_E ~ 1.722`, pinned to 6 digits against the frequency block for D-H. The error was diagnosed entrywise: it was EXACTLY proportional to `f_ij(1)` (error/f1 ~ 0.615 across diagonal AND off-diagonal), so a single scalar (the constant), not the kernel. With the fix, D-H's block residual against the zero-side Gram drops from 5.58 to ~0.12 and decreases with T_max (validated). The general-mu archimedean Bombieri kernel is now correct for every Gamma factor.

**Scientific result (honest, the verdict).** With validated non-circular blocks, `min eig(A_arch + P_fin + B_pole)`: zeta `+0.035` POS (RH, correct), D-H `+0.094` **POS** -- WRONG SIGN (D-H fails RH, should be indefinite). NO SEPARATION. Reason: D-H's off-line obstruction is at height `gamma ~ 85.7` and is only ~2.6% of the raw Weil spectrum (3D.3), while the test functions (`b <= 6`, scales `log b <= 1.79`) and the reconstruction-residual floor (~0.1) are far above it. The truncated NON-CIRCULAR form is blind to the off-line zeros -- the SAME stealth window as #18/#19 (raw Gram, `eps < 10^-5` float64) and #27 (Jensen basis), now confirmed on the non-circular Rosati side. The raw min-eig certificate is necessary-not-sufficient AND non-discriminating: the distinguishing signal sits below the marginal-positivity cancellation floor. Amplifying it (3J Schur complement, x30 to -78.7%) is an ANSWER-side (zero-location) move = circular.

**Consequence for the program.** M3 cannot be a finer numerical truncation; it must be ANALYTIC -- prove the archimedean-dominates-prime domination engaging the EXACT off-line structure (the Euler product / the precise Gamma-factor cancellation), which is what distinguishes zeta from D-H below the floor. This is the marginal-positivity thesis localized onto the Rosati side: no soft/numerical certificate separates zeta from D-H; the proof must use exact zeta structure. M2.5 (zeta positive, validated) + M2.6 (kernel fixed; discrimination blocked by the stealth window) together pin precisely what M3 must achieve and why it is hard. The technical infrastructure (correct non-circular Weil form for any Gamma factor) is now complete and validated; the open problem is the analytic domination, where it has always been (#20/3M's "archimedean block must dominate the prime obstruction" = the hard Weil/Connes core).

### 33. Milestone M2.5: the archimedean block A_arch fixed (validated Bombieri physical-space form), the diagnostic corrected (min eig, not the pencil balance), and the NON-CIRCULAR Weil form for zeta confirmed POSITIVE. Two corrections to #32, one genuine certificate, one honestly-deferred control.

2V ([e2v_rosati_balance_M2_5.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2v_rosati_balance_M2_5.py)) executes the named gate of #32. Three things happened, all kept honest by validation:

**(1) A_arch fixed and VALIDATED.** Ported 3F's validated Bombieri PHYSICAL-space archimedean integral into the matrix as a bilinear (cross-b) block: `A_ij = 2 log Q f_ij(1) + sum_mu[-C_mu f_ij(1) - INT g_mu(u) du]`, `f_ij(x)=x^{-1/2} overlap(L_i,L_j,log x)`, integrated in `u=log x` (uniform; kink splits at `|L_i-L_j|`, `L_i+L_j`). For mu=0 this is 3F-exact (`C_0 = log4pi+gamma_E = log pi - psi(1/2)`). KEY VALIDATION: the residual `||A_bomb+P+B - M_zero(T)||/||M_zero||` for zeta DECREASES monotonically with T_max (0.17 at T=200 -> 0.025 at T=2000), proving `A_bomb` is EXACT -- the apparent "error" of #32 was the zero-side Gram's own truncation (missing zeros above T_max), not the archimedean block. The physical-space integrand is non-oscillatory (unlike the digamma-frequency version), which is why it is accurate.

**(2) The diagnostic corrected.** #32's "archimedean-dominance balance" (generalized eigenvalue of the pencil `(-(P+B), A_arch)`) was the WRONG diagnostic: it presumes `A_arch` is positive-definite (the "cushion"), but `A_arch` has negative diagonal entries (it is not PD). The correct, framework-clean diagnostic is simply `min eig(A_arch + P_fin + B_pole)`: the reconstructed Weil form `M = A+P+B` equals `sum_rho Phi(rho)^2 >= 0` for zeta (sum of squares), so `min eig(M) > 0` is RH-compatible positivity, computed NON-CIRCULARLY (no zeros).

**(3) zeta certificate (validated).** `min eig(A_bomb + P_fin + B_pole) = +0.035` (stable +0.03..+0.05 across K=5..8) `> 0`: the non-circular reconstructed Weil form for zeta is positive. This is M2.5's deliverable -- a non-circular positivity certificate for zeta with a VALIDATED archimedean block. HONEST SCOPE: this is necessary-not-sufficient for RH (it is the finite-K, finite-prime truncation of the infinite-dimensional target positivity; a proof needs the archimedean-dominates-prime domination for ALL test functions, the M3/M4 analytic step). It confirms the truncated arithmetic Rosati form is positive, consistent with RH, and removes the #32 contamination.

**(4) Control DEFERRED honestly.** D-H's archimedean block uses a general-`mu`=1 Bombieri kernel (`C_mu = log pi - psi(1/2+mu)`, kernel `x^{-mu}`) that is MIS-NORMALIZED (residual 5.58, vs zeta's validated block): so D-H's `min eig = +0.25` is NOT trustworthy and the four-way RH-vs-Euler verdict CANNOT be rendered. Epstein d47-principal (non-Euler, RH-holds) shows `min eig = +0.69 > 0` (provisional; its mu>0 kernel is also unvalidated) -- IF it holds up under a validated kernel, a positive Epstein-principal would side with zeta and prove the certificate is a TRUE RH detector, not the #20 non-Euler trap. **Named next step M2.6:** derive and validate the general-`mu` archimedean Bombieri kernel so D-H (mu=1) and Epstein (mu in {0,1}) get trustworthy `min eig(M)`, making the four-way verdict decisive. zeta (mu=0, 3F-exact) is the load-bearing case and is done.

Only then does the four-way RH-vs-Euler verdict (and M3, the polarization) become decisive.
